===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_tabs.js =====
/* Left-tab nav for cDECK extra panes. Does not touch the beige header or JACK'S MESH.
   Pattern from Cowork / Claude Code desktop / Cursor Agents: short left rail, one canvas. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  // Tabs that hold ONE working surface fill the fold instead of becoming a
  // draggable tile on a grid. Measured at 1600px: Model Rater each
  // held a single 336x208 card on 1434px of empty board, with the model rater's
  // token estimate clipped off the bottom of it. Surfaces is its own tab
  // (ROLD·ITC·GDX·ODX·TB1), not on Studio and not under System. Forge is the CCr + N-coder grid.
  // Recents and Clock joined the fill list for the same measured reason: both
  // hold ONE working surface that a 200px tile clips. Recents is 200 rows plus
  // the OPENED transcript the row fetches, and on the board the transcript card
  // was laid out below the fold of its own tile — the pane fetched it, painted
  // it, and the operator could not see it. Clock is a six-column table.
  var FILL_TABS = { forge: 1, studio: 1, review: 1,
                    models: 1, surfaces: 1,
                    voice: 1, profiles: 1, settings: 1, crucible: 1, diligence: 1,
                    docket: 1, ups: 1, differentiator: 1, website: 1,
                    orders: 1, gitur: 1,
                    recents: 1, clock: 1, runs: 1, backup: 1, tools: 1,
                    system: 1, open: 1 };
  var paneStoreKey = "cdeckPaneTab";
  var TABS = [
    { id: "studio", label: "Profile Studio", hint: "MOTIF graph · custom profiles · seats",
      ids: ["panel-studio"] },
    { id: "runs", label: "Runs", hint: "jobs · events · each clock · each daemon",
      ids: ["panel-runs", "panel-events"] },
    { id: "orders", label: "Orders", hint: "work orders · agents · product",
      ids: ["panel-orders"] },
    { id: "review", label: "Review", hint: "approvals · blockers · logins · catalog",
      ids: ["panel-review"] },
    { id: "gitur", label: "Gitur", hint: "GitHub · GitLab · Cursor · Forge→Implement · SGH",
      ids: ["panel-gitur"] },
    { id: "surfaces", label: "Surfaces", hint: "storage · channels · tools",
      ids: ["panel-surfaces"] },
    { id: "recents", label: "Sessions", hint: "Open Sessions · stripper · scan/load/diff",
      ids: ["home-nav", "nav-opened", "panel-session-kit",
            "panel-session-stripper", "panel-session-tools"] },
    { id: "voice", label: "Voice", hint: "SGH → GitHub → daemon → GDX → SGH",
      ids: ["home-voice"] },
    { id: "system", label: "System", hint: "health · spend · rails · fleet · nodes",
      ids: ["advanced"] },
    { id: "models", label: "Model Rater", hint: "roles · ratings · blend · porosity",
      ids: ["panel-model-rater", "panel-model-catalog", "panel-model-coverage"] },
    { id: "backup", label: "Backup", hint: "clock 07/11/19/23 · 10 MOTIF panes",
      ids: ["panel-backup", "panel-backup-verified", "panel-backup-local",
            "panel-backup-offsite", "panel-backup-surface_test",
            "panel-backup-generate", "panel-backup-scheduler",
            "panel-backup-search", "panel-backup-profiles",
            "panel-backup-restore"] },
    { id: "tools", label: "Tools", hint: "built modules · routes · local · PATH · leftover named",
      ids: ["panel-tool-catalog", "panel-tool-built", "panel-tool-routes",
            "panel-tool-local", "panel-tool-hands", "panel-tool-leftover",
            "panel-create", "panel-makers", "panel-tools"] },
    { id: "clock", label: "Clock", hint: "clocks · automations · pool",
      ids: ["panel-clocks", "home-auto", "home-pool"] },
    { id: "open", label: "Open", hint: "OpenWork · display · composer",
      ids: ["home-openwork", "home-display", "home-talk", "home-work", "home-todos"] },
    { id: "settings", label: "Settings", hint: "account · usage · subscription · pay",
      ids: ["panel-settings"] }
  ];
  var PROFILE_PAGES = [
    { id: "forge", label: "Forge", hint: "coding MOTIF setup",
      ids: ["pfPage-forge", "home-code", "panel-forge-ccr", "panel-forge-job", "panel-forge-adv"] },
    { id: "crucible", label: "Crucible", hint: "legal MOTIF setup",
      ids: ["pfPage-crucible"] },
    { id: "diligence", label: "Diligence", hint: "deal MOTIF setup",
      ids: ["pfPage-diligence"] },
    { id: "docket", label: "Docket", hint: "IP MOTIF setup",
      ids: ["pfPage-docket"] },
    { id: "ups", label: "UPS", hint: "physics MOTIF setup",
      ids: ["pfPage-ups"] },
    { id: "differentiator", label: "Differentiator", hint: "medical MOTIF setup",
      ids: ["pfPage-differentiator"] },
    { id: "website", label: "Website GC", hint: "site MOTIF setup",
      ids: ["pfPage-website"] }
  ];

  var visibleTab = "";

  function currentTab() {
    var stage = document.getElementById("deckStage");
    if (stage) {
      var onView = stage.querySelector(":scope > .deck-view.on");
      var fromView = onView && onView.getAttribute("data-tab");
      if (fromView) return fromView;
    }
    var rail = document.getElementById("deckTabs");
    if (rail) {
      var onBtn = rail.querySelector(".deck-tab.on");
      var fromBtn = onBtn && onBtn.getAttribute("data-tab");
      if (fromBtn) return fromBtn;
    }
    return visibleTab || "";
  }
  window.deckVisibleTab = currentTab;
  window.deckTabVisible = function (id) {
    var cur = currentTab();
    return !!cur && cur === id;
  };

  function showTab(id) {
    var stage = document.getElementById("deckStage");
    var tabs = document.getElementById("deckTabs");
    if (!stage || !tabs) return;
    var on = id || "studio";
    visibleTab = on;
    stage.querySelectorAll(":scope > .deck-view").forEach(function (v) {
      v.classList.toggle("on", v.getAttribute("data-tab") === on);
    });
    tabs.querySelectorAll(".deck-tab").forEach(function (b) {
      var hit = b.getAttribute("data-tab") === on;
      b.classList.toggle("on", hit);
      b.setAttribute("aria-selected", hit ? "true" : "false");
      if (hit && b.scrollIntoView) b.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest", inline: "nearest" });
    });
    try { localStorage.setItem(paneStoreKey, on); } catch (e) {}
  }
  window.showDeckTab = showTab;

  var PROFILE_TAB = {
    forge: "forge",
    crucible: "crucible",
    diligence: "diligence",
    docket: "docket",
    ups: "ups",
    differentiator: "differentiator",
    website: "website"
  };

  function instanceProfile() {
    try {
      // header.js owns the profile list; this is the fallback when the tab
      // shell is loaded without it.
      if (typeof window.cdeckProfile === "function") {
        var owned = window.cdeckProfile();
        if (PROFILE_TAB[owned]) return owned;
      }
      var stamped = String(window.__CDECK_PROFILE__ || "").toLowerCase();
      if (PROFILE_TAB[stamped]) return stamped;
      var q = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search || "");
      var p = String(q.get("profile") || "").toLowerCase();
      if (PROFILE_TAB[p]) return p;
    } catch (e) {}
    return "";
  }
  window.deckInstanceProfile = instanceProfile;

  window.initDeckTabs = function () {
    var body = document.getElementById("cdeck-more-body");
    if (!body || body.querySelector(".deck-shell")) return;

    var shell = document.createElement("div");
    shell.className = "deck-shell";
    var nav = document.createElement("nav");
    nav.className = "deck-tabs";
    nav.id = "deckTabs";
    nav.setAttribute("aria-label", "cDeck pane tabs");
    var stage = document.createElement("div");
    stage.className = "deck-stage";
    stage.id = "deckStage";

    TABS.forEach(function (t) {
      if (t.id === "recents") {
        var gap = document.createElement("div");
        gap.className = "deck-tab-gap";
        gap.textContent = "ops";
        nav.appendChild(gap);
      }
      var b = document.createElement("button");
      b.type = "button";
      b.className = "deck-tab";
      b.setAttribute("data-tab", t.id);
      b.setAttribute("role", "tab");
      b.title = t.hint;
      b.innerHTML = t.label + "<small>" + t.hint + "</small>";
      b.addEventListener("click", function () { showTab(t.id); });
      nav.appendChild(b);
      var view = document.createElement("div");
      view.className = "deck-view";
      view.setAttribute("data-tab", t.id);
      t.ids.forEach(function (nid) {
        var el = document.getElementById(nid);
        if (el) view.appendChild(el);
      });
      stage.appendChild(view);
    });

    while (body.firstChild) stage.appendChild(body.firstChild);
    var leftover = document.createElement("div");
    leftover.className = "deck-view";
    leftover.setAttribute("data-tab", "settings");
    Array.prototype.slice.call(stage.childNodes).forEach(function (n) {
      if (n.nodeType === 1 && !n.classList.contains("deck-view")) leftover.appendChild(n);
    });
    var settingsView = stage.querySelector('.deck-view[data-tab="settings"]');
    if (leftover.childNodes.length && settingsView) {
      while (leftover.firstChild) settingsView.appendChild(leftover.firstChild);
    } else if (leftover.childNodes.length) {
      stage.appendChild(leftover);
    }

    var pgap = document.createElement("div");
    pgap.className = "deck-tab-gap";
    pgap.textContent = "skins";
    nav.appendChild(pgap);
    var pb = document.createElement("button");
    pb.type = "button";
    pb.className = "deck-tab deck-tab-profiles";
    pb.setAttribute("data-tab", "profiles");
    pb.setAttribute("role", "tab");
    pb.title = "stream schematic · occupancy skins";
    pb.innerHTML = "<b>Profiles</b><small>occupancy skins</small>";
    pb.addEventListener("click", function () { showTab("profiles"); });
    nav.appendChild(pb);
    var pview = document.createElement("div");
    pview.className = "deck-view";
    pview.setAttribute("data-tab", "profiles");
    var pEl = document.getElementById("panel-profiles");
    if (pEl) pview.appendChild(pEl);
    stage.appendChild(pview);

    PROFILE_PAGES.forEach(function (t) {
      var b = document.createElement("button");
      b.type = "button";
      b.className = "deck-tab deck-tab-profile";
      b.setAttribute("data-tab", t.id);
      b.setAttribute("role", "tab");
      b.title = t.hint;
      b.innerHTML = t.label + "<small>" + t.hint + "</small>";
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        showTab(t.id);
        if (window.openProfilePage) window.openProfilePage(t.id);
      });
      nav.appendChild(b);
      var view = document.createElement("div");
      view.className = "deck-view";
      view.setAttribute("data-tab", t.id);
      t.ids.forEach(function (nid) {
        var el = document.getElementById(nid);
        if (el) view.appendChild(el);
      });
      stage.appendChild(view);
    });

    ["edge-left", "edge-right"].forEach(function (id) {
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.style.display = "none";
    });
    var orch = document.getElementById("orch");
    if (orch && !orch.querySelector("[id]")) orch.style.display = "none";

    shell.appendChild(nav);
    shell.appendChild(stage);
    body.appendChild(shell);

    var inst = instanceProfile();
    paneStoreKey = inst ? ("cdeckPaneTab:" + inst) : "cdeckPaneTab";
    var want = inst ? (PROFILE_TAB[inst] || "studio") : "studio";
    try {
      var stampedPane = String(window.__CDECK_PANE__ || "").toLowerCase();
      if (stampedPane) want = stampedPane;
      else if (!inst) want = localStorage.getItem(paneStoreKey) || want;
    } catch (e) {}
    if (want === "jobs") want = "runs";
    if (want === "sessions") want = "recents";
    if (want === "prompts") want = "models";
    if (want === "talk") want = "open";
    if (want === "more") want = "settings";
    if (!stage.querySelector('.deck-view[data-tab="' + want + '"]')) want = "studio";
    if (inst) {
      var brand = document.querySelector("#cdeck-head h1 small");
      if (brand) brand.textContent = inst;
    }
    showTab(want);
    initPaneBoard();
  };

  var GRID = 16;
  var MIN_W = 320;
  var MIN_H = 240;
  var GEOM_KEY = "cdeckPaneBoard:v4";
  function geomStoreKey() {
    var p = instanceProfile();
    return p ? (GEOM_KEY + ":" + p) : GEOM_KEY;
  }
  var _drag = null;

  function snap(n, min) {
    var x = Math.round(Number(n) / GRID) * GRID;
    if (min && x < min) x = Math.ceil(min / GRID) * GRID;
    return x;
  }

  function loadGeom() {
    try {
      var raw = localStorage.getItem(geomStoreKey());
      var o = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : {};
      return o && typeof o === "object" ? o : {};
    } catch (e) { return {}; }
  }

  function saveGeom(g) {
    try { localStorage.setItem(geomStoreKey(), JSON.stringify(g)); } catch (e) {}
  }

  function tilesIn(view) {
    var out = [];
    function take(el) {
      if (!el || !el.id || el.classList.contains("pane-rh")) return;
      out.push(el);
    }
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(view.children, function (el) {
      if (el.classList && el.classList.contains("advanced")) {
        var grid = el.querySelector(".grid") || el;
        Array.prototype.forEach.call(grid.children, function (p) {
          if (p.id && (p.classList.contains("panel") || p.classList.contains("home-card") || p.tagName === "SECTION")) take(p);
        });
      } else if (el.id) take(el);
    });
    return out;
  }

  function applyTile(el, rec) {
    el.classList.add("pane-tile");
    el.style.position = "absolute";
    el.style.left = rec.x + "px";
    el.style.top = rec.y + "px";
    el.style.width = rec.w + "px";
    el.style.height = rec.h + "px";
    el.style.margin = "0";
  }

  function growBoard(view) {
    var max = 240;
    tilesIn(view).forEach(function (el) {
      var y = parseInt(el.style.top, 10) || 0;
      var h = parseInt(el.style.height, 10) || el.offsetHeight || 0;
      if (y + h > max) max = y + h;
    });
    view.style.minHeight = (max + GRID * 2) + "px";
    var adv = view.querySelector(".advanced");
    if (adv) {
      adv.style.position = "relative";
      adv.style.minHeight = view.style.minHeight;
      var grid = adv.querySelector(".grid");
      if (grid) {
        grid.style.display = "block";
        grid.style.position = "relative";
        grid.style.minHeight = view.style.minHeight;
        grid.style.height = view.style.minHeight;
      }
    }
  }

  function defaultLayout(view) {
    // A view with `display:none` measures 0, and the old `|| 640` fallback wrote
    // that guess straight into localStorage, where it stuck: measured in Chrome
    // at 1600px, every tab except the one showing at boot kept 304px columns
    // forever, because a record with an `x` is never recomputed. Lay a board out
    // only when it can be measured — showTab() calls back once it is on screen.
    var avail = view.clientWidth;
    if (!avail) return;
    var tiles = tilesIn(view);
    var sys = view.getAttribute("data-tab") === "system";
    var runs = view.getAttribute("data-tab") === "runs";
    var bak = view.getAttribute("data-tab") === "backup";
    var recents = view.getAttribute("data-tab") === "recents";
    var clk = view.getAttribute("data-tab") === "clock";
    var cols = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, Math.floor(avail / (MIN_W + GRID * 4))));
    if (sys) cols = Math.max(1, Math.floor(cols / 2) || 1);
    if (runs) cols = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, cols));
    if (bak) cols = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, cols));
    if (recents) cols = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, cols));
    if (clk) cols = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, cols));
    var colW = snap(Math.max(MIN_W, (avail - GRID * (cols - 1)) / cols - GRID), MIN_W);
    var rowPitch = sys ? 500 : ((runs || bak || recents || clk) ? 440 : 380);
    var tileH = sys ? 480 : ((runs || bak || recents || clk) ? 420 : 360);
    var fillOne = !!(FILL_TABS[view.getAttribute("data-tab")] && tiles.length === 1);
    if (fillOne) {
      colW = snap(Math.max(MIN_W, avail - GRID), MIN_W);
      tileH = snap(Math.max(MIN_H, view.clientHeight || 320), MIN_H);
    }
    var g = loadGeom();
    tiles.forEach(function (el, i) {
      var col = i % cols;
      var row = Math.floor(i / cols);
      var rec = g[el.id];
      if (rec && rec.user) {
        applyTile(el, rec);
        ensureHandles(el);
        return;
      }
      if (sys && rec && !rec.user) rec = null;
      if (!rec || rec.x == null) {
        rec = {
          x: fillOne ? 0 : snap(col * (colW + GRID), 0),
          y: fillOne ? 0 : snap(row * rowPitch, 0),
          w: colW,
          h: snap(tileH, MIN_H)
        };
        g[el.id] = rec;
      }
      applyTile(el, rec);
      ensureHandles(el);
    });
    saveGeom(g);
    growBoard(view);
  }

  function ensureHandles(el) {
    if (el.querySelector(":scope > .pane-rh")) return;
    [["se", "pane-rh pane-rh-se"], ["e", "pane-rh pane-rh-e"], ["s", "pane-rh pane-rh-s"]].forEach(function (pair) {
      var h = document.createElement("div");
      h.className = pair[1];
      h.setAttribute("data-rh", pair[0]);
      h.title = "drag to resize · double-click to reset";
      h.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
      el.appendChild(h);
    });
    var hd = el.querySelector(".panel-hd") || el.querySelector("h2");
    if (hd) hd.classList.add("pane-drag");
  }

  function writeTile(el) {
    var g = loadGeom();
    g[el.id] = {
      x: snap(parseInt(el.style.left, 10) || 0, 0),
      y: snap(parseInt(el.style.top, 10) || 0, 0),
      w: snap(parseInt(el.style.width, 10) || el.offsetWidth, MIN_W),
      h: snap(parseInt(el.style.height, 10) || el.offsetHeight, MIN_H),
      user: true
    };
    applyTile(el, g[el.id]);
    saveGeom(g);
    growBoard(el.closest(".deck-view") || el.parentNode);
  }

  function bindBoard() {
    if (bindBoard._on) return;
    bindBoard._on = true;
    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", function (e) {
      if (e.button != null && e.button !== 0) return;
      var t = e.target;
      if (!t || !t.closest) return;
      if (!t.closest("#cdeck-more")) return;
      if (t.closest("button, a, input, textarea, select, .mr-table, .deck-tab")) return;
      var rh = t.closest(".pane-rh");
      var tile = t.closest(".pane-tile");
      if (rh && rh.parentNode) {
        var el = rh.parentNode;
        var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        e.preventDefault();
        e.stopPropagation();
        _drag = {
          kind: "resize", mode: rh.getAttribute("data-rh") || "se",
          el: el, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY, w: r.width, h: r.height,
          left: parseInt(el.style.left, 10) || 0, top: parseInt(el.style.top, 10) || 0
        };
        try { rh.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
        return;
      }
      if (!tile) return;
      var grip = t.closest(".pane-drag, .panel-hd, h2");
      if (!grip || !tile.contains(grip)) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      _drag = {
        kind: "move", el: tile, x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
        left: parseInt(tile.style.left, 10) || 0,
        top: parseInt(tile.style.top, 10) || 0, armed: false
      };
      try { tile.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
    });
    document.addEventListener("pointermove", function (e) {
      if (!_drag) return;
      var d = _drag;
      var dx = e.clientX - d.x;
      var dy = e.clientY - d.y;
      if (d.kind === "move") {
        if (!d.armed) {
          if (Math.abs(dx) < 4 && Math.abs(dy) < 4) return;
          d.armed = true;
          d.el.classList.add("pane-moving");
        }
        d.el.style.left = snap(Math.max(0, d.left + dx), 0) + "px";
        d.el.style.top = snap(Math.max(0, d.top + dy), 0) + "px";
        d.el.style.zIndex = "5";
        return;
      }
      var w = d.w;
      var h = d.h;
      if (d.mode === "e" || d.mode === "se") w = snap(Math.max(MIN_W, d.w + dx), MIN_W);
      if (d.mode === "s" || d.mode === "se") h = snap(Math.max(MIN_H, d.h + dy), MIN_H);
      d.el.style.width = w + "px";
      d.el.style.height = h + "px";
    });
    function end() {
      if (!_drag) return;
      var el = _drag.el;
      var kind = _drag.kind;
      var armed = _drag.armed;
      _drag = null;
      el.classList.remove("pane-moving");
      el.style.zIndex = "";
      if (kind === "move" && !armed) return;
      writeTile(el);
    }
    document.addEventListener("pointerup", end, true);
    document.addEventListener("pointercancel", end, true);
    document.addEventListener("dblclick", function (e) {
      var rh = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest(".pane-rh");
      if (!rh || !rh.parentNode || !rh.parentNode.classList.contains("pane-tile")) return;
      var el = rh.parentNode;
      var g = loadGeom();
      delete g[el.id];
      saveGeom(g);
      var view = el.closest(".deck-view");
      if (view) defaultLayout(view);
    });
  }

  function wantsBoard(view) {
    // Every extra-pane view is a persist board. Fill-tabs with one tile
    // default to the fold; user move/resize is rec.user and survives reload.
    return !!(view && tilesIn(view).length);
  }

  function initPaneBoard() {
    bindBoard();
    document.querySelectorAll("#cdeck-more .deck-view").forEach(function (view) {
      if (!wantsBoard(view)) {
        view.classList.add("pane-fill");
        view.classList.remove("pane-board");
        return;
      }
      view.classList.remove("pane-fill");
      view.classList.add("pane-board");
      defaultLayout(view);
    });
  }

  var _show = showTab;
  var settingsReturnTab = "studio";
  showTab = function (id) {
    var tab = id || "studio";
    if (tab === "settings") {
      var cur = currentTab();
      if (cur && cur !== "settings") settingsReturnTab = cur;
      if (window.openDeckSettingsPopup) window.openDeckSettingsPopup();
      else _show("settings");
      if (window.cdeckKickSnapshots) window.cdeckKickSnapshots("settings");
      if (window.cdeckRefreshVisibleKit) window.cdeckRefreshVisibleKit("settings");
      return;
    }
    settingsReturnTab = tab;
    if (window.closeDeckSettingsPopup) window.closeDeckSettingsPopup(false);
    if (id !== "profiles" && window.unmountProfileSkin) window.unmountProfileSkin();
    _show(id);
    if (tab === "gitur" && typeof window.deckGiturShown === "function") {
      window.deckGiturShown();
    }
    var view = document.querySelector('#deckStage > .deck-view[data-tab="' + tab + '"]');
    if (view && wantsBoard(view)) {
      view.classList.remove("pane-fill");
      view.classList.add("pane-board");
      defaultLayout(view);
    }
    if (window.cdeckKickSnapshots) window.cdeckKickSnapshots(tab);
    if (window.cdeckRefreshVisibleKit) window.cdeckRefreshVisibleKit(tab);
  };
  window.showDeckTab = showTab;
  window.deckSettingsReturnTab = function () { return settingsReturnTab || "studio"; };
  window.layoutDeckView = function (id) {
    var view = document.querySelector('#deckStage > .deck-view[data-tab="' + (id || "") + '"]');
    if (!view || _drag) return;
    view.classList.remove("pane-fill");
    view.classList.add("pane-board");
    var g = loadGeom();
    var tiles = tilesIn(view);
    var missing = tiles.some(function (el) {
      return !g[el.id] || g[el.id].x == null;
    });
    if (missing) {
      defaultLayout(view);
      return;
    }
    tiles.forEach(function (el) {
      applyTile(el, g[el.id]);
      ensureHandles(el);
    });
    growBoard(view);
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/header.js =====
/* Beige half-head bar — tool / info / launch pad over the KDash cockpit. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function shell() {
    var t = window.__TAURI__;
    if (t && t.core && typeof t.core.invoke === "function") return t.core.invoke.bind(t.core);
    return null;
  }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  function chipValue(el) {
    if (typeof el === "string") el = $(el);
    if (!el) return "";
    var tag = el.tagName;
    if (tag === "SELECT" || tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA") return el.value;
    var on = el.querySelector(".chip.on");
    if (on) {
      return on.getAttribute("data-val") || on.getAttribute("data-via") ||
        on.getAttribute("data-type") || "";
    }
    return "";
  }
  window.chipValue = chipValue;
  document.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
    var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest(".mr-chiprow > .chip");
    if (!b) return;
    var row = b.parentNode;
    if (!row || !row.classList.contains("mr-chiprow")) return;
    row.querySelectorAll(":scope > .chip").forEach(function (x) {
      x.classList.toggle("on", x === b);
    });
  });

  var cfg = { base: "http://127.0.0.1:8770", token: "" };
  // COSMOS2 profiles (docs/PROFILES.md), same list normalize_profile() keeps in
  // Rust. A profile is a skin on one Core, not a second Core.
  var CDECK_PROFILES = ["forge", "crucible", "diligence", "docket", "ups", "differentiator", "website"];

  /* Which instance this window is. "" is the main window. A profile window is
     stamped by open_profile_window before first paint; /cdeck/ over HTTP reads
     ?profile= instead. */
  function cdeckProfile() {
    try {
      var stamped = String(window.__CDECK_PROFILE__ || "").toLowerCase();
      if (CDECK_PROFILES.indexOf(stamped) >= 0) return stamped;
      var q = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search || "");
      var p = String(q.get("profile") || "").toLowerCase();
      if (CDECK_PROFILES.indexOf(p) >= 0) return p;
    } catch (e) {}
    return "";
  }
  window.cdeckProfile = cdeckProfile;

  // tree_id is whatever GET /api/v1/status answered with in THIS window. Never
  // carried over from the opener, never defaulted.
  var inst = { profile: cdeckProfile(), treeId: null };
  var paused = false;
  var refreshS = 5;
  var nextAt = Date.now() + refreshS * 1000;
  var createKind = "AGENT";
  var lastRtt = null;

  var DEFAULT_PILLS = [
    { own: "F03-G4", kind: "$", col: "#ff9d5c", key: "f03-g46" },
    { own: "F03-PR", kind: "$", col: "#ff9d5c", key: "f03-probe" },
    { own: "GEM-AP", kind: "$", col: "#ff9d5c", key: "gem-api" },
    { own: "GW-API", kind: "$", col: "#ff9d5c", key: "gw-api" },
    { own: "CLA", kind: "Q", col: "#d97757", posted: true },
    { own: "GRO", kind: "Q", col: "#ffffff", posted: true },
    { own: "SGH", kind: "Q", col: "#ffffff", posted: true, prepaid: true }
  ];

  function meterPill(d) {
    var nodata = d.pct == null && (d.used == null || !d.cap);
    if (nodata) {
      return '<span class="mtr nodata" title="' + esc(d.own + "·" + d.kind) + ' — NO DATA">' +
        '<u style="color:' + d.col + '">' + esc(d.own + "·" + d.kind) + "</u>" +
        '<span class="mb"><i style="width:100%;background:#3a4a63"></i></span>' +
        "<b>--</b></span>";
    }
    var pct = d.pct != null ? d.pct : Math.round(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, 1 - d.used / d.cap)) * 100);
    var col = pct > 50 ? "#1a7f37" : pct > 20 ? "#9a6700" : "#cf222e";
    return '<span class="mtr' + (d.posted ? " posted" : "") + '" title="' + esc(d.own) + '">' +
      '<u style="color:' + d.col + '">' + esc(d.own + "·" + d.kind) + "</u>" +
      '<span class="mb"><i style="width:' + pct + "%;background:" + col + '"></i></span>' +
      '<b style="color:' + col + '">' + pct + "%</b></span>";
  }

  function renderMeters(spend) {
    var rack = $("mtrRack");
    if (!rack) return;
    var rails = (spend && spend.rails) || {};
    var rows = DEFAULT_PILLS.map(function (p) {
      var d = { own: p.own, kind: p.kind, col: p.col, posted: p.posted, prepaid: p.prepaid };
      if (p.key && rails[p.key]) {
        d.used = rails[p.key].used_usd;
        d.cap = rails[p.key].cap_usd;
      }
      return d;
    });
    rack.innerHTML = rows.map(meterPill).join("");
  }
  renderMeters(null);

  function setConn(text, cls) {
    var el = $("connState");
    if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = text;
    el.className = cls || "";
  }

  function api(path, opts) {
    var inv = shell();
    var method = (opts && opts.method) || "GET";
    var body = opts && opts.body !== undefined ? opts.body : null;
    if (inv) {
      return inv("api_request", {
        method: method, path: path, serverUrl: cfg.base,
        bearer: cfg.token || null, body: body
      }).then(function (r) {
        if (!r || !r.ok) throw new Error("HTTP " + ((r && r.status) || "?"));
        return r.json;
      });
    }
    var headers = { Accept: "application/json" };
    if (cfg.token) headers.Authorization = "Bearer " + cfg.token;
    var init = { method: method, headers: headers, cache: "no-store" };
    if (body) { headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json"; init.body = JSON.stringify(body); }
    var t0 = performance.now();
    return fetch(cfg.base.replace(/\/+$/, "") + path, init).then(function (r) {
      lastRtt = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error("HTTP " + r.status);
      return r.json();
    });
  }
  window.cdeckApi = api;

  function kitForTab(tab) {
    if (tab === "settings") return window.initDeckSettings;
    // Profile MOTIF child tabs share the profiles kit so INSTANCE Crucible
    // paints pfPage-crucible instead of a dead shell.
    if (tab === "profiles"
        || tab === "crucible" || tab === "diligence" || tab === "docket"
        || tab === "ups" || tab === "differentiator" || tab === "website") {
      return window.initDeckProfiles;
    }
    if (tab === "backup") return window.initDeckBackup;
    if (tab === "recents") return window.initDeckSessionKit;
    if (tab === "orders") return window.initDeckOrders;
    if (tab === "gitur") return window.initDeckGitur;
    if (tab === "forge") return window.initDeckForge;
    if (tab === "studio" || tab === "runs" || tab === "review") return window.initDeckStudio;
    if (tab === "models") return window.initModelRater;
    return null;
  }
  function refreshVisibleKit(tab) {
    var fn = kitForTab(tab);
    if (typeof fn === "function") fn(api);
  }
  window.cdeckRefreshVisibleKit = refreshVisibleKit;
  function refreshDeckPanes() {
    // Names stay listed so CONNECT pins hold. Only the visible tab's kit
    // actually inits — hidden extra panes do not GET until shown.
    var tab = typeof window.deckVisibleTab === "function" ? window.deckVisibleTab() : "";
    var want = kitForTab(tab);
    [
      window.initDeckSettings,
      window.initDeckProfiles,
      window.initDeckBackup,
      window.initDeckSessionKit,
      window.initDeckOrders,
      window.initDeckGitur,
      window.initDeckForge,
      window.initDeckStudio,
      window.initModelRater
    ].forEach(function (fn) {
      if (typeof fn !== "function") return;
      if (!tab || fn !== want) return;
      fn(api);
    });
  }
  window.cdeckRefreshPanes = refreshDeckPanes;

  function paintTransport() {
    var el = $("transport");
    if (el) el.textContent = "transport: " + (shell() ? "native shell" : "fetch");
  }
  paintTransport();

  function shortBase(u) {
    return String(u || "").replace(/^https?:\/\//, "").replace(/\/+$/, "");
  }

  function treeIdOf(st) {
    var t = st && typeof st.tree_id === "string" ? st.tree_id.trim() : "";
    return t || null;
  }

  /* The instance chip is the operator's answer to "which window am I, and is it
     really the same Core?". tree comes from /status or reads UNMEASURED; peer
     reads NO_HOST until Keith names a federated address. */
  function paintInstance() {
    var el = $("instanceChip");
    if (!el) return;
    var who = inst.profile || "main";
    el.textContent =
      "INST " + who +
      " · tree " + (inst.treeId || "UNMEASURED") +
      " · peer NO_HOST";
    el.title =
      "cDeck instance " + who + " · Core " + shortBase(cfg.base) +
      " · tree " + (inst.treeId || "UNMEASURED — no /api/v1/status answer yet") +
      " · peer NO_HOST — federation has no named address yet";
    el.classList.toggle("bound", !!inst.treeId);
  }

  /* INSTANCE opens a native second window. /cdeck/ over HTTP is one view of the
     same Core — it says so rather than faking a window. */
  function paintInstanceMode() {
    var btn = $("btnInstance");
    if (!btn) return;
    var native = !!shell();
    btn.classList.toggle("nonative", !native);
    btn.title = native
      ? "Open a parallel profile window (Forge or Crucible). Same pattern as Grok.com + OpenWork. Same Core :8770, not a second Core. Federation-ready; does not invent a peer host."
      : "INSTANCE is native cDeck only. /cdeck/ over HTTP is one view of the same Core — open the desktop deck for a second window.";
  }

  if (inst.profile) {
    try {
      document.title = "cDeck — " + inst.profile.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + inst.profile.slice(1);
    } catch (e) {}
    var pick = $("instanceProfile");
    if (pick) pick.value = inst.profile;
  }
  // A profile window inherits the Core its opener was on, so both windows land
  // on one Core instead of the child snapping back to the saved default.
  (function () {
    var stamped = String(window.__CDECK_CORE_BASE__ || "").trim();
    var baseEl = $("apiBase");
    if (!stamped || !baseEl) return;
    cfg.base = stamped;
    baseEl.value = stamped;
    baseEl.dataset.touched = "1";
  })();
  paintInstance();
  paintInstanceMode();

  function connect() {
    var baseEl = $("apiBase");
    var tokEl = $("token");
    var was = cfg.base;
    if (baseEl) cfg.base = (baseEl.value || "").trim() || cfg.base;
    if (tokEl) cfg.token = (tokEl.value || "").trim();
    // A tree read off the old Core is not this Core's tree.
    if (cfg.base !== was) inst.treeId = null;
    setConn("connecting…", "wait");
    var t0 = performance.now();
    var inv = shell();
    var go = function (tok) {
      if (tok && !cfg.token) {
        cfg.token = tok;
        if (tokEl && !tokEl.value) tokEl.value = tok;
      }
      return api("/api/v1/status").then(function (st) {
        lastRtt = Math.round(performance.now() - t0);
        var ready = st && st.ready;
        inst.treeId = treeIdOf(st);
        paintInstance();
        setConn(
          (ready ? "connected" : "answered") + " · " + cfg.base + " · rtt " + lastRtt + "ms",
          ready ? "ok" : "wait"
        );
        var pause = $("btnPause");
        if (pause) pause.disabled = false;
        paused = false;
        if (pause) pause.textContent = "PAUSE";
        api("/api/v1/spend").then(renderMeters).catch(function () { renderMeters(null); });
        tickMesh();
        refreshDeckPanes();
      });
    };
    paintInstance();
    if (inv && !cfg.token) {
      return inv("load_config").then(function (c) {
        if (c && (c.serverUrl || c.server_url) && baseEl && !baseEl.dataset.touched) {
          cfg.base = c.serverUrl || c.server_url;
          baseEl.value = cfg.base;
        }
        return inv("load_deck");
      }).then(function (deck) {
        var root = deck && deck.runtimeRoot ? String(deck.runtimeRoot).trim() : "";
        if (!root) return go("");
        return inv("load_install_token", { root: root }).then(go, function () { return go(""); });
      }).catch(function (e) {
        setConn(String(e && e.message || e), "bad");
      });
    }
    return go(cfg.token).catch(function (e) {
      setConn(String(e && e.message || e), "bad");
    });
  }

  if ($("btnConnect")) $("btnConnect").addEventListener("click", connect);
  ["apiBase", "token"].forEach(function (id) {
    if ($(id)) $(id).addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
      if (e.key === "Enter") connect();
    });
  });
  if ($("apiBase")) $("apiBase").addEventListener("input", function () { this.dataset.touched = "1"; });

  if ($("btnPause")) $("btnPause").addEventListener("click", function () {
    paused = !paused;
    this.textContent = paused ? "RESUME" : "PAUSE";
    $("countdown").classList.toggle("paused", paused);
  });
  if ($("btnReload")) $("btnReload").addEventListener("click", function () { location.reload(); });
  if ($("instancePicks")) {
    $("instancePicks").addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("[data-profile]") : null;
      if (!b) return;
      var profile = b.getAttribute("data-profile") || "forge";
      if ($("instanceProfile")) $("instanceProfile").value = profile;
      $("instancePicks").querySelectorAll("[data-profile]").forEach(function (x) {
        x.classList.toggle("on", x === b);
      });
    });
  }
  if ($("btnInstance")) $("btnInstance").addEventListener("click", function () {
    var inv = shell();
    var profile = (($("instanceProfile") && $("instanceProfile").value) || "forge");
    if (!inv) {
      setConn("INSTANCE is native cDeck — /cdeck/ HTTP is one view, not a second window", "wait");
      return;
    }
    inv("open_profile_window", {
      profile: profile,
      coreBase: cfg.base,
      treeId: inst.treeId || null
    }).then(function (rec) {
      setConn(
        "instance " + (rec && rec.profile || profile) +
          " · " + (rec && rec.core_base || cfg.base) +
          " · tree " + ((rec && rec.tree_id) || "UNMEASURED") +
          (rec && rec.peer ? " · peer " + rec.peer : " · peer NO_HOST"),
        "ok"
      );
    }).catch(function (e) {
      setConn(String(e && e.message || e), "bad");
    });
  });

  setInterval(function () {
    var cd = $("countdown");
    if (!cd) return;
    if (paused) { cd.textContent = "paused"; return; }
    var left = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((nextAt - Date.now()) / 1000));
    cd.textContent = "next refresh in " + left + "s";
    if (Date.now() >= nextAt) {
      nextAt = Date.now() + refreshS * 1000;
      var st = $("connState");
      if (st && st.className === "ok") {
        api("/api/v1/status").then(function (s) {
          inst.treeId = treeIdOf(s);
          paintInstance();
          if (lastRtt != null) {
            setConn("connected · " + cfg.base + " · rtt " + lastRtt + "ms", "ok");
          }
          tickMesh();
        }).catch(function (e) { setConn(String(e && e.message || e), "bad"); });
      }
    }
  }, 250);

  document.querySelectorAll("#cdeck-head .typesize button").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", function () {
      var map = { s: "12pt", m: "16pt", l: "20pt" };
      var head = $("cdeck-head");
      if (head) head.style.setProperty("--deck-pt", map[b.getAttribute("data-type")] || "12pt");
      document.querySelectorAll("#cdeck-head .typesize button").forEach(function (x) {
        x.classList.toggle("on", x === b);
      });
    });
  });

  document.querySelectorAll("#cdeck-head .top-kind").forEach(function (b) {
    b.addEventListener("click", function () {
      createKind = b.getAttribute("data-kind") || "AGENT";
      document.querySelectorAll("#cdeck-head .top-kind").forEach(function (x) {
        x.classList.toggle("on", x === b);
      });
    });
  });

  if ($("topModels")) $("topModels").addEventListener("click", function () {
    if (window.openModelRater) window.openModelRater();
    else setConn("MODEL RATER pane not loaded yet", "wait");
  });

  function openAttached(pane) {
    var inv = shell();
    if (inv) {
      inv("open_pane_window", { pane: pane }).then(function (rec) {
        setConn("attached " + (rec && rec.pane || pane) + " · " + (rec && rec.label || ""), "ok");
      }).catch(function (e) {
        setConn(String((e && e.message) || e), "bad");
        if (window.showDeckTab) {
          window.showDeckTab(pane === "backup" ? "backup"
            : (pane === "talk" || pane === "open" || pane === "openwork" ? "open" : "recents"));
        }
      });
      return;
    }
    if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab(pane === "backup" ? "backup"
      : (pane === "talk" || pane === "open" || pane === "openwork" ? "open" : "recents"));
    setConn("SESSION/BACKUP/TALK needs native cDeck for an attached window — opened the extra pane", "wait");
  }
  function ensureOrcPicker() {
    if ($("orcStreamBox")) return $("orcStreamBox");
    var box = document.createElement("div");
    box.id = "orcStreamBox";
    box.className = "orc-stream-box";
    box.hidden = true;
    box.innerHTML =
      '<div class="orc-stream-card">' +
        "<p>ORC stream to load. A good TidyUP SEED BootUPs as-is. A partial " +
        "or crashed close runs TU + TU2, reconstructs BU, closes that recovery, " +
        "then starts a new session with the carry-over.</p>" +
        '<div class="studio-targets" id="orcStreamPick">' +
          '<label><input type="radio" name="orcStream" value="Cm" checked> Cm — COSMOS</label>' +
          '<label><input type="radio" name="orcStream" value="plumbing"> plumbing</label>' +
          '<label><input type="radio" name="orcStream" value="physics"> physics</label>' +
          '<label><input type="radio" name="orcStream" value="chapter"> chapter</label>' +
          '<label><input type="radio" name="orcStream" value="legal"> legal</label>' +
        "</div>" +
        '<div class="orc-stream-actions">' +
          '<button type="button" id="orcStreamGo">Load</button>' +
          '<button type="button" id="orcStreamCancel">Cancel</button>' +
        "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny" id="orcStreamSay"></div>' +
      "</div>";
    var head = $("cdeck-head") || document.body;
    head.appendChild(box);
    $("orcStreamCancel").addEventListener("click", function () {
      box.hidden = true;
    });
    $("orcStreamGo").addEventListener("click", function () {
      var hit = document.querySelector('#orcStreamPick input[name="orcStream"]:checked');
      var stream = (hit && hit.value) || "Cm";
      var say = $("orcStreamSay");
      if (say) say.textContent = "POST /api/v1/orc …";
      api("/api/v1/orc", { method: "POST", body: { stream: stream } }).then(function (rec) {
        if (say) {
          say.textContent = (rec && rec.kind ? rec.kind : "ok") +
            (rec && rec.sid ? " · " + rec.sid : "") +
            (rec && rec.note ? " — " + rec.note : "");
        }
        setConn(
          "ORC " + stream + " · " + ((rec && rec.kind) || "started") +
            (rec && rec.recovered ? " · recovered via TU/TU2" : ""),
          "ok"
        );
        setTimeout(function () { box.hidden = true; }, 1600);
      }).catch(function (e) {
        if (say) say.textContent = "REFUSED — " + String(e && e.message || e);
        setConn(String(e && e.message || e), "bad");
      });
    });
    return box;
  }

  function showOrcStreamPicker() {
    var box = ensureOrcPicker();
    box.hidden = false;
    var say = $("orcStreamSay");
    if (say) say.textContent = "GET /api/v1/orc";
    api("/api/v1/orc").then(function (rec) {
      if (say) {
        say.textContent = (rec && rec.kind ? rec.kind : "UNMEASURED") +
          (rec && rec.seed ? " · SEED " + rec.seed : "") +
          (rec && rec.running ? " · running " + rec.running : "");
      }
    }).catch(function (e) {
      if (say) say.textContent = "inspect UNMEASURED — " + String(e && e.message || e);
    });
  }

  function runHeaderOpenwork() {
    var inv = shell();
    if (!inv) {
      setConn("OpenWork OPEN needs native cDeck — this browser window cannot snap or spawn", "wait");
      return;
    }
    setConn("OpenWork …", "wait");
    inv("open_openwork").then(function (rec) {
      var bits = (rec && rec.action || "ok");
      if (rec && rec.snapped) bits += " · snapped right";
      if (rec && rec.live_session) bits += " · live with cDeck";
      if (rec && rec.detail) bits += " — " + rec.detail;
      setConn(bits, rec && rec.action === "refused" ? "bad" : "ok");
      if (rec && rec.needs_stream) showOrcStreamPicker();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      setConn(String(e && e.message || e), "bad");
    });
  }
  window.runHeaderOpenwork = runHeaderOpenwork;
  window.showOrcStreamPicker = showOrcStreamPicker;

  if ($("topTalk")) $("topTalk").addEventListener("click", function () {
    runHeaderOpenwork();
  });
  function runHeaderOrc() {
    var inv = shell();
    if (!inv) {
      setConn("ORC OPEN needs native cDeck — this browser window cannot focus OpenWork.exe", "wait");
      return Promise.resolve(null);
    }
    setConn("ORC …", "wait");
    return inv("open_orc").then(function (rec) {
      setConn((rec && rec.detail) || (rec && rec.action) || "ORC",
        rec && rec.action === "refused" ? "bad" : "ok");
      return rec;
    }).catch(function (e) {
      setConn(String(e && e.message || e), "bad");
      throw e;
    });
  }
  // Open-tab GFO·ORC OPEN reuses this — same open_orc as topOrc; no second orch.
  window.runHeaderOrc = runHeaderOrc;
  function runHeaderCcr() {
    var inv = shell();
    if (!inv) {
      setConn("CCr OPEN needs native cDeck — this browser window cannot focus grok.exe", "wait");
      return;
    }
    setConn("CCr …", "wait");
    inv("open_ccr").then(function (rec) {
      setConn((rec && rec.detail) || (rec && rec.action) || "CCr",
        rec && rec.action === "refused" ? "bad" : "ok");
    }).catch(function (e) {
      setConn(String(e && e.message || e), "bad");
    });
  }
  if ($("topOrc")) $("topOrc").addEventListener("click", runHeaderOrc);
  if ($("topCcr")) $("topCcr").addEventListener("click", runHeaderCcr);
  if ($("topOpenSession")) $("topOpenSession").addEventListener("click", function () {
    openAttached("recents");
  });
  if ($("topBackup")) $("topBackup").addEventListener("click", function () {
    openAttached("backup");
  });
  if ($("btnStream")) $("btnStream").addEventListener("click", function () {
    openAttached("recents");
  });

  function paintMeshLed(cls, title, label) {
    var el = $("meshLed");
    if (!el) return;
    el.className = "mesh-led " + (cls || "");
    el.textContent = label || "MESH";
    if (title) el.title = title;
  }
  function railShort(r) {
    var id = String((r && (r.link_id || r.id || r.rail_type)) || "?");
    var parts = id.replace(/^https?:\/\//, "").split(/[\/:_-]/).filter(Boolean);
    var last = parts.length ? parts[parts.length - 1] : id;
    return last.slice(0, 8).toUpperCase();
  }
  function paintRailLeds(rows) {
    var box = $("railLeds");
    if (!box) return;
    if (!rows || !rows.length) {
      box.innerHTML = '<span class="rail-led" title="GET /rails unread — keys stay on the rail">RAILS</span>';
      box.title = "GET /rails unread — keys stay on the rail. Green = registered, red = FAIL, amber = unread.";
      return;
    }
    box.innerHTML = rows.slice(0, 12).map(function (r) {
      var v = r.verified;
      var cls = v === true ? "ok" : (v === false ? "bad" : "warn");
      var meaning = v === true ? "registered" : (v === false ? "FAIL" : "UNKNOWN");
      return '<span class="rail-led ' + cls + '" title="' +
        esc((r.link_id || r.id || "?") + " · " + meaning +
          " — green registered, red FAIL, amber unread") +
        '">' + esc(railShort(r)) + "</span>";
    }).join("");
    box.title = "Each chip is one rail from GET /rails. Green = registered, red = FAIL, amber = unread.";
  }
  function tickMesh() {
    // Counts from GET /jobs ({id: state}), not GET /jukebox. A 1s /jukebox
    // poll folded the scheduler ledger on every header tick, filled Core's
    // accept queue, and RUNS reported the server down while /jobs still
    // answered. RUNS already polls /jukebox on its 10s pane clock.
    // Stays /jobs + /rails. System tab owns GET /health — not this tick.
    // Do not add GET /health here — health is System-tab only (SNAPSHOT_TAB).
    api("/api/v1/jobs").then(function (rec) {
      var jobs = (rec && rec.jobs) || {};
      var running = 0, broke = 0, findings = 0;
      Object.keys(jobs).forEach(function (id) {
        var st = String(jobs[id] || "").toUpperCase();
        if (st === "RUNNING") running += 1;
        if (st === "BROKE") broke += 1;
        if (st === "FINDINGS") findings += 1;
      });
      var cls = "ok";
      if (broke) cls = "bad on";
      else if (findings) cls = "warn on";
      else if (running) cls = "ok on";
      var lab = broke ? ("BROKE " + broke)
        : (findings ? ("FIND " + findings)
          : (running ? (running + " RUN") : "MESH"));
      paintMeshLed(cls,
        "MESH jobs from GET /jobs (not invented). Green = RUNNING, amber = FINDINGS, red = BROKE, grey = idle/unread. " +
          "RUNNING " + running + " · FINDINGS " + findings + " · BROKE " + broke,
        lab);
    }).catch(function () { paintMeshLed("", "MESH UNMEASURED — GET /jobs did not answer", "MESH"); });
    api("/api/v1/rails").then(function (rec) {
      paintRailLeds((rec && (rec.matrix || rec.rails)) || []);
    }).catch(function () { paintRailLeds([]); });
  }

  var PANEL_TAB = {
    "panel-status": "system",
    "panel-health": "system",
    "panel-spend": "system",
    "panel-fleet": "system",
    "panel-nodemap": "system",
    "panel-surfaces": "surfaces",
    "advanced": "system"
  };
  document.querySelectorAll("#cdeck-head .toplink").forEach(function (a) {
    a.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var key = (a.getAttribute("href") || "").replace(/^#/, "");
      var tab = PANEL_TAB[key];
      if (tab && window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab(tab);
      var el = document.getElementById(key);
      if (el && el.scrollIntoView) el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "nearest" });
    });
  });

  var head = $("cdeck-head");
  var brand = head && head.querySelector("h1");
  function applyCollapse() {
    if (!head) return;
    var on = localStorage.getItem("cdeckHeadCollapsed") === "1";
    head.classList.toggle("collapsed", on);
    if (brand) brand.title = on ? "expand header" : "collapse header";
  }
  if (brand) {
    brand.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      var on = localStorage.getItem("cdeckHeadCollapsed") === "1";
      localStorage.setItem("cdeckHeadCollapsed", on ? "0" : "1");
      applyCollapse();
    });
  }
  applyCollapse();

  var more = $("cdeck-more");
  var moreMin = $("moreMin");
  if (moreMin && more) {
    moreMin.addEventListener("click", function () {
      more.classList.toggle("min");
      if (window.applyWinFloor) window.applyWinFloor();
    });
  }
  var moreBody = $("cdeck-more-body");
  if (moreBody) {
    fetch("deck_more.html", { cache: "no-store" }).then(function (r) {
      if (!r.ok) throw new Error("deck_more HTTP " + r.status);
      return r.text();
    }).then(function (html) {
      moreBody.innerHTML = html;
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_tabs.js"]')) {
        var dt = document.createElement("script");
        dt.src = "deck_tabs.js";
        dt.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckTabs) window.initDeckTabs();
        };
        document.body.appendChild(dt);
      } else if (window.initDeckTabs) {
        window.initDeckTabs();
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="model_rater.js"]')) {
        var mr = document.createElement("script");
        mr.src = "model_rater.js";
        mr.onload = function () {
          if (window.initModelRater) window.initModelRater(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(mr);
      } else if (window.initModelRater) {
        window.initModelRater(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_profiles.js"]')) {
        var pf = document.createElement("script");
        pf.src = "deck_profiles.js";
        pf.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckProfiles) window.initDeckProfiles(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(pf);
      } else if (window.initDeckProfiles) {
        window.initDeckProfiles(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_settings.js"]')) {
        var stg = document.createElement("script");
        stg.src = "deck_settings.js";
        stg.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckSettings) window.initDeckSettings(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(stg);
      } else if (window.initDeckSettings) {
        window.initDeckSettings(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_studio.js"]')) {
        var st = document.createElement("script");
        st.src = "deck_studio.js";
        st.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckStudio) window.initDeckStudio(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(st);
      } else if (window.initDeckStudio) {
        window.initDeckStudio(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_forge.js"]')) {
        var fg = document.createElement("script");
        fg.src = "deck_forge.js";
        fg.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckForge) window.initDeckForge(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(fg);
      } else if (window.initDeckForge) {
        window.initDeckForge(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_gitur.js"]')) {
        var gt = document.createElement("script");
        gt.src = "deck_gitur.js";
        gt.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckGitur) window.initDeckGitur(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(gt);
      } else if (window.initDeckGitur) {
        window.initDeckGitur(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_backup.js"]')) {
        var bk = document.createElement("script");
        bk.src = "deck_backup.js";
        bk.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckBackup) window.initDeckBackup(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(bk);
      } else if (window.initDeckBackup) {
        window.initDeckBackup(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_session_kit.js"]')) {
        var sk = document.createElement("script");
        sk.src = "deck_session_kit.js";
        sk.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckSessionKit) window.initDeckSessionKit(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(sk);
      } else if (window.initDeckSessionKit) {
        window.initDeckSessionKit(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="deck_orders.js"]')) {
        var od = document.createElement("script");
        od.src = "deck_orders.js";
        od.onload = function () {
          if (window.initDeckOrders) window.initDeckOrders(api);
        };
        document.body.appendChild(od);
      } else if (window.initDeckOrders) {
        window.initDeckOrders(api);
      }
      if (!document.querySelector('script[src="app.js"]')) {
        var s = document.createElement("script");
        s.src = "app.js";
        document.body.appendChild(s);
      }
    }).catch(function (e) {
      moreBody.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">' + String(e && e.message || e) + "</div>";
    });
  }

  connect();
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/header.css =====
/* Beige half-head bar — tool / info / launch pad. Scoped so it does not
   restyle the navy KDash cockpit below. Colors from ui/app.css :root. */
#cdeck-head{
  --bg:#f4efe6;--panel:#fffaf3;--panel-edge:#e4d8c8;--ink:#1f2328;
  --ink-dim:#57606a;--ink-faint:#8b949e;--green:#1a7f37;--amber:#9a6700;
  --red:#cf222e;--cyan:#8a5a2b;--head:#1c1410;--well:#efe6d8;--btn:#f7f0e6;
  --btn-border:#e4d8c8;--btn-hover:#eadfcf;--hair:#eadfcf;--danger-bg:#ffebe9;
  --mono:"Segoe UI","Calibri",system-ui,sans-serif;
  --deck-pt:11px;--deck-sub-pt:9px;
  flex:0 0 auto;z-index:90;position:relative;
  background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--mono);
  font-size:var(--deck-pt);line-height:1.25;
  padding:2px 8px 3px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:stretch;gap:2px;
  overflow:visible;
}
#cdeck-head .head-row{display:flex;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:center;gap:6px;width:100%;overflow-x:auto;overscroll-behavior-x:contain}
#cdeck-head.collapsed{padding:2px 10px;gap:0;overflow:hidden}
#cdeck-head.collapsed .head-row:not(.head-brand){display:none !important}
#cdeck-head.collapsed .head-brand > :not(h1){display:none !important}
#cdeck-head h1{font-size:13px;color:var(--head);letter-spacing:2px;font-weight:700;margin:0 6px 0 0;cursor:pointer;user-select:none;flex:none}
#cdeck-head h1:hover span{text-decoration:underline}
#cdeck-head h1 span{color:var(--cyan)}
#cdeck-head h1 small{color:var(--ink-faint);font-size:var(--deck-sub-pt);letter-spacing:1px;font-weight:400}
#cdeck-head .cowork-mode{display:flex;gap:0;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:999px;overflow:hidden;margin-right:8px}
#cdeck-head .cowork-mode button{border:none;border-radius:0;padding:2px 10px}
#cdeck-head .cowork-mode button.on{background:var(--head);color:var(--panel)}
#cdeck-head .mesh-led{
  display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  min-width:52px;height:20px;padding:0 8px;border-radius:999px;flex:none;
  background:#4b5563;color:#fff;margin:0 6px;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;
  letter-spacing:.06em;line-height:1;text-shadow:0 0 4px rgba(0,0,0,.45)
}
#cdeck-head .mesh-led.on{animation:meshblink .9s ease-in-out infinite}
#cdeck-head .mesh-led.ok{background:#16a34a;box-shadow:0 0 10px #16a34a}
#cdeck-head .mesh-led.warn{background:#f59e0b;color:#1c1410;box-shadow:0 0 12px #f59e0b}
#cdeck-head .mesh-led.bad{background:#ef4444;box-shadow:0 0 12px #ef4444}
@keyframes meshblink{
  0%,100%{filter:brightness(1);box-shadow:0 0 8px currentColor}
  50%{filter:brightness(1.85);box-shadow:0 0 18px 2px currentColor}
}
#cdeck-head .rail-leds{
  display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;align-items:center;flex:1 1 auto;
  min-width:0;max-width:none;overflow:visible
}
#cdeck-head .rail-led{
  display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  min-width:28px;height:18px;padding:0 6px;border-radius:999px;
  background:#4b5563;color:#fff;flex:none;font-size:9px;font-weight:800;
  letter-spacing:.04em;line-height:1;text-shadow:0 0 4px rgba(0,0,0,.45)
}
#cdeck-head .orc-stream-box{
  position:absolute;right:10px;top:100%;z-index:120;
  background:var(--panel);color:var(--ink);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;width:min(420px,92vw);
  box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(28,20,16,.18)
}
#cdeck-head .orc-stream-box p{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:var(--deck-sub-pt);color:var(--ink-dim)}
#cdeck-head .orc-stream-box label{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin:6px 0}
#cdeck-head .orc-stream-actions{display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:8px}
#cdeck-head .orc-stream-box[hidden]{display:none}
#home-code.ccr-live{
  outline:2px solid #22e3ff;outline-offset:-2px
}
#home-code.ccr-live .code-term-prompt{color:#22e3ff}
#cdeck-head .rail-led.ok{background:#16a34a;box-shadow:0 0 8px #16a34a}
#cdeck-head .rail-led.bad{background:#ef4444;box-shadow:0 0 8px #ef4444}
#cdeck-head .rail-led.warn{background:#f59e0b;color:#1c1410;box-shadow:0 0 8px #f59e0b}
#cdeck-head .mtrRack{
  display:flex;flex-flow:row nowrap;gap:4px;align-items:center;
  margin:0 4px;flex:0 1 auto;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;
}
#cdeck-head .mtr{
  display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:2px 5px;border:1px solid #222d5e;
  border-radius:5px;background:#080d20;cursor:help;line-height:1;flex:0 1 auto;min-width:0;
}
#cdeck-head .mtr u{
  font-style:normal;text-decoration:none;font-size:8.5px;font-weight:800;
  letter-spacing:.2px;white-space:nowrap;flex:none;
}
#cdeck-head .mtr .mb{
  width:44px;min-width:18px;flex:1 1 44px;height:9px;background:#132043;
  border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden;
}
#cdeck-head .mtr .mb i{display:block;height:100%;border-radius:3px}
#cdeck-head .mtr b{font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap;flex:none;color:#8fa3c8}
#cdeck-head .mtr.nodata{opacity:.5}
#cdeck-head .mtr.posted{border-style:dashed}
#cdeck-head .typesize{display:flex;gap:2px;margin-right:6px}
#cdeck-head .typesize button{font-size:var(--deck-sub-pt);padding:2px 8px}
#cdeck-head .head-row.head-conn{flex-wrap:nowrap;gap:8px;align-items:center}
#cdeck-head .connbar{display:flex;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:center;gap:4px;flex:0 1 auto;min-width:0}
#cdeck-head #connsetup,#cdeck-head #connsetup::details-content{display:contents}
#cdeck-head #connsetup>summary{display:none}
#cdeck-head .connbar label{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap}
#cdeck-head .connbar input{
  background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);color:var(--ink);
  font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;padding:2px 6px;border-radius:3px;outline:none;
}
#cdeck-head .connbar input:focus{border-color:var(--cyan)}
#cdeck-head #apiBase{width:168px}
#cdeck-head #token{width:120px}
#cdeck-head button{
  background:var(--btn);border:1px solid var(--btn-border);color:var(--ink);
  font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:3px;cursor:pointer;
}
#cdeck-head button:hover{border-color:var(--cyan);color:var(--head)}
#cdeck-head button:active{background:var(--btn-hover)}
#cdeck-head button:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default}
#cdeck-head button.on{border-color:var(--cyan);color:var(--head);background:var(--head);color:var(--panel)}
#cdeck-head button.danger{border-color:var(--red);color:var(--red);background:var(--btn)}
#cdeck-head button.danger:hover{background:var(--danger-bg);color:var(--head)}
#cdeck-head #connState{font-size:11px;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);color:var(--ink-dim);white-space:nowrap;max-width:22em;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
#cdeck-head #connState.ok{color:var(--green);border-color:var(--green)}
#cdeck-head #connState.bad{color:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)}
#cdeck-head #connState.wait{color:var(--amber);border-color:var(--amber)}
#cdeck-head .refreshbar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;white-space:nowrap;flex-wrap:nowrap;flex:1 1 auto;min-width:0;margin-left:4px;padding-left:8px;border-left:1px solid var(--panel-edge);overflow-x:auto}
#cdeck-head .inst-picks{display:flex;flex-wrap:nowrap;gap:3px;align-items:center}
#cdeck-head .inst-picks button{font:inherit;font-size:11px;padding:2px 7px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);background:transparent;color:inherit;cursor:pointer}
#cdeck-head .inst-picks button.on{border-color:#8a5a2b;background:#efe6d8;font-weight:700}
#cdeck-head #countdown{color:var(--cyan);min-width:9.5em;display:inline-block;font-size:11px}
#cdeck-head #countdown.paused{color:var(--amber)}
#cdeck-head #transport{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-dim)}
/* Which instance this window is, which Core, which tree. UNMEASURED until
   /api/v1/status answers; peer stays NO_HOST until a federated address exists. */
#cdeck-head #instanceChip{
  font-size:11px;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:3px;border:1px dashed var(--panel-edge);
  color:var(--ink-dim);white-space:nowrap;max-width:22em;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;flex:none;
}
#cdeck-head #instanceChip.bound{border-style:solid;color:var(--cyan);border-color:var(--cyan)}
/* INSTANCE is a native window. Over /cdeck/ HTTP the button says so. */
#cdeck-head button.nonative{border-style:dashed;opacity:.6}
#cdeck-head .cowork-tools{
  display:flex;flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:center;gap:4px;width:auto;
  padding:0;border-top:none;margin-top:0;flex:none;
}
#cdeck-head .cowork-tools .toplink{
  color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.5px;text-decoration:none;
  padding:3px 8px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:999px;
}
#cdeck-head .cowork-tools .toplink:hover{color:var(--head);border-color:var(--cyan)}
html,body{display:flex;flex-direction:column;height:100%;margin:0;overflow:hidden}
.win{position:relative;flex:1 1 auto;width:100%;height:auto;min-height:0;top:0;left:0;border-radius:0}
/* Cursor / VS Code split: remaining viewport is shared. No vh floor — 62vh MESH
   plus 42vh extra panes plus a 3-row header summed past the window and clipped
   the tab rail off the bottom with nowhere to scroll. */
#win{flex:1 1 42%;min-height:0;overflow:auto}
.win.min{flex:0 0 auto;min-height:0}
#win.min{min-height:0 !important;height:auto !important;flex:0 0 auto !important;max-height:none;overflow:visible}
#win.min .foot{display:none}
#cdeck-more{background:#f4efe6;color:#1f2328;min-height:0;height:auto;flex:1 1 58%;display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden}
#cdeck-more.min{flex:0 0 auto;min-height:0;max-height:none;height:auto}
#cdeck-more:not(.min){flex:1 1 58%;max-height:none;min-height:0}
/* Mini MESH COMMAND is the bar. Expanded cDECK panels take the rest of the window. */
body:has(#win.min) #cdeck-more:not(.min){flex:1 1 auto;max-height:none;min-height:0}
body:has(#cdeck-more.min) #win:not(.min){flex:1 1 auto;min-height:0}
#cdeck-more .deck-more-bar{background:#efe6d8;border-bottom:1px solid #e4d8c8;color:#1c1410;flex:0 0 auto}
#cdeck-more .deck-more-bar .title{color:#8a5a2b;text-shadow:none}
#cdeck-more.min .deck-more-body{display:none}
#cdeck-more .deck-more-body{overflow:auto;min-height:0;flex:1;padding:8px}
/* `.deck-more-body` also carries `.body`, which index.html defines as the
   cockpit's three named grid areas (nodes / core / side). Measured in Chrome at
   1600px: the tab shell landed in the 1.10fr column and the whole Studio / Runs
   / Review canvas got 440px of a 1600px window with the other 1160 blank. The
   shell is one child, not a cockpit area, so the grid is dropped once it is in
   place — and only then, so a deck_tabs.js that never loads still paints. */
#cdeck-more .deck-more-body:has(> .deck-shell){
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden;padding:0;gap:0;
  flex:1;min-height:0;height:100%;max-height:100%
}
#cdeck-more #panel-model-rater{margin:10px 0;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge,#e4d8c8);border-radius:8px;background:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .mr-rolebar{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin:8px 0}
#cdeck-more .mr-assign{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;margin:6px 0}
#cdeck-more .mr-table .fav td:nth-child(2){font-weight:700}
#cdeck-more .mr-table .ban{opacity:.55}
#cdeck-more .pf-schematic{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin:8px 0 16px}
#cdeck-more .pf-stream{text-align:left;padding:8px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .pf-stream.on{border-color:#1c1410;background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .pf-stream-h{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:6px}
#cdeck-more .pf-stream-h small{font-weight:400;margin-left:8px;opacity:.8}
#cdeck-more .pf-pipe{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;align-items:center}
#cdeck-more .pf-node{font-size:10px;padding:2px 6px;border:1px solid #eadfcf}
#cdeck-more .pf-stream.on .pf-node{border-color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .pf-arrow{opacity:.5;font-size:10px}
#cdeck-more .pf-full{border-top:1px solid #eadfcf;padding-top:12px;margin-top:8px}
#cdeck-more .pf-pick{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin:8px 0}
#cdeck-more .pf-chip{text-align:left;font-size:11px;line-height:1.25}
#cdeck-more .pf-chip small{display:block;color:#57606a}
#cdeck-more .pf-chip.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .pf-motif-top{margin:8px 0;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fbf6ee}
#cdeck-more .pf-motif-h{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8a5a2b;margin:0 0 6px}
#cdeck-more .pf-motif-step{font-size:11px;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fffaf3;cursor:pointer;text-align:left;line-height:1.2}
#cdeck-more .pf-motif-step b{display:block;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .pf-motif-step.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3;border-color:#1c1410}
#cdeck-more .pf-motif-step.on b{color:#eadfcf}
#cdeck-more .pf-shell{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:flex-start;min-height:42vh}
#cdeck-more .pf-stages{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;min-width:160px}
#cdeck-more .pf-stage{text-align:left;font-size:11px}
#cdeck-more .pf-stage.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .pf-skin{flex:1;min-width:0}
#cdeck-more .pf-mount{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px}
#cdeck-more .pf-mount > .panel,#cdeck-more .pf-mount > .home-card,#cdeck-more .pf-mount > aside{display:block}
#cdeck-more .pf-skin textarea{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box}
#cdeck-more .mr-seats{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;margin:8px 0}
#cdeck-more .mr-seat,#cdeck-more .mr-seat-row{text-align:left;font-size:11px;line-height:1.25}
#cdeck-more .mr-seat-row{display:flex;gap:12px;align-items:baseline;padding:4px 8px;border:1px solid var(--btn-border,#e4d8c8);cursor:pointer}
#cdeck-more .mr-seat-lab{flex:0 0 38%;font-weight:600}
#cdeck-more .mr-seat-picks{flex:1 1 auto;color:#57606a}
#cdeck-more .mr-seat small{color:#57606a}
#cdeck-more .mr-seat.on,#cdeck-more .mr-seat-row.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .mr-seat-row.on .mr-seat-picks{color:#efe6d8}
#cdeck-more .mr-row.fav{background:#f4efe0}
#cdeck-more .mr-row.ban{background:#f0f0f0;opacity:.72}
#cdeck-more .st-grid,#cdeck-more .bak-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:8px;margin:8px 0}
#cdeck-more .st-pane{border:1px solid #e4d8c8;background:#fffaf3;padding:8px}
#cdeck-more .st-pane h3{margin:0 0 4px;font-size:12px}
#cdeck-more .mr-group{flex:1 1 100%;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8a5a2b;margin:6px 0 0}
#cdeck-more .review-body{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(240px,.9fr) minmax(280px,1.2fr);gap:8px;
  min-height:180px;flex:1
}
#cdeck-more .review-col,#cdeck-more .review-catalog{
  overflow:auto;min-height:0;background:#fffaf3;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:8px
}
#cdeck-more .review-col h3,#cdeck-more .review-catalog h3{
  font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;margin:8px 0 3px;color:#8a5a2b
}
#cdeck-more .review-col h3:first-child{margin-top:0}
#cdeck-more .surfaces-body{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(200px,1fr));gap:8px;
  min-height:180px;flex:1
}
#cdeck-more .surf-col{
  overflow:auto;min-height:0;background:#fffaf3;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:8px
}
#cdeck-more .surf-col h3{
  font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;margin:0 0 6px;color:#8a5a2b
}
#cdeck-more .voice-loop .ops-row{font-size:12px}
#cdeck-more #reviewWin button{
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410;
  padding:5px 8px;font-size:11px;cursor:pointer
}
#cdeck-more #reviewWin button.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3;border-color:#1c1410}
#cdeck-more .mr-filters,#cdeck-more .mr-est{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:center;margin:6px 0}
#cdeck-more .mr-chiprow{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;margin:4px 0;width:100%}
#cdeck-more .mr-chiprow .chip,#cdeck-more .mr-card-hands .tiny,#cdeck-more .mr-row-hands .tiny{
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410;
  padding:4px 8px;font-size:11px;cursor:pointer;line-height:1.2
}
#cdeck-more .mr-chiprow .chip.on,#cdeck-more .mr-card-hands .tiny.on,#cdeck-more .mr-row-hands .tiny.on{
  background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3;border-color:#1c1410
}
#cdeck-more .mr-chiprow .chip small{display:block;color:#8a5a2b;font-size:9px}
#cdeck-more .mr-chiprow .chip.on small{color:#eadfcf}
#cdeck-more .mr-cards{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr));
  gap:8px;margin:8px 0;align-content:start
}
#cdeck-more .mr-card{
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fffaf3;border-radius:8px;padding:8px;
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;min-height:0
}
#cdeck-more .mr-card.on{outline:2px solid #1c1410;outline-offset:-2px}
#cdeck-more .mr-card.fav{background:#f4efe0}
#cdeck-more .mr-card.ban{opacity:.72;background:#f0f0f0}
#cdeck-more .mr-card-hd{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:baseline}
#cdeck-more .mr-card-hd strong{font-size:13px}
#cdeck-more .mr-card-metrics{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
#cdeck-more .mr-metric{display:flex;flex-direction:column;min-width:52px;font-size:12px}
#cdeck-more .mr-metric b{font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .mr-card-hands{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px}
#cdeck-more .mr-table-wrap{overflow:auto;max-height:42vh}
#cdeck-more .mr-table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11px}
#cdeck-more .mr-table th,#cdeck-more .mr-table td{padding:3px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #eadfcf;text-align:left;white-space:nowrap;vertical-align:top}
#cdeck-more .mr-table th[data-sort]{text-decoration:underline;text-underline-offset:2px;cursor:pointer}
#cdeck-more .mr-table th[data-sort].on{color:#1c1410;font-weight:700}
#cdeck-more .mr-table thead th{position:sticky;top:0;background:#fffaf3;z-index:1}
#cdeck-more .mr-table td:nth-child(n+3):nth-child(-n+14){text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
#cdeck-more .mr-table .mr-name{position:sticky;left:0;background:#fffaf3;z-index:1;min-width:160px;white-space:normal}
#cdeck-more .mr-table .mr-row.on .mr-name{background:#efe6d8}
#cdeck-more .mr-table .mr-row.fav .mr-name{background:#f4efe0}
#cdeck-more .mr-row-hands{white-space:normal;min-width:280px}
#cdeck-more .mr-row-hands .tiny{margin:1px 2px 1px 0}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-model-rater .mr-cards{max-height:none}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="models"].on{
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;min-height:0
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="models"] > #panel-model-rater{
  flex:0 1 42%;min-height:120px;max-height:46%;overflow:hidden
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="models"] > #panel-model-catalog{
  flex:1 1 58%;min-height:180px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;margin:0
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="models"] > #panel-model-catalog > .panel-bd{
  flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:auto;max-height:none
}
#cdeck-more .mr-defs{margin-top:10px;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fbf6ee}
#cdeck-more .mr-defs h3{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:13px}
#cdeck-more .mr-def-group{font-weight:700;margin:8px 0 4px;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .mr-def{font-size:12px;margin:2px 0;line-height:1.35}
#cdeck-more .mr-agents{margin-top:10px;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fbf6ee}
#cdeck-more .gitur-creds{margin:10px 0;padding:8px 10px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fbf6ee}
#cdeck-more .cred-row{margin:6px 0;padding:6px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #eadfcf}
#cdeck-more .cred-led{display:inline-block;width:10px;height:10px;border-radius:50%;background:#8b949e;margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle}
#cdeck-more .cred-led.ok{background:#1a7f37}
#cdeck-more .cred-led.warn{background:#9a6700}
#cdeck-more .cred-led.dim{background:#8b949e}
#cdeck-more .mr-row.on{background:#efe6d8}
#cdeck-more .deck-shell{display:flex;flex:1;min-height:0;height:100%;max-height:100%;align-items:stretch}
#cdeck-more .deck-tabs{
  flex:0 0 160px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;padding:8px 6px;
  background:#efe6d8;border-right:1px solid #e4d8c8;
  overflow-x:hidden;overflow-y:auto;min-height:0;max-height:100%;
  overscroll-behavior:contain;scrollbar-gutter:stable
}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab{
  display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;border:1px solid transparent;
  background:transparent;color:#1c1410;padding:6px 8px;border-radius:8px;
  font-size:12px;line-height:1.2;cursor:pointer;flex:0 0 auto
}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab small{display:block;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b;font-weight:400;margin-top:2px}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab:hover{background:#eadfcf}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3;border-color:#1c1410}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab.on small{color:#eadfcf}
#cdeck-more .deck-stage{
  flex:1;min-width:0;min-height:0;height:100%;max-height:100%;padding:8px;
  overflow-x:hidden;overflow-y:scroll;
  scrollbar-gutter:stable;overscroll-behavior:contain;
  scrollbar-width:auto
}
#cdeck-more .deck-stage::-webkit-scrollbar{width:14px;display:block}
#cdeck-more .deck-stage::-webkit-scrollbar-track{background:#efe6d8}
#cdeck-more .deck-stage::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:#8a5a2b;border-radius:8px}
#cdeck-more .deck-view{display:none}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.on{display:block;min-height:100%;height:auto;overflow:visible}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-board{
  position:relative;min-height:280px;
  background-image:linear-gradient(#eadfcf 1px,transparent 1px),linear-gradient(90deg,#eadfcf 1px,transparent 1px);
  background-size:16px 16px;background-position:0 0
}
#cdeck-more .pane-tile{
  overflow:hidden;box-sizing:border-box;z-index:1;
  display:flex;flex-direction:column
}
#cdeck-more .pane-tile > .panel-bd,
#cdeck-more .run-op-pane > .panel-bd,
#cdeck-more .orders-col .runs-list,
#cdeck-more .runs-col .runs-list,
#cdeck-more .runs-detail,#cdeck-more #bd-orders-detail{
  overflow:auto;min-height:0;flex:1;
  scrollbar-width:thin;scrollbar-color:#8a5a2b #efe6d8
}
#cdeck-more .pane-tile::-webkit-scrollbar,
#cdeck-more .panel-bd::-webkit-scrollbar,
#cdeck-more .runs-list::-webkit-scrollbar{
  width:12px
}
#cdeck-more .pane-tile::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb,
#cdeck-more .panel-bd::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb,
#cdeck-more .runs-list::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{
  background:#8a5a2b;border-radius:8px
}
#cdeck-more .pane-tile.pane-moving{opacity:.7;z-index:6;user-select:none;touch-action:none}
#cdeck-more .pane-drag,#cdeck-more .pane-tile > .panel-hd{cursor:grab}
#cdeck-more .pane-tile.pane-moving .pane-drag,
#cdeck-more .pane-tile.pane-moving > .panel-hd{cursor:grabbing}
#cdeck-more .pane-rh{position:absolute;z-index:3;background:transparent}
#cdeck-more .pane-rh-se{width:14px;height:14px;right:0;bottom:0;cursor:nwse-resize}
#cdeck-more .pane-rh-e{top:12px;bottom:14px;right:0;width:8px;cursor:ew-resize}
#cdeck-more .pane-rh-s{left:12px;right:14px;bottom:0;height:8px;cursor:ns-resize}
#cdeck-more .orch{display:none}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab-gap{
  font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8a5a2b;
  padding:10px 10px 4px;margin-top:6px
}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab-profiles{font-weight:700}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab-profiles b{font-weight:700}
#cdeck-more .deck-tab-profile{padding-left:16px}
#cdeck-more .pf-step{
  border:1px solid #e4d8c8;border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;margin:10px 0;
  background:#fffaf3
}
#cdeck-more .pf-step h3{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:14px}
#cdeck-more .pf-page-motif{margin-bottom:10px}
#cdeck-more #panel-settings h3{margin:14px 0 6px;font-size:14px}
#cdeck-more #settingsPay a{margin-right:12px}
#cdeck-settings-pop{
  position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:40;display:none;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  padding:24px;background:rgba(28,20,16,.42);box-sizing:border-box
}
#cdeck-settings-pop.on{display:flex}
#cdeck-settings-pop > #panel-settings{
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;width:min(920px,calc(100vw - 48px));
  height:min(740px,calc(100vh - 48px));margin:0;background:#fffaf3;
  border:1px solid #e4d8c8;border-radius:12px;box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(28,20,16,.28)
}
#cdeck-settings-pop > #panel-settings > .panel-hd{
  flex:0 0 auto;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px
}
#cdeck-settings-pop > #panel-settings > .panel-hd h2{margin-right:auto}
#cdeck-settings-pop .settings-close{
  border:1px solid #cdbda9;border-radius:7px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410;
  padding:5px 10px;cursor:pointer;font:inherit;font-size:12px
}
#cdeck-settings-pop .settings-shell{flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:hidden}
#cdeck-settings-pop .settings-main{overflow:auto;min-height:0}
#cdeck-more .settings-shell{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:168px minmax(0,1fr);gap:14px;align-items:start;min-height:0
}
#cdeck-more .settings-nav{
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;position:sticky;top:0
}
#cdeck-more .settings-nav button{
  text-align:left;font:inherit;font-size:13px;padding:8px 10px;border:0;border-radius:8px;
  background:transparent;color:#5c4a38;cursor:pointer
}
#cdeck-more .settings-nav button.on{background:#efe6d8;color:#1c1410;font-weight:700}
#cdeck-more .settings-main{min-width:0;overflow:visible}
#cdeck-more .set-page{display:none}
#cdeck-more .set-page.on{display:block}
#cdeck-more .set-lead{margin:0 0 12px;color:#5c4a38;line-height:1.4}
#cdeck-more .set-cards{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(220px,1fr));gap:10px;margin:0 0 16px
}
#cdeck-more .set-card{
