  display:block;margin:0;padding:12px 14px;border:1px solid #e4d8c8;border-radius:10px;
  background:#fffaf3;color:inherit;text-decoration:none
}
#cdeck-more .set-card h3{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .set-val{margin:0;font-size:15px;font-weight:700;word-break:break-word}
#cdeck-more .set-card .dim{margin:6px 0 0}
#cdeck-more .pf-seat-card{cursor:pointer}
#cdeck-more .pf-seat-card.on{border-color:#8a5a2b;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px #8a5a2b;background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .pf-seat-card.on h3{color:#ffcf5a}
#cdeck-more .pf-seat-card.on .dim{color:#efe6d8}
#cdeck-more .set-pick{cursor:pointer}
#cdeck-more .set-pick input{margin:0 0 8px}
#cdeck-more .set-pick:has(input:checked){border-color:#8a5a2b;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px #8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .set-metrics{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(140px,1fr));gap:10px;margin:0 0 16px
}
#cdeck-more .set-metric{
  padding:12px 14px;border:1px solid #e4d8c8;border-radius:10px;background:#fffaf3;
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px
}
#cdeck-more .set-metric .set-k{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .set-metric b{font-size:18px}
@media (max-width:720px){
  #cdeck-more .settings-shell{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  #cdeck-more .settings-nav{flex-direction:row;flex-wrap:wrap}
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill{position:relative;min-height:100%;height:auto}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill.on{display:flex;flex-direction:column;min-height:100%;height:auto;overflow:visible}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"].on{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(280px,1.2fr) minmax(240px,1fr);
  grid-template-rows:auto auto 1fr;gap:8px;align-items:stretch
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"] #home-code{grid-row:1 / span 3;min-height:0;overflow:auto}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"] #panel-forge-ccr{grid-column:2;grid-row:1}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"] #panel-forge-job{grid-column:2;grid-row:2}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"] #panel-forge-adv{grid-column:2;grid-row:3;min-height:0;overflow:auto}
#cdeck-more .forge-total{font-size:13px;margin-top:8px}
#cdeck-more .forge-adv{border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:8px;margin:0 0 8px;background:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .forge-adv-hd{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:6px}
#cdeck-more .forge-drop{font-size:11px}
#cdeck-more .forge-say{margin:6px 0 0;border-left:3px solid #eadfcf;padding-left:8px}
#cdeck-more .forge-say.ok{border-left-color:#1a7f37;color:#1a7f37}
#cdeck-more .forge-say.bad{border-left-color:#cf222e;color:#cf222e}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > .panel,
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > .home-card,
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > .studio-panel,
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-review,
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > .runs-panel{
  flex:1;min-height:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;margin:0
}
/* Studio and Runs lay out their own bodies; the single-card tabs need the body
   itself to take the height and do the scrolling, or the card keeps its tile
   size and clips. */
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-review > .panel-bd,
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-model-rater > .panel-bd,
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-surfaces > .panel-bd,
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-gitur > .panel-bd,
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-orders > .panel-bd{
  flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:auto
}
/* Recents: the session list and the transcript it opens, side by side. The
   list scrolls on its own so 200 rows cannot push OPENED off the fold. */
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="recents"].on{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(260px,1fr) minmax(320px,1.4fr);
  grid-template-rows:1fr;gap:8px;align-items:stretch
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="recents"] > #home-nav{
  display:grid;grid-template-rows:auto auto 1fr auto;gap:8px;min-height:0;margin:0
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="recents"] #nav-sessions{
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;min-height:0
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="recents"] #nav-session-list{flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:auto}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="recents"] > #nav-opened{
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;min-height:0;margin:0
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="recents"] #nav-opened-body{
  flex:1;min-height:0;max-height:none;overflow:auto
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="clock"] > #panel-clocks{flex:2}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="clock"] > #panel-clocks > .panel-bd{
  flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:auto
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="clock"] > #home-auto,
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="clock"] > #home-pool{flex:0 0 auto}
#cdeck-more .gitur-body{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;min-height:0;overflow:auto}
#cdeck-more .gitur-legs{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:8px;margin:0}
#cdeck-more .gitur-leg{border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:8px;background:#fffaf3;min-height:0}
#cdeck-more .gitur-leg h3,#cdeck-more .gitur-flow h3,#cdeck-more .gitur-sgh h3,#cdeck-more .gitur-log h3{margin:0 0 4px;font-size:13px}
#cdeck-more .gitur-flow,#cdeck-more .gitur-sgh,#cdeck-more .gitur-log{
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:8px;background:#fffaf3
}
#cdeck-more .gitur-flow-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto 1fr auto 1fr;gap:6px;align-items:stretch}
#cdeck-more .gitur-flow-step{border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:8px;background:#fff}
#cdeck-more .gitur-flow-arrow{align-self:center;color:#9a6700;font-weight:700}
#cdeck-more .gitur-log table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11px}
#cdeck-more .gitur-log th,#cdeck-more .gitur-log td{text-align:left;padding:3px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid #eadfcf;vertical-align:top}
#cdeck-more .gitur-log th{color:#6b5a4a;font-weight:600}
#cdeck-more .gitur-log tr[data-gitur-run]{cursor:pointer}
#cdeck-more .gitur-log tr[data-gitur-run]:hover{background:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .gitur-leg .ok{color:#1a7f37}
#cdeck-more .gitur-leg .bad{color:#cf222e}
#cdeck-more .gitur-leg .warn{color:#9a6700}
#cdeck-more .gitur-jobs .jobrow{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;border-bottom:1px solid #eadfcf;padding:4px 0}
#cdeck-more .gitur-jobs button.jobrow{width:100%;text-align:left;background:none;border-width:0 0 1px;cursor:pointer;color:inherit;font:inherit}
#cdeck-more .gitur-jobs button.jobrow:hover{background:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more .gitur-tools{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:0 0 6px}
#cdeck-more .gitur-tools .chip{cursor:pointer}
#cdeck-more .gitur-tools .chip.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3;border-color:#1c1410}
#cdeck-more .gitur-tools .chip.on b{color:inherit}
#cdeck-more #home-inflight button.jobrow{
  display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;width:100%;text-align:left;cursor:pointer;
  background:none;border:0;border-bottom:1px solid #eadfcf;padding:4px 0;color:inherit;font:inherit
}
#cdeck-more #home-inflight button.jobrow:hover{background:#fffaf3}
#cdeck-more #bd-clocks .chips{margin:6px 0}
#cdeck-more #nav-opened{margin-top:8px}
#cdeck-more #nav-opened-body{max-height:38vh;overflow:auto}
#cdeck-more .sess-open{
  white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;font-size:11px;line-height:1.45;
  margin:6px 0 0;padding:8px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;background:#fffaf3
}
#cdeck-more tr.surf-unmeasured td{opacity:.72}
#cdeck-more tr.surf-extra td:first-child::before{content:"· ";color:#9a6700}
#cdeck-more .gitur-tools button{
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410;
  padding:5px 8px;font-size:11px;cursor:pointer
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-model-rater{border-radius:8px}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-model-rater .mr-table-wrap{max-height:none}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-model-catalog{border-radius:8px}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-model-catalog .mr-table-wrap{max-height:none;flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:auto}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-events{
  flex:0 1 38%;min-height:120px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;margin:6px 0 0
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-fill > #panel-events > .panel-bd{flex:1;min-height:0;overflow:auto}
#cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="runs"].on{
  display:block;position:relative;height:auto;min-height:100%;overflow:visible
}
#cdeck-more .deck-view.pane-board.on{height:auto;min-height:100%;overflow:visible}
#cdeck-more .run-op-pane .panel-hd h2{font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.04em}
#cdeck-more .run-op-pane .set-val{font-size:16px}
#cdeck-more .run-op-led{
  display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;margin-right:6px;background:#8b949e
}
#cdeck-more .run-op-led.ok{background:#1a7f37}
#cdeck-more .run-op-led.bad{background:#cf222e}
#cdeck-more .run-op-led.warn{background:#9a6700}
#cdeck-more .runs-body{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(260px,1.15fr) minmax(240px,.95fr);gap:8px;
  min-height:180px;height:100%;flex:1
}
#cdeck-more .orders-body{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(280px,1.2fr) minmax(260px,1fr);gap:8px;
  min-height:180px;flex:1
}
#cdeck-more .runs-ops{
  overflow:auto;min-height:0;min-width:0;background:#fffaf3;border:1px solid #eadfcf;
  border-radius:8px;padding:8px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px
}
#cdeck-more .runs-ops h3{
  font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;margin:8px 0 3px;color:#8a5a2b
}
#cdeck-more .runs-ops h3:first-of-type{margin-top:0}
#cdeck-more .tool-kit-cards{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(220px,1fr));
  gap:8px;margin:6px 0;align-content:start
}
#cdeck-more .tool-kit-card{
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fffaf3;border-radius:8px;padding:8px;
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;min-height:0
}
#cdeck-more .tool-kit-card strong{font-size:12px;line-height:1.25}
#cdeck-more .tool-kit-card .tiny{line-height:1.35}
#cdeck-more .ops-row{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) auto;gap:2px 8px;
  font-size:11px;border-bottom:1px solid #eadfcf;padding:2px 0;align-items:baseline
}
#cdeck-more .ops-row .ops-k{min-width:0;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
#cdeck-more .ops-free{color:#2f6b2f}
#cdeck-more .ops-loop{font-weight:700}
#cdeck-more .runs-col,#cdeck-more .orders-col{display:flex;flex-direction:column;min-height:0;min-width:0;gap:4px}
#cdeck-more .runs-tools{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:center;flex:0 0 auto}
#cdeck-more .runs-tools #runsQ{
  flex:1;min-width:0;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;background:#fffaf3;
  color:#1c1410;padding:5px 8px;font-size:11px
}
#cdeck-more .runs-tools select,#cdeck-more .runs-tools button{
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410;
  padding:5px 8px;font-size:11px;cursor:pointer
}
#cdeck-more .runs-chips,#cdeck-more .runs-spend{margin:0;flex:0 0 auto}
#cdeck-more .runs-note{flex:0 0 auto;line-height:1.35}
#cdeck-more .nowrap{white-space:nowrap}
#cdeck-more .runs-list,#cdeck-more .runs-detail{
  overflow:auto;min-height:0;background:#fffaf3;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:8px
}
#cdeck-more .runs-list{flex:1}
#cdeck-more .runs-list:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #1c1410;outline-offset:-2px}
#cdeck-more .runs-chips .chip,#cdeck-more .studio-q .chip{
  cursor:pointer;border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fffaf3
}
#cdeck-more .runs-chips .chip.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3;border-color:#1c1410}
#cdeck-more .runs-chips .stale-chip{border-color:#9a6700}
#cdeck-more .runs-spend .chip{cursor:default}
/* A run row is four facts, not four spans jammed against each other: id and
   state on top, command and the age/priority/lane meta underneath. `.jobrow`
   sets display:flex but `button.sess` in app.css outranks it, which is why the
   list used to read "j-1200QUEUED". */
#cdeck-more .runrow{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) auto auto;gap:2px 8px;
  width:100%;text-align:left;align-items:baseline;padding:5px 6px;margin:0;
  border:1px solid transparent;border-bottom:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:6px;
  background:transparent;color:#1c1410;cursor:pointer;font-size:12px
}
#cdeck-more .runrow:hover{background:#efe6d8}
#cdeck-more .runrow.on{background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3;border-color:#1c1410}
/* `color:inherit` is load-bearing: app.css sets `.jobrow .jid{color:var(--head)}`
   and specificity is decided per property, so the id kept the dark cockpit ink
   and vanished into the selected row's dark background. */
#cdeck-more .runrow .jid{grid-column:1;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.02em;color:inherit}
#cdeck-more .runrow .rstale{
  grid-column:2;font-size:9px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.1em;
  border:1px solid #9a6700;color:#9a6700;border-radius:4px;padding:0 4px
}
#cdeck-more .runrow .jst{grid-column:3;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.08em;font-size:11px}
#cdeck-more .runrow .jcmd{grid-column:1;grid-row:2;margin:0}
#cdeck-more .runrow .rmeta{
  grid-column:2 / 4;grid-row:2;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap
}
#cdeck-more .runrow.on .jcmd,#cdeck-more .runrow.on .rmeta{color:#d7c8b2}
#cdeck-more .runrow.on .rstale{border-color:#f0b429;color:#f0b429}
#cdeck-more .runs-tally{padding:6px 2px}
#cdeck-more .read-stale{
  border:1px solid #cf222e;border-radius:6px;background:#fdf0ef;color:#8a2a22;
  padding:4px 8px;margin-bottom:6px;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.04em
}
#cdeck-more .rev-hd{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:10px;margin-top:12px}
#cdeck-more .rev-hd h3{margin:0;font-size:13px;white-space:nowrap;flex:0 0 auto}
#cdeck-more .rev-hd h3 small{font-weight:400;color:#8a5a2b;margin-left:4px}
/* `.studio-go` is `display:block;width:100%`, which stretched this jump button
   across the whole review pane. Same specificity, so it needs the extra class. */
#cdeck-more .rev-hd .rev-jump{
  display:inline-block;width:auto;margin:0;padding:2px 8px;font-size:10px;flex:0 0 auto
}
#cdeck-more .armnote{border-left:2px solid #9a6700;padding-left:6px}
#cdeck-more .studio-go.arm{border-color:#9a6700;background:#fdf3dd;font-weight:700}
#cdeck-more .jcmd{display:block;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b;margin-top:2px;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
#cdeck-more .studio-node.hot-run{border-color:#1a7f37;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px #1a7f37}
#cdeck-more .studio-node.hot-wait{border-color:#9a6700;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px #9a6700}
#cdeck-more .studio-node.hot-review{border-color:#9a6700;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px #9a6700}
#cdeck-more .studio-node.hot-broke{border-color:#cf222e;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px #cf222e}
#cdeck-more .studio-heat{display:block;margin-top:4px;font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .studio-node.on .studio-heat{color:#eadfcf}
#cdeck-more .studio-panel .studio-body{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:132px minmax(0,1fr) minmax(280px,400px);gap:8px;
  min-height:240px;flex:1
}
#cdeck-more .studio-pal,#cdeck-more .studio-insp{
  background:#fffaf3;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:8px;
  overflow:auto;min-height:0;font-size:11px
}
#cdeck-more .studio-pal-h{font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .studio-pal-g{margin:8px 0 4px;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em}
#cdeck-more .studio-chip,#cdeck-more .studio-surf,#cdeck-more .studio-node,#cdeck-more .studio-go{
  display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;border:1px solid #eadfcf;background:#fffaf3;
  color:#1c1410;border-radius:8px;padding:6px 8px;margin:0 0 4px;cursor:pointer;font-size:11px
}
#cdeck-more .studio-chip.on,#cdeck-more .studio-node.on,#cdeck-more .studio-surf.on,#cdeck-more .studio-lane.on{
  background:#1c1410;color:#fffaf3;border-color:#1c1410
}
#cdeck-more .studio-canvas{overflow:auto;min-width:0;padding:4px}
#cdeck-more .studio-flow{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:stretch;gap:6px;margin:8px 0}
#cdeck-more .studio-board.studio-flow{
  display:block;position:relative;min-height:420px;height:calc(100% - 48px);
  background-image:linear-gradient(#eadfcf 1px,transparent 1px),linear-gradient(90deg,#eadfcf 1px,transparent 1px);
  background-size:16px 16px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;margin:8px 0
}
#cdeck-more .studio-gnode{
  position:absolute;width:176px;min-width:176px;margin:0;flex:none;z-index:1;cursor:grab
}
#cdeck-more .studio-gnode:active{cursor:grabbing}
#cdeck-more .studio-node{width:auto;min-width:92px;margin:0;flex:0 0 auto}
#cdeck-more .studio-n{display:block;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .studio-node.on .studio-n{color:#eadfcf}
#cdeck-more .studio-nm{display:block;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em}
#cdeck-more .studio-arrow{align-self:center;color:#8a5a2b;font-size:14px}
#cdeck-more .studio-lanes{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;margin-top:6px}
#cdeck-more .studio-lane{
  display:block;font-size:10px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:6px;padding:2px 6px;background:#efe6d8
}
#cdeck-more .studio-back{display:block;margin-top:6px;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .studio-cons-rules{
  margin:8px 0;padding:8px 10px;border:1px dashed #c4b49a;background:#f7f1e6
}
#cdeck-more .studio-cons-ul{margin:6px 0 0 1.1em;padding:0;font-size:11px;line-height:1.4}
#cdeck-more .studio-cons-ul li{margin:0 0 3px}
#cdeck-more .studio-surfrow{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-top:10px}
#cdeck-more .studio-surf{width:auto;margin:0}
#cdeck-more .studio-surf small{display:block;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .studio-surf.on small{color:#eadfcf}
/* A job id is case-sensitive. The cockpit's global h3 uppercases, which turned
   `j-1184` into `J-1184` at the top of the inspector — a different string from
   the one on the row. */
#cdeck-more .studio-insp h3,#cdeck-more .runs-detail h3,#cdeck-more .rev-hd h3,
#cdeck-more #bd-orders-detail h3{
  text-transform:none;color:#1c1410
}
#cdeck-more .studio-insp h3{margin:0 0 6px;font-size:13px}
#cdeck-more .studio-insp p{margin:0 0 8px;line-height:1.35}
#cdeck-more #studioDefineText,#cdeck-more #studioExtraUrls,
#cdeck-more #studioConsensusNote,#cdeck-more #studioBuildInput,
#cdeck-more #studioImplementNote{
  width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;min-height:8em;font:11px/1.4 ui-monospace,Consolas,monospace;
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410;padding:6px 8px;margin:0 0 6px
}
#cdeck-more #studioConsensusNote{min-height:4.5em}
#cdeck-more #studioArchAddLabel,#cdeck-more #studioArchAddBy,#cdeck-more #studioArchPick{
  flex:1;min-width:0;font-size:11px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:6px;
  padding:3px 6px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410
}
#cdeck-more #studioArchPickWrap{margin:0 0 8px}
#cdeck-more #studioArchPickWrap[hidden],#cdeck-more #studioArchPick:disabled{opacity:.55}
#cdeck-more #studioDefine,#cdeck-more #studioResearch,
#cdeck-more #studioArch,#cdeck-more #studioConsensus,
#cdeck-more #studioBuild,#cdeck-more #studioCritics,
#cdeck-more #studioImplement,#cdeck-more #studioIterate{margin:0 0 8px}
#cdeck-more #studioBuildDestPath,#cdeck-more #studioImplementDestPath,
#cdeck-more #studioBuildSim,#cdeck-more #studioIterateRounds,
#cdeck-more #studioIterateTimed,#cdeck-more #studioIterateBudget{
  width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;font-size:11px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;
  border-radius:6px;padding:3px 6px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410;margin:0 0 6px
}
#cdeck-more #studioBuildInput{min-height:6em}
#cdeck-more .studio-rrow{
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:8px;padding:6px;margin:0 0 6px;background:#fffaf3
}
#cdeck-more .studio-rrow label{display:block;margin:3px 0;font-size:10px;color:#8a5a2b}
#cdeck-more .studio-rrow select,#cdeck-more .studio-rrow input[type="text"]{
  width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;font-size:11px;
  border:1px solid #eadfcf;border-radius:6px;padding:3px 6px;background:#fffaf3;color:#1c1410
}
#cdeck-more .studio-rrow .formrow{margin:0 0 4px;align-items:center}
#cdeck-more .studio-drop{
  width:auto;margin:0 0 0 auto;padding:2px 8px;font-size:10px;border:1px solid #eadfcf;
  background:#fffaf3;border-radius:6px;cursor:pointer
}
#cdeck-more #studioAddVia{flex:1;min-width:0;font-size:11px}
#cdeck-more .studio-targets{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;margin:0 0 8px}
#cdeck-more .studio-targets label{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:flex-start;font-size:11px}
#cdeck-more .studio-q{margin:6px 0}
@media (max-width:720px){
  #cdeck-more .studio-panel .studio-body{grid-template-columns:1fr;grid-template-rows:auto minmax(160px,1fr) auto}
  #cdeck-more .runs-body,#cdeck-more .orders-body,#cdeck-more .review-body,
  #cdeck-more .surfaces-body{grid-template-columns:1fr}
  #cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"].on{grid-template-columns:1fr;grid-template-rows:auto}
  #cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"] #home-code,
  #cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"] #panel-forge-ccr,
  #cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"] #panel-forge-job,
  #cdeck-more .deck-view[data-tab="forge"] #panel-forge-adv{grid-column:1;grid-row:auto}
}

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_more.html =====
<div id="downBanner" class="down-banner hidden" role="status">
  SERVER DOWN — COSMOS is not answering. Panels keep last good data and its measured age. They will not hang.
</div>

<!-- Keith 2026-09-06: KDash wall is the main canvas. OpenWork C0 is a bottom
     strip (separate app/window). Recents stay far-left. Tools on top + edges. -->
<div class="edge-rail edge-left" id="edge-left" aria-label="left edge tools">
  <button type="button" id="edgeNew" title="New">+</button>
  <button type="button" id="edgeKill" class="danger" title="KILL">K</button>
  <button type="button" id="edgeCvm" title="CVM">V</button>
  <a href="#panel-jobs" title="jobs">J</a>
  <a href="#panel-nodemap" title="nodes">N</a>
  <a href="#panel-fleet" title="drives / fleet">D</a>
  <a href="#advanced" title="meters">↓</a>
</div>
<div class="orch" id="orch">
  <aside class="orch-nav" id="home-nav">
    <button type="button" id="btnNavNew" class="nav-new">New</button>
    <section class="home-card" id="nav-streams">
      <div class="panel-hd"><h2>PROJECTS</h2><span class="dim tiny">streams</span></div>
      <div class="panel-bd nav-list" id="nav-stream-list">
        <button type="button" class="sess on" data-stream="Cm" title="this OpenWork session">
          <span class="sess-title">Cm</span>
          <span class="sess-meta">COSMOS development · this session</span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>
    <section class="home-card" id="nav-sessions">
      <div class="panel-hd"><h2>RECENTS</h2><span class="age unknown" id="age-nav-sessions">UNMEASURED</span><button type="button" id="btnOpenSessions" class="tiny">open sessions</button></div>
      <input id="navSessFilter" type="search" placeholder="resume any session" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" aria-label="filter prior sessions">
      <div class="chips" id="sessionLocations" title="search locations — every surface. n UNMEASURED until indexed"></div>
      <div class="panel-bd nav-list" id="nav-session-list">
        <div class="empty">no session list loaded — not a fake history</div>
      </div>
    </section>
    <section class="home-card" id="nav-sched">
      <div class="panel-hd"><h2>SCHEDULED</h2><span class="age unknown" id="age-nav-sched">UNMEASURED</span></div>
      <div class="panel-bd nav-list" id="nav-sched-list">
        <div class="empty">UNMEASURED — clocks not projected here yet</div>
      </div>
    </section>
  </aside>
  <!-- The OPENED transcript is fetched by the Recents pane, so it is READ on the
       Recents pane. It used to land only in #home-display-body, which the tab
       shell moves into the Open tab: clicking a recent here fetched a transcript
       and painted it on a tab that was not on screen, which looked like a dead
       row. Both boxes are filled now. It is a sibling of the nav aside, not a
       card inside it, so the Recents tab can give it its own column instead of
       laying it out below the fold of the list's tile. -->
  <section class="home-card" id="nav-opened">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>OPENED</h2><span class="age never" id="age-nav-opened">nothing opened</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="nav-opened-body">
      <div class="empty">pick a recent — GET /api/v1/recents?open=1&amp;id= returns the OPENED transcript. No AppData scrape, no invented OpenWork port. Interrupted Grok Build / GBW rows resume with grok -r &lt;id&gt; from the Forge left pane.</div>
    </div>
  </section>
  <section class="panel home-card" id="panel-session-kit">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>COS · AUTOSAVE · RESESSION</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="sessionKitAge">GET /api/v1/session_kit</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd">
      <p class="dim tiny">COS system panes (ROLD, TidyUP, TU2, BU). Autosave ephemeral artifacts on the system clock. Auto-resession triggers are config — this SAVE does not fire a resession. Resume interrupted Grok Build from Forge (grok -c / grok -r).</p>
      <h3>COS panes</h3>
      <div class="studio-targets" id="sessionCos"></div>
      <h3>autosave (minutes)</h3>
      <div class="studio-targets" id="sessionAutosave"></div>
      <h3>auto-resession when</h3>
      <label class="dim tiny">time limit seconds (0 = off)
        <input id="sessionTimeS" type="number" min="0" max="86400" step="1">
      </label>
      <label class="dim tiny">context window tokens (0 = off)
        <input id="sessionCtxTok" type="number" min="0" step="1000">
      </label>
      <label class="studio-targets"><input type="checkbox" id="sessionOnCompaction"> compaction event</label>
      <label class="studio-targets"><input type="checkbox" id="sessionOnTokens"> token count</label>
      <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="sessionKitSave">SAVE SESSION KIT</button>
      <div class="tiny" id="sessionKitSay"></div>
    </div>
  </section>
  <section class="panel home-card" id="panel-session-stripper">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>SESSION STRIPPER</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="sessionStripAge">GET /api/v1/session_tools</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd">
      <p class="dim tiny">BTS leftover <code>strip_transcript.py</code>. Keeps verbatim human &amp; assistant dialogue; drops tool payloads, file dumps, thinking, system-reminder. Writes <code>STRIPPED_&lt;stem&gt;.md</code> beside the source. Original stays. Default Legal dir is LEGAL_OMITTED from this TUI. Not a summarizer. Not a miner.</p>
      <div id="bd-session-stripper"><div class="empty">unread — GET /api/v1/session_tools</div></div>
      <div class="dim tiny">session chip from GET /recents — no hunt box. Legal OMITTED.</div>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="stripPathChips"></div>
      <div class="formrow">
        <button type="button" id="stripDry">DRY-RUN</button>
        <button type="button" id="stripOne">STRIP ONE</button>
      </div>
      <div class="tiny" id="stripSay"></div>
    </div>
  </section>
  <section class="panel home-card" id="panel-session-tools">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>OPEN SESSIONS SUITE</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="sessionToolsAge">GET /api/v1/session_tools</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd">
      <p class="dim tiny">Open Sessions is list/open. Each leftover verb is its own pane on this board. POST runs scan/load/convert/diff/check/anonymize/doi/crash-recover. Ids and stores are chips from GET /recents — no hunt boxes. Legal omitted. Do not re-ingest 666. GET never mutates.</p>
      <div class="dim tiny">store</div>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="stStore"></div>
      <div class="dim tiny">session id</div>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="stId"></div>
      <div class="dim tiny">check what</div>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="stWhat">
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-val="catalog">catalog</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="sqlite">sqlite</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="seed">seed</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="sit">sit</button>
      </div>
      <p class="dim tiny">research_call FILE/INGEST lives on Profile Studio RESEARCH — jump if missing here. Does not invent Crossref/DOI existence. UPS-JUDGE stays NAMED.</p>
      <div class="formrow">
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="stJumpResearchFile">FILE CALL · Studio</button>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="stJumpResearchIngest">INGEST RETURN · Studio</button>
      </div>
      <div class="tiny" id="stSay"></div>
      <div class="st-grid" id="bd-session-tools"><div class="empty">unread — GET /api/v1/session_tools. MOTIF suite panes.</div></div>
    </div>
  </section>
  <div class="orch-home" id="orch-home">
    <div class="orch-stage">
    <section class="home-card" id="home-work">
      <div class="panel-hd"><h2>WORK IN FLIGHT</h2><span class="age never" id="age-home-jobs">no data</span></div>
      <div class="panel-bd" id="home-inflight"><div class="empty">queue unread — not an empty jukebox</div></div>
    </section>

    <section class="panel home-card" id="panel-tool-catalog">
      <div class="panel-hd"><h2>CATALOG</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="toolsKitAge">GET /api/v1/tools_kit</span></div>
      <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-tool-catalog">
        <div class="empty">unread — GET /api/v1/tools_kit. Built Core modules, named routes, local callables, incumbent BTS-MESH leftovers (stripper / DOI / UPS-JUDGE / legal / rebuild), PATH hands — filed under extra-pane tabs. UPS-JUDGE is NAMED until Physics Profile. Legal invoke LEGAL_OMITTED. GET never mkdir — open/measure only. Cards not dropdowns unless 20+.</div>
      </div>
      <div class="formrow">
        <input id="doiCheck" type="text" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="DOI 10.… — shape gate, existence UNPROVEN">
        <button type="button" id="doiVerify">VERIFY DOI</button>
        <span class="tiny" id="doiSay"></span>
      </div>
      <p class="dim tiny">VERIFY DOI is a shape gate — existence UNPROVEN. Not UPS-JUDGE. Not Crossref. Not a live DOI verifier.</p>
  </section>
    <section class="panel home-card" id="panel-tool-built">

    </section>

    <section class="panel home-card" id="panel-create">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>TOOLS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-create">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-create">
      <div class="dim tiny">CREATE kinds on the home. GET /tools is a contract report, not invoke. A CREATE ORDER is a queued work order, not a finished tool.</div>
      <div class="quickcmds" id="create-kinds">
        <button type="button" data-kind="AGENT" class="on">Agent</button>
        <button type="button" data-kind="TOOL">Tool</button>
        <button type="button" data-kind="CONNECTOR">Connector</button>
        <button type="button" data-kind="SKILL">Skill</button>
      </div>
      <div id="create-cards"><div class="empty">loading AGENT makers — cards list WHERE that kind can be made</div></div>
      <details class="tooltable" id="create-order">
        <summary>file a CREATE ORDER (POST /api/v1/jobs)</summary>
        <div class="dim tiny">A CREATE ORDER is a <b>queued work order</b>, not a finished creation. The
          COSMOS runner executes only <code>py:&lt;script&gt;</code> / <code>argv:[…]</code> confined to
          the tools root (<code>&lt;root&gt;/cosmos</code>) and refuses anything else as BAD_COMMAND — so the
          order names the executor that will make the thing. The exact command is shown before you send it.</div>
        <div class="formgrid">
          <label>maker <input id="coMaker" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" readonly placeholder="pick a card above"></label>
          <label>name <input id="coName" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="what to create"></label>
          <label>executor <input id="coExec" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="maker.py — under the tools root"></label>
          <label>brief <input id="coBrief" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="what it should do"></label>
          <label>priority
            <div class="mr-chiprow" id="coPri">
              <button type="button" class="chip on" data-val="normal">normal</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="low">low</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="high">high</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="critical">critical</button>
            </div>
          </label>
          <button type="button" id="btnCreateOrder" disabled>FILE CREATE ORDER</button>
        </div>
        <div class="dim tiny">command this deck will POST:</div>
        <pre id="coPreview" class="cmdpreview">—</pre>
        <div class="gatewhy" id="coGate">—</div>
      </details>
      <details class="tooltable" id="create-register">
        <summary>register a maker (POST /makers)</summary>
        <div class="formgrid">
          <label>id <input id="mkId" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off"></label>
          <label>kind
            <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mkKind">
              <button type="button" class="chip on" data-val="AGENT">AGENT</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="TOOL">TOOL</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="CONNECTOR">CONNECTOR</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="SKILL">SKILL</button>
            </div>
          </label>
          <label>location <input id="mkLoc" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off"></label>
          <label>function <input id="mkFn" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off"></label>
          <label>access <input id="mkAccess" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off"></label>
          <label>sources <input id="mkSrc" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="comma-separated"></label>
          <label>tags <input id="mkTags" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="comma-separated"></label>
          <button type="button" id="btnMakerAdd" disabled>ADD MAKER</button>
        </div>
        <div class="dim tiny">Unknown kind is refused by COSMOS. This is a write, not a capability claim.</div>
        <div class="gatewhy" id="mkGate">—</div>
      </details>
    </div>
    </section>

    <!-- The only clocks COSMOS actually serves: the heartbeat roster inside GET
         /api/v1/fleet (same witness as the FLEET panel). There is no clock GET
         beyond it, so this pane does not grow schtasks rows, cron lines or a
         next-run column nobody measured — each row is a last_run_epoch and what
         that makes its age. -->
    <section class="home-card" id="panel-clocks">

Schedule: weekdays, 8:00 AM America/Chicago

  <!-- ══ THE LIFTED KDASH CHROME ═══════════════════════════════════════════
       Source: _lift/jack_command_2026-08-16.html (BTS KDash, 2026-08-16 18:48,
       250959 B, sha256 in _lift/SOURCE.txt) — the cockpit from the night before
       the 2026-08-17 floor. These three panels are hoisted into the main KDash
       wall by hoistKdashWall(); they live here so the wall is a MOVE of panels
       that exist, not a second definition of them.
       This is a LIFT: the centre core, the movable seats, the meter rack and the
       jukebox furniture come from that file. What did NOT come across, and why,
       is recorded in app.js § KDASH LIFT — including the YouTube iframe and its
       localStorage API key, which occupancy forbids twice over (no iframe, no
       Chrome host, and the bearer comes from native load_install_token and is
       never written to disk). -->
  <section class="panel wide" id="panel-core">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>SIGNAL CORE</h2><span class="age never" id="age-core">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-core">
      <div class="nmap-toolbar">
        <button type="button" id="coreReset">RESET SEATS</button>
        <span class="dim">drag any node, surface or the hub — every line is recomputed from the seats, so a dragged box keeps its channels · double-click re-seats · seats persist in deck.json (with a localStorage copy) · UNMEASURED is never painted as 0</span>
      </div>
      <div id="core-banner" class="dim tiny">no mesh read yet</div>
      <svg id="deck-core" viewBox="0 0 640 375" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
           aria-label="Signal core — the COSMOS bus: measured nodes, surfaces and channels around the kernel"></svg>
      <div id="core-detail" class="dim tiny">The mesh is <code>buildTopology()</code>'s — the same witness the NODE MAP binds to (<code>registry/rails.json</code> when the runtime root is set, else GET /api/v1/rails), so the two views cannot disagree. Boxes badged UI-ONLY are the deck's own furniture, drawn dashed and dim, never a measured node. Travelling packets are ledger events that NAMED both ends; an event that names nothing here animates nothing.</div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-telem">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>TELEMETRY</h2><span class="age never" id="age-telem">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-telem">
      <div class="gcol">
        <div class="g"><svg class="gg" data-g="thru"></svg><div><div class="gval" id="gThru">—</div><div class="glbl">EVENTS/min</div></div></div>
        <div class="g"><svg class="gg" data-g="lat"></svg><div><div class="gval" id="gLat">—</div><div class="glbl">LEDGER ms</div></div></div>
        <div class="g"><svg class="gg" data-g="cost"></svg><div><div class="gval" id="gCost">—</div><div class="glbl">SPEND $</div></div></div>
      </div>
      <div id="telem-facts" class="dim tiny"></div>
      <div class="dim tiny">EVENTS/min counts the ledger rows this deck actually received in the last 60s. LEDGER ms is the median of the latencies COSMOS wrote into its own records — never this deck's HTTP round trips, which measure the browser and not the mesh. SPEND $ is GET /api/v1/spend against its own caps. A dial with no source reads UNMEASURED and shows a grey arc; none of the three is modelled.</div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-music">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>MUSIC</h2><span class="age never" id="age-music">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-music">
      <!-- Two panels, two names. FEATURES_KEITH.md:54 defines "Jukebox" as the
           job/queue control surface and that is #panel-jukebox, which keeps the
           name. KDECK_BACKLOG row 20 ruled the BTS #pMusic widget DO-NOT unless
           Keith re-asked and said the two cannot share a panel name. He re-asked
           (2026-09-06 lift order: "jukebox #jvol"), so it is here as MUSIC. -->
      <div class="formrow jukebar">
        <button type="button" id="jukeAuto">AUTO –</button>
        <span class="dim tiny">VOL</span>
        <input id="deck-jvol" type="range" min="0" max="100" value="70" title="jukebox volume — the master gain on the deck's bus cues">
        <span id="deck-jvolv" class="dim tiny">70</span>
        <button type="button" id="jukeMute" title="mute the cues without losing the volume setting">MUTE</button>
        <button type="button" id="jukeTest" title="play one cue now, so the slider proves it drives a real output">TEST CUE</button>
      </div>
      <div class="np" id="jukeNp">♪ idle — AUTO is off, so the bus is silent</div>
      <div class="gatewhy" id="jukeGate">AUTO plays one short tone per ledger event, pitched by event class (verify · traffic · deny). #jvol is the master gain, persisted on the deck.</div>
      <div class="dim tiny">NO TRACK EMBED, and not because it was forgotten: occupancy is native cdeck.exe with no iframe and no Chrome host, and the lifted player was an iframe plus a YouTube API key in localStorage. This deck's bearer comes from the native <code>load_install_token</code> and is never written to disk, so a widget that persists a vendor credential does not come across. What #jvol drives instead is the COSMOS bus, and it is audible.</div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-status">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>STATUS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-status">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-status"><div class="empty">not connected — set the COSMOS URL and CONNECT</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-health">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>HEALTH</h2><span class="age never" id="age-health">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-health"><div class="empty">not connected</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-spend">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>SPEND</h2><span class="age never" id="age-spend">no data</span></div>
    <!-- F-03 live write: a raise is 409 WIDEN_REQUIRES_CONFIRM until the operator
         POSTs again with JSON boolean allow_widen:true. Lives OUTSIDE #bd-spend so
         a poll re-render cannot wipe the confirm. Same shape as #cvmConfirm. -->
    <div id="spendConfirm" class="hidden cvm-confirm" role="status"></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-spend"><div class="empty">not connected</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-jobs">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>JOBS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-jobs">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-jobs"><div class="empty">not connected</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-rails">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>RAILS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-rails">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-rails"><div class="empty">not connected</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-makers">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>MAKERS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-makers">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-makers"><div class="empty">not connected</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel span2" id="panel-events">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>LIVE EVENTS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-events">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd">
      <div class="dim tiny" style="margin-bottom:6px">append-only ledger tail — GET /events?since_seq=N · never refetches old seq · cursor resets on server change or ledger rewind · <b>click a row</b> for the payload COSMOS wrote, and to follow its job_id / link_id · scrolling up PAUSES the tail</div>
      <div class="quickcmds" id="feed-filters">
        <button type="button" data-feed="ALL" class="on">all</button>
        <button type="button" data-feed="SPEND">spend</button>
        <button type="button" data-feed="JOB">jobs</button>
        <button type="button" data-feed="VOICE">voice</button>
      </div>
      <div class="feedbar">
        <span id="feedFollow" class="hidden"></span>
        <button type="button" id="feedJump" class="hidden"></button>
      </div>
      <div id="feed"><div class="empty">not connected — the ledger tail streams here once connected</div></div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-audit">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>AUDIT</h2><span class="age never" id="age-audit">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-audit"><div class="empty">not connected</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-tools">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>TOOLS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-tools">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-tools"><div class="empty">not connected</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-jukebox">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>JUKEBOX</h2><span class="age never" id="age-jukebox">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-jukebox">
      <div class="dim tiny">job/queue control — POST /jobs {command, priority} · GET /jobs is the measured queue</div>
      <div class="formrow">
        <input id="jobCmd" type="text" placeholder="command to queue" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off">
        <div class="mr-chiprow" id="jobPri" title="priority">
          <button type="button" class="chip on" data-val="normal">normal</button>
          <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="low">low</button>
          <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="high">high</button>
          <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="critical">critical</button>
        </div>
        <button type="button" id="btnJobSubmit">QUEUE</button>
      </div>
      <!-- cosmos_sched's OWN five words. `done` and `failed` used to be here and
           are two states COSMOS never emits (OUTCOMES = CLEAN/FINDINGS/BROKE),
           so those two buttons could not match a job that had ever existed. -->
      <div class="quickcmds" id="jukebox-prios">
        <button type="button" data-filter="ALL" class="on">all</button>
        <button type="button" data-filter="QUEUED">queued</button>
        <button type="button" data-filter="RUNNING">running</button>
        <button type="button" data-filter="CLEAN">clean</button>
        <button type="button" data-filter="FINDINGS">findings</button>
        <button type="button" data-filter="BROKE">broke</button>
      </div>
      <div id="jukebox-list"><div class="empty">not connected</div></div>
      <div class="dim tiny">no cancel / hold / retry control: cosmos_sched has no such verb — a stale RUNNING job is reported, never retried. The full on-disk projection (command, priority, lane, claimant, stale flag) is <code>cosmos_jukebox_panel.py</code>.</div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel wide" id="panel-nodemap">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>NODE MAP</h2><span class="age never" id="age-nodemap">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-nodemap">
      <div class="nmap-toolbar">
        <button type="button" id="nmapReset">RESET LAYOUT</button>
        <span class="dim">drag nodes · positions persist locally (deck.json, with a localStorage copy) · UNMEASURED is never painted as 0</span>
      </div>
      <div id="nmap-banner" class="dim tiny">no mesh read yet</div>
      <div id="nmap-stage">
        <svg id="nmap-edges" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"></svg>
        <div id="nmap-nodes"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="nmap-detail" class="empty">select a node — the mesh comes from <code>registry/rails.json</code> (the rails prober's projection of nodes COSMOS has PROVEN live) when the runtime root is set, else from GET /rails. Boxes badged UI-ONLY are the deck's own furniture, never a measured node.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="phonenote" data-degraded><b>PHONE MODE — MAP, DRAG IS LOCAL TO THIS WIDTH.</b> Nodes
      stay absolutely placed on a stage that grows to fit them (overflow visible, nothing clipped).
      Positions persist in a <b>phone-keyed store</b> — a drag here cannot overwrite the desktop
      layout in deck.json. RESET LAYOUT clears only this width. Tapping a node still opens its
      detail.</div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-batteries">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>BATTERIES</h2><span class="age never" id="age-batteries">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-batteries"><div class="empty">not connected — per-node health / quota / headroom</div></div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel span2" id="panel-fleet">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>FLEET</h2><span class="age never" id="age-fleet">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-fleet">
      <div class="dim tiny">The COSMOS daemons, read from the projections the fleet writes to disk — <code>state/cdeck/feed.json</code> (cosmos_cdeck_feed) and <code>state/drive/meter.json</code> (cosmos_drive_meter) — <b>not</b> over HTTP, so this panel still sees the fleet when Core is not serving. Age is measured (now &minus; the clock's own <code>last_run_epoch</code>). Cadence is <b>not</b> on this projection, so an age band is not a health verdict: a 15-minute clock reads OLD between runs and is fine. <code>resident</code> means the feed found that pid running.</div>
      <div class="formrow">
        <input id="fleetRoot" type="text" placeholder="COSMOS runtime root on THIS machine — e.g. V:\A\Ai\COSMOS\live" data-example-root="V:\A\Ai\COSMOS\live" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" title="Local filesystem path to the runtime root. Learned from GET /status when the deck runs on the COSMOS host; set it by hand otherwise. Verified by sentinel content, not by existence.">
        <button type="button" id="btnFleetRoot">SET ROOT</button>
        <span id="fleetBind" class="dim">no runtime root</span>
      </div>
      <div id="fleet-body"><div class="empty">no runtime root set — the deck adopts it from GET /status when Core answers, or set it above</div></div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-caps">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>CAPS &amp; SPEEDS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-caps">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-caps"><div class="empty">not connected — token caps + measured latency per node/channel</div></div>
    <div class="phonenote" data-degraded><b>PHONE MODE — WIDE TABLE, SCROLLS SIDEWAYS.</b> Seven
      columns do not fit a handset. The table keeps its real width and this panel scrolls
      horizontally: <b>no column is dropped and no value is truncated</b> — swipe the table left.</div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel span2" id="panel-cvm">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>CVM</h2><span class="age never" id="age-cvm">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-cvm">
      <div class="dim tiny">COSMOS voice — POST /voice {transcript, session_id?, confirm_id?} · GET /control · POST /kill (off-switch) · POST /control/resume. Transcripts are never auto-executed. A SPEND_BLOCKED reply is the voice-breaker signal; RESUME clears voice-spend counters, not rail caps.</div>
      <div class="cvm-flags" id="cvm-flags"><span class="dim">control flags unknown until connected</span></div>
      <div class="formrow">
        <button type="button" id="btnCvmKill" class="danger">KILL</button>
        <button type="button" id="btnCvmResume">RESUME</button>
        <button type="button" id="btnCvmMic" title="voice input">&#127908;</button>
        <span id="cvmMicState" class="dim">mic idle</span>
        <span id="cvmSid" class="dim">no session</span>
      </div>
      <div id="cvmConfirm" class="hidden cvm-confirm"></div>
      <div class="formrow">
        <input id="cvmStream" type="text" placeholder="stream for session start" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off">
        <button type="button" id="btnSessStart">SESSION START</button>
        <button type="button" id="btnSessClose">SESSION CLOSE</button>
      </div>
      <div class="formrow">
        <input id="cvmInput" type="text" placeholder="transcript for POST /voice — review, then SEND" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off">
        <button type="button" id="btnCvmSend">SEND</button>
      </div>
      <div id="cvmHeard" class="dim tiny"></div>
      <div id="cvmLog"><div class="empty">no voice turns yet</div></div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-channels">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>CHANNELS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-channels">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-channels"><div class="empty">not connected — rails as channels: probe age, cap, speed</div></div>
    <div class="phonenote" data-degraded><b>PHONE MODE — WIDE TABLE, SCROLLS SIDEWAYS.</b> Nine
      columns in a 320px viewport is 32px a column; the deck refuses to crush them into
      unreadable stubs. The table keeps its real width and this panel scrolls horizontally —
      <b>no column is dropped</b>. Swipe the table left.</div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel home-card" id="panel-surfaces">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>SURFACES</h2><span class="age never" id="age-surfaces">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd surfaces-body">
      <div class="surf-col">
        <h3>Storage</h3>
        <div id="bd-surfaces"><div class="empty">not connected — GET /api/v1/surfaces</div></div>
        <h3>Add a surface</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">R2, Google Drive, local / removable, NAS / network, GitHub, online URL. Keith pastes the path. GET never mkdir and never invents reach. POST /api/v1/surfaces.</p>
        <div class="formgrid" id="surfAdd">
          <label>type
            <div class="mr-chiprow" id="surfType"></div>
          </label>
          <label>id <input id="surfId" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="nas-home"></label>
          <label>path / url <input id="surfPath" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="\\host\share or https://…"></label>
          <button type="button" id="surfAddBtn">ADD</button>
        </div>
        <div class="tiny" id="surfAddSay"></div>
        <div id="bd-surf-catalog"><div class="empty">catalog unread</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="surf-col">
        <h3>Channels</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">API · CLI · DOM · MCP — GET /api/v1/surfaces_kit. MCP is a channel even when n=0.</p>
        <div id="bd-surf-channels"><div class="empty">channels unread</div></div>
      </div>
      <div class="surf-col">
        <h3>Callable tools</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Local: DOI, PDF, OCR, UPS-JUDGE, ROLD, scar list. Online: API/DOM rails. UPS-JUDGE is NAMED — not invented.</p>
        <div id="bd-surf-tools"><div class="empty">tools unread</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section class="panel" id="panel-pools">
    <div class="panel-hd"><h2>HUMAN POOLS</h2><span class="age never" id="age-pools">no data</span></div>
    <div class="panel-bd" id="bd-pools">
      <div class="dim tiny">Claude / Grok subscription pools are <b>UI-only / human-posted</b>. COSMOS does not measure them. Posting here does not spend, does not call a model, and is never treated as live API data.</div>
      <div class="poolgrid">
        <div class="poolcard" data-pool="claude">
          <div class="rail-hd"><span class="rname">CLAUDE</span> <span class="badge">HUMAN-POSTED</span></div>
          <label>remaining <input id="poolClaudeRem" type="number" step="any" min="0"></label>
          <label>unit
            <div class="mr-chiprow" id="poolClaudeUnit">
              <button type="button" class="chip on" data-val="pct">% remaining</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="usd">$ remaining</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="tokens">tokens remaining</button>
            </div>
          </label>
          <label>note <input id="poolClaudeNote" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off"></label>
          <button type="button" data-pool="claude" class="btnPoolPost">POST</button>
          <div class="poolmeta dim tiny" id="poolClaudeMeta">never posted</div>
        </div>
        <div class="poolcard" data-pool="grok">
          <div class="rail-hd"><span class="rname">GROK</span> <span class="badge">HUMAN-POSTED</span></div>
          <label>remaining <input id="poolGrokRem" type="number" step="any" min="0"></label>
          <label>unit
            <div class="mr-chiprow" id="poolGrokUnit">
              <button type="button" class="chip on" data-val="pct">% remaining</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="usd">$ remaining</button>
              <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="tokens">tokens remaining</button>
            </div>
          </label>
          <label>note <input id="poolGrokNote" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off"></label>
          <button type="button" data-pool="grok" class="btnPoolPost">POST</button>
          <div class="poolmeta dim tiny" id="poolGrokMeta">never posted</div>
        </div>
        <div class="poolcard" data-pool="sgh">
          <div class="rail-hd"><span class="rname">SUPERGROK HEAVY</span> <span class="badge">HUMAN-POSTED</span></div>
          <div class="dim tiny">Three slices. Reset <b>September 3, 2026 at 5:35 PM</b> America/Chicago. Not sent to /spend.</div>
          <label>Build % used <input id="sghBuild" type="number" step="any" min="0" max="100" placeholder="36"></label>
          <label>Chat % used <input id="sghChat" type="number" step="any" min="0" max="100" placeholder="1"></label>
          <label>Automations % used <input id="sghAuto" type="number" step="any" min="0" max="100" placeholder="1"></label>
          <button type="button" id="btnSghPost" class="btnSghPost">POST SLICES</button>
          <div class="poolmeta dim tiny" id="sghMeta">never posted</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

</div>
</section>

<section class="panel home-card" id="panel-model-rater">
  <div class="panel-hd">
    <h2>MODEL RATER</h2>
    <a class="dim tiny" id="mrOpenRouter" href="https://openrouter.ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener">openrouter.ai</a>
    <a class="dim tiny" id="mrDocsCut" href="https://openrouter.ai/models?fmt=cards&amp;output_modalities=text&amp;input_modalities=text,file,image" target="_blank" rel="noopener">text/file/image → text</a>
    <a class="dim tiny" id="mrTableCut" href="https://openrouter.ai/models?fmt=table&amp;output_modalities=text&amp;category=programming" target="_blank" rel="noopener">table · programming</a>
    <span class="age unknown" id="mrAge">no catalog</span>
    <button type="button" id="mrRefresh">REFRESH</button>
  </div>
  <div class="panel-bd" id="mrBody">
    <p class="dim tiny">Live OpenRouter catalog via Core. GET /api/v1/model_rater. Catalog and keys: <a href="https://openrouter.ai" target="_blank" rel="noopener">openrouter.ai</a> · models <a href="https://openrouter.ai/models" target="_blank" rel="noopener">/models</a> · cards cut <a href="https://openrouter.ai/models?fmt=cards&amp;output_modalities=text&amp;input_modalities=text,file,image" target="_blank" rel="noopener">text/file/image → text</a> · table cut <a href="https://openrouter.ai/models?fmt=table&amp;output_modalities=text&amp;category=programming" target="_blank" rel="noopener">programming · text · table</a> · usage <a href="https://openrouter.ai/docs/cookbook/administration/usage-accounting" target="_blank" rel="noopener">usage-accounting</a>. Type <code>docs</code> is the cards cut; category <code>programming</code> is the table cut. Missing stays UNMEASURED. Blend is 75% input / 25% output $/M (estimate). Catalog is a spreadsheet pane at the bottom of this tab — one row per model, buttons on the row. Not a 400-row dropdown. Favored pin to the top; banned are hidden.</p>
    <section class="st-pane" id="mrFreePane">
      <h3>:free pins (vendor)</h3>
      <p class="dim tiny">Named ids only. Rotator REFUSED. 20 RPM · 50 RPD · $10 lifetime → 1000 RPD. Keith pastes. This pane does not top-up.</p>
      <div class="mr-chiprow studio-targets">
        <button type="button" class="chip on" name="mrFreePin" value="google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free">gemma-4-26b (default)</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" name="mrFreePin" value="google/gemma-4-31b-it:free">gemma-4-31b</button>
      </div>
    </section>
    <div class="mr-rolebar">
      <button type="button" id="mrRoleSearch">INCLUDE PROFILES + STUDIO</button>
      <span class="dim tiny" id="mrRoleN">roles unread</span>
    </div>
    <div class="mr-seats" id="mrSeats"></div>
    <div class="mr-poro-c" id="mrPoroC"><div class="empty">complement tensor C unread — GET /api/v1/porosity</div></div>
    <div class="mr-assign">
      <span class="dim tiny">via</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrVia"></div>
      <span class="dim tiny">2nd via</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrVia2"></div>
      <span class="dim tiny">3rd via</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrVia3"></div>
      <span class="dim tiny">effort</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrEffort">
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-effort="low">low</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-effort="medium">medium</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-effort="high">high</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-effort="max">max</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="mr-est">
      <span class="dim tiny">estimate in</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrTin">
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-tin="1000">1k</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-tin="4000">4k</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-tin="8000">8k</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-tin="24000">24k</button>
      </div>
      <span class="dim tiny">out</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrTout">
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-tout="400">400</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-tout="800">800</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-tout="2000">2k</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-tout="8000">8k</button>
      </div>
      <span id="mrEst" class="dim tiny">pick a model</span>
    </div>
    <div class="mr-filters">
      <span class="dim tiny">type</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrType">
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-type="">all</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-type="coding">coding</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-type="reasoning">reasoning</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-type="images">images</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-type="audio">audio</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-type="video">video</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-type="chat">chat</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-type="docs" value="docs">docs</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-type="free">free</button>
      </div>
      <span class="dim tiny">category</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrCategory">
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="">all</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-cat="programming">programming</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="roleplay">roleplay</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="marketing">marketing</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="marketing/seo">marketing/seo</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="technology">technology</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="science">science</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="translation">translation</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="legal">legal</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="finance">finance</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="health">health</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="trivia">trivia</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cat="academia">academia</button>
      </div>
      <span class="dim tiny">sort</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrSort">
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-sort="quality">Q</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="quality_per_cost">Q/$</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="intelligence">INT</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="intelligence_per_cost">INT/$</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="coding">COD</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="coding_per_cost">COD/$</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="agentic">AGT</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="agentic_per_cost">AGT/$</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="blended">blend</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="porosity">porosity</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="speed">speed</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="price">price</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="recency">recency</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="popularity">popularity</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="office">office</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="file">file</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="stability">stability</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="context">ctx</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="name">name</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="latency">latency</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="gpqa">GPQA</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="hle">HLE</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="ifbench">IFB</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="tau2">τ²</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="lcr">LCR</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="gdpval">GDP</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="critpt">CritPt</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="scicode">Sci</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="terminal_bench">TB</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="omniscience">Omni</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sort="omniscience_nh">OmniNH</button>
      </div>
      <span class="dim tiny">family</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrFam"></div>
      <div class="mr-chiprow">
        <button type="button" id="mrAssign">ASSIGN DEFAULT</button>
        <button type="button" id="mrAssign2">SET 2ND FALLBACK</button>
        <button type="button" id="mrAssign3">SET 3RD FALLBACK</button>
        <button type="button" id="mrSetCap">SET MODEL CAP</button>
        <button type="button" id="mrFav">FAVOR</button>
        <button type="button" id="mrBan">BAN</button>
        <button type="button" id="mrFavFam">FAVOR FAMILY</button>
        <button type="button" id="mrBanFam">BAN FAMILY</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" id="mrShowBanned">show banned</button>
      </div>
      <span class="dim tiny">cap USD</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrCap">
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-cap="0">off</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cap="1">$1</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cap="5">$5</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cap="10">$10</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-cap="25">$25</button>
      </div>
      <span class="dim tiny">err / 100 LOC</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrPoroLoc">
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-loc="0">0</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-loc="0.5">0.5</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-loc="1">1</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-loc="2">2</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-loc="5">5</button>
      </div>
      <span class="dim tiny">severity</span>
      <div class="mr-chiprow" id="mrPoroSev">
        <button type="button" class="chip on" data-sev="1">1</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sev="3">3</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sev="5">5</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sev="8">8</button>
        <button type="button" class="chip" data-sev="10">10</button>
      </div>
      <button type="button" id="mrPoro">RECORD POROSITY</button>
      <span class="dim tiny" id="mrCapSay">per-model cap is on the rater, not the Core spend gate</span>
    </div>
    <div class="mr-defs" id="mrDefs">
      <h3>Metric definitions</h3>
      <p class="dim tiny">From the OpenRouter model Benchmarks tab (Artificial Analysis). Scores copy when the vendor sends them; missing stays UNMEASURED — never invented. Rank percentile is OpenRouter's, among models they compared.</p>
      <div id="mrDefsBody"><div class="empty">definitions unread — GET /api/v1/model_rater</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="mr-agents" id="mrAgents">
      <h3>SDK · CLI · localhost agents</h3>
      <p class="dim tiny">Presence is PATH or import, not a live session. OpenWork/cDeck plugin-shaped RPC <code>GET /api/v1/agents</code>. Not a published pack.</p>
      <div id="mrAgentsBody"><div class="empty">agents unread — GET /api/v1/model_rater</div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="panel home-card orders-panel" id="panel-orders">
  <div class="panel-hd"><h2>WORK ORDERS</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="ordersAge">GET /api/v1/work_orders</span></div>
  <div class="panel-bd orders-body">
    <div class="orders-col">
      <div class="chips runs-chips" id="ordersChips"></div>
      <div class="dim tiny runs-note">Timestamped live list from GET /api/v1/work_orders
        (agent, product, checks). Folders are not the ledger.
        <span class="nowrap">↑/↓ walks the list. Click a row for work product.</span></div>

    </div>
