  </div>
</section>

<section class="panel home-card studio-panel" id="panel-studio">
  <div class="panel-hd"><h2>MOTIF STUDIO</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="studioAge">GET /api/v1/studio · PROBLEM / GOAL → IMPLEMENT</span></div>
  <div class="panel-bd studio-body">
    <aside class="studio-pal" id="studioPal" aria-label="node palette"></aside>
    <div class="studio-canvas" id="studioCanvas"></div>
    <aside class="studio-insp" aria-label="state inspector">
      <div id="studioDefine" hidden>
        <h3>1 · PROBLEM STATEMENT / STATED GOAL</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">PROBLEM STATEMENT / STATED GOAL. Frozen verbatim to every model. RESEARCH does not start without it. SAVE does not start MOTIF.</p>
        <textarea id="studioDefineText" rows="12" spellcheck="false"
          placeholder="WHAT&#10;WHY&#10;acceptance (live emit)&#10;off-limits"></textarea>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioDefineSave">SAVE STATEMENT</button>
        <div class="dim tiny" id="studioDefineAge">not saved</div>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioDefineSay"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioResearch" hidden>
        <h3>2 · RESEARCH</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Search agents via Model Rater (API / CLI / MCP / DOM, DEFAULT + secondary + tertiary fallback). Fallback runs only if the first call fails. Keys stay on the named rail — never pasted here. Targets are where RESEARCH looks (GitHub, docs, Reddit, social, USPTO search, legal, corporate, federal / state / local). USPTO is a search target, not Patent Center filing. This pack does not start MOTIF.</p>
        <div class="studio-cons-rules" data-cons-where="research" id="studioResearchRules"></div>
        <div id="studioResearchModels"></div>
        <div class="formrow">
          <div class="mr-chiprow" id="studioAddVia" aria-label="via for a new research model"></div>
          <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioAddModel">+ research model</button>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioOpenRater">open Model Rater</button>
        <h3>targets</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioTargets"></div>
        <label class="dim tiny">extra URLs — repos, company docs, specific forums (one per line)
          <textarea id="studioExtraUrls" rows="3" spellcheck="false" placeholder="https://…"></textarea>
        </label>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioResearchSave">SAVE RESEARCH</button>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioResearchSay"></div>
        <h3>research call (chat function)</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">COSMOS files <code>cosmos-research-call/1</code>. Perplexity chat (search_web / fetch_url) or a DOM rail returns <code>cosmos-research-result/1</code>. Core does not fetch. Core does not call Perplexity API. Legal URLs OMITTED. Invented green DONE is refused.</p>
        <label class="dim tiny">task
          <div class="mr-chiprow" id="rcTask">
            <button type="button" class="chip on" data-val="extract">extract</button>
            <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="summarize">summarize</button>
            <button type="button" class="chip" data-val="answer">answer</button>
          </div>
        </label>
        <label class="dim tiny">query
          <input id="rcQuery" type="text" spellcheck="false" autocomplete="off" placeholder="list API endpoints and their methods">
        </label>
        <label class="dim tiny">urls (one per line)
          <textarea id="rcUrls" rows="3" spellcheck="false" placeholder="https://…"></textarea>
        </label>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="rcFile">FILE CALL</button>
        <pre class="tiny" id="rcPayload"></pre>
        <label class="dim tiny">ingest result JSON
          <textarea id="rcIngest" rows="5" spellcheck="false" placeholder='{"schema":"cosmos-research-result/1","status":"ok","result":[],"sources":[]}'></textarea>
        </label>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="rcIngestBtn">INGEST RETURN</button>
        <div class="tiny" id="rcSay"></div>
        <div id="rcLog" class="dim tiny"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioArch" hidden>
        <h3>3 · ARCH</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Decision rubric first. Each node designs independently — no peeking. Same DEFINE text. Proposals land here. CONSENSUS chooses (HITL or AUTO by the bar). SAVE does not start MOTIF.</p>
        <div class="studio-cons-rules" data-cons-where="arch" id="studioArchRules"></div>
        <div id="studioArchProposals"></div>
        <div class="formrow">
          <input id="studioArchAddLabel" type="text" spellcheck="false" placeholder="architecture label">
          <input id="studioArchAddBy" type="text" spellcheck="false" placeholder="by (seat / model)">
          <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioArchAdd">+ architecture</button>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioArchSave">SAVE ARCH</button>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioArchToConsensus">open CONSENSUS</button>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioArchSay"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioConsensus" hidden>
        <h3 id="studioConsensusH">4 · CONSENSUS</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Set the bar. AUTO (default) picks architecture when the bar is met; otherwise CONTESTED — one line to Keith, no third model resolves. HITL always chooses. SAVE does not start MOTIF.</p>
        <div class="studio-cons-rules" data-cons-where="consensus" id="studioConsensusRules"></div>
        <h3>bar</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioBars"></div>
        <h3>architecture</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioArchChoice"></div>
        <div id="studioArchPickWrap">
          <label class="dim tiny">HITL pick
            <div class="mr-chiprow" id="studioArchPick" aria-label="choose architecture"></div>
          </label>
        </div>
        <label class="dim tiny">note (CONTESTED line / why this pick)
          <textarea id="studioConsensusNote" rows="3" spellcheck="false"></textarea>
        </label>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioConsensusSave">SAVE CONSENSUS</button>
        <div class="dim tiny" id="studioConsensusAge">not saved</div>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioConsensusSay"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioBuild" hidden>
        <h3>5 · BUILD</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Common artifact (JSON / TOML / SQL). N simultaneous builders. Input text is audited here — the same prompt for every parallel coder (DEFINE if left empty). Add as many agents as you like. Target is where the work lands. GitHub/GitLab runs through Gitur before any live-tree write. SAVE does not start MOTIF.</p>
        <div class="studio-cons-rules" data-cons-where="build" id="studioBuildRules"></div>
        <h3>target</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioBuildDest"></div>
        <label class="dim tiny">path / repo / folder
          <input id="studioBuildDestPath" type="text" spellcheck="false"
            placeholder="owner/repo, local file, or drive folder">
        </label>
        <div class="dim tiny" id="studioBuildDestGitur"></div>
        <h3>artifact</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioBuildArtifact"></div>
        <label class="dim tiny">simultaneous builders
          <input id="studioBuildSim" type="number" min="1" max="24" step="1">
        </label>
        <label class="dim tiny">audited input (same for all parallel coders)
          <textarea id="studioBuildInput" rows="8" spellcheck="false"
            placeholder="leave empty to use DEFINE verbatim"></textarea>
        </label>
        <div id="studioBuildAgents"></div>
        <div class="formrow">
          <div class="mr-chiprow" id="studioAddBuildVia" aria-label="via for a new builder"></div>
          <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioAddBuilder">+ builder</button>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioBuildSave">SAVE BUILD</button>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioBuildSay"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioCritics" hidden>
        <h3>6 · CRITICS</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Any number of critics, different families. Continuation when the bar is met, or HITL. Once met, IMPLEMENT (CCr via Gitur). SAVE does not start MOTIF.</p>
        <div class="studio-cons-rules" data-cons-where="critics" id="studioCriticsRules"></div>
        <h3>bar</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioCriticsBar"></div>
        <h3>continuation</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioCriticsCont"></div>
        <div id="studioCriticsAgents"></div>
        <div class="formrow">
          <div class="mr-chiprow" id="studioAddCriticVia" aria-label="via for a new critic"></div>
          <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioAddCritic">+ critic</button>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioCriticsSave">SAVE CRITICS</button>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioCriticsSay"></div>
        <h3>run composed round</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">POST /api/v1/crucible on Core. Empty critics = attached pool. Sources are docs-role names only. SAVE does not run. 501 CRUCIBLE_NOT_RUNNABLE if none attached. LEGAL_OMITTED.</p>
        <label class="dim tiny">sources (one docs name per line)
          <textarea id="studioCruSources" rows="2" spellcheck="false">FINAL_ARCHITECTURE.md</textarea>
        </label>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioCruRun">RUN CRUCIBLE ROUND</button>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioCruRuns">open RUNS</button>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioCruRunSay"></div>
        <pre class="tiny" id="studioCruRunLog"></pre>
      </div>
      <div id="studioImplement" hidden>
        <h3>8 · IMPLEMENT</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Was IMPROVE. CCr applies once CRITICS continuation is met. If the target is GitHub or GitLab, the code runs through Gitur before any live-tree write. Local file and cloud drive write there. This pane does not apply anything itself and does not start MOTIF.</p>
        <h3>target</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioImplementDest"></div>
        <label class="dim tiny">path / repo / folder
          <input id="studioImplementDestPath" type="text" spellcheck="false"
            placeholder="owner/repo, local file, or drive folder">
        </label>
        <div class="dim tiny" id="studioImplementDestGitur"></div>
        <label class="dim tiny">note
          <textarea id="studioImplementNote" rows="4" spellcheck="false"
            placeholder="what CCr applies, once continuation is met"></textarea>
        </label>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioImplementSave">SAVE IMPLEMENT</button>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioImplementSay"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioIterate" hidden>
        <h3>9 · ITERATE</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Conditional loop back to DEFINE. Set N rounds, a timer, a spend cap, or stop when the bar / runtime-binding / Keith says so. First limit that hits, stops. SAVE does not start MOTIF.</p>
        <div class="studio-cons-rules" data-cons-where="iterate" id="studioIterateRules"></div>
        <h3>stop when</h3>
        <div class="studio-targets" id="studioIterateUntil"></div>
        <label class="dim tiny">max rounds (0 = no round cap)
          <input id="studioIterateRounds" type="number" min="0" max="99" step="1">
        </label>
        <label class="dim tiny">timer seconds (0 = off)
          <input id="studioIterateTimed" type="number" min="0" max="86400" step="1">
        </label>
        <label class="dim tiny">budget USD (0 = off)
          <input id="studioIterateBudget" type="number" min="0" step="0.01">
        </label>
        <button type="button" class="studio-go" id="studioIterateSave">SAVE ITERATE</button>
        <div class="tiny" id="studioIterateSay"></div>
      </div>
      <div id="studioInsp"></div>
    </aside>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="panel home-card runs-panel" id="panel-runs">
  <div class="panel-hd"><h2>RUNS</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="runsAge">GET /api/v1/jukebox · GET /api/v1/runs_ops</span></div>
  <div class="panel-bd runs-body">
    <div class="runs-col">
      <div class="chips runs-chips" id="runsChips"></div>
      <div class="chips runs-chips" id="runsSortChips"></div>
      <div class="chips runs-spend" id="runsSpend"></div>
      <div class="dim tiny runs-note">n8n execution list from GET /api/v1/jukebox
        (command, priority, lane, age, stale). No per-step trace — Core does not emit one.
        <span class="nowrap">↑/↓ walks the list.</span></div>
      <div class="runs-list" id="bd-runs-list" tabindex="0"><div class="empty">queue unread — not an empty run list</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="runs-detail" id="bd-runs-detail"><div class="empty">pick a run — inspector is GET /jukebox fields, not a fabricated trace</div></div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="panel home-card" id="panel-review">
  <div class="panel-hd"><h2>REVIEW</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="reviewAge">GET /api/v1/review</span></div>
  <div class="panel-bd review-body">
    <div class="review-col">
      <h3>Spending approvals</h3>
      <div id="reviewSpend"><div class="empty">unread — GET /api/v1/review</div></div>
      <h3>Blocking decisions</h3>
      <div id="reviewBlock"><div class="empty">unread</div></div>
      <h3>Required logins</h3>
      <div id="reviewLogin"><div class="empty">unread</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="review-catalog">
      <div class="runs-tools" id="reviewWin">
        <button type="button" data-win="day">day</button>
        <button type="button" data-win="week" class="on">week</button>
        <button type="button" data-win="month">month</button>
        <button type="button" data-win="90">90 days</button>
      </div>
      <p class="dim tiny">Catalog of work products and session summaries. Sample is the current OpenWork GFO session — COSMOS does not copy Legal files.</p>
      <div id="reviewCatalog"><div class="empty">catalog unread — GET /api/v1/review</div></div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="panel home-card" id="panel-settings">
  <div class="panel-hd"><h2 id="settingsPopTitle">SETTINGS</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="settingsAge">GET /api/v1/status · /usage · /spend</span><button type="button" class="settings-close" id="settingsClose" aria-label="Close Settings">Close</button></div>
  <div class="panel-bd settings-shell" id="bd-settings">
    <aside class="settings-nav" id="settingsNav" aria-label="settings sections">
      <button type="button" class="on" data-set="profile">Profile</button>
      <button type="button" data-set="billing">Billing</button>
      <button type="button" data-set="quota">Quota/Usage</button>
    </aside>
    <div class="settings-main">
      <section class="set-page on" data-set="account">
        <p class="set-lead">Who this deck is talking to. GET never echoes a secret. Keith pastes keys.</p>
        <div class="set-cards" id="settingsAccount">
          <article class="set-card"><h3>Tree</h3><p class="set-val">UNMEASURED</p><p class="dim tiny">GET /status tree_id</p></article>
          <article class="set-card"><h3>Core</h3><p class="set-val">UNMEASURED</p><p class="dim tiny">:8770 ready</p></article>
        </div>
        <h3>Keys</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">LED from GET /api/v1/cred. PRESENT / LIVE / NO_SOURCE. Does not echo the secret.</p>
        <div class="set-cards" id="settingsKeys"><div class="empty">keys unread</div></div>
      </section>
      <section class="set-page" data-set="usage">
        <p class="set-lead">OpenRouter Activity shape: spend · tokens · requests. GET /api/v1/usage. UNMEASURED until a dispatch is recorded. Not a billing page. Does not invent tokens.</p>
        <div class="set-metrics" id="settingsUsage"><div class="empty">usage unread</div></div>
        <div class="set-cards" id="settingsUsageBreakdown"><div class="empty">per-API / sub-account UNMEASURED until Core emits rows</div></div>
        <h3>Time and subscription length</h3>
        <p class="dim tiny">Hours and term are UNMEASURED until a vendor or Core emits them. Do not invent a remaining-hours bar.</p>
        <div class="set-cards" id="settingsSubTime">
          <article class="set-card"><h3>Hours</h3><p class="set-val">UNMEASURED</p></article>
          <article class="set-card"><h3>Term</h3><p class="set-val">UNMEASURED — Keith names the term</p></article>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="set-page" data-set="pins">
        <p class="set-lead">Named <code>:free</code> ids only. Rotator <code>openrouter/free</code> / <code>openrouter/auto</code> REFUSED. Vendor caps: <b>20 RPM</b> · <b>50 RPD</b> until a <b>$10</b> lifetime top-up, then <b>1000 RPD</b>. The $10 is not consumed by free-model usage. This TUI does not complete a top-up.</p>
        <div class="set-cards" id="settingsFreePins">
          <label class="set-card set-pick">
            <input type="radio" name="settingsFreePin" value="google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free" checked>
            <h3>Gemma 4 26B</h3>
            <p class="set-val">google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free</p>
            <p class="dim tiny">Default. Low latency. allow_fallbacks=false.</p>
          </label>
          <label class="set-card set-pick">
            <input type="radio" name="settingsFreePin" value="google/gemma-4-31b-it:free">
            <h3>Gemma 4 31B</h3>
            <p class="set-val">google/gemma-4-31b-it:free</p>
            <p class="dim tiny">Named pin. Not the rotator.</p>
          </label>
        </div>
        <p class="dim tiny">Runtime bind = <code>response.model</code>. Keys: <a href="https://openrouter.ai/keys" target="_blank" rel="noopener">openrouter.ai/keys</a> — Keith click.</p>
      </section>
      <section class="set-page" data-set="plan">
        <p class="set-lead">Phone ChatBot pitch is FREE FOREVER — YOU PICK THE MODEL. This operator deck is not that product. Premium price UNMEASURED.</p>
        <div class="set-cards" id="settingsPlans">
          <label class="set-card set-pick">
            <input type="radio" name="settingsPlan" value="free" checked>
            <h3>Free</h3>
            <p class="dim tiny">Consumer ChatBot named :free pins. Not this CCr seat.</p>
          </label>
          <label class="set-card set-pick">
            <input type="radio" name="settingsPlan" value="freemium">
            <h3>Freemium desktop</h3>
            <p class="dim tiny">Free download. Premium sit UNMEASURED.</p>
          </label>
          <label class="set-card set-pick">
            <input type="radio" name="settingsPlan" value="premium">
            <h3>Premium</h3>
            <p class="dim tiny">Price and length UNMEASURED until Keith names them.</p>
          </label>
        </div>
      </section>
      <section class="set-page" data-set="pay">
        <p class="set-lead">Keith does money. This TUI does not complete a charge and does not open vendor keys/billing pages that spend. Homepages only — Keith click.</p>
        <div class="set-cards" id="settingsPay">
          <a class="set-card" href="https://dashboard.stripe.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><h3>Stripe</h3><p class="dim tiny">Vendor homepage. Not a charge from this deck.</p></a>
          <a class="set-card" href="https://accounts.google.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><h3>Google</h3><p class="dim tiny">Login. Keith click.</p></a>
          <a class="set-card" href="https://www.paypal.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><h3>PayPal</h3><p class="dim tiny">Vendor homepage.</p></a>
          <a class="set-card" href="https://pay.amazon.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><h3>Amazon</h3><p class="dim tiny">Vendor homepage.</p></a>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<section class="panel home-card pf-page" id="pfPage-forge" data-profile="forge"></section>
<section class="panel home-card pf-page" id="pfPage-crucible" data-profile="crucible"></section>
<section class="panel home-card pf-page" id="pfPage-diligence" data-profile="diligence"></section>
<section class="panel home-card pf-page" id="pfPage-docket" data-profile="docket"></section>
<section class="panel home-card pf-page" id="pfPage-ups" data-profile="ups"></section>
<section class="panel home-card pf-page" id="pfPage-differentiator" data-profile="differentiator"></section>
<section class="panel home-card pf-page" id="pfPage-website" data-profile="website"></section>

<section class="panel home-card" id="panel-profiles">
  <div class="panel-hd"><h2>PROFILES</h2><span class="dim tiny" id="pfAge">GET /api/v1/profiles · MOTIF skins</span></div>
  <div class="panel-bd pf-body">
    <p class="dim tiny">Stream schematic for every occupancy profile. Click a profile to open its full section at the bottom — that profile's own left tabs and tools. Coding (Forge) shows the MOTIF sequence at the top and a left tab per step for setup (Session · Seats · Estimate · TidyUP/BOOTUP, then MOTIF 1–9). RESEARCH→CONSENSUS can RUN BACKGROUND FREE CLI. SAVE does not start IMPLEMENT and does not publish.</p>
    <div class="pf-schematic" id="pfSchematic"><div class="empty">schematic unread — GET /api/v1/profiles</div></div>
    <div class="pf-full" id="pfFull">
      <h3>Full section</h3>
      <p class="dim tiny">Forge: MOTIF sequence on top, one setup pane per step. Other profiles: left tabs are the 9 MOTIF stages. Dest depends on the profile.</p>
      <div class="pf-pick" id="pfPick"></div>
      <div class="pf-motif-top" id="pfMotifTop" hidden></div>
      <div class="pf-shell">
        <nav class="pf-stages" id="pfStages" aria-label="profile left tabs"></nav>
        <div class="pf-skin" id="pfSkin"><div class="empty">click a profile on the schematic</div></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>

<footer>
  <span>cDeck v0.3.0 — KDash cockpit + every panel below · bearer never written to disk · pools human-posted</span>
  <!-- Says whether this host can install the deck, and if not, WHICH of the
       three reasons applied. Filled only by app.js from what the browser and
       the worker actually reported — never from the presence of the files.
       It lives in the FOOTER, not the refresh bar: measured at 390px, a third
       chip up there wrapped the bar to a second line and pushed header chrome
       from 65.6% to 70.6% of the first screen (MOBILE_PROBE.json chromeVh),
       and the mobile pass spent real work reclaiming exactly that space.
       Install status is "about this client", which is what the footer is. -->
  <span id="pwaState" class="pwa dim">PWA: checking…</span>
  <span id="liveBind">live bind: not connected</span>
  <span id="deckClock"></span>
</footer>

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_more.css =====
#cdeck-more .edge-rail{position:absolute}
#cdeck-more{
  --bg:#f4efe6;
  --panel:#fffaf3;
  --panel-edge:#e4d8c8;
  --ink:#1f2328;
  --ink-dim:#57606a;
  --ink-faint:#8b949e;
  --green:#1a7f37;
  --amber:#9a6700;
  --red:#cf222e;
  --cyan:#8a5a2b;
  --head:#1c1410;
  --well:#efe6d8;
  --btn:#f7f0e6;
  --btn-border:#e4d8c8;
  --btn-hover:#eadfcf;
  --hair:#eadfcf;
  --danger-bg:#ffebe9;
  --sidebar:#ebe2d4;
  --mono:"Segoe UI","Calibri",system-ui,sans-serif;
  --deck-pt:12pt;
  --deck-sub-pt:9pt;
}
#cdeck-more *{box-sizing:border-box}
#cdeck-more .deck-more-body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:var(--deck-pt);line-height:1.45;-webkit-text-size-adjust:100%}
/* Nested under JACK'S MESH — never a full-viewport page. 100vh here hid the cockpit. */
#cdeck-more{padding:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;min-height:0;height:auto}

#cdeck-more header{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin-bottom:8px}
#cdeck-more header h1{font-size:1.15em;color:var(--head);letter-spacing:2px;font-weight:700;margin-right:6px}
#cdeck-more header h1 span{color:var(--cyan)}
#cdeck-more header h1 small{color:var(--ink-faint);font-size:var(--deck-sub-pt);letter-spacing:1px;font-weight:400}
/* ══ THE METER RACK — lifted from the KDash title bar ══════════════════════════
   Compact pills so several fit where one battery was. Each: OWNER·KIND label + a
   short bar + a value. Colour is by STATE, never decorative:
     green = plenty · amber = getting on · red = nearly out · GREY = NO DATA.
   A grey pill is the honest default: "unknown", not "probably fine".
   THE SIZING IS NOT PIXEL ARITHMETIC, and that is deliberate. The lift's own
   comments carry three failed layouts' worth of measured budgets ("bar = 908px,
   demand = 930, over by 108") and record that the arithmetic broke every time the
   bar changed. Every element here is flex with min-width:0, so the rack ABSORBS a
   shortfall by shrinking instead of wrapping the header onto another line. If it
   must shrink further, drop the % text (it is in the tooltip) BEFORE shrinking the
   bar — the bar is the thing you read at a glance, and that is the whole point of
   a cockpit meter. */
.mtrRack{
  display:flex;flex-flow:row nowrap;gap:4px;align-items:center;
  margin:0 4px;flex:0 1 auto;min-width:0;overflow:hidden;
}
.mtr{
  display:flex;align-items:center;gap:4px;padding:2px 5px;border:1px solid #222d5e;
  border-radius:5px;background:#080d20;cursor:help;line-height:1;flex:0 1 auto;min-width:0;
}
.mtr u{
  font-style:normal;text-decoration:none;font-size:8.5px;font-weight:800;
  letter-spacing:.2px;white-space:nowrap;flex:none;
}
.mtr .mb{
  width:44px;min-width:18px;flex:1 1 44px;height:9px;background:#132043;
  border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden;
}
.mtr .mb i{display:block;height:100%;border-radius:3px}
.mtr b{font-size:9.5px;font-weight:800;text-align:right;white-space:nowrap;flex:none;color:#8fa3c8}
/* NO DATA is dimmed, and a POSTED figure is dashed: a number a human typed must
   never look like one COSMOS measured. */
.mtr.nodata{opacity:.5}
.mtr.posted{border-style:dashed}
.typesize{display:flex;gap:2px;margin-right:6px}
.typesize button{font-size:var(--deck-sub-pt);padding:2px 8px}
.connbar{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex:1;min-width:280px}
/* The connect fields live in a <details> so a phone can fold them away (see
   index.html). It ships `open` and the summary is hidden, so on a desktop the
   row is byte-for-byte the flex row it was. `::details-content` is Chrome 131+;
   engines without it ignore that rule and the flex on #connsetup does the same
   job — which is why both are declared rather than one. */
#connsetup,#connsetup::details-content{display:contents}
#connsetup>summary{display:none}
.connbar label{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap}
.connbar input{
  background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);color:var(--ink);
  font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;padding:4px 7px;border-radius:3px;outline:none;
}
.connbar input:focus{border-color:var(--cyan)}
#apiBase{width:240px}
#token{width:180px}
button{
  background:var(--btn);border:1px solid var(--btn-border);color:var(--ink);
  font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;padding:4px 12px;border-radius:3px;cursor:pointer;
}
button:hover{border-color:var(--cyan);color:var(--head)}
button:active{background:var(--btn-hover)}
button:disabled{opacity:.45;cursor:default}
button:disabled:hover{border-color:var(--btn-border);color:var(--ink)}
button.on{border-color:var(--cyan);color:var(--head)}
button.danger{border-color:var(--red);color:var(--red)}
button.danger:hover{background:var(--danger-bg);color:var(--head)}
#connState{font-size:11px;padding:3px 8px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);color:var(--ink-dim);white-space:nowrap}
#connState.ok{color:var(--green);border-color:var(--green)}
#connState.bad{color:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)}
#connState.wait{color:var(--amber);border-color:var(--amber)}

.refreshbar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;white-space:nowrap}
#countdown{color:var(--cyan);min-width:9.5em;display:inline-block;font-size:11px}
#countdown.paused{color:var(--amber)}

.down-banner{
  background:var(--danger-bg);border:1px solid var(--red);color:var(--red);
  padding:6px 10px;border-radius:3px;margin-bottom:10px;letter-spacing:.5px;font-size:12px;
}
.down-banner.hidden{display:none}

.cowork-mode{display:flex;gap:0;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:999px;overflow:hidden;margin-right:8px}
.cowork-mode button{border:none;border-radius:0;padding:4px 14px}
.cowork-mode button.on{background:var(--head);color:var(--panel)}
.cowork-tools{
  display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:6px;width:100%;
  padding:6px 0 2px;border-top:1px solid var(--hair);margin-top:4px;
}
.cowork-tools .toplink{
  color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.5px;text-decoration:none;
  padding:3px 8px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:999px;
}
.cowork-tools .toplink:hover{color:var(--head);border-color:var(--cyan)}
.edge-rail{
  position:fixed;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);z-index:8;
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;
}
.edge-left{left:6px}
.edge-right{right:6px}
.edge-rail a,.edge-rail button{
  width:26px;height:26px;padding:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  font-size:11px;text-decoration:none;color:var(--ink);border-radius:6px;
}
#cdeck-more.mode-code .orch-stage{display:none}
#cdeck-more.mode-code #orch-home{grid-template-rows:1fr minmax(64px,.18fr);grid-template-areas:"kdash" "openwork"}
#cdeck-more.mode-code #home-kdash{display:block}
/* Keith 2026-09-06: OpenWork slot dropped to the bottom; KDash tools take that space. */
.orch{
  flex:none;min-height:0;display:grid;
  grid-template-columns:minmax(168px,.42fr) minmax(0,2fr) minmax(260px,.9fr);
  gap:10px;margin-bottom:8px;align-items:stretch;
}
.orch-nav{
  min-width:0;min-height:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;overflow:auto;
  background:var(--sidebar);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:10px;padding:8px;
}
.orch-nav .home-card{flex:1 1 0}
.nav-list{padding:0 0 6px}
.orch-east{
  min-width:0;min-height:0;display:grid;gap:10px;
  grid-template-rows:minmax(72px,.28fr) minmax(120px,1.15fr) minmax(100px,.9fr);
}
.orch-east > *{min-height:0;overflow:auto}
#home-todos{overflow:auto}
#home-todo-list .chips{margin:6px 10px 4px}
#home-todo-list .jobrow{cursor:pointer;padding:4px 10px}
#home-todo-list .jobrow.on{background:var(--btn-hover)}
#nav-sched-list .sess-meta .ok{color:var(--green)}
#nav-stream-note,#nav-session-note,#nav-sched-note{
  padding:4px 10px 6px;color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:var(--deck-sub-pt);
}
.orch-home{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);gap:10px;min-width:0;min-height:0;
  grid-template-rows:minmax(120px,.4fr) minmax(220px,4fr) minmax(64px,.18fr);
  grid-template-areas:
    "stage"
    "kdash"
    "openwork";
  overflow:hidden;
}
.orch-stage{
  grid-area:stage;display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px;
  min-width:0;min-height:0;overflow:auto;
}
#home-kdash{
  grid-area:kdash;min-height:0;overflow:auto;
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));gap:10px;
}
#home-kdash .panel.wide{grid-column:1 / -1}
/* ══ THE KDASH SKIN — lifted, and scoped to the wall ═══════════════════════════
   Source: _lift/jack_command_2026-08-16.html (the pre-wipe cockpit). Keith's
   standing line is that the KDash VISUAL is the base model and the orch home does
   not delete or hide it (FEATURES_KEITH.md, 2026-09-06). So the wall wears the
   cockpit and the rest of the deck stays paper.
   IT IS DONE WITH CUSTOM PROPERTIES, NOT A SECOND STYLESHEET. Re-binding the
   deck's own variables on this one container re-skins every panel inside it —
   health, spend, rails, node map, surfaces and the three lifted panels — without
   forking a single rule. A parallel `.kdash .panel{…}` copy is how two panel
   styles drift until one of them is quietly wrong, which is the same failure the
   lift's own CSS notes record four generations of (`.grid` vs `.body`).
   The deck-wide theme-color and the paper header are deliberately untouched:
   the phone install banner and the PWA chip are measured against them. */
#home-kdash{
  --panel:#080b1c;
  --panel-edge:#1b2450;
  --ink:#cfe3ff;
  --ink-dim:#5f6ea0;
  --ink-faint:#41506f;
  --head:#22e3ff;
  --cyan:#22e3ff;
  --well:#0a0f28;
  --btn:#0a0f28;
  --btn-border:#23306a;
  --btn-hover:#132043;
  --hair:#1b2450;
  --green:#4dff9e;
  --amber:#ffb347;
  --red:#ff4d6d;
  --danger-bg:#2a0e18;
  background:#02030a;
  background-image:radial-gradient(circle at 20% 10%,#0a1030 0,transparent 40%),
                   radial-gradient(circle at 80% 90%,#1a0a26 0,transparent 45%);
  border:1px solid #1b2450;border-radius:10px;padding:8px;
  color:var(--ink);
}
#home-kdash .panel{
  background:radial-gradient(120% 120% at 0 0,#0d1430,#080b1c);
  border-radius:9px;
}
#home-kdash .panel-hd h2{color:var(--head);text-shadow:0 0 8px currentColor}
/* SIGNAL CORE. The svg carries its own 640x375 viewBox and scales into whatever
   the wall gives it — which is why a seat is stored in USER UNITS and needs no
   phone-keyed twin (see CORE_SEAT_KEY in app.js). */
#panel-core #core{
  width:100%;flex:1;min-height:260px;display:block;
  background:#05060f;border:1px solid #1b2450;border-radius:8px;
}
#panel-core #core text{font-family:"Consolas",ui-monospace,monospace}
#panel-core .dragn:hover circle{filter:drop-shadow(0 0 8px currentColor)}
#core-banner,#telem-facts{padding:4px 0}
#core-banner .chip,#telem-facts .chip{border-color:#23306a}
/* TELEMETRY gauges — side by side, so three fit across (the lift moved them off
   a stacked column for the same reason: ~130px of height for three numbers). */
.gcol{display:flex;flex-flow:row nowrap;justify-content:space-between;gap:6px;padding:6px 0}
.gcol .g{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;gap:1px;flex:1 1 0;min-width:0}
.gcol .g svg{width:100%;max-width:72px;height:44px;flex:0 0 auto}
.gcol .gval{font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:var(--head);text-shadow:0 0 8px currentColor;line-height:1.05}
.gcol .gval.unmeasured{font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink-dim);text-shadow:none}
.gcol .glbl{font-size:8px;color:var(--ink-dim);letter-spacing:.04em;white-space:nowrap}
/* MUSIC — the lifted jukebox transport row. */
.jukebar{align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.jukebar #jvol{flex:1;min-width:70px;max-width:140px;accent-color:var(--cyan);cursor:pointer}
/* `flex:1 1 0` + min-width:0 — the now-playing line is the row's SHOCK ABSORBER
   in the lift too: it ellipsises, so it surrenders width before the buttons do. */
.np{
  flex:1 1 0;min-width:0;margin:6px 10px 2px;font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-dim);
  white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;
}
.np b{color:var(--green)}
#home-openwork{
  grid-area:openwork;min-height:0;max-height:120px;overflow:hidden;
  display:flex;flex-direction:row;align-items:stretch;
}
.orch-east > #home-openwork{grid-area:auto}
.orch-home > #home-code,
.orch-home > #home-display,
.orch-home > #home-todos{grid-area:auto;min-height:120px}
#home-openwork .ow-pane{
  flex:1;min-height:0;display:flex;flex-direction:row;flex-wrap:wrap;
  align-items:center;gap:8px;padding:4px 10px 6px;
}
#home-openwork .code-term{display:none}
#home-openwork .ow-facts{margin:0;flex:1;min-width:180px}
#home-talk{
  min-height:0;overflow:hidden;
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;
}
#home-talk .cmdwrap{
  flex:1;min-height:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
  background:transparent;border:none;margin:0;padding:0 10px 8px;
}
#home-talk #console{
  flex:1;min-height:80px;max-height:160px;
}
.ow-chip{
  font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.6px;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:999px;
  border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);margin-left:auto;
}
.ow-chip.live{color:var(--green);border-color:var(--green)}
.ow-chip.down{color:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)}
.ow-chip.unknown{color:var(--ink-dim)}
.ow-facts{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:8px;
  margin:8px 0 0;padding:0;
}
.ow-facts dt{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.5px}
.ow-facts dd{margin:2px 0 0;word-break:break-word}
#home-work,#panel-create{min-height:0;max-height:none;overflow:auto}
.orch-code{
  background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:4px;
  min-height:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
}
.orch-code .code-pane{flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;min-height:0}
.home-card{
  background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:4px;
  padding:0 0 8px;min-width:0;
}
.dim,.tiny,.age,.sess-meta,.sess-turn,.gatewhy{
  font-size:var(--deck-sub-pt);
}
.home-card,.orch-code,.panel,.advanced,#orch-home,#orch-east,#home-nav{
  resize:both;overflow:auto;min-width:100px;min-height:64px;
}
.searchrow{
  display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:6px;margin-top:8px;
  padding-top:8px;border-top:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
}
.searchrow input{
  flex:1;min-width:160px;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;padding:5px 8px;border-radius:3px;
}
.code-term{
  flex:0 0 auto;min-height:0;margin:8px 10px 8px;padding:10px 12px;
  background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:3px;
  color:var(--ink-dim);
}
.code-term-prompt{color:var(--cyan);letter-spacing:1px;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px}
.code-term-body{white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;font-size:12px}
#codeHistFilter{
  margin:0 10px 6px;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  color:var(--ink);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;padding:5px 8px;border-radius:3px;
}
.code-hist{
  flex:1;min-height:140px;max-height:46vh;overflow:auto;margin:0 10px 10px;
  border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:3px;background:var(--well);
}
.code-hist .empty{padding:10px;color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:12px}
button.sess{
  display:block;width:100%;text-align:left;border:none;border-bottom:1px solid var(--hair);
  background:transparent;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:0;
}
button.sess:hover{background:var(--btn-hover);border-color:transparent;color:var(--head)}
button.sess .sess-title{color:var(--head);font-size:12px;display:block}
button.sess .sess-meta{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:10px;display:block;margin-top:2px}
button.sess .sess-turn{color:var(--ink-faint);font-size:11px;display:block;margin-top:2px}
.sghgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));gap:8px;margin:8px 0}
.sghgrid label,.poolcard label{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-dim)}
.sghgrid input{
  background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);color:var(--ink);
  font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;padding:4px 7px;border-radius:3px;
}
.nav-new{
  width:100%;padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px;
}
#navSessFilter{
  margin:0 8px 6px;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  color:var(--ink);font-family:inherit;font-size:12px;padding:5px 8px;border-radius:8px;
}
#home-talk .cmdbar.composer{
  align-items:flex-end;gap:8px;padding:10px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  border-radius:16px;background:var(--panel);box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(28,20,16,.06);
}
#home-talk .cmdbar.composer textarea{
  flex:1;min-height:72px;resize:vertical;border:none;background:transparent;
  font-family:inherit;font-size:15px;color:var(--ink);outline:none;padding:6px 4px;
}
.advanced{
  margin:12px 0 12px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:10px;padding:12px 10px;
}
.deck-below-hd{
  font-size:13px;letter-spacing:.6px;color:var(--head);font-weight:700;
  margin:0 0 10px;padding:0 4px;
}
@media(max-width:820px){
  .orch{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);flex:none}
  .orch-east{grid-template-rows:auto auto auto}
  .orch-home{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr);grid-template-rows:minmax(100px,.35fr) minmax(180px,1fr) minmax(64px,.2fr);grid-template-areas:
    "stage" "kdash" "openwork"}
  #home-kdash{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}
  #home-openwork{flex-direction:column;max-height:none}
  .ow-facts{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}
  .orch-stage{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}
  #home-work,#panel-create{max-height:none}
  .sghgrid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}
}

.decknav{
  display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px 8px;margin-bottom:10px;
  padding:4px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
}
.decknav a{
  color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;text-decoration:none;
  padding:2px 6px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid transparent;
}
.decknav a:hover{color:var(--head);border-color:var(--panel-edge)}

.cmdwrap{
  background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:4px;
  padding:8px 10px;margin-bottom:10px;
}
.cmdbar{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.cmdbar .prompt{color:var(--cyan);font-weight:700}
#cmdInput,#cvmInput,#jobCmd{
  flex:1;min-width:200px;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);color:var(--ink);
  font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;padding:5px 8px;border-radius:3px;outline:none;
}
#cmdInput:focus,#cvmInput:focus,#jobCmd:focus{border-color:var(--cyan)}
#btnMic,#btnCvmMic{padding:4px 9px;font-size:13px;line-height:1}
#btnMic.listening,#btnCvmMic.listening{border-color:var(--red);color:var(--red)}
#btnMic.unavailable,#btnCvmMic.unavailable{opacity:.4;cursor:help}
#btnMic.unavailable:hover,#btnCvmMic.unavailable:hover{border-color:var(--btn-border);color:var(--ink)}
#btnRun{border-color:var(--btn-border);color:var(--head);font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px}
.quickcmds{display:flex;gap:5px;margin-top:6px;flex-wrap:wrap}
.quickcmds button{font-size:11px;padding:2px 9px;color:var(--ink-dim)}
.quickcmds button:hover{color:var(--head)}
#console,#cvmLog{
  margin-top:8px;max-height:180px;overflow-y:auto;
  background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:3px;padding:6px 8px;
}
#console .empty,#cvmLog .empty{padding:2px 0}
.centry{margin-bottom:6px;border-left:2px solid var(--panel-edge);padding-left:8px}
.centry:last-child{margin-bottom:0}
.centry .cts{color:var(--ink-faint);font-size:10px}
.centry .ckind{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1px;font-weight:700;margin-left:6px}
.centry.cmd{border-left-color:var(--cyan)}
.centry.cmd .cbody{color:var(--cyan)}
.centry.ok{border-left-color:var(--green)}
.centry.ok .ckind{color:var(--green)}
.centry.err{border-left-color:var(--amber)}
.centry.err .ckind{color:var(--amber)}
.centry.err .cbody{color:var(--amber)}
.centry.heard{border-left-color:var(--amber)}
.centry.heard .ckind{color:var(--amber)}
.centry pre{
  font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;white-space:pre-wrap;word-break:break-word;
  color:var(--ink);margin-top:1px;
}

/* Which transport this window actually got — shell or browser fetch — and
   whether this host allowed the PWA install. Neither is a status guess:
   paintTransport() reports what shell()/fetch resolved to, and paintPwa()
   reports what navigator.serviceWorker and the worker itself answered. One
   rule for both chips; they are the same object in two colours. */
.transport,.pwa{font-size:11px;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:3px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  color:var(--ink-dim);white-space:nowrap;cursor:help}
.transport.ok,.pwa.ok{color:var(--green);border-color:var(--green)}
.transport.warn,.pwa.warn{color:var(--amber);border-color:var(--amber)}
.transport.bad,.pwa.bad{color:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)}
.pwa.wait{color:var(--ink-dim)}
.pwa.dim{color:var(--ink-dim);opacity:.75}

.grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:10px}
@media(max-width:1280px){.grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}}
@media(max-width:820px){.grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}}

/* The phone block used to live HERE, and that is precisely why it did not work:
   a later rule of equal specificity (`.formgrid input{font-size:12px}`, below)
   won the cascade over `@media(max-width:640px){.formgrid input{font-size:16px}}`
   by source order alone. The whole block now lives at the END of this file —
   see "PHONE" there, with the measurement that found it. */
.panel.wide{grid-column:1 / -1}
.panel.span2{grid-column:span 2}
@media(max-width:1280px){.panel.span2{grid-column:1 / -1}}

.panel{
  background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:4px;
  padding:0 0 8px;min-height:150px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
  transition:opacity .3s,border-color .3s;
}
.panel-hd{
  display:flex;align-items:baseline;justify-content:space-between;
  padding:7px 10px 6px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--panel-edge);margin-bottom:8px;
}
.panel-hd h2{font-size:1em;letter-spacing:2px;color:var(--head);font-weight:700}
.age{font-size:11px;color:var(--green)}
.age::before{content:"measured "}
.age::after{content:" ago"}
.age.never{color:var(--ink-faint)}
.age.never::before,.age.never::after{content:""}
.age.unknown{color:var(--amber)}
.age.unknown::before,.age.unknown::after{content:""}
.age.posted{color:var(--amber)}
.age.posted::before{content:"posted "}
.age.posted::after{content:" ago"}
.age.posted.never::before,.age.posted.never::after{content:""}
.panel-bd{padding:0 10px;flex:1;min-height:0}

.panel.stale{opacity:.55;border-color:var(--amber)}
.panel.stale .age{color:var(--amber);font-weight:700}
.panel.error{border-color:var(--red)}
.panel.error .age{color:var(--red)}
.errbox{
  color:var(--red);border:1px dashed var(--red);border-radius:3px;
  padding:6px 8px;margin-bottom:8px;font-size:12px;word-break:break-word;
}
.errbox b{letter-spacing:1px}

.kv{display:grid;grid-template-columns:max-content 1fr;gap:3px 14px}
.kv dt{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:12px}
.kv dd{color:var(--ink);word-break:break-all;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.ok{color:var(--green)} .warn{color:var(--amber)} .bad{color:var(--red)} .dim{color:var(--ink-faint)}
.big{font-size:15px;font-weight:700}
.tiny{font-size:var(--deck-sub-pt);letter-spacing:.3px;margin-bottom:8px}
.hidden{display:none !important}

.verdict{font-size:20px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:3px;margin-bottom:6px}
.verdict.green{color:var(--green)}
.verdict.red{color:var(--red)}
.verdict.broken{color:var(--red);animation:vflash .8s steps(2,start) infinite}
.verdict.unknown{color:var(--amber)}
@keyframes vflash{to{visibility:hidden}}
.diagnosis{color:var(--amber);font-size:12px;margin-bottom:6px;word-break:break-word}
.dot{display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;vertical-align:baseline}
.dot.g{background:var(--green)} .dot.r{background:var(--red)} .dot.a{background:var(--amber)}
tr.ncrow td{background:rgba(230,180,80,.07)}
.nclabel{color:var(--amber);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1px;white-space:nowrap}

.rail{margin-bottom:10px}
.rail-hd{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:2px}
.rail-hd .rname{color:var(--head);font-weight:700}
.badge{
  display:inline-block;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1px;padding:1px 6px;border-radius:3px;
  border:1px solid var(--amber);color:var(--amber);white-space:nowrap;
}
.badge.bad{border-color:var(--red);color:var(--red)}
.badge.ok{border-color:var(--green);color:var(--green)}
.badge.human{border-color:var(--cyan);color:var(--cyan)}
.bar{
  position:relative;height:10px;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden;margin:3px 0 2px;
}
.bar .seg{position:absolute;top:0;bottom:0}
.bar .seg.settled{background:var(--cyan);opacity:.8}
.bar .seg.reserved{background:var(--amber);opacity:.8}
.bar .seg.over{background:var(--red);opacity:.85}
.bar .seg.intend{background:transparent;border-right:2px solid var(--head);opacity:.9}
.rail-ft{font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-dim);display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.barlegend{font-size:10px;color:var(--ink-faint);margin-bottom:8px}
.barlegend i{display:inline-block;width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:2px;vertical-align:middle;margin:0 3px 0 8px}
.barlegend i.s{background:var(--cyan)} .barlegend i.r{background:var(--amber)}

.spend-edit{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(90px,1fr));gap:6px;
  margin-top:6px;padding:6px;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:3px;
}
.spend-edit label,.formgrid label,.poolcard label{
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.5px;
}
.spend-edit input,.spend-edit select,.formgrid input,.formgrid select,.poolcard input,.poolcard select,
.formrow select{
  background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);color:var(--ink);
  font-family:var(--mono);font-size:12px;padding:3px 6px;border-radius:3px;outline:none;width:100%;
}
.spend-edit input:focus,.formgrid input:focus,.poolcard input:focus{border-color:var(--cyan)}
.spend-edit button{align-self:end}
.spend-edit .btnrow{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:end;flex-wrap:wrap;grid-column:1 / -1}
.spend-layer{
  border:1px dashed var(--panel-edge);padding:6px 8px;margin-bottom:8px;
  font-size:11px;color:var(--ink-dim);
}
.spend-layer b{color:var(--head)}
.rail.flash{outline:1px solid var(--cyan)}
.fevent.hide{display:none}

.chips{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:8px}
.chip{
  border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:3px;padding:2px 8px;font-size:11px;
  color:var(--ink-dim);white-space:nowrap;
}
.chip b{color:var(--head);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.chip.v b{color:var(--green)}
/* A job state cosmos_sched does not emit — a client/OS mismatch, shown as one. */
.chip.bad{border-color:var(--red)}
.chip.bad b{color:var(--red)}

.jobstate{margin-bottom:8px}
.jobstate-hd{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:8px;margin-bottom:2px}
.jobstate-hd .st{font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px}
.jobstate-hd .ct{color:var(--cyan);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
.jobids{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11px;word-break:break-all;padding-left:1em}

.formrow{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin:6px 0}
.formgrid{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:6px;margin:8px 0;
}
.formgrid button{align-self:end}

/* A control COSMOS cannot perform is disabled AND says why, in the same place a
   human is already looking. A button that silently does nothing is the defect. */
.gatewhy{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.3px;color:var(--ink-dim);margin-top:4px}
.gatewhy .why-kind{
  font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;color:var(--red);margin-right:6px;
}
.gatewhy.ready .why-kind{color:var(--green)}
.cmdpreview{
  margin:2px 0 4px;padding:6px 8px;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  font-size:10px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--ink);white-space:pre-wrap;
  word-break:break-all;max-height:110px;overflow-y:auto;
}
.mcard .mkuse{margin-top:6px}
.mcard.picked{border-color:var(--cyan)}

.jobrow{
  display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline;padding:3px 0;
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--hair);font-size:12px;
}
.jobrow .jid{color:var(--head);word-break:break-all;flex:1}
.jobrow .jst{font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;min-width:6em}

#feed{max-height:280px;overflow-y:auto}
.fevent{
  display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:baseline;padding:2px 0;
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--hair);font-size:12px;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;
}
.fevent:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.fevent .fseq{color:var(--ink-faint);min-width:4.5em;text-align:right}
.fevent .fname{color:var(--head);word-break:break-all;flex:1}
.fevent .fwriter{color:var(--cyan);font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap}
.fevent .fage{color:var(--ink-dim);font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap;min-width:5em;text-align:right}

/* EVENT INSPECTOR (RESEARCH_1 § 9.2). The row is clickable and says so; the
   expanded record is a FULL-WIDTH child of the same flex row, so it cannot push
   the row past the panel — `flex-wrap` plus `flex:1 1 100%` rather than a second
   absolutely-positioned layer that a phone would clip. */
.fevent{cursor:pointer;flex-wrap:wrap}
.fevent .fmore{color:var(--ink-faint);font-size:11px;min-width:1em;text-align:center}
.fevent.open{background:var(--well)}
.fevent.open .fmore{color:var(--cyan)}
.finspect{flex:1 1 100%;margin:4px 0 6px;padding:6px 8px;border-left:2px solid var(--cyan);background:var(--well)}
/* pre-wrap, never a horizontal scroller: a payload line longer than the panel
   would otherwise be a silent clip at phone width (the class mobile_probe.py
   counts). Nothing here can overflow sideways. */
.finspect pre{
  margin:4px 0 0;white-space:pre-wrap;overflow-wrap:anywhere;word-break:break-word;
  font-size:11px;color:var(--ink);max-height:220px;overflow-y:auto;
}
.finspect .chips{margin-top:6px}
.feedbar{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;align-items:center;margin:0 0 4px}
.feedbar #feedJump{border-color:var(--amber);color:var(--amber)}

.mcard{
  border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:3px;padding:8px;margin-bottom:8px;background:var(--well);
}
.mcard .mtop{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:4px}
.mcard details{margin-top:4px}
.mcard summary,.tooltable summary{
  cursor:pointer;color:var(--cyan);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;list-style:none;
}
.mcard summary::-webkit-details-marker,.tooltable summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}
.mcard summary::before,.tooltable summary::before{content:"+ "}
.mcard details[open] summary::before,.tooltable[open] summary::before{content:"- "}
details.tooltable{margin-top:8px}
details.tooltable .twrap{max-height:260px;overflow-y:auto;margin-top:4px}

.twrap{overflow-x:auto}
table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:12px}
th{color:var(--ink-dim);text-align:left;font-weight:400;letter-spacing:1px;
   border-bottom:1px solid var(--panel-edge);padding:2px 10px 4px 0;font-size:11px}
td{padding:3px 10px 3px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--hair);word-break:break-all;
   font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
tr:last-child td{border-bottom:none}

.nmap-toolbar{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:6px}
#nmap-stage{
  position:relative;height:420px;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);
  border-radius:3px;overflow:hidden;touch-action:none;
}
#nmap-edges{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;pointer-events:none}
#nmap-nodes{position:absolute;inset:0}
.nmap-node{
  position:absolute;min-width:88px;max-width:140px;padding:6px 8px;
  background:var(--panel);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:4px;
  cursor:grab;user-select:none;z-index:1;
}
.nmap-node:active{cursor:grabbing}
.nmap-node.sel{border-color:var(--cyan);z-index:2}
.nmap-node .nid{font-weight:700;color:var(--head);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px}
.nmap-node .nkind{color:var(--ink-faint);font-size:10px}
.nmap-node .nst{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:1px;font-weight:700;margin-top:2px}
.nmap-node .nage{font-size:9px;color:var(--ink-faint);letter-spacing:.5px}
.nmap-node .nage.warn{color:var(--amber)}
/* A box the deck drew itself, not a node COSMOS measured. Dashed + dimmed so
   the difference between the map and the drawing is visible at a glance. */
.nmap-node.uionly{border-style:dashed;opacity:.72}
.nmap-node.mesh{border-color:var(--cyan)}
.nmap-node.prober{border-style:dashed;border-color:var(--green)}
.nmap-node.group{border-style:dotted;color:var(--ink-faint)}
.nmap-node.kernel{border-color:var(--cyan)}
.nmap-node.pool{border-style:dashed;border-color:var(--amber)}
.nmap-node.surface{border-style:dotted}
.nmap-node.control{border-color:var(--green)}
.nmap-node.unmeasured .nst{color:var(--red)}
.nmap-node.ok .nst{color:var(--green)}
.nmap-node.warn .nst{color:var(--amber)}
.nmap-node.bad .nst{color:var(--red)}

/* MAP PULSES (RESEARCH_1 § 5.4). A ledger event that NAMES a node lights that
   node; an event that names nothing on the map animates nothing. The colour is
   what kind of thing happened, never where it went — see PULSE_CLASS/PULSE_HOME
   in app.js, and the count of un-animated events in the map banner.
   prefers-reduced-motion drops the animation and keeps the outline, so the
   information survives for a reader who cannot take the flash. */
@keyframes nmap-pulse{
  0%{box-shadow:0 0 0 0 currentColor}
  100%{box-shadow:0 0 0 10px transparent}
}
.nmap-node.pulse-verify,.nmap-node.pulse-traffic,.nmap-node.pulse-deny{
  animation:nmap-pulse 1.5s ease-out 1
}
.nmap-node.pulse-verify{color:var(--green);border-color:var(--green)}
.nmap-node.pulse-traffic{color:var(--cyan);border-color:var(--cyan)}
.nmap-node.pulse-deny{color:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)}
.npacket{pointer-events:none}
.npacket.verify{fill:var(--green)}
.npacket.traffic{fill:var(--cyan)}
.npacket.deny{fill:var(--red)}
@media(prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
  .nmap-node.pulse-verify,.nmap-node.pulse-traffic,.nmap-node.pulse-deny{animation:none}
  .npacket{display:none}
}
#nmap-detail{margin-top:8px;min-height:2.4em}

.batgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(160px,1fr));gap:8px}
.bat{
  background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:3px;padding:8px;
}
.bat .bname{font-weight:700;color:var(--head);letter-spacing:1px;font-size:11px}
.bat .bkind{color:var(--ink-faint);font-size:10px;margin-bottom:4px}
.batcell{
  height:14px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:2px;display:flex;overflow:hidden;
  background:var(--bg);margin:4px 0 2px;
}
.batseg{flex:1;border-right:1px solid var(--bg)}
.batseg:last-child{border-right:none}
.batseg.on.g{background:var(--green)}
.batseg.on.a{background:var(--amber)}
.batseg.on.r{background:var(--red)}
.bat .bmeta{font-size:10px;color:var(--ink-dim)}

.poolgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:10px}
@media(max-width:820px){.poolgrid{grid-template-columns:1fr}}
.poolcard{
  background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge);border-radius:3px;padding:8px;
  display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;
}

.cvm-flags{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:4px 0 8px}
.cvm-confirm{
  border:1px solid var(--amber);color:var(--amber);padding:6px 8px;border-radius:3px;
  margin-bottom:8px;font-size:12px;
}
.pill{display:inline-block;padding:1px 8px;border-radius:10px;font-size:10px;
  font-weight:700;letter-spacing:1px;border:1px solid var(--panel-edge)}
.pill.green{color:var(--green);border-color:var(--green)}
.pill.red{color:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)}
.pill.amber{color:var(--amber);border-color:var(--amber)}
.pill.dim{color:var(--ink-faint)}

.empty{color:var(--ink-faint);font-style:italic;padding:4px 0}
footer{margin-top:10px;color:var(--ink-faint);font-size:11px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
#liveBind{color:var(--cyan)}

/* A panel that cannot render usefully at phone width says so, in the panel, in
   the reader's own words — it never just clips. `data-degraded` is on the
   element so the layout probe can COUNT them; `.phonenote` hides it on desktop,
   where none of it is true. */
.phonenote{display:none}
.phonenote b{color:var(--amber);letter-spacing:1px}

/* Pointer drag-handles. CSS resize:both (v9) stays — do not rewind it.
   Grid/flex fr tracks swallow the UA grip so it looks gone. These .rh
   nodes are the visible hit-target JS pointer-drags; they sit AFTER
   .panel{background} so they are not a background-image cascade fight.
   KDash wall 4fr / three orch columns stay the default until panelGeom has a size. */
.home-card,.orch-code,.panel,.advanced,#orch-home,#orch-east,#home-nav{
  position:relative;
}
.rh{
  position:absolute;z-index:6;touch-action:none;user-select:none;
  width:14px;height:14px;right:0;bottom:0;cursor:nwse-resize;
  background:linear-gradient(135deg,transparent 46%,var(--ink-faint) 46% 54%,transparent 54% 66%,var(--ink-faint) 66% 74%,transparent 74%);
}
.rh-col{
  top:0;bottom:0;right:0;left:auto;width:8px;height:auto;cursor:ew-resize;
  background:linear-gradient(to left,var(--ink-faint) 0 1px,transparent 1px);
}
.rh-col-east{
  left:0;right:auto;
  background:linear-gradient(to right,var(--ink-faint) 0 1px,transparent 1px);
}
.rh-row{
  top:auto;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;width:auto;height:8px;cursor:ns-resize;
  background:linear-gradient(to top,var(--ink-faint) 0 1px,transparent 1px);
}
.col-grip{
  position:absolute;z-index:7;top:0;left:8px;right:8px;height:10px;
  cursor:grab;touch-action:none;user-select:none;
  background:repeating-linear-gradient(90deg,transparent 0 6px,var(--ink-faint) 6px 7px);
  opacity:.35;
}
.col-grip:hover,.col-grip:active{opacity:.8;cursor:grabbing}
.panel-hd{cursor:grab}
.panel-hd button,.panel-hd a,.panel-hd input{cursor:pointer}
.pane-drop{outline:2px dashed var(--cyan);outline-offset:-2px}
.pane-moving{opacity:.55}
.geom-user{
  flex:0 0 auto;
  align-self:start;
  justify-self:start;
}
.orch-nav .home-card.geom-user{flex:0 0 auto}

/* ==========================================================================
   PHONE — everything below 640px, and nothing above it.

   MEASURED 2026-08-31 by `builds/cdeck/mobile_probe.py` (headless Chromium,
   Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride, the deck served same-origin with a
   read-only proxy to Core). The previous phone block was written by reading
   kdash/mobile.html and was never laid out by an engine. What the engine said:

     ask=390  ->  LAYOUT VIEWPORT 501px,  scale 0.778
     ask=320  ->  LAYOUT VIEWPORT 502px,  scale 0.637

   The deck's own min-content width was 501px, so Blink widened the layout
   viewport to fit it and shrank the whole page. `@media(max-width:640px)` still
   MATCHED (501 < 640) and its rules still "applied" — at 78% of the size they
   claim. The 44px touch target arrived as 34 device px; the 16px form text that
   exists to stop iOS zooming arrived as 12.4px, i.e. it still zooms. A rule that
   is present is not a rule that reached the glass, and only a browser can tell
   you which one you have.

   So rule group 1 is the whole fix: nothing may set a min-content floor wider
   than the handset. Everything after it only works because of it.
   ========================================================================== */
@media(max-width:640px){

  /* -- 1. no min-content floor wider than the phone ----------------------- */
  /* Grid tracks default to `min-width:auto`, so ONE unbreakable panel child
     sets the width of the whole page. minmax(0,…) + min-width:0 breaks that
     chain; `overflow-wrap:anywhere` is the one that also shrinks intrinsic
     min-content (break-word does not), which matters because panel prose is
     full of unbreakable paths like V:\A\Ai\COSMOS\live. */
  .grid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}
  header,.connbar,.refreshbar,.cmdwrap,.cmdbar,.grid,.panel,.panel-bd,.rail,
  .mcard,.twrap,.formrow,.formgrid,.spend-edit,.poolgrid,.poolcard,.batgrid,
  .chips,.jobrow,.fevent,.decknav,.orch,.orch-home,.orch-code,.home-card,
  .advanced,.sghgrid,.searchrow,.code-term{min-width:0}
  .panel-bd,.centry,.errbox,.gatewhy,.spend-layer,.jobids{overflow-wrap:anywhere}
  /* Three nowrap labels carried live text — a URL, an RTT, a transport name —
     and each one alone was wider than the handset. */
  #connState,.transport,.pwa,.refreshbar,#countdown,.badge,.nclabel{white-space:normal}
  #countdown{min-width:0}
  /* A flex row with min-width:0 children SHRINKS them rather than going to a
     second line unless it is told it may wrap: a third chip in this bar
     squeezed #countdown to 32px against 42px of text (measured, silentClip in
     MOBILE_PROBE.json). The chip moved to the footer instead, but the wrap
     stays — at 320px this bar has no margin left, and a silent squeeze is the
     failure mode this whole media block exists to prevent. */
  .refreshbar{flex-wrap:wrap;row-gap:4px}
  header,.refreshbar,.connbar{width:100%}
  #apiBase,#token{width:100%}
  .spend-edit{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}
  .formgrid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr)}
  /* `minmax(0,1fr)` under auto-fill is degenerate — it packs as many tracks as
     it can and the probe measured the battery cards collapsed to 16px wide with
     their cells clipped at clientWidth 0. `min(140px,100%)` keeps a real track
     floor without letting that floor become a page-wide min-content. */
  .batgrid{grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(min(140px,100%),1fr))}

  /* -- 2. form text at 16px, and this time it WINS the cascade ------------ */
  /* Enumerated by ELEMENT, not by parent class, so a form control added to a
     panel later cannot quietly fall back to 12px; and last in the file, so no
     equal-specificity rule below can outrank it the way one used to. */
  input,select,textarea{font-size:16px;padding:9px 10px;min-height:44px}
  .cmdbar input,.formrow input,.formrow select,.formgrid input,.formgrid select,
  .spend-edit input,.spend-edit select,.poolcard input,.poolcard select,
  .connbar input{font-size:16px;min-height:44px}
  /* These three need their own rule twice over: they carry an ID selector
     upstream, which outranks any element/class rule no matter where it sits
     (measured still at 12px after the block above landed), and their 200px
     flex floor plus two buttons cannot share a 320px row — so the input takes
     its own line and the buttons stay full-size beneath it. */
  #cmdInput,#cvmInput,#jobCmd{flex:1 1 100%;min-width:0;font-size:16px;min-height:44px}
  #homeSearch,#sghBuildHome,#sghChatHome,#sghAutoHome,#sghBuild,#sghChat,#sghAuto{font-size:16px;min-height:44px}
  .sghgrid input{font-size:16px;min-height:44px}

  /* -- 3. touch targets at 44 DEVICE px ----------------------------------- */
  button,summary{min-height:44px;padding:8px 14px;font-size:13px}
  a.navjump,.navjump{
    min-height:44px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:8px 14px}
  /* Eight 44px chips wrapped to three rows at 320px and the empty console
     still occupied 120px of "no commands yet" — together they were most of
     the 71.3% first-screen chrome MOBILE_PROBE.json measured. One scrolling
     row (same shape as .decknav) and hiding the empty console is the kdash
     compact-command rule applied to a 19-panel deck. */
  .quickcmds{
    gap:6px;flex-wrap:nowrap;overflow-x:auto;overscroll-behavior-x:contain;
    scrollbar-width:thin;
  }
  .quickcmds button{flex:0 0 auto;min-height:44px;padding:6px 12px}
  #console:has(> .empty:only-child){display:none}
  .mcard summary,.tooltable summary{display:flex;align-items:center}
  /* The 19-link nav wrapped to FIVE rows (109px, 10% of the viewport) of 22px
     links. One sticky scrolling row: 44px targets, ~65px of screen back, and
     the sideways scroll is a visible affordance rather than a hidden overflow. */
  .decknav{
    position:sticky;top:0;z-index:5;background:var(--bg);
    flex-wrap:nowrap;overflow-x:auto;overscroll-behavior-x:contain;
    gap:0 4px;padding:2px 0;scrollbar-width:thin;
  }
  .decknav a{flex:0 0 auto;min-height:44px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:0 10px}

  /* -- 4. wide tables scroll sideways instead of being crushed ------------ */
  /* 9 columns in 292px is 32px a column — the data is "there" and unreadable.
     A min-width turns that into a horizontal scroll inside .twrap (which is
     already overflow-x:auto and min-width:0, so it cannot push the page). */
  /* ONLY the two panels that carry a degrade note. HEALTH (3 columns) and RAILS
     (5) measured fitting the width without one, and a table that scrolls
     sideways with nothing on screen saying so is the defect this rule exists to
     avoid — so the scroll and the note ship together or not at all. */
  .twrap{overflow-x:auto;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch}
  #bd-channels .twrap table{min-width:660px}
  #bd-caps .twrap table{min-width:560px}

  /* -- 5. NODE MAP: positioned + scrollable, viewport-keyed drag ----------- */
  /* The 5-of-25 clip was a FIXED 420px stage + overflow:hidden + desktop
     coordinates (CLAUDE at x=720). List-mode (position:static) closed the
     clip by giving up drag. F-05 leftover is the drag Keith named, so the
     stage is now height:auto (JS sizes it to the lowest node), overflow
     visible (nodesOutsideStage stays 0), nodes stay absolute (offsetLeft
     is a map coordinate, not a flow coordinate), edges stay on, RESET
     stays on. Positions persist in cdeck.nodePositions.phone — a phone
     drag cannot overwrite the desktop layout. */
  #nmap-stage{height:auto;min-height:220px;overflow:visible;background:var(--well);border:1px solid var(--panel-edge)}
  #nmap-edges{display:block}
  #nmap-nodes{position:relative;inset:auto;display:block;min-height:220px}
  .nmap-node{position:absolute;max-width:140px;min-width:88px;flex:none;cursor:grab;touch-action:none}
  .nmap-toolbar #nmapReset{display:inline-block}

  /* The fold is the phone's; the summary becomes a real 44px control.
     `display:contents` above is what keeps the DESKTOP row byte-identical, and
     it is also what defeats the fold: a box-less element cannot be hidden by
     the UA's `content-visibility` on `::details-content`. Measured — the deck
     read 84.6% chrome with it left in. Both are put back to real boxes here. */
  #connsetup{display:block;flex:1 1 100%}
  #connsetup::details-content{display:block}
  #connsetup>summary{
    display:flex;align-items:center;min-height:44px;cursor:pointer;
    color:var(--cyan);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:1px;list-style:none;
  }
  #connsetup>summary::-webkit-details-marker{display:none}
  #connsetup>summary::before{content:"+ "}
  #connsetup[open]>summary::before{content:"- "}
  #connsetup[open]{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap}
  #connsetup[open]>summary{flex:1 1 100%}

  /* -- 6. the degrade notes themselves, and the chrome they cost ---------- */
  .phonenote{
    display:block;margin:6px 10px 2px;padding:6px 8px;font-size:11px;
    border:1px dashed var(--amber);border-radius:3px;color:var(--ink-dim);
  }
  /* 61% of the first screen was header + nav + command box before any datum. */
  #console,#cvmLog{max-height:120px}
  #feed{max-height:200px}
  footer{font-size:10px}

  /* -- 7. a panel that grows with the tree scrolls internally -------------- */
  /* Same artifact, 320px: bodyScrollH 29074, panel-jukebox 9030, panel-jobs
     3812, panel-batteries 2730, panel-spend 2252, nmapStage 130.9vh.
     kdash/mobile.html caps #feed at 180px and shows the last 8 jobs. The
     19-panel deck cannot shrink to five cards (backlog row 19 is Keith-ask);
     it CAN stop being a 29k-px page. max-height is on .panel-bd so the
     degrade note stays visible above the scroll and #nmap-stage can still
     contain every node (nodesOutsideStage stays 0 — overflow:visible on the
     stage is load-bearing; JS sizes the stage to the lowest node). */
  .panel-bd{
    max-height:min(55vh, 460px);
    overflow-y:auto;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;
  }

  /* -- 8. desktop panelGeom does not land on the 640px stack -------------- */
  /* Same reason node map keys phone separately: a desktop width/height is
     not a phone layout. Hide the handles; native resize is not a touch
     affordance. JS restorePanelGeom no-ops here and clears inline sizes
     if the viewport crosses 640. */
  .home-card,.orch-code,.panel,.advanced,#orch-home,#orch-east,#home-nav{
    resize:none;
  }
  .rh{display:none}
}

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_studio.js =====
/* MOTIF Studio / Runs / Review for extra panes.
   Steal from LangSmith (graph + inspector + heat), n8n (run list + detail),
   Dify (tabs + spend), Vellum (seats + estimates). Backed by GET /jukebox,
   /jobs, /spend, /model_rater, /runs_ops — never invented traces, time-travel,
   or a job_id follow Core has never written. Header and JACK'S MESH untouched. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var STAGES = [
    { id: "define", n: 1, name: "PROBLEM / GOAL",
      hint: "PROBLEM STATEMENT / STATED GOAL. Freeze WHAT / WHY / acceptance as one clean prompt. Verbatim to every model. Research does not start without it." },
    { id: "research", n: 2, name: "RESEARCH",
      hint: "DOM rails. SGH + GEM first, both, in parallel. Returns on disk before BUILD." },
    { id: "arch", n: 3, name: "ARCH",
      hint: "Decision rubric first. Each node designs independently — no peeking. CONSENSUS chooses." },
    { id: "consensus1", n: 4, name: "CONSENSUS",
      hint: "Set the bar (plurality / majority / complete). HITL chooses architecture, or AUTO picks by the bar. CONTESTED if the bar is not met — no third model resolves." },
    { id: "build", n: 5, name: "BUILD",
      hint: "JSON/TOML/SQL artifact. N simultaneous builders. Target is local file, GitHub, or cloud drive. GitHub/GitLab runs through Gitur before the live tree." },
    { id: "critics", n: 6, name: "CRITICS",
      hint: "Any number of critics. Continuation when the bar is met, or HITL. Then IMPLEMENT via Gitur." },
    { id: "consensus2", n: 7, name: "CONSENSUS",
      hint: "Adjudication. Same bar as stage 4. Reconcile the critiques. Agree the fixes. No third model auto-resolves." },
    { id: "improve", n: 8, name: "IMPLEMENT",
      hint: "Was IMPROVE. CCr applies once continuation is met. GitHub/GitLab: Gitur then live tree. Local/cloud: write there." },
    { id: "iterate", n: 9, name: "ITERATE",
      hint: "Conditional return to PROBLEM STATEMENT / STATED GOAL. N rounds, timer, budget, bar, runtime-binding, or Keith." }
  ];
  var JOB_ORDER = ["QUEUED", "RUNNING", "FINDINGS", "CLEAN", "BROKE"];
  var POLL_MS = 10000;
  var MAX_RESEARCH = 12;

  ];
  /* Keith 2026-09-07 MOTIF + ADVERSARIAL_LOOP. Painted on CONSENSUS 4/7

  var CONSENSUS_RULES = [
    "Same DEFINE text, verbatim. No peeking across lanes.",
    "Different families (swiss cheese). N clones of one family are the same holes.",
    "Dual-lane BUILD: no shared context. Two unbiased takes, not builder-plus-checker.",
    "AUTO fires only when the bar is met. Else CONTESTED — both positions, one line to Keith. No third model resolves.",
    "Disagreement is the signal. Perfect agreement on a hard task is suspicious.",
    "Orthogonal porosity seats the next pair. UNMEASURED until observed."
  ];
  function consensusRulesHtml(where) {
    var c = (typeof ensureConsensus === "function")
      ? ensureConsensus()
      : { bar: "majority", arch_choice: "auto" };
    var bar = c.bar || "majority";
    var choice = c.arch_choice || "auto";
    var h = '<div class="chips"><span class="chip">bar <b>' + esc(bar) +
      "</b></span><span class=\"chip\">" + esc(choice) + "</span>";
    if (where === "critics" && typeof ensureCritics === "function") {
      var cri = ensureCritics();
      h += '<span class="chip">critics <b>' + esc(cri.bar || bar) +
        "</b></span><span class=\"chip\">continue <b>" +
        esc(cri.continue_when || "bar") + "</b></span>";
    }
    h += "</div><ul class=\"studio-cons-ul\">";
    CONSENSUS_RULES.forEach(function (line) {
      h += "<li>" + esc(line) + "</li>";
    });
    h += "</ul>";
    return h;
  }
  function paintConsensusRules() {
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-cons-where]").forEach(function (el) {
      el.innerHTML = consensusRulesHtml(el.getAttribute("data-cons-where") || "");

  }
  window.deckConsensusRulesHtml = consensusRulesHtml;
  var ARCH_CHOICES = [

  var DEFAULT_BUILD = [

  var DEFAULT_VIA = [

    { id: "mcp:bts", label: "MCP · BTS" }
  ];
  //: Rows painted before the list asks to be extended. A cap is not a filter:
  //: the line under the list says how many rows it is holding back.
  var PAGE = 60;

  var state = {
    api: null,
    jobs: null,
    jukebox: null,
    spend: null,
    runsOps: null,
    review: null,
    reviewWindow: "week",
    surfaces: null,
    seats: [],
    estimates: {},
    selected: { kind: "stage", id: "define" },
    studio: null,
    models: [],
    viaOptions: [],
    rails: [],
    targetCatalog: [],
    defineDraft: "",
    defineDirty: false,
    researchDraft: null,
    researchDirty: false,
    archDraft: null,
    archDirty: false,
    consensusDraft: null,
    consensusDirty: false,
    buildDraft: null,
    buildDirty: false,
    criticsDraft: null,
    criticsDirty: false,
    destDraft: null,
    destDirty: false,
    implementDraft: null,
    implementDirty: false,
    iterateDraft: null,
    iterateDirty: false,
    studioSay: { define: null, research: null, arch: null, consensus: null,
      build: null, critics: null, implement: null, iterate: null, graph: null },
    graphDraft: null,
    graphDirty: false,
    runId: "",
    runFilter: "ALL",
    runQuery: "",
    runSort: "new",
    runShown: PAGE,
    // A POST is a write. `arm` holds the command a second click would file, and
    // `post` holds what Core actually answered — the old painter threw both the
    // new job id and the failure away inside an empty catch.
    arm: "",
    post: null,
    err: {},
    _sig: ""
  };

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  function stCls(st) {
    var s = String(st).toUpperCase();
    if (s === "BROKE") return "bad";
    if (s === "FINDINGS" || s === "QUEUED") return "warn";
    if (s === "CLEAN" || s === "RUNNING") return "ok";
    return "dim";
  }
  function fmtAge(s) {
    if (s == null || s === "") return "UNMEASURED";
    var n = Number(s);
    if (!isFinite(n)) return "UNMEASURED";
    if (n < 90) return Math.round(n) + "s";
    if (n < 3600) return Math.round(n / 60) + "m";
    return (n / 3600).toFixed(1) + "h";
  }
  function jobsMap() {
    var d = state.jobs;
    return (d && d.jobs && typeof d.jobs === "object") ? d.jobs : null;
  }
  function jukeRows() {
    var q = state.jukebox && state.jukebox.queue;
    return (q && q.available && Array.isArray(q.jobs)) ? q.jobs : [];
  }
  function jukeById(id) {
    var rows = jukeRows();
    var i;
    for (i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      if (String(rows[i].job_id) === String(id)) return rows[i];
    }
    return null;
  }
  function staleRow(r) {
    return !!(r && (r.stale_flag || r.stale_reported));
  }
  function jobCounts() {
    var out = { total: 0, stale: 0 };
    JOB_ORDER.forEach(function (s) { out[s] = 0; });
    var rows = jukeRows();
    if (rows.length) {
      rows.forEach(function (r) {
        var st = String(r.st || "").toUpperCase();
        out.total += 1;
        if (staleRow(r)) out.stale += 1;
        if (out[st] == null) return;
        out[st] += 1;
      });
      var c = state.jukebox.queue.counts;
      if (c && typeof c.total === "number") out.total = c.total;
      return out;
    }
    var jobs = jobsMap();
    if (!jobs) return out;
    Object.keys(jobs).forEach(function (id) {
      var st = String(jobs[id]).toUpperCase();
      out.total += 1;
      if (out[st] != null) out[st] += 1;
    });
    return out;
  }
  function seatFor(profile, seat) {
    var i, s;
    for (i = 0; i < state.seats.length; i++) {
      s = state.seats[i];
      if (s.profile === profile && s.seat === seat) return s;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function heatFor(stageId) {
    var c = jobCounts();
    if (stageId === "build") {
      if (c.RUNNING) return "hot-run";
      if (c.QUEUED) return "hot-wait";
    }
    if (stageId === "critics" && c.FINDINGS) return "hot-review";
    if (stageId === "improve" && c.BROKE) return "hot-broke";
    return "";
  }

  function paintPalette() {
    var box = $("studioPal");
    if (!box) return;
    var h = '<div class="studio-pal-h">nodes</div>';
    h += '<div class="studio-pal-g">MOTIF stages</div>';
    STAGES.forEach(function (s) {
      h += '<button type="button" class="studio-chip" data-kind="stage" data-id="' +
        esc(s.id) + '"><b>' + s.n + "</b> " + esc(s.name) + "</button>";
    });
    h += '<div class="studio-pal-g">lanes</div>';
    h += '<button type="button" class="studio-chip" data-kind="lane" data-id="lane_a">Lane A · Grok 4.6</button>';
