    h += '<button type="button" class="studio-chip" data-kind="lane" data-id="lane_b">Lane B · Cursor</button>';
    h += '<div class="studio-pal-g">profile workflow</div>';
    h += '<button type="button" class="studio-chip" data-kind="tool" data-id="new">NEW WORKFLOW</button>';
    h += '<button type="button" class="studio-chip" data-kind="tool" data-id="save">SAVE GRAPH</button>';
    h += '<div class="dim tiny">Drag a stage, lane, or seat onto the center board. Drag nodes to place them. SAVE posts GET /studio graph. Does not start MOTIF. Does not invent an occupancy id.</div>';
    h += '<div class="studio-pal-g">seats</div>';
    if (!state.seats.length) {
      h += '<div class="dim tiny">GET /api/v1/model_rater unread — seats UNMEASURED</div>';
    } else {
      state.seats.forEach(function (s) {
        h += '<button type="button" class="studio-chip" data-kind="seat" data-id="' +
          esc(s.profile + "." + s.seat) + '">' + esc(s.label) + "</button>";
      });
    }
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll(".studio-chip").forEach(function (b) {
      var kind = b.getAttribute("data-kind");
      var id = b.getAttribute("data-id");
      b.classList.toggle("on", state.selected.kind === kind && state.selected.id === id);
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        if (kind === "tool") {
          if (id === "new") resetGraph();
          else if (id === "save") saveGraph();
          return;
        }
        state.selected = { kind: kind, id: id };
        paintStudio();
      });
      if (kind !== "tool") {
        b.draggable = true;
        b.addEventListener("dragstart", function (e) {
          e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", kind + ":" + id);
          e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "copy";
        });
      }
    });
  }

  function defaultGraph() {
    var nodes = [];
    STAGES.forEach(function (s, i) {
      nodes.push({
        id: s.id, kind: "stage", n: s.n, label: s.name,
        x: (i % 3) * 200, y: (i / 3 | 0) * 140
      });
    });
    return { nodes: nodes, saved_at: null };
  }
  function ensureGraph() {
    if (!state.graphDraft || !state.graphDraft.nodes) {
      state.graphDraft = defaultGraph();
    }
    return state.graphDraft;
  }
  function snapG(n) {
    var g = 16;
    var x = Math.round(Number(n) / g) * g;
    return x < 0 ? 0 : x;
  }
  function saveGraph() {
    var g = ensureGraph();
    postStudio({ graph: { nodes: g.nodes } }, "graph",
      "workflow graph saved. Does not start MOTIF. Does not invent an occupancy id.");
    state.graphDirty = false;
  }
  function resetGraph() {
    state.graphDraft = defaultGraph();
    state.graphDirty = true;
    paintGraph();
    saveGraph();
  }
  function addGraphNode(kind, id, x, y) {
    var g = ensureGraph();
    var hit = null;
    g.nodes.forEach(function (n) {
      if (n.kind === kind && n.id === id) hit = n;
    });
    if (hit) {
      hit.x = snapG(x);
      hit.y = snapG(y);
    } else {
      var label = id;
      var n = null;
      if (kind === "stage") {
        STAGES.forEach(function (s) {
          if (s.id === id) { label = s.name; n = s.n; }
        });
      } else if (kind === "lane") {
        label = id === "lane_b" ? "Lane B · Cursor" : "Lane A · Grok 4.6";
      } else if (kind === "seat") {
        state.seats.forEach(function (s) {
          if ((s.profile + "." + s.seat) === id) label = s.label || id;
        });
      }
      g.nodes.push({
        id: id, kind: kind, n: n, label: label,
        x: snapG(x), y: snapG(y)
      });
    }
    state.graphDirty = true;
    state.selected = { kind: kind, id: id };
    paintStudio();
    saveGraph();
  }
  var _gDrag = null;
  function paintGraph() {
    var box = $("studioCanvas");
    if (!box) return;
    var c = jobCounts();
    var a = seatFor("motif", "lane_a");
    var b = seatFor("motif", "lane_b");
    var g = ensureGraph();
    var h = '<div class="studio-snap dim tiny">Heat is a queue-word overlay on GET /api/v1/jukebox, not a MOTIF stage stamped on the job. Time-travel / fork / per-step cost are not Core verbs. Center board: drag palette nodes onto it, drag placed nodes to compose a custom MOTIF workflow. LangGraph is not installed.</div>';
    h += '<div class="chips studio-q">';
    JOB_ORDER.forEach(function (s) {
      h += '<span class="chip">' + s.toLowerCase() + " <b>" + (c[s] || 0) + "</b></span>";
    });
    h += '<span class="chip">total <b>' + c.total + "</b></span>";
    if (c.stale) h += '<span class="chip warn">stale <b>' + c.stale + "</b></span>";
    h += spendChips();
    var cns = (typeof ensureConsensus === "function")
      ? ensureConsensus() : { bar: "majority", arch_choice: "auto" };
    h += '<span class="chip">consensus <b>' + esc(cns.bar || "majority") +
      "</b> · " + esc(cns.arch_choice || "auto") + "</span></div>";
    h += '<div class="studio-board studio-flow" role="list">';
    g.nodes.forEach(function (node) {
      var on = state.selected.kind === node.kind && state.selected.id === node.id ? " on" : "";
      var heat = node.kind === "stage" ? heatFor(node.id) : "";
      h += '<button type="button" class="studio-node studio-gnode' + on +
        (heat ? " " + heat : "") +
        '" data-kind="' + esc(node.kind) + '" data-id="' + esc(node.id) +
        '" role="listitem" style="left:' + snapG(node.x) + "px;top:" +
        snapG(node.y) + 'px"><span class="studio-n">' +
        esc(node.n != null ? String(node.n) : node.kind) +
        '</span><span class="studio-nm">' + esc(node.label) + "</span>";
      if (heat) {
        h += '<span class="studio-heat">' +
          (heat === "hot-run" ? "RUNNING" : heat === "hot-wait" ? "QUEUED" :
            heat === "hot-review" ? "FINDINGS" : "BROKE") + "</span>";
      }
      if (node.id === "build") {
        h += '<span class="studio-lanes">' +
          '<span class="studio-lane' + (state.selected.kind === "lane" && state.selected.id === "lane_a" ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-kind="lane" data-id="lane_a">A · ' +
          esc((a && a.model) || "unassigned") + "</span>" +
          '<span class="studio-lane' + (state.selected.kind === "lane" && state.selected.id === "lane_b" ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-kind="lane" data-id="lane_b">B · ' +
          esc((b && b.model) || "unassigned") + "</span></span>";
      }
      if (node.id === "iterate") h += '<span class="studio-back">↻ PROBLEM</span>';
      h += "</button>";
    });
    h += "</div>";
    var say = state.studioSay.graph;
    if (say && say.text) {
      h += '<div class="tiny">' + esc(say.text) + "</div>";
    }
    box.innerHTML = h;
    var board = box.querySelector(".studio-board");
    if (board) {
      board.addEventListener("dragover", function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = "copy";
      });
      board.addEventListener("drop", function (e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        var raw = (e.dataTransfer.getData("text/plain") || "").split(":");
        var kind = raw[0];
        var id = raw.slice(1).join(":");
        if (!kind || !id || kind === "tool") return;
        var r = board.getBoundingClientRect();
        addGraphNode(kind, id, e.clientX - r.left - 48, e.clientY - r.top - 24);
      });
    }
    box.querySelectorAll(".studio-gnode").forEach(function (el) {
      el.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        if (_gDrag && _gDrag.moved) return;
        e.stopPropagation();
        state.selected = {
          kind: el.getAttribute("data-kind"),
          id: el.getAttribute("data-id")
        };
        paintStudio();
      });
      el.addEventListener("pointerdown", function (e) {
        if (e.button != null && e.button !== 0) return;
        if (e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest(".studio-lane")) return;
        var r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
        var br = board ? board.getBoundingClientRect() : r;
        _gDrag = {
          el: el, id: el.getAttribute("data-id"),
          kind: el.getAttribute("data-kind"),
          x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY,
          left: r.left - br.left, top: r.top - br.top, moved: false
        };
        try { el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
        e.preventDefault();
      });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll(".studio-lane").forEach(function (el) {
      el.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        state.selected = {
          kind: el.getAttribute("data-kind"),
          id: el.getAttribute("data-id")
        };
        paintStudio();
      });
    });
    if (!paintGraph._move) {
      paintGraph._move = true;
      window.addEventListener("pointermove", function (e) {
        if (!_gDrag) return;
        var dx = e.clientX - _gDrag.x;
        var dy = e.clientY - _gDrag.y;
        if (!_gDrag.moved && Math.abs(dx) < 4 && Math.abs(dy) < 4) return;
        _gDrag.moved = true;
        _gDrag.el.style.left = snapG(Math.max(0, _gDrag.left + dx)) + "px";
        _gDrag.el.style.top = snapG(Math.max(0, _gDrag.top + dy)) + "px";
      });
      window.addEventListener("pointerup", function () {
        if (!_gDrag) return;
        var d = _gDrag;
        _gDrag = null;
        if (!d.moved) return;
        var g = ensureGraph();
        g.nodes.forEach(function (n) {
          if (n.kind === d.kind && n.id === d.id) {
            n.x = snapG(parseInt(d.el.style.left, 10) || 0);
            n.y = snapG(parseInt(d.el.style.top, 10) || 0);
          }
        });
        state.graphDirty = true;
        saveGraph();
      });
    }
  }

  function spendChips() {
    var rails = state.spend && state.spend.rails;
    if (!rails) {
      return '<span class="chip dim">' +
        (state.err.spend ? esc(state.err.spend) : "spend UNMEASURED") + "</span>";
    }
    var keys = Object.keys(rails);
    if (!keys.length) return '<span class="chip dim">spend empty</span>';
    var h = "";
    keys.slice(0, 6).forEach(function (k) {
      var r = rails[k] || {};
      var used = r.settled_usd;
      var cap = r.cap_usd;
      var txt = (typeof used === "number" ? "$" + used.toFixed(2) : "—") +
        (typeof cap === "number" ? " / $" + cap.toFixed(0) : "");
      h += '<span class="chip" title="' + esc(k) + '">' + esc(k) + " <b>" + esc(txt) + "</b></span>";
    });
    return h;
  }

  function inspectHtml() {
    var sel = state.selected;
    var h = "";
    var s, seat, row;
    // DEFINE / RESEARCH use persistent markup so a 10s poll cannot drop the caret.
    if (sel.kind === "stage" && (sel.id === "define" || sel.id === "research"
        || sel.id === "arch" || sel.id === "consensus1" || sel.id === "consensus2"
        || sel.id === "build" || sel.id === "critics" || sel.id === "improve"
        || sel.id === "iterate")) {
      return "";
    }
    if (sel.kind === "stage") {
      s = STAGES.filter(function (x) { return x.id === sel.id; })[0];
      if (!s) return "<div class='empty'>no stage</div>";
      h = "<h3>" + s.n + " · " + esc(s.name) + "</h3>" +
        "<p>" + esc(s.hint) + "</p>" +
        "<p class='dim tiny'>LangSmith-style inspector: this is the SOP node, not a LangGraph runtime. " +
        "GET /jobs does not name a MOTIF stage. Heat on BUILD/CRITICS/IMPROVE is the live queue overlay.</p>";
      if (s.id === "build") {
        var a = seatFor("motif", "lane_a");
        var b = seatFor("motif", "lane_b");
        h += "<dl class='kv'><dt>Lane A</dt><dd>" + esc((a && (a.model || a.label)) || "unassigned") +
          "</dd><dt>Lane B</dt><dd>" + esc((b && (b.model || b.label)) || "unassigned") + "</dd></dl>";
        h += "<button type='button' class='studio-go' data-go='models'>open Model Rater to assign</button>";
        h += "<button type='button' class='studio-go' data-go='runs'>open Runs</button>";
      }
      if (s.id === "critics") {
        h += "<p class='dim tiny'>Crucible seats are the closest HITL analogue Core actually stores.</p>";
        ["plaintiff", "defense", "judge"].forEach(function (k) {
          seat = seatFor("crucible", k);
          h += "<div class='jobrow'><span class='jid'>" + esc(k) + "</span><span class='jst'>" +
            esc((seat && seat.model) || "unassigned") + "</span></div>";
        });
        h += "<button type='button' class='studio-go' data-go='review'>open Review</button>";
      }
      if (s.id === "improve") {
        h += "<button type='button' class='studio-go' data-filter-runs='BROKE'>open BROKE pile</button>";
      }
      return h;
    }
    if (sel.kind === "lane") {
      seat = seatFor("motif", sel.id);
      h = "<h3>" + (sel.id === "lane_a" ? "Lane A — Grok 4.6" : "Lane B — Cursor") + "</h3>" +
        "<p>Adversarial BUILD seat. Model is assigned in Prompts (model_rater), never guessed here.</p>" +
        "<dl class='kv'><dt>model</dt><dd>" + esc((seat && seat.model) || "unassigned") +
        "</dd><dt>label</dt><dd>" + esc((seat && seat.label) || sel.id) + "</dd></dl>" +
        "<button type='button' class='studio-go' data-go='models'>open Model Rater</button>";
      return h;
    }
    if (sel.kind === "seat") {
      seat = state.seats.filter(function (x) {
        return (x.profile + "." + x.seat) === sel.id;
      })[0];
      h = "<h3>" + esc((seat && seat.label) || sel.id) + "</h3>" +
        "<dl class='kv'><dt>model</dt><dd>" + esc((seat && seat.model) || "unassigned") +
        "</dd><dt>profile</dt><dd>" + esc(seat && seat.profile) + "</dd></dl>";
      if (seat && seat.model && state.estimates[seat.model]) {
        h += "<p class='dim tiny'>estimate " + esc(state.estimates[seat.model]) + "</p>";
      }
      h += "<button type='button' class='studio-go' data-go='models'>open Model Rater</button>";
      return h;
    }
    if (sel.kind === "run") {
      return runDetailHtml(sel.id);
    }
    return "<div class='empty'>pick a node</div>";
  }

  function runDetailHtml(id) {
    var row = jukeById(id);
    var jobs = jobsMap();
    var st = row ? row.st : (jobs && jobs[id]);
    var h = "<h3>" + esc(id) + "</h3>";
    if (st == null && !row) {
      // A job filed seconds ago is not on the last poll yet. Saying so beats
      // the old bare "not in /jukebox", and the POST receipt below still shows.
      return h + "<p class='dim tiny'>not on the last GET /jukebox or GET /jobs. " +
        "A job filed since the last poll appears within " + Math.round(POLL_MS / 1000) +
        "s; one that never appears was never queued.</p>" + postNoteHtml() + detailFootHtml();
    }
    h += "<dl class='kv'>";
    h += "<dt>state</dt><dd class='" + stCls(st) + "'>" + esc(st) + "</dd>";
    if (row) {
      h += "<dt>command</dt><dd>" + esc(row.command || "UNMEASURED") + "</dd>";
      h += "<dt>priority</dt><dd>" + esc(row.priority || "—") + "</dd>";
      h += "<dt>lane</dt><dd>" + esc(row.lane || "—") + "</dd>";
      h += "<dt>submitter</dt><dd>" + esc(row.submitter || "—") + "</dd>";
      h += "<dt>age</dt><dd>" + esc(fmtAge(row.age_s)) + "</dd>";
      h += "<dt>running</dt><dd>" + esc(row.st === "RUNNING" ? fmtAge(row.running_s) : "—") + "</dd>";
      h += "<dt>claimed by</dt><dd>" + esc(row.by || "—") + "</dd>";
      h += "<dt>stale</dt><dd>" +
        esc(row.stale_flag ? "FLAGGED >30m RUNNING" : (row.stale_reported ? "reported" : "no")) +
        "</dd>";
      h += "<dt>manifest</dt><dd>" + (row.manifest_present ? "present" : "missing") + "</dd>";
      if (String(st).toUpperCase() === "FINDINGS") {
        h += "<dt>HITL</dt><dd>awaiting CCr — interrupt.value analog is this command; " +
          "notes UNMEASURED unless Core sent them. Resume is <code>--accept</code>, " +
          "not a snapshot replay.</dd>";
      }
    } else {
      h += "<dt>command</dt><dd>UNMEASURED — GET /jobs is {id: state}; open jukebox fold</dd>";
    }
    h += "</dl>";
    h += "<p class='dim tiny'>COSMOS has never written event.job_id (0 of 989 measured) — follow-by-job is not invented. Token cost per job is UNMEASURED. Time-travel is not a Core verb.</p>";
    if (row && row.command) {
      // Two clicks, because this is a WRITE. The first arms it and shows the
      // command that would be filed; the second POSTs. cosmos_sched has no
      // retry verb, so what comes back is a different job with a new id, and
      // the button says that rather than reading like a re-run of this one.
      if (state.arm === row.command) {
        h += "<p class='dim tiny armnote'>POST /api/v1/jobs · priority normal · this files a " +
          "<b>separate job</b> with a new id. " + esc(id) + " is not re-run, cancelled or changed.</p>";
        h += '<button type="button" class="studio-go arm" data-requeue="' + esc(row.command) +
          '">CONFIRM — file NEW job</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-disarm="1">cancel</button>';
      } else {
        h += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-arm="' + esc(row.command) +
          '">file NEW job with this command</button>';
      }
    } else if (row) {
      h += "<p class='dim tiny'>no command on this row — nothing to file. A manifest that is gone is " +
        "<code>manifest_present: false</code>, and the row keeps the ledger facts.</p>";
    }
    return h + postNoteHtml() + detailFootHtml();
  }

  function postNoteHtml() {
    var p = state.post;
    if (!p || !p.cmd) return "";
    if (p.state === "sending") return "<p class='dim tiny'>POST /api/v1/jobs …</p>";
    if (p.state === "bad") {
      return "<p class='tiny bad'>POST /api/v1/jobs failed — " + esc(p.err) +
        ". Nothing was queued.</p>";
    }
    if (!p.id) {
      return "<p class='tiny warn'>POST /api/v1/jobs answered without a job_id. " +
        "Something may be queued; this pane will not name an id Core did not send.</p>";
    }
    return "<p class='tiny ok'>filed <b>" + esc(p.id) + "</b> — a NEW job, not a re-run. " +
      "It joins the list on the next poll.</p>" +
      '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-open-run="' + esc(p.id) + '">open ' +
      esc(p.id) + "</button>";
  }

  function detailFootHtml() {
    return "<button type='button' class='studio-go' data-clear='1'>clear selection</button>";
  }

  function paintInspector() {
    var def = $("studioDefine");
    var res = $("studioResearch");
    var arch = $("studioArch");
    var cons = $("studioConsensus");
    var bld = $("studioBuild");
    var cri = $("studioCritics");
    var imp = $("studioImplement");
    var ite = $("studioIterate");
    var box = $("studioInsp");
    var sel = state.selected;
    var isDef = sel.kind === "stage" && sel.id === "define";
    var isRes = sel.kind === "stage" && sel.id === "research";
    var isArch = sel.kind === "stage" && sel.id === "arch";
    var isCons = sel.kind === "stage" && (sel.id === "consensus1" || sel.id === "consensus2");
    var isBld = sel.kind === "stage" && sel.id === "build";
    var isCri = sel.kind === "stage" && sel.id === "critics";
    var isImp = sel.kind === "stage" && sel.id === "improve";
    var isIte = sel.kind === "stage" && sel.id === "iterate";
    if (def) def.hidden = !isDef;
    if (res) res.hidden = !isRes;
    if (arch) arch.hidden = !isArch;
    if (cons) cons.hidden = !isCons;
    if (bld) bld.hidden = !isBld;
    if (cri) cri.hidden = !isCri;
    if (imp) imp.hidden = !isImp;
    if (ite) ite.hidden = !isIte;
    bindStudioForms();
    if (isDef) {
      fillDefine();
      if (box) box.innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }
    if (isRes) {
      fillResearch(false);
      if (box) box.innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }
    if (isArch) {
      fillArch(false);
      if (box) box.innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }
    if (isCons) {
      fillConsensus(false);
      if (box) box.innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }
    if (isBld) {
      fillBuild(false);
      if (box) box.innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }
    if (isCri) {
      fillCritics(false);
      if (box) box.innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }
    if (isImp) {
      fillImplement();
      if (box) box.innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }
    if (isIte) {
      fillIterate(false);
      if (box) box.innerHTML = "";
      return;
    }
    if (box) {
      box.innerHTML = inspectHtml();
      bindGo(box);
    }
  }

  /* DEFINE textarea and RESEARCH rows live in deck_more.html so the 10s poll
     cannot rebuild them and dump the caret. GET /api/v1/studio fills them
     only when the field is idle. SAVE POSTs the pack. Does not start MOTIF. */
  function defaultTargets() {
    var on = { github: 1, docs: 1, reddit: 1 };
    var out = {};
    targetCatalog().forEach(function (t) { out[t.id] = !!on[t.id]; });
    return out;
  }
  function targetCatalog() {
    return (state.targetCatalog && state.targetCatalog.length)
      ? state.targetCatalog : TARGETS;
  }
  function cloneResearch(r) {
    r = r || {};
    return {
      models: (r.models && r.models.length ? r.models : DEFAULT_RESEARCH).map(function (m) {
        return {
          id: m.id || "",
          label: m.label || "",
          model: m.model || "",
          via: m.via || "",
          fallback_model: m.fallback_model || "",
          fallback_via: m.fallback_via || "",
          fallback_model_2: m.fallback_model_2 || "",
          fallback_via_2: m.fallback_via_2 || "",
          effort: m.effort || "",
          budget_usd: m.budget_usd
        };
      }),
      targets: Object.assign(defaultTargets(), r.targets || {}),
      extra_urls: (r.extra_urls || []).slice()
    };
  }
  function ensureResearch() {
    if (!state.researchDraft) {
      state.researchDraft = cloneResearch(state.studio && state.studio.research);
    }
    return state.researchDraft;
  }
  function parseUrls(text) {
    var out = [];
    String(text || "").split(/\r?\n/).forEach(function (u) {
      var s = String(u || "").trim();
      if (s && out.indexOf(s) < 0 && out.length < 24) out.push(s.slice(0, 400));
    });
    return out;
  }
  function researchFocused() {
    var box = $("studioResearch");
    var el = document.activeElement;
    if (!box || !el) return false;
    if (!box.contains(el)) return false;
    var tag = el.tagName;
    return tag === "INPUT" || tag === "SELECT" || tag === "TEXTAREA";
  }
  function railPresent(via) {
    if (!via || via.indexOf("cli:") === 0 || via === "dom") return "";
    var rows = state.rails || [];
    if (!rows.length) return "";
    var hit = rows.filter(function (r) {
      return (r.link_id || r.id) === via;
    })[0];
    if (!hit) return " · not in /rails";
    if (hit.verified === true) return " · registered";
    if (hit.verified === false) return " · FAIL";
    return " · UNKNOWN";
  }
  function cv(el) {
    if (typeof el === "string") el = $(el);
    if (window.chipValue) return window.chipValue(el) || "";
    return (el && el.value) || "";
  }
  function viaChipHtml(selected) {
    var opts = (state.viaOptions && state.viaOptions.length) ? state.viaOptions : DEFAULT_VIA;
    var h = "";
    opts.forEach(function (v) {
      h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (v.id === (selected || "") ? " on" : "") +
        '" data-val="' + esc(v.id) + '" data-via="' + esc(v.id) + '">' +
        esc(v.label || v.id) + "</button>";
    });
    return h;
  }
  function viaOptionHtml(selected) {
    return viaChipHtml(selected);
  }
  function modelChipHtml(selected, allowEmpty) {
    var picks = [];
    var seen = {};
    function add(id, name) {
      var key = id || "";
      if (!key && !allowEmpty) return;
      if (seen[key]) return;
      seen[key] = 1;
      picks.push({ id: key, name: name || id || "none" });
    }
    if (allowEmpty) add("", "none");
    add(selected, selected);
    (state.models || []).forEach(function (m) {
      if (m.favored || m.id === selected) add(m.id, m.name || m.id);
    });
    ["grok-4.6", "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free", "composer-2.5", "gemini-2.5-flash"].forEach(function (id) {
      add(id, id);
    });
    var h = "";
    picks.forEach(function (m) {
      h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (m.id === (selected || "") ? " on" : "") +
        '" data-val="' + esc(m.id) + '">' + esc(m.name) + "</button>";
    });
    h += '<button type="button" class="chip mr-open-rater">open rater</button>';
    return h;
  }
  function modelOptions(selected, allowEmpty) {
    return modelChipHtml(selected, allowEmpty);
  }
  function nextRid(models) {
    var n = 0;
    (models || []).forEach(function (m) {
      var x = parseInt(String(m.id || "").replace(/^r_/, ""), 10);
      if (isFinite(x) && x > n) n = x;
    });
    return "r_" + (n + 1);
  }
  function paintStudioSay(where) {
    var ids = {
      define: "studioDefineSay",
      research: "studioResearchSay",
      arch: "studioArchSay",
      consensus: "studioConsensusSay",
      build: "studioBuildSay",
      critics: "studioCriticsSay",
      implement: "studioImplementSay",
      iterate: "studioIterateSay"
    };
    var el = $(ids[where] || "");
    if (!el) return;
    var s = state.studioSay[where];
    if (!s) { el.textContent = ""; el.className = "tiny"; return; }
    if (s.state === "sending") {
      el.className = "dim tiny";
      el.textContent = "POST /api/v1/studio …";
      return;
    }
    if (s.state === "bad") {
      el.className = "tiny bad";
      el.textContent = "POST /api/v1/studio REFUSED — " + s.text + ". Nothing changed.";
      return;
    }
    el.className = "tiny ok";
    el.textContent = s.text;
  }
  function fillDefine() {
    var ta = $("studioDefineText");
    if (ta && !state.defineDirty && document.activeElement !== ta) {
      var next = state.defineDraft || "";
      if (ta.value !== next) ta.value = next;
    }
    var age = $("studioDefineAge");
    var d = state.studio && state.studio.define;
    if (age) {
      age.textContent = (d && d.saved_at) ? ("saved " + d.saved_at) : "not saved";
    }
    paintStudioSay("define");
  }
  function populateAddVia() {
    var sel = $("studioAddVia");
    if (!sel) return;
    var cur = cv(sel) || "openrouter-api";
    sel.innerHTML = viaChipHtml(cur);
  }
  function fillUrls(d) {
    var ta = $("studioExtraUrls");
    if (!ta) return;
    if (state.researchDirty && document.activeElement === ta) return;
    var next = (d.extra_urls || []).join("\n");
    if (ta.value !== next) ta.value = next;
  }
  function fillTargets(d, force) {
    var box = $("studioTargets");
    if (!box) return;
    if (!force && researchFocused() && box.querySelector("input")) return;
    var h = "";
    targetCatalog().forEach(function (t) {
      var on = d.targets && d.targets[t.id];
      h += '<label><input type="checkbox" data-target="' + esc(t.id) + '"' +
        (on ? " checked" : "") + "> " + esc(t.label) + "</label>";
    });
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll("input[data-target]").forEach(function (cb) {
      cb.addEventListener("change", function () {
        ensureResearch().targets[cb.getAttribute("data-target")] = !!cb.checked;
        state.researchDirty = true;
      });
    });
  }
  function paintResearchModels(d) {
    var box = $("studioResearchModels");
    if (!box) return;
    var h = "";
    (d.models || []).forEach(function (m) {
      h += '<div class="studio-rrow" data-rid="' + esc(m.id) + '">' +
        '<div class="formrow"><input type="text" data-rlabel value="' +
        esc(m.label) + '" aria-label="research label">' +
        '<button type="button" class="studio-drop" data-rdrop="' + esc(m.id) +
        '"' + ((d.models || []).length <= 1 ? " disabled" : "") +
        ">remove</button></div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">model</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-rmodel>' +
        modelChipHtml(m.model, false) + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">via</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-rvia>' +
        viaChipHtml(m.via || "openrouter-api") + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">' + esc(m.via || "") + railPresent(m.via || "") +
        " — key stays on this rail. Full catalog is Model Rater, not a 400-row dropdown.</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">2nd fallback model</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-rfbmodel>' +
        modelChipHtml(m.fallback_model, true) + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">2nd fallback via</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-rfbvia>' +
        viaChipHtml(m.fallback_via || "openrouter-api") + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">3rd fallback model</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-rfbmodel2>' +
        modelChipHtml(m.fallback_model_2, true) + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">3rd fallback via</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-rfbvia2>' +
        viaChipHtml(m.fallback_via_2 || "") + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">FALLBACK if the first call fails · ' +
        esc(m.fallback_via || "none") + railPresent(m.fallback_via || "") +
        "</div>" +
        (m.optional ? '<div class="dim tiny">optional search agent</div>' : "") +
        (m.note ? '<div class="dim tiny">' + esc(m.note) + "</div>" : "") +
        "</div>";
    });
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-rlabel]").forEach(function (inp) {
      inp.addEventListener("input", function () {
        var row = rowDraft(inp);
        if (row) { row.label = inp.value; state.researchDirty = true; }
      });
    });
    function bindChip(sel, apply) {
      box.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(function (row) {
        row.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
          if (ev.target && ev.target.classList && ev.target.classList.contains("mr-open-rater")) {
            if (window.openModelRater) window.openModelRater();
            else if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("models");
            return;
          }
          var rec = rowDraft(row);
          if (rec) apply(rec, cv(row));
        });
      });
    }
    bindChip("[data-rmodel]", function (row, v) { row.model = v; state.researchDirty = true; });
    bindChip("[data-rvia]", function (row, v) { row.via = v; state.researchDirty = true; });
    bindChip("[data-rfbmodel]", function (row, v) { row.fallback_model = v; state.researchDirty = true; });
    bindChip("[data-rfbvia]", function (row, v) { row.fallback_via = v; state.researchDirty = true; });
    bindChip("[data-rfbmodel2]", function (row, v) { row.fallback_model_2 = v; state.researchDirty = true; });
    bindChip("[data-rfbvia2]", function (row, v) { row.fallback_via_2 = v; state.researchDirty = true; });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-rdrop]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        dropResearchModel(b.getAttribute("data-rdrop"));
      });
    });
  }
  function rowDraft(el) {
    var wrap = el.closest ? el.closest(".studio-rrow") : null;
    if (!wrap) return null;
    var id = wrap.getAttribute("data-rid");
    var d = ensureResearch();
    var i;
    for (i = 0; i < d.models.length; i++) {
      if (d.models[i].id === id) return d.models[i];
    }
    return null;
  }
  function fillResearch(force) {
    populateAddVia();
    var d = ensureResearch();
    var box = $("studioResearchModels");
    if (box && (force || !researchFocused())) paintResearchModels(d);
    fillTargets(d, force);
    fillUrls(d);
    var add = $("studioAddModel");
    if (add) add.disabled = (d.models || []).length >= MAX_RESEARCH;
    paintStudioSay("research");
    paintConsensusRules();
  }
  function harvestResearchRows() {
    var box = $("studioResearchModels");
    var d = ensureResearch();
    if (box) {
      box.querySelectorAll(".studio-rrow").forEach(function (row, i) {
        var m = d.models[i];
        if (!m) return;
        var lab = row.querySelector("[data-rlabel]");
        var model = row.querySelector("[data-rmodel]");
        var via = row.querySelector("[data-rvia]");
        var fbM = row.querySelector("[data-rfbmodel]");
        var fbV = row.querySelector("[data-rfbvia]");
        var fbM2 = row.querySelector("[data-rfbmodel2]");
        var fbV2 = row.querySelector("[data-rfbvia2]");
        if (lab) m.label = lab.value;
        if (model) m.model = cv(model);
        if (via) m.via = cv(via);
        if (fbM) m.fallback_model = cv(fbM);
        if (fbV) m.fallback_via = cv(fbV);
        if (fbM2) m.fallback_model_2 = cv(fbM2);
        if (fbV2) m.fallback_via_2 = cv(fbV2);
      });
    }
    var tbox = $("studioTargets");
    if (tbox) {
      tbox.querySelectorAll("input[data-target]").forEach(function (cb) {
        d.targets[cb.getAttribute("data-target")] = !!cb.checked;
      });
    }
    var urls = $("studioExtraUrls");
    if (urls) d.extra_urls = parseUrls(urls.value);
  }
  function addResearchModel() {
    var d = ensureResearch();
    if (d.models.length >= MAX_RESEARCH) return;
    harvestResearchRows();
    var viaEl = $("studioAddVia");
    var via = cv(viaEl) || "openrouter-api";
    var model = "grok-4.6";
    if (via === "gem-api" || via === "cli:gemini") model = "gemini-2.5-flash";
    if (via === "openrouter-api") model = "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free";
    d.models.push({
      id: nextRid(d.models),
      label: "Research " + (d.models.length + 1),
      model: model,
      via: via,
      fallback_model: via === "openrouter-api" ? "grok-4.6" : "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free",
      fallback_via: via === "openrouter-api" ? "cli:grok" : "openrouter-api",
      fallback_model_2: "",
      fallback_via_2: ""
    });
    state.researchDirty = true;
    fillResearch(true);
  }
  function dropResearchModel(id) {
    var d = ensureResearch();
    if (d.models.length <= 1) return;
    harvestResearchRows();
    d.models = d.models.filter(function (m) { return m.id !== id; });
    state.researchDirty = true;
    fillResearch(true);
  }
  function cloneArch(a) {
    a = a || {};
    return {
      proposals: (a.proposals || []).map(function (p) {
        return {
          id: p.id || "",
          label: p.label || "",
          path: p.path || "",
          by: p.by || ""
        };
      })
    };
  }
  function cloneConsensus(c) {
    c = c || {};
    return {
      bar: c.bar || "majority",
      arch_choice: c.arch_choice || "auto",
      chosen_id: c.chosen_id || null,
      note: c.note || "",
      saved_at: c.saved_at || null
    };
  }
  function ensureArch() {
    if (!state.archDraft) {
      state.archDraft = cloneArch(state.studio && state.studio.arch);
    }
    return state.archDraft;
  }
  function ensureConsensus() {
    if (!state.consensusDraft) {
      state.consensusDraft = cloneConsensus(state.studio && state.studio.consensus);
    }
    return state.consensusDraft;
  }
  function nextAid(proposals) {
    var n = 0;
    (proposals || []).forEach(function (p) {
      var x = parseInt(String(p.id || "").replace(/^a_/, ""), 10);
      if (isFinite(x) && x > n) n = x;
    });
    return "a_" + (n + 1);
  }
  function barCatalog() {
    return (state.studio && state.studio.bar_catalog && state.studio.bar_catalog.length)
      ? state.studio.bar_catalog : BARS;
  }
  function archChoiceCatalog() {
    return (state.studio && state.studio.arch_choice_catalog
      && state.studio.arch_choice_catalog.length)
      ? state.studio.arch_choice_catalog : ARCH_CHOICES;
  }
  function paintArchProposals(d) {
    var box = $("studioArchProposals");
    if (!box) return;
    var rows = (d && d.proposals) || [];
    var h = "";
    if (!rows.length) {
      h = '<div class="empty">no architecture proposals yet — ARCH returns land here, or add one. Empty is UNMEASURED, not a fake design.</div>';
    } else {
      rows.forEach(function (p) {
        h += '<div class="studio-rrow" data-aid="' + esc(p.id) + '">' +
          '<div class="formrow"><input type="text" data-alabel value="' +
          esc(p.label) + '" aria-label="architecture label">' +
          '<button type="button" class="studio-drop" data-adrop="' + esc(p.id) +
          '">remove</button></div>' +
          '<label>by <input type="text" data-aby value="' + esc(p.by) +
          '"></label>' +
          '<label>path <input type="text" data-apath value="' + esc(p.path) +
          '" placeholder="docs/arch/…"></label></div>';
      });
    }
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-alabel]").forEach(function (inp) {
      inp.addEventListener("input", function () {
        var row = archRow(inp);
        if (row) { row.label = inp.value; state.archDirty = true; }
      });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-aby]").forEach(function (inp) {
      inp.addEventListener("input", function () {
        var row = archRow(inp);
        if (row) { row.by = inp.value; state.archDirty = true; }
      });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-apath]").forEach(function (inp) {
      inp.addEventListener("input", function () {
        var row = archRow(inp);
        if (row) { row.path = inp.value; state.archDirty = true; }
      });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-adrop]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () { dropArch(b.getAttribute("data-adrop")); });
    });
  }
  function archRow(el) {
    var wrap = el.closest ? el.closest(".studio-rrow") : null;
    if (!wrap) return null;
    var id = wrap.getAttribute("data-aid");
    var d = ensureArch();
    var i;
    for (i = 0; i < d.proposals.length; i++) {
      if (d.proposals[i].id === id) return d.proposals[i];
    }
    return null;
  }
  function harvestArch() {
    var box = $("studioArchProposals");
    var d = ensureArch();
    if (!box) return;
    box.querySelectorAll(".studio-rrow").forEach(function (row, i) {
      var p = d.proposals[i];
      if (!p) return;
      var lab = row.querySelector("[data-alabel]");
      var by = row.querySelector("[data-aby]");
      var path = row.querySelector("[data-apath]");
      if (lab) p.label = lab.value;
      if (by) p.by = by.value;
      if (path) p.path = path.value;
    });
  }
  function fillArch(force) {
    var d = ensureArch();
    var box = $("studioArchProposals");
    var el = document.activeElement;
    var focused = box && el && box.contains(el);
    if (box && (force || !focused)) paintArchProposals(d);
    var add = $("studioArchAdd");
    if (add) add.disabled = (d.proposals || []).length >= MAX_PROPOSALS;
    paintStudioSay("arch");
    paintConsensusRules();
  }
  function addArch() {
    var d = ensureArch();
    if (d.proposals.length >= MAX_PROPOSALS) return;
    harvestArch();
    var lab = $("studioArchAddLabel");
    var by = $("studioArchAddBy");
    d.proposals.push({
      id: nextAid(d.proposals),
      label: (lab && lab.value.trim()) || ("Architecture " + (d.proposals.length + 1)),
      by: (by && by.value.trim()) || "",
      path: ""
    });
    if (lab) lab.value = "";
    if (by) by.value = "";
    state.archDirty = true;
    fillArch(true);
  }
  function dropArch(id) {
    var d = ensureArch();
    harvestArch();
    d.proposals = d.proposals.filter(function (p) { return p.id !== id; });
    var c = ensureConsensus();
    if (c.chosen_id === id) { c.chosen_id = null; state.consensusDirty = true; }
    state.archDirty = true;
    fillArch(true);
    fillConsensus(true);
  }
  function fillConsensus(force) {
    var c = ensureConsensus();
    var h = $("studioConsensusH");
    if (h) {
      h.textContent = state.selected.id === "consensus2"
        ? "7 · CONSENSUS (adjudication)" : "4 · CONSENSUS";
    }
    var bars = $("studioBars");
    var choices = $("studioArchChoice");
    var focused = document.activeElement
      && ($("studioConsensus") || {}).contains
      && $("studioConsensus").contains(document.activeElement);
    if (bars && (force || !focused || !bars.querySelector("input"))) {
      var bh = "";
      barCatalog().forEach(function (b) {
        bh += '<label><input type="radio" name="studioBar" value="' +
          esc(b.id) + '"' + (c.bar === b.id ? " checked" : "") + "> " +
          esc(b.label) + "</label>";
      });
      bars.innerHTML = bh;
      bars.querySelectorAll("input").forEach(function (inp) {
        inp.addEventListener("change", function () {
          ensureConsensus().bar = inp.value;
          state.consensusDirty = true;
        });
      });
    }
    if (choices && (force || !focused || !choices.querySelector("input"))) {
      var ch = "";
      archChoiceCatalog().forEach(function (b) {
        ch += '<label><input type="radio" name="studioArchChoice" value="' +
          esc(b.id) + '"' + (c.arch_choice === b.id ? " checked" : "") + "> " +
          esc(b.label) + "</label>";
      });
      choices.innerHTML = ch;
      choices.querySelectorAll("input").forEach(function (inp) {
        inp.addEventListener("change", function () {
          ensureConsensus().arch_choice = inp.value;
          state.consensusDirty = true;
          syncArchPick();
        });
      });
    }
    var note = $("studioConsensusNote");
    if (note && (force || document.activeElement !== note) && !state.consensusDirty) {
      if (note.value !== (c.note || "")) note.value = c.note || "";
    }
    var age = $("studioConsensusAge");
    if (age) age.textContent = c.saved_at ? ("saved " + c.saved_at) : "not saved";
    syncArchPick();
    paintStudioSay("consensus");
    paintConsensusRules();
  }
  function syncArchPick() {
    var c = ensureConsensus();
    var d = ensureArch();
    var sel = $("studioArchPick");
    var wrap = $("studioArchPickWrap");
    var hitl = c.arch_choice === "hitl";
    if (wrap) wrap.hidden = false;
    if (!sel) return;
    var h = '<button type="button" class="chip' + (!c.chosen_id ? " on" : "") +
      '" data-val="">' +
      (d.proposals.length ? "(pick an architecture)" : "UNMEASURED — no ARCH proposals yet") +
      "</button>";
    d.proposals.forEach(function (p) {
      h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (c.chosen_id === p.id ? " on" : "") +
        '" data-val="' + esc(p.id) + '">' +
        esc(p.label || p.id) + (p.by ? (" · " + p.by) : "") + "</button>";
    });
    sel.innerHTML = h;
    sel.style.opacity = hitl ? "1" : ".55";
  }
  function harvestConsensus() {
    var c = ensureConsensus();
    var bar = document.querySelector('#studioBars input[name="studioBar"]:checked');
    var ch = document.querySelector('#studioArchChoice input[name="studioArchChoice"]:checked');
    if (bar) c.bar = bar.value;
    if (ch) c.arch_choice = ch.value;
    var sel = $("studioArchPick");
    if (sel && c.arch_choice === "hitl") c.chosen_id = cv(sel) || null;
    if (c.arch_choice === "auto") c.chosen_id = null;
    var note = $("studioConsensusNote");
    if (note) c.note = note.value;
  }
  function saveArch() {
    harvestArch();
    var d = ensureArch();
    postStudio({ arch: { proposals: d.proposals } }, "arch",
      "ARCH proposals saved. Does not start MOTIF.");
  }
  function saveConsensus() {
    harvestConsensus();
    var c = ensureConsensus();
    postStudio({
      consensus: {
        bar: c.bar,
        arch_choice: c.arch_choice,
        chosen_id: c.chosen_id,
        note: c.note
      }
    }, "consensus", "CONSENSUS bar saved. Does not start MOTIF.");
  }

  function cloneAgents(rows, fallback) {
    var src = (rows && rows.length) ? rows : fallback;
    return src.map(function (m) {
      return {
        id: m.id || "",
        label: m.label || "",
        model: m.model || "",
        via: m.via || "",
        fallback_model: m.fallback_model || "",
        fallback_via: m.fallback_via || "",
        fallback_model_2: m.fallback_model_2 || "",
        fallback_via_2: m.fallback_via_2 || "",
        effort: m.effort || "",
        budget_usd: m.budget_usd
      };
    });
  }
  function cloneBuild(b) {
    b = b || {};
    return {
      artifact: b.artifact || "json",
      simultaneous: b.simultaneous || 2,
      input_text: b.input_text || "",
      agents: cloneAgents(b.agents, DEFAULT_BUILD),
      saved_at: b.saved_at || null
    };
  }
  function cloneCritics(c) {
    c = c || {};
    return {
      bar: c.bar || "majority",
      continue_when: c.continue_when || "bar",
      agents: cloneAgents(c.agents, DEFAULT_CRITICS),
      saved_at: c.saved_at || null
    };
  }
  function destViaGitur(kind) {
    return kind === "github" || kind === "gitlab";
  }
  function destGiturText(kind) {
    return destViaGitur(kind)
      ? "Gitur first (branch + PR). CCr writes the live tree only after that."
      : "Not Gitur — write to this target. Live tree is CCr only when the target is GitHub or GitLab.";
  }
  function cloneDest(d) {
    d = d || {};
    var kind = d.kind || "github";
    return {
      kind: kind,
      path: d.path || "",
      via_gitur: destViaGitur(kind),
      saved_at: d.saved_at || null
    };
  }
  function cloneImplement(im) {
    im = im || {};
    return {
      via_gitur: destViaGitur((state.destDraft && state.destDraft.kind) || (im && im.via_gitur === false ? "local" : "github")),
      note: im.note || "",
      saved_at: im.saved_at || null
    };
  }
  function cloneIterate(it) {
    it = it || {};
    return {
      max_rounds: it.max_rounds == null ? 1 : it.max_rounds,
      timed_s: it.timed_s || 0,
      budget_usd: it.budget_usd || 0,
      until: it.until || "rounds",
      saved_at: it.saved_at || null
    };
  }
  function ensureDest() {
    if (!state.destDraft) state.destDraft = cloneDest(state.studio && state.studio.dest);
    return state.destDraft;
  }
  function ensureBuild() {
    if (!state.buildDraft) state.buildDraft = cloneBuild(state.studio && state.studio.build);
    return state.buildDraft;
  }
  function ensureCritics() {
    if (!state.criticsDraft) state.criticsDraft = cloneCritics(state.studio && state.studio.critics);
    return state.criticsDraft;
  }
  function ensureImplement() {
    if (!state.implementDraft) {
      state.implementDraft = cloneImplement(state.studio && state.studio.implement);
    }
    return state.implementDraft;
  }
  function ensureIterate() {
    if (!state.iterateDraft) {
      state.iterateDraft = cloneIterate(state.studio && state.studio.iterate);
    }
    return state.iterateDraft;
  }
  function nextSeatId(rows, prefix) {
    var n = 0;
    (rows || []).forEach(function (m) {
      var x = parseInt(String(m.id || "").replace(new RegExp("^" + prefix + "_"), ""), 10);
      if (isFinite(x) && x > n) n = x;
    });
    return prefix + "_" + (n + 1);
  }
  function paintRadios(box, catalog, selected, name, onChange) {
    if (!box) return;
    var h = "";
    catalog.forEach(function (b) {
      h += '<label><input type="radio" name="' + esc(name) + '" value="' +
        esc(b.id) + '"' + (selected === b.id ? " checked" : "") + "> " +
        esc(b.label) + "</label>";
    });
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll("input").forEach(function (inp) {
      inp.addEventListener("change", function () { onChange(inp.value); });
    });
  }
  function paintSeatRows(box, agents, kind) {
    if (!box) return;
    var h = "";
    (agents || []).forEach(function (m) {
      h += '<div class="studio-rrow" data-kind="' + esc(kind) + '" data-sid="' +
        esc(m.id) + '">' +
        '<div class="formrow"><input type="text" data-slabel value="' +
        esc(m.label) + '" aria-label="' + esc(kind) + ' label">' +
        '<button type="button" class="studio-drop" data-sdrop="' + esc(m.id) +
        '"' + ((agents || []).length <= 1 ? " disabled" : "") +
        ">remove</button></div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">model</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-smodel>' +
        modelChipHtml(m.model, false) + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">via</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-svia>' +
        viaChipHtml(m.via || "openrouter-api") + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">' + esc(m.via || "") + railPresent(m.via || "") +
        " — key stays on this rail. Full catalog is Model Rater.</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">2nd fallback model</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-sfbmodel>' +
        modelChipHtml(m.fallback_model, true) + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">2nd fallback via</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-sfbvia>' +
        viaChipHtml(m.fallback_via || "openrouter-api") + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">3rd fallback model</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-sfbmodel2>' +
        modelChipHtml(m.fallback_model_2, true) + "</div>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">3rd fallback via</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-sfbvia2>' +
        viaChipHtml(m.fallback_via_2 || "") + "</div></div>";
    });
    box.innerHTML = h || '<div class="empty">none — add one</div>';
    function draftOf(el) {
      var wrap = el.closest ? el.closest(".studio-rrow") : null;
      if (!wrap) return null;
      var id = wrap.getAttribute("data-sid");
      var list = kind === "build" ? ensureBuild().agents : ensureCritics().agents;
      var i;
      for (i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
        if (list[i].id === id) return list[i];
      }
      return null;
    }
    function dirty() {
      if (kind === "build") state.buildDirty = true;
      else state.criticsDirty = true;
    }
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-slabel]").forEach(function (inp) {
      inp.addEventListener("input", function () {
        var row = draftOf(inp);
        if (row) { row.label = inp.value; dirty(); }
      });
    });
    function bindSeatChip(sel, apply) {
      box.querySelectorAll(sel).forEach(function (row) {
        row.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
          if (ev.target && ev.target.classList && ev.target.classList.contains("mr-open-rater")) {
            if (window.openModelRater) window.openModelRater();
            else if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("models");
            return;
          }
          var rec = draftOf(row);
          if (rec) apply(rec, cv(row));
        });
      });
    }
    bindSeatChip("[data-smodel]", function (row, v) { row.model = v; dirty(); });
    bindSeatChip("[data-svia]", function (row, v) { row.via = v; dirty(); });
    bindSeatChip("[data-sfbmodel]", function (row, v) { row.fallback_model = v; dirty(); });
    bindSeatChip("[data-sfbvia]", function (row, v) { row.fallback_via = v; dirty(); });
    bindSeatChip("[data-sfbmodel2]", function (row, v) { row.fallback_model_2 = v; dirty(); });
    bindSeatChip("[data-sfbvia2]", function (row, v) { row.fallback_via_2 = v; dirty(); });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-sdrop]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        dropSeat(kind, b.getAttribute("data-sdrop"));
      });
    });
  }
  function harvestSeats(kind) {
    var box = $(kind === "build" ? "studioBuildAgents" : "studioCriticsAgents");
    var list = kind === "build" ? ensureBuild().agents : ensureCritics().agents;
    if (!box) return;
    box.querySelectorAll(".studio-rrow").forEach(function (row, i) {
      var m = list[i];
      if (!m) return;
      var lab = row.querySelector("[data-slabel]");
      var model = row.querySelector("[data-smodel]");
      var via = row.querySelector("[data-svia]");
      var fbM = row.querySelector("[data-sfbmodel]");
      var fbV = row.querySelector("[data-sfbvia]");
      var fbM2 = row.querySelector("[data-sfbmodel2]");
      var fbV2 = row.querySelector("[data-sfbvia2]");
      if (lab) m.label = lab.value;
      if (model) m.model = cv(model);
      if (via) m.via = cv(via);
      if (fbM) m.fallback_model = cv(fbM);
      if (fbV) m.fallback_via = cv(fbV);
      if (fbM2) m.fallback_model_2 = cv(fbM2);
      if (fbV2) m.fallback_via_2 = cv(fbV2);
    });
  }
  function addSeat(kind) {
    var d = kind === "build" ? ensureBuild() : ensureCritics();
    if (d.agents.length >= MAX_AGENTS) return;
    harvestSeats(kind);
    var viaEl = $(kind === "build" ? "studioAddBuildVia" : "studioAddCriticVia");
    var via = cv(viaEl) || "openrouter-api";
    var prefix = kind === "build" ? "b" : "c";
    d.agents.push({
      id: nextSeatId(d.agents, prefix),
      label: (kind === "build" ? "Builder " : "Critic ") + (d.agents.length + 1),
      model: via === "cli:grok" ? "grok-4.6" : (via === "cursor-api" ? "composer-2.5" : ""),
      via: via,
      fallback_model: "",
      fallback_via: "openrouter-api",
      fallback_model_2: "",
      fallback_via_2: ""
    });
    if (kind === "build") {
      state.buildDirty = true;
      if (d.simultaneous < d.agents.length) d.simultaneous = d.agents.length;
      fillBuild(true);
    } else {
      state.criticsDirty = true;
      fillCritics(true);
    }
  }
  function dropSeat(kind, id) {
    var d = kind === "build" ? ensureBuild() : ensureCritics();
    if (d.agents.length <= 1) return;
    harvestSeats(kind);
    d.agents = d.agents.filter(function (m) { return m.id !== id; });
    if (kind === "build") {
      state.buildDirty = true;
      if (d.simultaneous > d.agents.length) d.simultaneous = d.agents.length;
      fillBuild(true);
    } else {
      state.criticsDirty = true;
      fillCritics(true);
    }
  }
  function fillDest(radioId, pathId, lineId, radioName, force) {
    var d = ensureDest();
    var box = $(radioId);
    var focused = box && document.activeElement
      && box.contains && box.contains(document.activeElement);
    if (box && (force || !focused || !box.querySelector("input"))) {
      paintRadios(box, DEST_KINDS, d.kind, radioName, function (v) {
        var dest = ensureDest();
        dest.kind = v;
        dest.via_gitur = destViaGitur(v);
        state.destDirty = true;
        fillDestLine("studioBuildDestGitur");
        fillDestLine("studioImplementDestGitur");
      });
    }
    var path = $(pathId);
    if (path && document.activeElement !== path && !state.destDirty) {
      if (path.value !== (d.path || "")) path.value = d.path || "";
    }
    fillDestLine(lineId);
  }
  function fillDestLine(id) {
    var el = $(id);
    if (el) el.textContent = destGiturText(ensureDest().kind);
  }
  function harvestDest() {
    var d = ensureDest();
    var hit = document.querySelector("#studioBuildDest input:checked")
      || document.querySelector("#studioImplementDest input:checked");
    if (hit) d.kind = hit.value;
    d.via_gitur = destViaGitur(d.kind);
    var p1 = $("studioBuildDestPath");
    var p2 = $("studioImplementDestPath");
    var el = document.activeElement;
    if (el === p2 && p2) d.path = p2.value;
    else if (el === p1 && p1) d.path = p1.value;
    else if (p1 && p1.value) d.path = p1.value;
    else if (p2) d.path = p2.value;
  }
  function fillBuild(force) {
    var d = ensureBuild();
    fillDest("studioBuildDest", "studioBuildDestPath", "studioBuildDestGitur",
      "studioBDest", force);
    var artBox = $("studioBuildArtifact");
    var focused = $("studioBuild") && document.activeElement
      && $("studioBuild").contains(document.activeElement);
    if (artBox && (force || !focused || !artBox.querySelector("input"))) {
      paintRadios(artBox, ARTIFACTS, d.artifact, "studioBuildArt", function (v) {
        ensureBuild().artifact = v;
        state.buildDirty = true;
      });
    }
    var sim = $("studioBuildSim");
    if (sim && document.activeElement !== sim) sim.value = String(d.simultaneous || 1);
    var ta = $("studioBuildInput");
    if (ta && !state.buildDirty && document.activeElement !== ta) {
      if (ta.value !== (d.input_text || "")) ta.value = d.input_text || "";
    }
    var box = $("studioBuildAgents");
    if (box && (force || !focused || document.activeElement === sim || document.activeElement === ta)) {
      if (force || document.activeElement !== box && !(box.contains && box.contains(document.activeElement))) {
        paintSeatRows(box, d.agents, "build");
      }
    }
    var addVia = $("studioAddBuildVia");
    if (addVia) {
      addVia.innerHTML = viaChipHtml(cv(addVia) || "openrouter-api");
    }
    var add = $("studioAddBuilder");
    if (add) add.disabled = d.agents.length >= MAX_AGENTS;
    paintStudioSay("build");
    paintConsensusRules();
  }
  function fillCritics(force) {
    var d = ensureCritics();
    var focused = $("studioCritics") && document.activeElement
      && $("studioCritics").contains(document.activeElement);
    var barBox = $("studioCriticsBar");
    if (barBox && (force || !focused || !barBox.querySelector("input"))) {
      paintRadios(barBox, barCatalog(), d.bar, "studioCriBar", function (v) {
        ensureCritics().bar = v;
        state.criticsDirty = true;
      });
    }
    var contBox = $("studioCriticsCont");
    if (contBox && (force || !focused || !contBox.querySelector("input"))) {
      paintRadios(contBox, CONTINUE_WHEN, d.continue_when, "studioCriCont", function (v) {
        ensureCritics().continue_when = v;
        state.criticsDirty = true;
      });
    }
    var box = $("studioCriticsAgents");
    if (box && (force || !focused || !(box.contains && box.contains(document.activeElement)))) {
      paintSeatRows(box, d.agents, "critics");
    }
    var addVia = $("studioAddCriticVia");
    if (addVia) {
      addVia.innerHTML = viaChipHtml(cv(addVia) || "openrouter-api");
    }
    var add = $("studioAddCritic");
    if (add) add.disabled = d.agents.length >= MAX_AGENTS;
    paintStudioSay("critics");
    paintConsensusRules();
  }
  function fillImplement() {
    var d = ensureImplement();
    fillDest("studioImplementDest", "studioImplementDestPath",
      "studioImplementDestGitur", "studioIDest", false);
    var note = $("studioImplementNote");
    if (note && !state.implementDirty && document.activeElement !== note) {
      if (note.value !== (d.note || "")) note.value = d.note || "";
    }
    paintStudioSay("implement");
  }
  function fillIterate(force) {
    var d = ensureIterate();
    var box = $("studioIterateUntil");
    var focused = $("studioIterate") && document.activeElement
      && $("studioIterate").contains(document.activeElement);
    if (box && (force || !focused || !box.querySelector("input"))) {
      paintRadios(box, ITERATE_UNTIL, d.until, "studioItUntil", function (v) {
        ensureIterate().until = v;
        state.iterateDirty = true;
      });
    }
    function num(id, val) {
      var el = $(id);
      if (el && document.activeElement !== el) el.value = String(val);
    }
    num("studioIterateRounds", d.max_rounds);
    num("studioIterateTimed", d.timed_s);
    num("studioIterateBudget", d.budget_usd);
    paintStudioSay("iterate");
    paintConsensusRules();
  }
  function harvestBuild() {
    var d = ensureBuild();
    var art = document.querySelector('#studioBuildArtifact input:checked');
    if (art) d.artifact = art.value;
    var sim = $("studioBuildSim");
    if (sim) d.simultaneous = parseInt(sim.value, 10) || 1;
    var ta = $("studioBuildInput");
    if (ta) d.input_text = ta.value;
    harvestSeats("build");
  }
  function harvestCritics() {
    var d = ensureCritics();
    var bar = document.querySelector('#studioCriticsBar input:checked');
    var cont = document.querySelector('#studioCriticsCont input:checked');
    if (bar) d.bar = bar.value;
    if (cont) d.continue_when = cont.value;
    harvestSeats("critics");
  }
  function saveBuild() {
    harvestBuild();
    harvestDest();
    var d = ensureBuild();
    var dest = ensureDest();
    postStudio({
      build: {
        artifact: d.artifact,
        simultaneous: d.simultaneous,
        input_text: d.input_text,
        agents: d.agents
      },
      dest: { kind: dest.kind, path: dest.path }
    }, "build", "BUILD pack saved. Does not start MOTIF.");
  }
  function saveCritics() {
    harvestCritics();
    var d = ensureCritics();
    postStudio({
      critics: {
        bar: d.bar,
        continue_when: d.continue_when,
        agents: d.agents
      }
    }, "critics", "CRITICS pack saved. Does not start MOTIF.");
  }
  function saveImplement() {
    harvestDest();
    var d = ensureImplement();
    var dest = ensureDest();
    var note = $("studioImplementNote");
    if (note) d.note = note.value;
    postStudio({
      dest: { kind: dest.kind, path: dest.path },
      implement: { note: d.note }
    }, "implement", destViaGitur(dest.kind)
      ? "IMPLEMENT saved. GitHub/GitLab: Gitur then live tree. Does not start MOTIF."
      : "IMPLEMENT saved. Writes the named target, not Gitur. Does not start MOTIF.");
  }
  function saveIterate() {
    var d = ensureIterate();
    var until = document.querySelector('#studioIterateUntil input:checked');
    if (until) d.until = until.value;
    var r = $("studioIterateRounds");
    var t = $("studioIterateTimed");
    var b = $("studioIterateBudget");
    if (r) d.max_rounds = parseInt(r.value, 10) || 0;
    if (t) d.timed_s = parseInt(t.value, 10) || 0;
    if (b) d.budget_usd = parseFloat(b.value) || 0;
    postStudio({
      iterate: {
        max_rounds: d.max_rounds,
        timed_s: d.timed_s,
        budget_usd: d.budget_usd,
        until: d.until
      }
    }, "iterate", "ITERATE conditions saved. Does not start MOTIF.");
  }

  function ingestStudio(rec) {
    if (!rec) return;
    state.studio = rec;
    if (rec.via_options && rec.via_options.length) state.viaOptions = rec.via_options;
    if (rec.target_catalog && rec.target_catalog.length) state.targetCatalog = rec.target_catalog;
    if (!state.defineDirty && document.activeElement !== $("studioDefineText")) {
      state.defineDraft = (rec.define && rec.define.text) || "";
    }
    if (!state.researchDirty && !researchFocused()) {
      state.researchDraft = cloneResearch(rec.research);
    }
    if (!state.archDirty) {
      state.archDraft = cloneArch(rec.arch);
    }
    if (!state.consensusDirty) {
      state.consensusDraft = cloneConsensus(rec.consensus);
    }
    if (!state.buildDirty) state.buildDraft = cloneBuild(rec.build);
    if (!state.criticsDirty) state.criticsDraft = cloneCritics(rec.critics);
    if (!state.destDirty) state.destDraft = cloneDest(rec.dest);
    if (!state.implementDirty) state.implementDraft = cloneImplement(rec.implement);
    if (!state.iterateDirty) state.iterateDraft = cloneIterate(rec.iterate);
    if (!state.graphDirty && rec.graph && rec.graph.nodes) {
      state.graphDraft = rec.graph;
    }
    var age = $("studioAge");
    if (age) {
      age.textContent = rec.kind === "NO_SOURCE"
        ? "GET /api/v1/studio · no pack yet — SAVE STATEMENT to create"
        : ("GET /api/v1/studio · " + (rec.updated_at || rec.kind || "ok"));
      age.className = rec.kind === "BROKE" ? "tiny bad" : "dim tiny";
    }
  }
  function postStudio(body, where, okText) {
    if (!state.api) return;
    state.studioSay[where] = { state: "sending" };
    paintStudioSay(where);
    state.api("/api/v1/studio", { method: "POST", body: body }).then(function (rec) {
      if (rec && rec.error) {
        state.studioSay[where] = {
          state: "bad",
          text: String(rec.error || rec.detail || "refused")
        };
      } else {
        if (where === "define") state.defineDirty = false;
        if (where === "research") state.researchDirty = false;
        if (where === "arch") state.archDirty = false;
        if (where === "consensus") state.consensusDirty = false;
        if (where === "build") {
          state.buildDirty = false;
          state.destDirty = false;
        }
        if (where === "critics") state.criticsDirty = false;
        if (where === "implement") {
          state.implementDirty = false;
          state.destDirty = false;
        }
        if (where === "iterate") state.iterateDirty = false;
        if (where === "graph") state.graphDirty = false;
        state.studioSay[where] = { state: "ok", text: okText };
        ingestStudio(rec);
      }
      paintStudioSay(where);
      state._sig = "";
      paintAll();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.studioSay[where] = {
        state: "bad",
        text: String((e && e.message) || e)
      };
      paintStudioSay(where);
    });
  }
  function saveDefine() {
    var ta = $("studioDefineText");
    var text = ta ? ta.value : (state.defineDraft || "");
    state.defineDraft = text;
    postStudio({ define: { text: text } }, "define",
      "PROBLEM STATEMENT saved. Does not start MOTIF.");
  }
  function saveResearch() {
    harvestResearchRows();
    var d = ensureResearch();
    postStudio({
      research: {
        models: d.models,
        targets: d.targets,
        extra_urls: d.extra_urls
      }
    }, "research", "RESEARCH pack saved. Does not start MOTIF.");
  }
  function rcSay(t) {
    var el = $("rcSay");
    if (el) el.textContent = t || "";
  }
  function paintResearchCall(rec) {
    var log = $("rcLog");
    if (!log) return;
    if (!rec) {
      log.textContent = "unread — GET /api/v1/research_call";
      return;
    }
    var h = "kind " + (rec.kind || "") +
      " · calls " + (rec.n_calls != null ? rec.n_calls : 0) +
      " · results " + (rec.n_results != null ? rec.n_results : 0);
    if (rec.note) h += "\n" + String(rec.note).slice(0, 240);
    (rec.calls || []).slice(-4).forEach(function (c) {
      h += "\ncall " + (c.id || "") + " · " + (c.task || "") + " · " +
        (c.query || "").slice(0, 80);
    });
    (rec.results || []).slice(-4).forEach(function (r) {
      h += "\nresult " + (r.status || "") + " · " +
        (r.detail || "").slice(0, 80);
    });
    log.textContent = h;
    var pre = $("rcPayload");
    if (pre && !pre.textContent.trim() && (rec.call_shape || rec.result_shape)) {
      pre.textContent = JSON.stringify({
        call_shape: rec.call_shape || null,
        result_shape: rec.result_shape || null
      }, null, 2);
    }
  }
  function researchCallOn() {
    var vis = window.deckTabVisible;
    if (typeof vis === "function") {
      if (vis("ups") || vis("crucible")) return true;
      if (!vis("studio")) return false;
    }
    var el = $("rcFile") || $("studioResearchCall") || $("studioResearch");
    if (!el) return false;
    return el.offsetParent !== null;
  }
  function loadResearchCall() {
    if (!state.api) {
      paintResearchCall(null);
      return;
    }
    if (!researchCallOn()) return;
    state.api("/api/v1/research_call").then(paintResearchCall).catch(function (e) {
      paintResearchCall(null);
      rcSay(String((e && e.message) || e));
    });
  }
  /* Crucible + UPS occupancy jump — one Core :8770 research_call FILE/INGEST. */
  window.openStudioResearchCall = function (focus) {
    if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("studio");
    state.selected = { kind: "stage", id: "research" };
    paintStudio();
    loadResearchCall();
    var el = focus === "ingest" ? $("rcIngest")
      : (focus === "file" ? $("rcFile") : ($("rcFile") || $("studioResearch")));
    if (el && el.scrollIntoView) {
      try { el.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "nearest" }); } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (focus === "ingest" && $("rcIngest") && $("rcIngest").focus) {
      $("rcIngest").focus();
    } else if (focus === "file" && $("rcQuery") && $("rcQuery").focus) {
      $("rcQuery").focus();
    }
  };
  window.deckOpenResearchCall = window.openStudioResearchCall;
  };
  function bindStudioForms() {
    if (bindStudioForms._on) return;
    var ta = $("studioDefineText");
    var saveD = $("studioDefineSave");
    var saveR = $("studioResearchSave");
    var add = $("studioAddModel");
    var urls = $("studioExtraUrls");
    if (!ta || !saveD || !saveR || !add) return;
    bindStudioForms._on = true;
    ta.addEventListener("input", function () {
      state.defineDirty = true;
      state.defineDraft = ta.value;
    });
    saveD.addEventListener("click", saveDefine);
    add.addEventListener("click", addResearchModel);
    saveR.addEventListener("click", saveResearch);
    if (urls) {
      urls.addEventListener("input", function () {
        ensureResearch();
        state.researchDirty = true;
        state.researchDraft.extra_urls = parseUrls(urls.value);
      });
    }
    var go = $("studioOpenRater");
    if (go) {
      go.addEventListener("click", function () {
        if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("models");
      });
    }
    var fileBtn = $("rcFile");
    if (fileBtn && !fileBtn._rcBound) {
      fileBtn._rcBound = true;
      fileBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
        if (!state.api) {
          rcSay("connect to Core :8770 — one Core, no second serve");
          return;
        }
        var urls = (($("rcUrls") && $("rcUrls").value) || "").split(/\n/)
          .map(function (s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
        var body = {
          action: "call",
          task: cv("rcTask") || "extract",
          query: ($("rcQuery") && $("rcQuery").value) || "",
          urls: urls
        };
        rcSay("filing…");
        state.api("/api/v1/research_call", { method: "POST", body: body }).then(function (rec) {
          var call = rec && rec.call;
          var pre = $("rcPayload");
          if (pre && call) pre.textContent = JSON.stringify({
            schema: call.schema,
            task: call.task,
            urls: call.urls,
            query: call.query
          }, null, 2);
          rcSay(call ? ("filed " + call.id) : ((rec && rec.error) || "filed"));
          loadResearchCall();
        }).catch(function (e) { rcSay(String((e && e.message) || e)); });
      });
    }
    var ingBtn = $("rcIngestBtn");
    if (ingBtn && !ingBtn._rcBound) {
      ingBtn._rcBound = true;
      ingBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
        if (!state.api) {
          rcSay("connect to Core :8770 — one Core, no second serve");
          return;
        }
        var raw = ($("rcIngest") && $("rcIngest").value) || "";
        rcSay("ingest…");
        state.api("/api/v1/research_call", {
          method: "POST",
          body: { action: "ingest", result_json: raw }
        }).then(function (rec) {
          var res = rec && rec.result;
          var pre = $("rcPayload");
          if (pre && res) {
            pre.textContent = JSON.stringify({
              schema: res.schema,
              status: res.status,
              result: res.result,
              sources: res.sources,
              detail: res.detail
            }, null, 2);
          }
          rcSay(res && res.status
            ? ("ingested " + res.status)
            : ((rec && rec.error) || "ingested"));
          loadResearchCall();
        }).catch(function (e) { rcSay(String((e && e.message) || e)); });
      });
    }
    loadResearchCall();
    var addA = $("studioArchAdd");
    var saveA = $("studioArchSave");
    var toC = $("studioArchToConsensus");
    var saveC = $("studioConsensusSave");
    var note = $("studioConsensusNote");
    var pick = $("studioArchPick");
    if (addA) addA.addEventListener("click", addArch);
    if (saveA) saveA.addEventListener("click", saveArch);
    if (toC) {
      toC.addEventListener("click", function () {
        state.selected = { kind: "stage", id: "consensus1" };
        paintStudio();
      });
    }
    if (saveC) saveC.addEventListener("click", saveConsensus);
    if (note) {
      note.addEventListener("input", function () {
        ensureConsensus().note = note.value;
        state.consensusDirty = true;
      });
    }
    if (pick) {
      pick.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var c = ensureConsensus();
        if (c.arch_choice === "hitl") {
          c.chosen_id = cv(pick) || null;
          state.consensusDirty = true;
        }
      });
    }
    var addB = $("studioAddBuilder");
    var saveB = $("studioBuildSave");
    var sim = $("studioBuildSim");
    var bin = $("studioBuildInput");
    if (addB) addB.addEventListener("click", function () { addSeat("build"); });
    if (saveB) saveB.addEventListener("click", saveBuild);
    ["studioBuildDestPath", "studioImplementDestPath"].forEach(function (id) {
      var el = $(id);
      if (!el) return;
      el.addEventListener("input", function () {
        ensureDest().path = el.value;
        state.destDirty = true;
      });
    });
    if (sim) {
      sim.addEventListener("input", function () {
        ensureBuild().simultaneous = parseInt(sim.value, 10) || 1;
        state.buildDirty = true;
      });
    }
    if (bin) {
      bin.addEventListener("input", function () {
        ensureBuild().input_text = bin.value;
        state.buildDirty = true;
      });
    }
    var addC = $("studioAddCritic");
    var saveK = $("studioCriticsSave");
    if (addC) addC.addEventListener("click", function () { addSeat("critics"); });
    if (saveK) saveK.addEventListener("click", saveCritics);
    var cruRuns = $("studioCruRuns");
    if (cruRuns) {
      cruRuns.addEventListener("click", function () {
        if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("runs");
      });
    }
    var cruRun = $("studioCruRun");
    if (cruRun) {
      cruRun.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var say = $("studioCruRunSay");
        var log = $("studioCruRunLog");
        var raw = (($("studioCruSources") || {}).value || "").split(/\n/);
        var sources = raw.map(function (s) { return String(s || "").trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
        var bad = sources.filter(function (s) {
          var l = s.toLowerCase();
          return l.indexOf("legal") >= 0 || l.indexOf(":\\") >= 0
            || s.charAt(0) === "/" || s.indexOf("..") >= 0;
        });
        if (bad.length) {
          if (say) say.textContent = "LEGAL_OMITTED / IDENTITY_MISMATCH — docs-role names only";
          return;
        }
        if (!sources.length) sources = ["FINAL_ARCHITECTURE.md"];
        if (!state.api) {
          if (say) say.textContent = "connect to Core :8770 — one Core, no second serve";
          return;
        }
        if (say) say.textContent = "POST /api/v1/crucible …";
        state.api("/api/v1/crucible", {
          method: "POST",
          body: { sources: sources, priority: "high" }
        }).then(function (rec) {
          if (say) {
            say.textContent = rec && rec.job_id
              ? ("201 job " + rec.job_id + " · " + (rec.outcome || rec.error || "queued"))
              : String((rec && (rec.error || rec.detail)) || "returned");
          }
          if (log) log.textContent = JSON.stringify({
            job_id: rec && rec.job_id,
            outcome: rec && rec.outcome,
            error: rec && rec.error,
            detail: rec && rec.detail
          }, null, 2);
        }).catch(function (e) {
          if (say) say.textContent = String((e && e.message) || e);
        });
      });
    }
    var saveI = $("studioImplementSave");
    var inote = $("studioImplementNote");
    if (saveI) saveI.addEventListener("click", saveImplement);
    if (inote) {
      inote.addEventListener("input", function () {
        ensureImplement().note = inote.value;
        state.implementDirty = true;
      });
    }
    var saveT = $("studioIterateSave");
    if (saveT) saveT.addEventListener("click", saveIterate);
    ["studioIterateRounds", "studioIterateTimed", "studioIterateBudget"].forEach(function (id) {
      var el = $(id);
      if (!el) return;
      el.addEventListener("input", function () {
        var d = ensureIterate();
        if (id.indexOf("Rounds") >= 0) d.max_rounds = parseInt(el.value, 10) || 0;
        else if (id.indexOf("Timed") >= 0) d.timed_s = parseInt(el.value, 10) || 0;
        else d.budget_usd = parseFloat(el.value) || 0;
        state.iterateDirty = true;
      });
    });
  }

  function bindGo(root) {
    if (!root) return;
    root.querySelectorAll(".studio-go").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var arm = b.getAttribute("data-arm");
        var cmd = b.getAttribute("data-requeue");
        if (arm) {
          state.arm = arm;
          state.post = null;
          repaintRunViews();
          return;
        }
        if (b.getAttribute("data-disarm")) {
          state.arm = "";
          repaintRunViews();
          return;
        }
        var open = b.getAttribute("data-open-run");
        if (open) {
          selectRun(open);
          return;
        }
        if (b.getAttribute("data-clear")) {
          state.runId = "";
          state.arm = "";
          state.post = null;
          if (state.selected.kind === "run") state.selected = { kind: "stage", id: "build" };
          paintRunsList();
          paintStudio();
          return;
        }
        if (cmd) {
          fileNewJob(cmd);
          return;
        }
        var filt = b.getAttribute("data-filter-runs");
        if (filt) {
          state.runFilter = filt;
          state.runShown = PAGE;
          if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("runs");
          paintRuns();
          return;
        }
        var tab = b.getAttribute("data-go");
        if (tab && window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab(tab);
      });
    });
  }

  function repaintRunViews() {
    paintRunsList();
    paintInspector();
  }

  /* POST /api/v1/jobs. NOT a retry: cosmos_sched has no such verb, so this
     files a separate job with the same command and Core hands back a new id.
     The old version swallowed both the id and the failure in an empty catch,
     which read as a button that did nothing. */
  function fileNewJob(cmd) {
    if (!state.api || !cmd) return;
    state.arm = "";
    state.post = { state: "sending", cmd: cmd };
    repaintRunViews();
    state.api("/api/v1/jobs", { method: "POST", body: { command: cmd, priority: "normal" } })
      .then(function (d) {
        // The selection stays on the row the operator was reading. Jumping to
        // the new id lands on a job the last poll has never seen, which reads
        // as a failure; the receipt below offers the jump instead.
        state.post = { state: "ok", cmd: cmd, id: d && d.job_id };
        state._sig = "";
        tick();
        repaintRunViews();
      })
      .catch(function (e) {
        state.post = { state: "bad", cmd: cmd, err: String((e && e.message) || e) };
        repaintRunViews();
      });
  }

  function paintStudio() {
    paintPalette();
    paintGraph();
    paintInspector();
  }

  /* Every row Core sent, in one shape, whichever endpoint answered. GET /jobs
     is {id: state} and carries no command, age or lane; a row built from it is
     marked thin rather than padded out with placeholders. */
  function allRows() {
    var rows = jukeRows();
    if (rows.length) return rows;
    var jobs = jobsMap();
    if (!jobs) return [];
    return Object.keys(jobs).map(function (id) {
      return { job_id: id, st: jobs[id], thin: true };
    });
  }

  /* Data on screen that the LAST read did not confirm. The rows stay — the
     last measured queue is still what was measured — but nothing here may
     imply it is current. */
  function staleReadHtml() {
    if (!state.err.jukebox || !allRows().length) return "";
    return '<div class="read-stale">last good read shown · ' + esc(state.err.jukebox) + "</div>";
  }

  function matchesQuery(r) {
    var q = state.runQuery;
    if (!q) return true;
    return (String(r.job_id) + " " + String(r.command || "")).toLowerCase().indexOf(q) >= 0;
  }

  function shownRows() {
    var f = state.runFilter;
    var out = allRows().filter(function (r) {
      var hit = f === "ALL" || (f === "STALE" ? staleRow(r) : String(r.st).toUpperCase() === f);
      return hit && matchesQuery(r);
    });
    var sort = state.runSort;
    if (sort === "old" || sort === "new") {
      out.sort(function (a, b) {
        var x = Number(a.submitted);
        var y = Number(b.submitted);
        if (!isFinite(x) || !isFinite(y)) return 0;
        return sort === "old" ? x - y : y - x;
      });
    } else if (sort === "age") {
      out.sort(function (a, b) { return (Number(b.age_s) || 0) - (Number(a.age_s) || 0); });
    } else if (sort === "state") {
      out.sort(function (a, b) {
        var x = JOB_ORDER.indexOf(String(a.st).toUpperCase());
        var y = JOB_ORDER.indexOf(String(b.st).toUpperCase());
        return (x < 0 ? 99 : x) - (y < 0 ? 99 : y);
      });
    }
    return out;
  }

  function rowMeta(r) {
    var bits = [];
    if (r.age_s != null) bits.push("waited " + fmtAge(r.age_s));
    if (String(r.st).toUpperCase() === "RUNNING" && r.running_s != null) {
      bits.push("running " + fmtAge(r.running_s));
    }
