    if (r.priority) bits.push(esc(r.priority));
    if (r.lane) bits.push(esc(r.lane));
    if (r.thin) bits.push("GET /jobs — no command on this endpoint");
    return bits.join(" · ");
  }

  function rowHtml(r) {
    var id = r.job_id;
    var st = r.st;
    var meta = rowMeta(r);
    return '<button type="button" class="runrow jobrow sess' +
      (state.runId === id ? " on" : "") + (staleRow(r) ? " is-stale" : "") +
      '" data-run="' + esc(id) + '" aria-pressed="' + (state.runId === id ? "true" : "false") +
      '"><span class="jid">' + esc(id) + "</span>" +
      (staleRow(r) ? '<span class="rstale" title="RUNNING past 30m — reported, never retried">STALE</span>' : "") +
      '<span class="jst ' + stCls(st) + '">' + esc(st) + "</span>" +
      '<span class="jcmd">' + (r.command ? esc(r.command) : "—") + "</span>" +
      '<span class="rmeta">' + meta + "</span></button>";
  }

  function paintRunsChips() {
    var box = $("runsChips");
    var spend = $("runsSpend");
    var c = jobCounts();
    if (spend) spend.innerHTML = spendChips();
    if (!box) return;
    var h = '<button type="button" class="chip' + (state.runFilter === "ALL" ? " on" : "") +
      '" data-filter="ALL">all <b>' + c.total + "</b></button>";
    JOB_ORDER.forEach(function (s) {
      h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (state.runFilter === s ? " on" : "") +
        '" data-filter="' + s + '">' + s.toLowerCase() + " <b>" + (c[s] || 0) + "</b></button>";
    });
    // The stale pile is the one an operator acts on, so it is a filter and not
    // just a number: cosmos_sched reports stale and never retries.
    h += '<button type="button" class="chip stale-chip' + (state.runFilter === "STALE" ? " on" : "") +
      '" data-filter="STALE" title="RUNNING past 30m, or STALE reported on the ledger">stale <b>' +
      c.stale + "</b></button>";
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-filter]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        state.runFilter = b.getAttribute("data-filter") || "ALL";
        state.runShown = PAGE;
        paintRunsChips();
        paintRunsList();
      });
    });
    var sorts = $("runsSortChips");
    if (sorts) {
      var sh = "";
      [["new", "newest"], ["old", "oldest"], ["age", "waiting"], ["state", "state"]].forEach(function (p) {
        sh += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (state.runSort === p[0] ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-sort="' + p[0] + '">' + p[1] + "</button>";
      });
      sorts.innerHTML = sh;
      sorts.querySelectorAll("[data-sort]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          state.runSort = b.getAttribute("data-sort") || "new";
          paintRunsChips();
          paintRunsList();
        });
      });
    }
  }

  function paintRunsList() {
    var list = $("bd-runs-list");
    var detail = $("bd-runs-detail");
    if (list) {
      var keepScroll = list.scrollTop;
      var rows = shownRows();
      var c = jobCounts();
      var h = staleReadHtml();
      if (!state.jukebox && !jobsMap()) {
        h += '<div class="empty">GET /api/v1/jukebox unread — the run list is not empty, it is unread' +
          (state.err.jukebox ? "<br>" + esc(state.err.jukebox) : "") + "</div>";
      } else if (!rows.length) {
        h += '<div class="empty">' + (c.total === 0 ? "queue empty — Core returned zero jobs"
          : "no job matches " + esc(state.runFilter.toLowerCase()) +
            (state.runQuery ? ' + "' + esc(state.runQuery) + '"' : "")) + "</div>";
      } else {
        rows.slice(0, state.runShown).forEach(function (r) { h += rowHtml(r); });
        if (rows.length > state.runShown) {
          h += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-more="1">show ' +
            Math.min(PAGE, rows.length - state.runShown) + " more of " +
            (rows.length - state.runShown) + " held back</button>";
        } else {
          h += '<div class="dim tiny runs-tally">' + rows.length + " of " + c.total +
            " jobs listed</div>";
        }
      }
      list.innerHTML = h;
      list.scrollTop = keepScroll;
      list.querySelectorAll("[data-run]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () { selectRun(b.getAttribute("data-run")); });
      });
      var more = list.querySelector("[data-more]");
      if (more) {
        more.addEventListener("click", function () {
          state.runShown += PAGE;
          paintRunsList();
        });
      }
    }
    if (detail) {
      detail.innerHTML = state.runId
        ? runDetailHtml(state.runId)
        : '<div class="empty">pick a run — inspector is GET /jukebox fields, not a fabricated trace</div>';
      bindGo(detail);
    }
  }

  function selectRun(id) {
    state.runId = id || "";
    state.arm = "";
    // Keep the POST receipt when the operator follows it to the job it filed;
    // drop it the moment they look at anything else.
    if (!state.post || state.post.id !== state.runId) state.post = null;
    state.selected = { kind: "run", id: state.runId };
    paintRunsList();
    paintRunsChips();
    paintStudio();
    var on = $("bd-runs-list") && $("bd-runs-list").querySelector(".runrow.on");
    if (on && on.scrollIntoView) on.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
  }

  function stepRun(delta) {
    var rows = shownRows().slice(0, state.runShown);
    if (!rows.length) return;
    var at = -1;
    var i;
    for (i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      if (String(rows[i].job_id) === String(state.runId)) { at = i; break; }
    }
    var next = at < 0 ? (delta > 0 ? 0 : rows.length - 1) : at + delta;
    if (next < 0 || next >= rows.length) return;
    selectRun(rows[next].job_id);
  }

  function fmtOpsAge(s) {
    if (s == null || s === "") return "NO_SOURCE";
    var n = Number(s);
    if (!isFinite(n)) return "UNMEASURED";
    if (n < 90) return Math.round(n) + "s";
    if (n < 3600) return Math.round(n / 60) + "m";
    return Math.round(n / 3600) + "h";
  }

  function usdOps(n) {
    return typeof n === "number" && isFinite(n) ? "$" + n.toFixed(2) : "—";
  }

  function opsRow(k, v, cls) {
    return '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">' + esc(k) +
      '</span><span class="' + (cls || "dim") + '">' + esc(v) + "</span></div>";
  }

  function runOpPane(id, title, html, led) {
    var view = document.querySelector('.deck-view[data-tab="runs"]');
    if (!view) return false;
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    var fresh = false;
    if (!el) {
      el = document.createElement("section");
      el.id = id;
      el.className = "panel home-card run-op-pane";
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="panel-hd"><span class="run-op-led"></span><h2></h2>' +
        '<span class="age dim tiny"></span></div><div class="panel-bd"></div>';
      view.appendChild(el);
      fresh = true;
    }
    var h2 = el.querySelector("h2");
    if (h2) h2.textContent = title;
    var ledEl = el.querySelector(".run-op-led");
    if (ledEl) ledEl.className = "run-op-led " + (led || "");
    var bd = el.querySelector(".panel-bd");
    if (bd) bd.innerHTML = html;
    return fresh;
  }

  function paintRunsOps() {
    var rec = state.runsOps;
    var err = state.err.runs_ops;
    var age = $("runsAge");
    if (err && !rec) {
      runOpPane("run-op-unread", "OPS",
        '<div class="empty">' + esc(err) + ". Not an empty ops fold.</div>", "bad");
      if (window.layoutDeckView) window.layoutDeckView("runs");
      return;
    }
    if (!rec) {
      runOpPane("run-op-unread", "OPS",
        '<div class="empty">ops unread — GET /api/v1/runs_ops has not answered</div>', "");
      return;
    }
    var unread = document.getElementById("run-op-unread");
    if (unread && unread.parentNode) unread.parentNode.removeChild(unread);
    var fresh = false;
    var wd = rec.watchdog || {};
    fresh = runOpPane("run-op-watchdog", "Watchdog2",
      opsRow(wd.heartbeat || "watchdog2",
        (wd.kind || "NO_SOURCE") + (wd.age_s != null ? " · " + fmtOpsAge(wd.age_s) : ""),
        wd.kind === "OK" ? "ok" : "dim") +
      (wd.last_run ? opsRow("last_run", String(wd.last_run)) : "") +
      (wd.pid ? opsRow("pid", String(wd.pid)) : ""),
      wd.kind === "OK" ? "ok" : "warn") || fresh;

    (rec.clocks || []).forEach(function (c) {
      var hb = c.heartbeat || {};
      var cid = "run-op-clock-" + String(c.id || c.clock || "x").replace(/\s+/g, "-");
      fresh = runOpPane(cid, c.clock || c.task || ("clock " + c.id),
        opsRow("cadence", c.cadence || "UNMEASURED") +
        opsRow("heartbeat",
          (hb.kind || "NO_SOURCE") + (hb.age_s != null ? " · " + fmtOpsAge(hb.age_s) : ""),
          hb.kind === "OK" ? "ok" : "dim") +
        (hb.pid ? opsRow("pid", String(hb.pid)) : "") +
        (c.task ? opsRow("task", c.task) : ""),
        hb.kind === "OK" ? "ok" : "warn") || fresh;
    });

    var wo = rec.work_orders || {};
    var wh = "";
    if (wo.kind && wo.kind !== "OK") {
      wh = '<div class="empty">' + esc(wo.kind) + "</div>";
    } else {
      wh = opsRow("n_total", String(wo.n_total == null ? "UNMEASURED" : wo.n_total));
      Object.keys(wo.counts || {}).forEach(function (k) {
        wh += opsRow(k, String(wo.counts[k]));
      });
      (wo.recent || []).slice(0, 6).forEach(function (r) {
        wh += opsRow((r.id || "—") + " · " + (r.state || ""), r.product || r.task || "");
      });
    }
    fresh = runOpPane("run-op-orders", "Work orders", wh, wo.kind === "OK" ? "ok" : "warn") || fresh;

    var sh = "";
    (rec.streams || []).forEach(function (s) {
      sh += opsRow(
        (s.active ? "ACTIVE · " : "") + (s.profile || s.stream || "session"),
        s.location || "UNMEASURED");
    });
    if (!sh) sh = '<div class="empty">no CCr lease and recents unread</div>';
    fresh = runOpPane("run-op-streams", "Streams", sh, "") || fresh;

    var voice = rec.voice || {};
    fresh = runOpPane("run-op-voice", "SGH-Voice",
      opsRow("loop", voice.sgh_voice_loop ? "LOOPING" : "idle",
        voice.sgh_voice_loop ? "ops-loop ok" : "dim") +
      opsRow("cvm", (voice.cvm && voice.cvm.kind) || "NO_SOURCE"),
      voice.sgh_voice_loop ? "ok" : "") || fresh;

    var gitur = rec.gitur || {};
    var gh = "";
    if (gitur.kind === "BROKE") {
      gh = '<div class="empty">' + esc(gitur.detail || "BROKE") + "</div>";
    } else {
      var ccr = gitur.ccr || {};
      gh += opsRow("CCr", ccr.held ? ("held · " + (ccr.stream || "")) : "ABSENT");
      (gitur.legs || []).forEach(function (leg) {
        gh += opsRow(leg.name || leg.id || "leg",
          (leg.present ? "in registry" : "absent") + (leg.role ? " · " + leg.role : ""));
      });
      gh += opsRow("jobs", String(gitur.jobs_n != null ? gitur.jobs_n : "0"));
    }
    fresh = runOpPane("run-op-gitur", "Gitur", gh, gitur.kind === "BROKE" ? "bad" : "ok") || fresh;

    var spend = rec.spend || {};
    var sph = "";
    (spend.meters || []).forEach(function (m) {
      sph += opsRow((m.rail || "rail") + (m.free ? " · FREE" : ""),
        usdOps(m.settled_usd) + " / " + usdOps(m.cap_usd),
        m.free ? "ops-free" : "");
    });
    if (!sph) sph = '<div class="empty">' + esc(spend.kind || "NO_SOURCE") + " — tokens UNMEASURED</div>";
    fresh = runOpPane("run-op-spend", "Spend", sph, "") || fresh;

    if (err) {
      runOpPane("run-op-stale", "Last good",
        '<div class="read-stale">' + esc(err) + "</div>", "warn");
    }
    if (fresh && window.layoutDeckView) window.layoutDeckView("runs");
  }

  function paintRuns() {
    bindRunsTools();
    var age = $("runsAge");
    if (age) {
      age.textContent = state.err.jukebox
        ? state.err.jukebox
        : (state.jukebox
          ? ("GET /jukebox · " + (state.jukebox.measured_at_iso || "ok"))
          : (jobsMap() ? "GET /jobs fallback — no command, age or lane on that endpoint" : "unread"));
      age.className = state.err.jukebox ? "tiny bad" : "dim tiny";
    }
    paintRunsOps();
    paintRunsChips();
    paintRunsList();
  }

  function bindRunsTools() {
    if (bindRunsTools._on) return;
    var list = $("bd-runs-list");
    if (!list) return;
    bindRunsTools._on = true;
    list.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
      if (e.key === "ArrowDown") { e.preventDefault(); stepRun(1); }
      else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") { e.preventDefault(); stepRun(-1); }
    });
  }

  function bindReviewWin() {
    if (bindReviewWin._on) return;
    var box = $("reviewWin");
    if (!box) return;
    bindReviewWin._on = true;
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-win]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        state.reviewWindow = b.getAttribute("data-win") || "week";
        box.querySelectorAll("[data-win]").forEach(function (x) {
          x.classList.toggle("on", x === b);
        });
        if (!state.api) return;
        fetchInto("/api/v1/review?window=" + encodeURIComponent(state.reviewWindow),
          "review", function (rec) { state.review = rec; });
      });
    });
  }

  function paintReviewBox(id, html) {
    var el = $(id);
    if (el) el.innerHTML = html;
  }

  function paintReview() {
    bindReviewWin();
    var age = $("reviewAge");
    if (age) {
      age.textContent = state.err.review
        ? state.err.review
        : (state.review
          ? ("GET /api/v1/review · " + (state.review.window || "week"))
          : "unread");
      age.className = state.err.review ? "tiny bad" : "dim tiny";
    }
    if (!$("reviewSpend")) return;
    if (!state.review && !state.err.review) {
      paintReviewBox("reviewSpend", '<div class="empty">unread — GET /api/v1/review has not answered</div>');
      return;
    }
    if (state.err.review && !state.review) {
      var bad = '<div class="empty">' + esc(state.err.review) + ". Not an empty inbox.</div>";
      paintReviewBox("reviewSpend", bad);
      paintReviewBox("reviewBlock", bad);
      paintReviewBox("reviewLogin", bad);
      paintReviewBox("reviewCatalog", bad);
      return;
    }
    var rec = state.review || {};
    var sp = rec.spend_approvals || {};
    var sh = "<p class='dim tiny'>" + esc(sp.note ||
      "POST /spend is fail-closed: a widen is 409 WIDEN_REQUIRES_CONFIRM until allow_widen is true.") + "</p>";
    (sp.pending || []).forEach(function (p) {
      sh += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">' + esc(p.kind || "SPEND_CAP_REFUSED") +
        '</span><span>' + esc(p.detail || p.iso || "") + "</span></div>";
    });
    if (!(sp.pending || []).length) {
      sh += '<div class="empty">no refused widens on the ledger tail — not a silent pending pile</div>';
    }
    (sp.meters || []).slice(0, 8).forEach(function (m) {
      sh += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">' + esc(m.rail || "rail") +
        "</span><span>$" + esc(m.settled_usd) + " / $" + esc(m.cap_usd) + "</span></div>";
    });
    paintReviewBox("reviewSpend", sh);

    var bl = rec.blockers || {};
    var bh = "<p class='dim tiny'>" + esc(bl.note ||
      "FINDINGS = awaiting CCr (n8n wait analog: the LLM is not polling). Resume surface is CCr --accept or a new GitHub work_orders/drop/ — this job is not re-run. Core has no annotation-queue POST. Stale RUNNING: cosmos_sched is report-never-retry. POST /crucible needs sources[] — 501 CRUCIBLE_NOT_RUNNABLE without critics.") + "</p>";
    (bl.rows || []).forEach(function (r) {
      bh += '<div class="ops-row">' +
        (r.id && r.kind === "job"
          ? '<button type="button" class="runrow" data-run="' + esc(r.id) + '">'
          : "<span>") +
        esc((r.why || r.state || r.kind || "") + (r.id ? " · " + r.id : "")) +
        (r.id && r.kind === "job" ? "</button>" : "</span>") + "</div>";
    });
    if (!(bl.rows || []).length) {
      bh += '<div class="empty">no blocking FINDINGS / HITL / work-order piles</div>';
    }
    bh += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-filter-runs="FINDINGS">open FINDINGS in Runs</button>';
    paintReviewBox("reviewBlock", bh);

    var lg = rec.logins || {};
    var lh = "<p class='dim tiny'>" + esc(lg.note ||
      "Required logins are Keith's click. Core never automates AUTH.") + "</p>";
    (lg.needed || []).forEach(function (n) {
      lh += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">' +
        esc((n.label || n.id || "key") + " · " + (n.led || "NO_SOURCE")) +
        "</span><span>" + esc(n.why || "") +
        (n.docs ? (" · " + n.docs) : "") + "</span></div>";
    });
    (lg.rails || []).forEach(function (r) {
      lh += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">' + esc(r.link_id || r.route || "rail") +
        '</span><span>' + esc(r.why || "") + "</span></div>";
    });
    (lg.events || []).forEach(function (e) {
      lh += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">AUTH_REQUIRED</span><span>' +
        esc(e.link_id || e.iso || "") + "</span></div>";
    });
    if (!(lg.needed || []).length && !(lg.rails || []).length && !(lg.events || []).length) {
      lh += '<div class="empty">no unverified rails, no AUTH_REQUIRED events, no missing keys</div>';
    }
    paintReviewBox("reviewLogin", lh);

    var cat = rec.catalog || {};
    var ch = "<p class='dim tiny'>window " + esc(rec.window || "week") +
      " · " + esc(String(cat.n != null ? cat.n : 0)) + " rows. COSMOS does not copy Legal files.</p>";
    (cat.rows || []).forEach(function (r) {
      ch += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">' +
        esc((r.kind || "item") + " · " + (r.title || r.product || r.id || "—")) +
        '</span><span>' + esc(r.iso || r.stream || "") + "</span></div>";
      if (r.summary) {
        ch += '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(String(r.summary).slice(0, 600)) + "</p>";
      }
      (r.products || []).forEach(function (p) {
        ch += '<div class="dim tiny">' + esc(p.name || p.path || "") +
          (p.exists ? " · " + esc(String(p.bytes || "")) + " B" : " · missing") + "</div>";
      });
    });
    if (!(cat.rows || []).length) {
      ch += '<div class="empty">no work products in this window — not an empty archive</div>';
    }
    paintReviewBox("reviewCatalog", ch);

    var block = $("reviewBlock");
    if (block) {
      block.querySelectorAll("[data-run]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("runs");
          selectRun(b.getAttribute("data-run"));
        });
      });
      block.querySelectorAll("[data-filter-runs]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          state.runFilter = b.getAttribute("data-filter-runs") || "ALL";
          state.runShown = PAGE;
          if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("runs");
          paintRuns();
        });
      });
    }
  }

  function paintAll() {
    // The old signature was counts-only, so a queue whose totals happened to
    // hold still never repainted: a job that swapped state with another, a
    // command arriving on a row, and every age on screen all froze. Key on the
    // rows themselves, and on a 30s bucket so the ages keep moving.
    var sig = JSON.stringify({
      rows: allRows().map(function (r) {
        return [r.job_id, r.st, staleRow(r) ? 1 : 0, r.command ? 1 : 0].join("|");
      }),
      sel: state.selected,
      run: state.runId,
      f: state.runFilter,
      q: state.runQuery,
      s: state.runSort,
      shown: state.runShown,
      arm: state.arm,
      post: state.post,
      err: state.err,
      n: state.seats.length,
      nmod: (state.models || []).length,
      studio: state.studio && (state.studio.updated_at || state.studio.kind),
      say: state.studioSay,
      sp: state.spend && state.spend.measured_at_epoch,
      ops: state.runsOps && state.runsOps.measured_at,
      rv: state.review && state.review.measured_at,
      rw: state.reviewWindow,
      age: Math.floor(Date.now() / 30000)
    });
    if (sig === state._sig) return;
    state._sig = sig;
    paintStudio();
    paintRuns();
    paintReview();
  }

  function estimateSeats() {
    if (!state.api) return;
    state.seats.forEach(function (s) {
      if (!s.model || state.estimates[s.model]) return;
      state.api("/api/v1/model_rater/estimate", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { model: s.model, tokens_in: 4000, tokens_out: 800 }
      }).then(function (rec) {
        state.estimates[s.model] = rec.free
          ? s.model + " · $0 (free card) · 4k/800"
          : s.model + " · ~$" + rec.usd + " · 4k/800 tok";
        state._sig = "";
        paintAll();
      }).catch(function () {});
    });
  }

  /* A read that failed is not an empty queue. Every catch here used to be
     empty, so a Core that stopped answering left the last good list on screen
     with a timestamp that never moved. Keep the reason and show it. */
  function fetchInto(path, key, apply) {
    state.api(path).then(function (rec) {
      delete state.err[key];
      apply(rec);
      paintAll();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.err[key] = "GET " + path.split("?")[0] + " — " + String((e && e.message) || e);
      paintAll();
    });
  }

  function tabOn() {
    var vis = window.deckTabVisible;
    if (typeof vis !== "function") return false;
    return vis("studio") || vis("runs") || vis("review");
  }
  function tick() {
    if (!state.api) return;
    if (!tabOn()) return;
    fetchInto("/api/v1/jukebox", "jukebox", function (rec) { state.jukebox = rec; });
    fetchInto("/api/v1/spend", "spend", function (rec) { state.spend = rec; });
    fetchInto("/api/v1/runs_ops", "runs_ops", function (rec) { state.runsOps = rec; });
    fetchInto("/api/v1/review?window=" + encodeURIComponent(state.reviewWindow || "week"),
      "review", function (rec) { state.review = rec; });
    fetchInto("/api/v1/studio", "studio", function (rec) { ingestStudio(rec); });
    fetchInto("/api/v1/rails", "rails", function (rec) {
      state.rails = (rec && (rec.matrix || rec.rails)) || [];
    });
    fetchInto("/api/v1/model_rater?sort=quality&limit=250", "seats", function (rec) {
      state.seats = rec.seats || [];
      if (rec.models) state.models = rec.models;
      if (rec.via_options && rec.via_options.length) state.viaOptions = rec.via_options;
      estimateSeats();
    });
  }

  /* The last GET /api/v1/jukebox this pane read, for panes that only have
     GET /jobs ({id: state}, no command, no age, no lane). Returns [] when the
     queue is unread — a caller must not read that as an empty queue. */
  window.deckJukeboxRows = function () {
    return jukeRows();
  };

  /* Runs owns the /jukebox record, so any other pane that names a job opens it
     HERE instead of growing a second inspector of its own. */
  window.deckOpenRun = function (id) {
    if (!id) return;
    if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("runs");
    state.runFilter = "ALL";
    state.runQuery = "";
    var q = $("runsQ");
    if (q) q.value = "";
    paintRunsChips();
    selectRun(id);
  };

  window.paintDeckStudio = function (patch) {
    if (patch && patch.jobs) state.jobs = patch.jobs;
    if (patch && patch.surfaces) state.surfaces = patch.surfaces;
    if (patch && patch.seats) state.seats = patch.seats;
    paintAll();
  };

  window.initDeckStudio = function (api) {
    state.api = api || null;
    bindRunsTools();
    bindReviewWin();
    bindStudioForms();
    paintAll();
    tick();
    if (window._deckStudioTimer) clearInterval(window._deckStudioTimer);
    window._deckStudioTimer = setInterval(tick, POLL_MS);
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_orders.js =====
/* Work-order extra pane.
   GET /api/v1/work_orders — timestamped live list: agent, product, checks.
   GET ?id= returns output_head. Does not POST --accept. Header / MESH untouched. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var POLL_MS = 10000;
  var PAGE = 80;
  var STATES = ["ALL", "PICKED_UP", "DONE", "COMPLETED", "FAILED", "DROPPED"];
  var state = {
    api: null, rec: null, err: "", timer: null, inflight: false,
    q: "", filter: "ALL", selected: "", detail: null, detailErr: "",
    shown: PAGE
  };

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  function stCls(st) {
    var s = String(st || "").toUpperCase();
    if (s === "FAILED" || s === "BROKE") return "bad";
    if (s === "PICKED_UP" || s === "DROPPED") return "warn";
    if (s === "DONE" || s === "COMPLETED") return "ok";
    return "dim";
  }
  function when(row) {
    return row.picked_at || row.filed_at || row.dropped_at || row.timestamp || "";
  }

  function filtered() {
    var rows = (state.rec && state.rec.rows) || [];
    var q = String(state.q || "").toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return rows;
    return rows.filter(function (r) {
      var blob = [r.order_id, r.agent, r.task, r.output, r.state, r.product]
        .join(" ").toLowerCase();
      return blob.indexOf(q) >= 0;
    });
  }

  function paintList() {
    var box = $("bd-orders-list");
    if (!box) return;
    if (state.err && !state.rec) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">' + esc(state.err) + "</div>";
      return;
    }
    if (!state.rec) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">orders unread — GET /api/v1/work_orders</div>';
      return;
    }
    var rows = filtered();
    if (!rows.length) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">' +
        (state.rec.kind === "NO_SOURCE"
          ? "NO_SOURCE — state/work_orders has not been written. Not an empty list."
          : "no rows match this filter — Core census is in the chips") +
        "</div>";
      return;
    }
    var h = "";
    rows.slice(0, state.shown).forEach(function (r) {
      var on = r.order_id === state.selected ? " on" : "";
      h += '<button type="button" class="jobrow' + on + '" data-oid="' +
        esc(r.order_id) + '">' +
        '<span class="tiny ' + stCls(r.state) + '">' + esc(r.state) + "</span>" +
        "<b>" + esc(r.order_id) + "</b>" +
        '<span class="dim tiny">' + esc(when(r)) + "</span>" +
        '<span class="dim">' + esc(r.agent || "agent UNMEASURED") + "</span>" +
        '<span class="dim tiny">' + esc(r.product || r.output || "no product yet") +
        "</span></button>";
    });
    if (rows.length > state.shown) {
      h += '<div class="dim tiny">holding back ' + (rows.length - state.shown) +
        " of " + rows.length + " — Core n_total=" + esc(state.rec.n_total) +
        (state.rec.truncated ? " truncated" : "") + "</div>";
    }
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-oid]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        openOrder(b.getAttribute("data-oid"));
      });
    });
  }

  function paintChips() {
    var box = $("ordersChips");
    if (!box) return;
    var c = (state.rec && state.rec.counts) || {};
    var h = '<button type="button" class="chip' +
      (state.filter === "ALL" ? " on" : "") + '" data-st="ALL">all</button>';
    [["picked", "PICKED_UP"], ["assigned", "DONE"], ["completed", "COMPLETED"],
     ["failed", "FAILED"], ["bucket", "DROPPED"]].forEach(function (pair) {
      var n = c[pair[0]] || 0;
      h += '<button type="button" class="chip' +
        (state.filter === pair[1] ? " on" : "") +
        '" data-st="' + pair[1] + '">' + pair[0] + " " + n + "</button>";
    });
    box.innerHTML = h;
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-st]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var st = b.getAttribute("data-st") || "ALL";
        state.filter = (state.filter === st && st !== "ALL") ? "ALL" : st;
        tick();
      });
    });
  }

  function paintDetail() {
    var box = $("bd-orders-detail");
    if (!box) return;
    if (!state.selected) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">pick an order — inspector is GET ?id=, not a fabricated report</div>';
      return;
    }
    if (state.detailErr && !state.detail) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">' + esc(state.detailErr) + "</div>";
      return;
    }
    var rec = state.detail || {};
    var o = rec.order;
    if (!o) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">' +
        esc(rec.kind || "NOT_FOUND") + " — " + esc(state.selected) + "</div>";
      return;
    }
    var h = "<h3>" + esc(o.order_id) + "</h3>";
    h += '<p class="tiny ' + stCls(o.state) + '">' + esc(o.state) +
      " · " + esc(o.folder) + "</p>";
    h += '<dl class="kv">';
    h += "<dt>when</dt><dd>" + esc(when(o)) + "</dd>";
    h += "<dt>agent</dt><dd>" + esc(o.agent) + "</dd>";
    h += "<dt>output</dt><dd>" + esc(o.output) +
      (o.output_exists ? ' <span class="ok">exists</span>' : ' <span class="warn">missing</span>') +
      "</dd>";
    if (o.observed_rc != null) h += "<dt>rc</dt><dd>" + esc(o.observed_rc) + "</dd>";
    if (o.elapsed_s != null) h += "<dt>elapsed</dt><dd>" + esc(o.elapsed_s) + "s</dd>";
    if (o.fail_kind) h += "<dt>fail</dt><dd class='bad'>" + esc(o.fail_kind) +
      " — " + esc(o.fail_detail || "") + "</dd>";
    var cp = o.core_picked;
    if (cp) {
      h += "<dt>picked ledger</dt><dd>" +
        (cp.ok ? ("seq " + esc(cp.seq) + (cp.already ? " already" : ""))
          : esc(cp.kind || "CORE_NOT_COMPOSED")) + "</dd>";
    }
    var ch = o.checks || {};
    ["github", "cursor", "gitlab"].forEach(function (n) {
      var row = ch[n];
      h += "<dt>" + n + "</dt><dd>" +
        (row ? (esc(row.status) + (row.url ? " · " + esc(row.url) : ""))
          : "UNMEASURED") + "</dd>";
    });
    if (o.source_url) h += "<dt>drop</dt><dd>" + esc(o.source_url) + "</dd>";
    h += "</dl>";
    h += "<h3>task</h3><pre class='cmdpreview'>" + esc(o.task || "") + "</pre>";
    h += "<h3>work product</h3>";
    if (rec.output_head) {
      h += "<pre class='sess-open'>" + esc(rec.output_head) + "</pre>";
    } else {
      h += '<div class="empty">' +
        esc(rec.output_head_error || "no Output file — DONE is Output exists") +
        "</div>";
    }
    box.innerHTML = h;
  }

  function paintAge() {
    var el = $("ordersAge");
    if (!el) return;
    if (state.err) {
      el.textContent = state.err;
      el.className = "tiny bad";
      return;
    }
    var rec = state.rec || {};
    el.textContent = "GET /api/v1/work_orders · n=" +
      (rec.n_total != null ? rec.n_total : "—") +
      " shown " + (rec.n_shown != null ? rec.n_shown : "—");
    el.className = "dim tiny";
  }

  function paint() {
    paintAge();
    paintChips();
    paintList();
    paintDetail();
  }

  function openOrder(oid) {
    state.selected = oid;
    state.detail = null;
    state.detailErr = "";
    paint();
    if (typeof state.api !== "function" || !oid) return;
    state.api("/api/v1/work_orders?id=" + encodeURIComponent(oid)).then(function (rec) {
      state.detail = rec;
      state.detailErr = "";
      paint();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.detailErr = String(e && e.message || e);
      paint();
    });
  }

  function tabOn() {
    return typeof window.deckTabVisible === "function" && window.deckTabVisible("orders");
  }
  function tick() {
    if (!tabOn()) return;
    if (typeof state.api !== "function") {
      state.err = "no api transport — extra pane was initialised without one";
      paint();
      return;
    }
    if (state.inflight) return;
    var path = "/api/v1/work_orders";
    if (state.filter && state.filter !== "ALL") path += "?state=" + encodeURIComponent(state.filter);
    state.inflight = true;
    state.api(path).then(function (rec) {
      state.rec = rec;
      state.err = "";
      paint();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.err = String(e && e.message || e);
      paint();
    }).then(function () { state.inflight = false; });
  }

  function bind() {
    var list = $("bd-orders-list");
    if (list && !list._keys) {
      list._keys = 1;
      list.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
        if (e.key !== "ArrowDown" && e.key !== "ArrowUp") return;
        var rows = filtered();
        if (!rows.length) return;
        var ids = rows.map(function (r) { return r.order_id; });
        var i = ids.indexOf(state.selected);
        if (e.key === "ArrowDown") i = Math.min(ids.length - 1, i + 1);
        else i = Math.max(0, i < 0 ? 0 : i - 1);
        e.preventDefault();
        openOrder(ids[i]);
      });
    }
  }

  window.initDeckOrders = function (api) {
    state.api = api;
    bind();
    tick();
    if (state.timer) clearInterval(state.timer);
    state.timer = setInterval(tick, POLL_MS);
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_gitur.js =====
/* Gitur extra pane: GitHub + GitLab + Cursor.
   GET /api/v1/gitur only while the Gitur tab is visible — rails + Cursor
   probe/launch + jukebox names. Log filter is chips-not-hunt (stage / leg /
   state) and prints kept and dropped counts. Three panes (GitHub, GitLab,
   Cursor), Forge→Gitur→Implement flow, SGH Voice drops, timestamped
   submitted/completed log. Does not invent PR lists or poll vendor APIs.
   Cursor GET /v1/repositories is 1/min — not called here. Header / JACK'S
   MESH untouched. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var POLL_MS = 10000;
  var STAGE_ORDER = ["forge", "gitur", "implement", "sgh"];
  var LEG_ORDER = ["github", "gitlab", "cursor"];
  var ST_ORDER = ["QUEUED", "RUNNING", "FINDINGS", "CLEAN", "BROKE",
                  "DONE", "COMPLETED", "FAILED"];
  var state = {
    api: null, rec: null, err: "", timer: null,
    filter: { kind: "ALL" }, inflight: false
  };

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  function age(s) {
    if (s == null || s === "") return "UNMEASURED";
    var n = Number(s);
    if (!isFinite(n)) return "UNMEASURED";
    if (n < 90) return Math.round(n) + "s";
    if (n < 3600) return Math.round(n / 60) + "m";
    return Math.round(n / 3600) + "h";
  }
  function verLabel(v) {
    if (v === true) return { t: "verified", c: "ok" };
    if (v === false) return { t: "FAIL", c: "bad" };
    return { t: "UNKNOWN — not probed", c: "warn" };
  }

  function whenOf(j) {
    return j && (j.finished || j.submitted_iso || j.submitted) || "UNMEASURED";
  }

  function prRows(list, empty) {
    var rows = list || [];
    if (!rows.length) {
      return '<div class="empty">' + esc(empty || "no open PRs — measured zero, not invented") + "</div>";
    }
    var h = '<div class="gitur-jobs">';
    rows.forEach(function (p) {
      h += '<a class="jobrow sess" href="' + esc(p.url || "#") +
        '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class="jid">#' +
        esc(p.number) + "</span>" +
        '<span class="jst">' + esc(p.st || (p.draft ? "DRAFT" : "OPEN")) + "</span>" +
        (p.parked ? '<span class="tiny warn">parked</span>' : "") +
        '<span class="dim tiny">' + esc(p.repo || "") + "</span>" +
        "<b>" + esc(p.title || "") + "</b></a>";
    });
    return h + "</div>";
  }

  function runRows(list) {
    var rows = list || [];
    if (!rows.length) return "";
    var h = "<h3>Actions (CI)</h3><div class=\"gitur-jobs\">";
    rows.forEach(function (r) {
      h += '<a class="jobrow sess" href="' + esc(r.url || "#") +
        '" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class="jst">' +
        esc(r.st) + "</span>" +
        '<span class="dim tiny">' + esc(r.branch || "") + "</span>" +
        "<b>" + esc(r.name || "build") + "</b>" +
        '<span class="dim tiny">' + esc(r.repo || "") + "</span></a>";
    });
    return h + "</div>";
  }

  function jobRows(list) {
    var jobs = list || [];
    if (!jobs.length) {
      return "";
    }
    var h = '<div class="gitur-jobs">';
    jobs.forEach(function (j) {
      h += '<button type="button" class="jobrow sess" data-gitur-run="' +
        esc(j.job_id) + '"><span class="jid">' + esc(j.job_id) + "</span>" +
        '<span class="jst">' + esc(j.st) + "</span>" +
        '<span class="dim tiny">' + esc(whenOf(j)) + "</span>" +
        '<span class="dim tiny">' + esc(j.command) + "</span></button>";
    });
    return h + "</div>";
  }

  function paintLeg(id, pane, extra) {
    var box = $("bd-gitur-" + id);
    if (!box) return;
    if (!pane) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">unread — GET /api/v1/gitur</div>';
      return;
    }
    var leg = pane.leg || {};
    var v = verLabel(leg.present ? leg.verified : null);
    var h = '<div class="dim tiny">' + esc(pane.role || leg.role || "") + "</div>";
    h += '<dl class="kv"><dt>link</dt><dd>' + esc(pane.id || leg.id) + "</dd>" +
      "<dt>in registry</dt><dd>" + (leg.present ? "yes" : "no") + "</dd>" +
      '<dt>probe</dt><dd class="' + v.c + '">' + v.t +
      (leg.age_s != null ? " · age " + age(leg.age_s) : "") + "</dd>" +
      (leg.route ? "<dt>route</dt><dd>" + esc(leg.route) + "</dd>" : "") +
      "<dt>jobs</dt><dd>" + esc(String(pane.n != null ? pane.n : 0)) + "</dd>" +
      (leg.bound ? "<dt>live</dt><dd>" + esc(leg.bound) + "</dd>" : "") +
      "</dl>";
    var live = pane.live || {};
    if (live.kind && live.kind !== "OK") {
      h += '<div class="dim tiny">live ' + esc(live.kind) +
        (live.detail ? " · " + esc(live.detail) : "") + "</div>";
    }
    if (extra) h += extra;
    if (pane.prs) {
      h += "<h3>Open PRs</h3>" + prRows(pane.prs);
    }
    if (pane.runs && pane.runs.length) h += runRows(pane.runs);
    if (pane.mrs && pane.mrs.length) {
      h += "<h3>GitLab MRs</h3>" + prRows(pane.mrs, "no open MRs — measured zero");
    }
    h += jobRows(pane.jobs);
    box.innerHTML = h;
  }

  function paintCursorExtra(pane) {
    var h = "";
    var live = pane && pane.live;
    h += "<h3>Cursor live GET /v1/me</h3>";
    if (!live) {
      h += '<p class="dim tiny">live unread</p>';
    } else {
      h += '<dl class="kv"><dt>kind</dt><dd class="' +
        (live.ok ? "ok" : "bad") + '">' + esc(live.kind || "") + "</dd>" +
        "<dt>http</dt><dd>" + esc(String(live.http != null ? live.http : "UNMEASURED")) + "</dd>" +
        "<dt>apiKeyName</dt><dd>" + esc(live.apiKeyName || "UNMEASURED") + "</dd>" +
        "<dt>age</dt><dd>" + age(live.age_s) + "</dd></dl>";
      if (live.note) h += '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(live.note) + "</p>";
    }
    var cur = pane && pane.probe;
    h += "<h3>Cursor probe file (on disk)</h3>";
    if (!cur) {
      h += '<p class="dim tiny">no cursor_rail_probe.json — UNMEASURED, not a fake PASS</p>';
    } else if (cur.kind === "BROKE") {
      h += '<p class="bad">probe file BROKE</p>';
    } else {
      h += '<dl class="kv"><dt>gate</dt><dd>' + esc(cur.gate) + "</dd>" +
        "<dt>gated_at</dt><dd>" + esc(cur.gated_at) + "</dd>" +
        "<dt>identity</dt><dd>" + esc(String(cur.identity_ok)) + "</dd>" +
        "<dt>key</dt><dd>" + esc(cur.key_last4 || (cur.key_ok ? "ok" : "missing")) + "</dd>" +
        "<dt>kernel attach</dt><dd>" + esc(String(cur.kernel_attached)) +
        (cur.attach_refused ? " · isolated --gate REFUSED authority (designed)" : "") + "</dd>" +
        (cur.http != null ? "<dt>http</dt><dd>" + esc(String(cur.http)) + "</dd>" : "") +
        "</dl>";
    }
    var ln = pane && pane.launch;
    h += "<h3>Last launch record</h3>";
    if (!ln) {
      h += '<p class="dim tiny">no cursor_rail_launch.json — no launch this Core, not a hidden agent</p>';
    } else {
      h += '<dl class="kv">';
      Object.keys(ln).forEach(function (k) {
        if (k === "kind" && ln.kind === "BROKE") return;
        h += "<dt>" + esc(k) + "</dt><dd>" + esc(ln[k]) + "</dd>";
      });
      h += "</dl>";
    }
    return h;
  }

  function paintFlow(rec) {
    var box = $("bd-gitur-flow");
    if (!box) return;
    var steps = rec && rec.flow;
    if (!steps || !steps.length) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">flow unread — Forge → Gitur → Implement</div>';
      return;
    }
    var h = '<p class="dim tiny">Jobs move Forge (MOTIF BUILD) into Gitur (GitHub/GitLab/Cursor), then Implement — CCr writes the live tree. Not a fourth writer.</p>';
    h += '<div class="gitur-flow-row">';
    steps.forEach(function (step, i) {
      if (i) h += '<div class="gitur-flow-arrow" aria-hidden="true">→</div>';
      h += '<div class="gitur-flow-step"><b>' + esc(step.label) + "</b>" +
        '<div class="dim tiny">' + esc(step.role) + "</div>" +
        '<div>n ' + esc(String(step.n != null ? step.n : 0)) + "</div>";
      if (step.ccr) {
        h += '<div class="dim tiny">CCr ' +
          (step.ccr.held
            ? ("held · " + esc(step.ccr.stream || "") + " · " +
              esc(String(step.ccr.sid || "").slice(0, 8)))
            : "ABSENT — not CCr") + "</div>";
      }
      h += jobRows(step.jobs) + "</div>";
    });
    h += "</div>";
    box.innerHTML = h;
  }

  function paintSgh(rec) {
    var box = $("bd-gitur-sgh");
    if (!box) return;
    var s = rec && rec.sgh;
    if (!s) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">SGH unread — GitHub work_orders/drop</div>';
      return;
    }
    var h = '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(s.note || "") + "</p>";
    h += '<dl class="kv"><dt>kind</dt><dd>' + esc(s.kind) + "</dd>" +
      "<dt>repo</dt><dd>" + esc(s.repo) + "</dd>" +
      "<dt>path</dt><dd>" + esc(s.path) + " @ " + esc(s.branch) + "</dd>" +
      "<dt>last_run</dt><dd>" + esc(s.last_run || "UNMEASURED") +
      (s.age_s != null ? " · age " + age(s.age_s) : "") + "</dd>" +
      "<dt>filed this tick</dt><dd>" + esc(String(s.filed_this_tick != null ? s.filed_this_tick : "UNMEASURED")) + "</dd>" +
      "<dt>seen</dt><dd>" + esc(s.seen_kind) +
      (s.seen_n != null ? " · n " + s.seen_n : "") + "</dd>" +
      (s.github_via ? "<dt>via</dt><dd>" + esc(s.github_via) + "</dd>" : "") +
      "</dl>";
    var sghJobs = ((rec.log) || []).filter(function (j) { return j.stage === "sgh"; });
    h += jobRows(sghJobs);
    box.innerHTML = h;
  }

  function logMatch(j) {
    var f = state.filter;
    if (!f || f.kind === "ALL") return true;
    if (f.kind === "stage") return String(j.stage || "") === f.id;
    if (f.kind === "leg") return String(j.leg || "") === f.id;
    if (f.kind === "st") {
      return String(j.st || "").toUpperCase() === String(f.id || "").toUpperCase();
    }
    return true;
  }

  function filterLabel(f) {
    if (!f || f.kind === "ALL") return "all";
    return String(f.kind || "") + " " + String(f.id || "");
  }

  function orderedKeys(counts, order) {
    var seen = {};
    var out = [];
    (order || []).forEach(function (k) {
      if (counts[k]) { out.push(k); seen[k] = 1; }
    });
    Object.keys(counts).forEach(function (k) {
      if (!seen[k] && counts[k]) out.push(k);
    });
    return out;
  }

  function chipHtml(kind, id, label, n, on) {
    return '<button type="button" class="chip' + (on ? " on" : "") +
      '" data-giturf="' + esc(kind) + ":" + esc(id) + '">' +
      esc(label) + " <b>" + n + "</b></button>";
  }

  function paintLog(rec) {
    var box = $("bd-gitur-log");
    if (!box) return;
    var rows = (rec && rec.log) || [];
    var shown = rows.filter(logMatch);
    var f = state.filter || { kind: "ALL" };
    var stages = {}, legs = {}, sts = {};
    rows.forEach(function (j) {
      if (j.stage) stages[j.stage] = (stages[j.stage] || 0) + 1;
      if (j.leg) legs[j.leg] = (legs[j.leg] || 0) + 1;
      var st = String(j.st || "").toUpperCase();
      if (st) sts[st] = (sts[st] || 0) + 1;
    });
    var h = '<p class="dim tiny">kind ' + esc((rec && rec.jobs_kind) || "") +
      " · submitted " + esc(String((rec && rec.submitted_n) != null ? rec.submitted_n : "UNMEASURED")) +
      " · completed " + esc(String((rec && rec.completed_n) != null ? rec.completed_n : "UNMEASURED")) +
      " · log n " + esc(String((rec && rec.log_n) != null ? rec.log_n : rows.length)) +
      " — disk fold, not a GitHub PR API</p>";
    // chips-not-hunt: census chips from the fold, never a search box.
    h += '<div class="chips gitur-tools" id="giturChips">';
    h += chipHtml("ALL", "ALL", "all", rows.length, f.kind === "ALL");
    orderedKeys(stages, STAGE_ORDER).forEach(function (k) {
      h += chipHtml("stage", k, k, stages[k], f.kind === "stage" && f.id === k);
    });
    orderedKeys(legs, LEG_ORDER).forEach(function (k) {
      h += chipHtml("leg", k, k, legs[k], f.kind === "leg" && f.id === k);
    });
    orderedKeys(sts, ST_ORDER).forEach(function (k) {
      h += chipHtml("st", k, k.toLowerCase(), sts[k],
        f.kind === "st" && String(f.id).toUpperCase() === k);
    });
    h += "</div>";
    var census = '<div class="dim tiny">kept ' + shown.length + " · dropped " +
      (rows.length - shown.length) + " · click a row to open it in Runs</div>";
    if (!rows.length) {
      h += '<div class="empty">no submitted/completed Gitur jobs on disk</div>';
    } else if (!shown.length) {
      h += '<div class="empty">no gitur log row matches ' + esc(filterLabel(f)) + "</div>";
      h += census;
    } else {
      h += "<table><thead><tr><th>when</th><th>stage</th><th>leg</th><th>id</th><th>st</th><th>command</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
      shown.forEach(function (j) {
        h += '<tr data-gitur-run="' + esc(j.job_id) + '"><td>' + esc(whenOf(j)) +
          "</td><td>" + esc(j.stage || "—") + "</td><td>" + esc(j.leg || "—") +
          "</td><td>" + esc(j.job_id) + "</td><td>" + esc(j.st) +
          "</td><td>" + esc(j.command) + "</td></tr>";
      });
      h += "</tbody></table>";
      h += census;
    }
    box.innerHTML = h;
  }

  function paint() {
    var rec = state.rec;
    var ageEl = $("giturAge");
    if (ageEl) {
      ageEl.textContent = state.err
        ? state.err
        : (rec
          ? ("GET /api/v1/gitur · " + (rec.gitur || "GitHub + GitLab + Cursor") +
            (rec.log_n != null ? " · log " + rec.log_n : ""))
          : "GET /api/v1/gitur · GitHub + GitLab + Cursor");
      ageEl.className = state.err ? "tiny bad" : "dim tiny";
    }
    var stale = $("giturStale");
    if (stale) {
      if (state.err && rec) {
        stale.textContent = "last good read shown · " + state.err;
        stale.className = "read-stale";
      } else {
        stale.textContent = "";
        stale.className = "read-stale hidden";
      }
    }
    var panes = (rec && rec.panes) || {};
    paintLeg("github", panes.github);
    paintLeg("gitlab", panes.gitlab);
    paintLeg("cursor", panes.cursor, rec ? paintCursorExtra(panes.cursor || { probe: rec.cursor, launch: rec.launch }) : "");
    paintFlow(rec);
    paintSgh(rec);
    paintLog(rec);
    var keys = $("bd-gitur-keys");
    if (keys) {
      keys.innerHTML = rec ? paintCreds(rec.creds) : "";
      bindTools(keys);
    }
    var logBox = $("bd-gitur-log");
    if (logBox) bindTools(logBox);
    ["github", "gitlab", "cursor", "flow", "sgh"].forEach(function (id) {
      var el = $("bd-gitur-" + id) || $("panel-gitur-" + id);
      if (el) bindTools(el);
    });
  }

  function ledCls(led) {
    if (led === "LIVE") return "ok";
    if (led === "PRESENT") return "warn";
    return "dim";
  }

  function paintCreds(creds) {
    var h = '<div class="gitur-creds" id="giturCreds">';
    h += "<h3>Keys · Gitur integrations</h3>";
    if (!creds || creds.kind === "BROKE") {
      h += '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc((creds && creds.detail) || "creds unread") + "</p>";
      return h + "</div>";
    }
    var book = creds.cursor_cookbook || {};
    var usageBook = creds.usage_cookbook || {};
    h += '<p class="dim tiny">OpenRouter → Cursor cookbook: <a href="' +
      esc(book.url || "https://openrouter.ai/docs/cookbook/coding-agents/cursor-integration") +
      '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">openrouter.ai/docs/…/cursor-integration</a>. ' +
      "Override OpenAI Base URL <code>" + esc(book.base_url || "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/cursor") +
      "</code>. Keith pastes keys. GET never echoes them. LED LIVE is a rails probe already on file, not a 20-host poll.</p>";
    h += '<p class="dim tiny">Usage accounting: <a href="' +
      esc(usageBook.url || "https://openrouter.ai/docs/cookbook/administration/usage-accounting") +
      '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">openrouter.ai/docs/…/usage-accounting</a>. ' +
      "Tokens, cost, and cache ride on every chat response (native tokenizer). " +
      "Do not send <code>usage.include</code> — deprecated. GET /api/v1/usage. Not a billing page.</p>";
    var mcpBook = creds.mcp_cookbook || {};
    h += '<p class="dim tiny">MCP servers: <a href="' +
      esc(mcpBook.url || "https://openrouter.ai/docs/cookbook/coding-agents/mcp-servers") +
      '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">openrouter.ai/docs/…/mcp-servers</a>. ' +
      "MCP tools/list → OpenAI tools on OpenRouter. Named pins (cosmos, playwright, mcp:openwork). " +
      "GET /api/v1/mcp. Does not spawn the cookbook filesystem server. Does not vendor @openrouter/mcp.</p>";
    if (book.note) h += '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(book.note) + "</p>";
    (creds.sources || []).forEach(function (s) {
      h += '<div class="cred-row" data-cred="' + esc(s.id) + '">' +
        '<span class="cred-led ' + ledCls(s.led) + '" title="' + esc(s.led) + '"></span>' +
        "<b>" + esc(s.label) + "</b> <small>" + esc(s.kind) + " · " + esc(s.led) + "</small>";
      if (s.docs) {
        h += ' <a class="dim tiny" href="' + esc(s.docs) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">docs</a>';
      }
      if (s.cookbook) {
        h += ' <a class="dim tiny" href="' + esc(s.cookbook) + '" target="_blank" rel="noopener">cookbook</a>';
      }
      if (s.gcloud && s.gcloud.accounts && s.gcloud.accounts.length) {
        h += '<div class="dim tiny">gcloud accounts: ' +
          s.gcloud.accounts.map(function (a) {
            return esc(a.account || "") + (a.active ? " (ACTIVE)" : "");
          }).join(" · ") + "</div>";
      }
      h += '<div class="formrow">' +
        '<input type="password" class="cred-secret" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="paste key — never shown back">' +
        '<button type="button" data-act="set">UPDATE</button>' +
        '<button type="button" data-act="grab">GRAB</button>' +
        '<button type="button" data-act="delete">DELETE</button>' +
        "</div></div>";
    });
    h += '<div class="formrow cred-custom">' +
      '<input id="credCustomId" placeholder="custom id" spellcheck="false">' +
      '<input id="credCustomLabel" placeholder="label">' +
      '<button type="button" id="credCustomAdd">ADD SOURCE</button></div>';
    h += '<div class="tiny" id="credSay"></div>';
    return h + "</div>";
  }

  function bindTools(box) {
    box.querySelectorAll(".cred-row [data-act]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var row = b.closest(".cred-row");
        var id = row && row.getAttribute("data-cred");
        var act = b.getAttribute("data-act");
        var secret = row && row.querySelector(".cred-secret");
        var say = $("credSay");
        var body = { action: act, id: id };
        if (act === "set") body.secret = secret ? secret.value : "";
        if (!state.api) return;
        state.api("/api/v1/cred", { method: "POST", body: body }).then(function (rec) {
          if (secret) secret.value = "";
          if (rec && rec.error) {
            if (say) say.textContent = String(rec.error || rec.detail || "refused");
            return;
          }
          if (state.rec) state.rec.creds = rec;
          paint();
        }).catch(function (e) {
          if (say) say.textContent = String((e && e.message) || e);
        });
      });
    });
    var add = box.querySelector("#credCustomAdd");
    if (add) add.addEventListener("click", function () {
      var idEl = $("credCustomId");
      var lab = $("credCustomLabel");
      var say = $("credSay");
      if (!state.api) return;
      state.api("/api/v1/cred", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { action: "custom", id: idEl ? idEl.value : "",
                label: lab ? lab.value : "" }
      }).then(function (rec) {
        if (rec && rec.error) {
          if (say) say.textContent = String(rec.error || rec.detail);
          return;
        }
        if (state.rec) state.rec.creds = rec;
        paint();
      }).catch(function (e) {
        if (say) say.textContent = String((e && e.message) || e);
      });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-giturf]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var raw = b.getAttribute("data-giturf") || "ALL:ALL";
        var i = raw.indexOf(":");
        var kind = i < 0 ? raw : raw.slice(0, i);
        var id = i < 0 ? "ALL" : raw.slice(i + 1);
        if (kind === "ALL" || (state.filter.kind === kind && state.filter.id === id)) {
          state.filter = { kind: "ALL" };
        } else {
          state.filter = { kind: kind, id: id };
        }
        paint();
      });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-gitur-run]").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var id = b.getAttribute("data-gitur-run");
        if (window.deckOpenRun) window.deckOpenRun(id);
        else if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("runs");
      });
    });
  }

  /* `api` is the same api(path, opts) FUNCTION header.js hands every other
     extra pane. This asked it for an `api.get` method that has never existed,
     so the guard below was false on every tick and the pane never made a single
     request: "gitur unread" was not an empty triad, it was a pane that never
     called Core. GET /api/v1/gitur only while the Gitur tab is visible —
     initDeckGitur still stores the transport and the 10s timer. */
  function giturTabOn() {
    var view = document.querySelector('#deckStage > .deck-view[data-tab="gitur"]');
    return !!(view && view.classList.contains("on"));
  }

  function tick() {
    if (typeof state.api !== "function") {
      state.err = "no api transport — extra pane was initialised without one";
      paint();
      return;
    }
    if (!giturTabOn()) return;
    if (state.inflight) return;
    state.inflight = true;
    state.api("/api/v1/gitur").then(function (rec) {
      state.rec = rec;
      state.err = "";
      paint();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.err = String(e && e.message || e);
      paint();
    }).then(function () { state.inflight = false; });
  }

  window.deckGiturShown = function () { tick(); };

  window.initDeckGitur = function (api) {
    state.api = api;
    tick();
    if (state.timer) clearInterval(state.timer);
    state.timer = setInterval(tick, POLL_MS);
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_backup.js =====
/* Backup extra pane. GET /api/v1/backup — never runs a backup.
   MOTIF suite is 10 sibling panes (clock, verified, local, offsite,
   surface test, generate, scheduler, search, profiles, restore) — not a dump. */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  var state = {
    api: null, rec: null, err: "", timer: null,
    bak: "", dest: "", dests: [], action: null,
    surfaces: null, profiles: null
  };
  var POLL_MS = 10000;
  var SUITE = [
    { id: "clock", el: "panel-backup", bd: "bd-backup-clock", label: "CLOCK" },
    { id: "verified", el: "panel-backup-verified", bd: "bd-backup-verified", label: "VERIFIED STORE" },
    { id: "local", el: "panel-backup-local", bd: "bd-backup-local", label: "LOCAL SNAPSHOT" },
    { id: "offsite", el: "panel-backup-offsite", bd: "bd-backup-offsite", label: "OFFSITE / CLOUD" },
    { id: "surface_test", el: "panel-backup-surface_test", bd: "bd-backup-surface_test", label: "SURFACE TEST" },
    { id: "generate", el: "panel-backup-generate", bd: "bd-backup-generate", label: "GENERATE" },
    { id: "scheduler", el: "panel-backup-scheduler", bd: "bd-backup-scheduler", label: "SCHEDULER" },
    { id: "search", el: "panel-backup-search", bd: "bd-backup-search", label: "SEARCH SURFACES" },
    { id: "profiles", el: "panel-backup-profiles", bd: "bd-backup-profiles", label: "PROFILE SKINS" },
    { id: "restore", el: "panel-backup-restore", bd: "bd-backup-restore", label: "RESTORE" }
  ];

  function ledClass(kind) {
    if (kind === "OK") return "ok";
    if (kind === "BROKE") return "bad";
    if (kind === "NO_SOURCE") return "warn";
    return "";
  }

  function suiteNote(id) {
    var rec = state.rec || {};
    var suite = rec.suite || [];
    var i;
    for (i = 0; i < suite.length; i++) {
      if (suite[i].id === id) return suite[i];
    }
    return { id: id, kind: "NAMED", note: "" };
  }

  function fillPane(id, html, kind) {
    var spec = null;
    SUITE.forEach(function (s) { if (s.id === id) spec = s; });
    if (!spec) return;
    var bd = $(spec.bd);
    if (bd) bd.innerHTML = html;
    var pan = $(spec.el);
    if (!pan) return;
    var led = pan.querySelector(".run-op-led");
    if (led) led.className = "run-op-led " + ledClass(kind || "");
    var age = pan.querySelector(".panel-hd .age");
    if (age) age.textContent = kind || "NAMED";
  }

  function paintClock() {
    var rec = state.rec || {};
    var hours = (rec.hours || [7, 11, 19, 23]).map(function (h) {
      var n = Number(h);
      var lab = (n < 10 ? "0" : "") + n + ":00";
      return '<button type="button" class="chip on" data-hour="' + esc(String(h)) +
        '">' + esc(lab) + "</button>";
    }).join("");
    var hb = rec.heartbeat || {};
    var h = '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(rec.note || "GET never runs a backup.") + "</p>";
    h += '<div class="mr-chiprow">' + hours + "</div>";
    h += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">kind</span><span>' +
      esc(rec.kind || "NO_SOURCE") + "</span></div>";
    if (!rec.heartbeat) {
      h += '<div class="empty">heartbeat NO_SOURCE — backup_clock_heartbeat.json is not on disk</div>';
    } else {
      h += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">last</span><span>' +
        esc(hb.last_run || hb.iso || hb.at || "UNMEASURED") + "</span></div>";
      if (hb.pid) h += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">pid</span><span>' +
        esc(String(hb.pid)) + "</span></div>";
      if (hb.kind) h += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">clock</span><span>' +
        esc(String(hb.kind)) + "</span></div>";
    }
    fillPane("clock", h, rec.kind);
  }

  function paintVerified() {
    var rec = state.rec || {};
    var v = rec.verified;
    var names = (v && v.recent) || [];
    var h = '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(suiteNote("verified").note ||
      "backups/verified recent names. Authority is the ledger pointer.") + "</p>";
    if (!names.length) {
      h += '<div class="dim tiny">verified dir unread or empty — not a fake VERIFIED set</div>';
    } else {
      h += '<div class="bak-grid mr-chiprow" id="bakVerifiedChips">';
      names.forEach(function (n) {
        h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (state.bak === n ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-bak-name="' + esc(n) + '">' + esc(n) + "</button>";
      });
      h += "</div>";
    }
    fillPane("verified", h, v ? "OK" : "NO_SOURCE");
  }

  function paintNamed(id, extra) {
    var s = suiteNote(id);
    var h = '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(s.note || "") + "</p>" + (extra || "");
    fillPane(id, h, s.kind);
  }

  function locKind(row) {
    return String((row && (row.kind || row.type)) || "").toUpperCase();
  }

  function surfaceRows() {
    var rec = state.surfaces || {};
    return rec.surfaces || rec.rows || rec.locations || [];
  }

  function destChip(id, label, kind, extra) {
    var on = state.dest === id ? " on" : "";
    return '<button type="button" class="chip' + on + '" data-dest="' +
      esc(id) + '">' + esc(label || id) +
      "<small>" + esc(kind || "") + (extra ? " · " + esc(extra) : "") +
      "</small></button>";
  }

  function actionErr(forAction) {
    var act = state.action;
    if (!act) return "";
    if (act.action && act.action !== forAction) return "";
    var h = "";
    if (act.error) {
      h += '<div class="bad tiny">' + esc(act.error) +
        (act.detail ? " · " + esc(act.detail) : "") + "</div>";
    }
    return h;
  }

  function paintLocal() {
    var rows = surfaceRows().filter(function (r) { return locKind(r) === "LOCAL"; });
    var extra = '<p class="dim tiny">LOCAL cannot qualify as off-machine backup. ' +
      "n UNMEASURED until indexed. GET never mkdir. GET never runs a backup.</p>";
    extra += actionErr("generate");
    if (!state.surfaces) {
      extra += '<div class="empty">surfaces unread — GET /api/v1/surfaces</div>';
    } else if (!rows.length) {
      extra += '<div class="dim tiny">no LOCAL rows on GET /surfaces — UNMEASURED, not an empty backup</div>';
    } else {
      extra += '<div class="mr-chiprow">';
      rows.forEach(function (r) {
        extra += destChip(r.id || r.path_or_url || "", r.label || r.id,
          r.kind || "LOCAL", (r.role || "") + " · n UNMEASURED");
      });
      extra += "</div>";
    }
    paintNamed("local", extra);
  }

  function paintOffsite() {
    var rows = surfaceRows().filter(function (r) {
      var k = locKind(r);
      return k === "LAN" || k === "CLOUD";
    });
    var extra = '<p class="dim tiny">LAN / CLOUD only. Publishing is not backup. ' +
      "n UNMEASURED until indexed. GET never mkdir.</p>";
    extra += actionErr("search");
    if (!state.surfaces) {
      extra += '<div class="empty">surfaces unread — GET /api/v1/surfaces</div>';
    } else if (!rows.length) {
      extra += '<div class="dim tiny">no LAN/CLOUD rows on GET /surfaces — UNMEASURED, not an empty offsite set</div>';
    } else {
      extra += '<div class="mr-chiprow">';
      rows.forEach(function (r) {
        extra += destChip(r.id || r.path_or_url || "", r.label || r.id,
          r.kind || "", (r.role || "") + " · n UNMEASURED");
      });
      extra += "</div>";
    }
    paintNamed("offsite", extra);
  }

  function paintProfiles() {
    var rows = (state.profiles && state.profiles.profiles) || [];
    var extra = '<p class="dim tiny">Per-profile backup dest (Forge / Crucible / Website). ' +
      "A profile is a skin on one Core, not a second Core. Pick a dest chip for GENERATE.</p>";
    extra += actionErr("generate");
    if (!state.profiles) {
      extra += '<div class="empty">profiles unread — GET /api/v1/profiles</div>';
    } else if (!rows.length) {
      extra += '<div class="dim tiny">no named profiles on GET /profiles — UNMEASURED, not a fake dest set</div>';
    } else {
      extra += '<div class="mr-chiprow">';
      rows.forEach(function (p) {
        extra += destChip("profile:" + (p.id || ""), p.label || p.id,
          p.kind || "profile", p.status || "NAMED");
      });
      extra += "</div>";
    }
    paintNamed("profiles", extra);
  }

  function paintSurface() {
    var extra = actionErr("surface_test");
    extra += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-bak="surface_test">RUN MEASURE</button>';
    var act = state.action;
    if (act && act.action === "surface_test") {
      extra += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">measure</span><span>' +
        esc(act.note || "timed probe") + "</span></div>";
      var meas = act.measure || {};
      var rows = meas.surfaces || meas.rows || [];
      if (rows.length) {
        extra += '<div class="mr-chiprow">';
        rows.forEach(function (r) {
          extra += '<button type="button" class="chip" disabled>' +
            esc(r.label || r.id) + " · " + esc(r.kind || r.led || "") + "</button>";
        });
        extra += "</div>";
      }
    }
    paintNamed("surface_test", extra);
  }

  function paintSearch() {
    var extra = actionErr("search");
    extra += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-bak="search">LIST DEST CANDIDATES</button>';
    var dests = state.dests || [];
    if (dests.length) {
      extra += '<div class="mr-chiprow" id="bakDestChips">';
      dests.forEach(function (d) {
        var id = d.id || d.path_or_url || "";
        extra += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (state.dest === id ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-dest="' + esc(id) + '">' + esc(d.label || id) +
          '<small>' + esc(d.kind || "") + " · n " + esc(String(d.n || "UNMEASURED")) +
          "</small></button>";
      });
      extra += "</div>";
    }
    paintNamed("search", extra);
  }

  function paintGenerate() {
    var extra = "";
    if (state.dest) {
      extra += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">dest</span><span>' +
        esc(state.dest) + "</span></div>";
    } else {
      extra += '<div class="dim tiny">pick a dest chip on SEARCH / LOCAL / OFFSITE / PROFILE SKINS first</div>';
    }
    extra += actionErr("generate");
    extra += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-bak="generate">GENERATE (refuses without dest)</button>';
    paintNamed("generate", extra);
  }

  function paintRestore() {
    var extra = "";
    if (state.bak) {
      extra += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">bak</span><span>' +
        esc(state.bak) + "</span></div>";
    } else {
      extra += '<div class="dim tiny">pick a verified name chip first</div>';
    }
    extra += actionErr("restore");
    extra += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" data-bak="restore">RESTORE (refuses without bak)</button>';
    paintNamed("restore", extra);
  }

  function paintScheduler() {
    var rec = state.rec || {};
    var hb = rec.heartbeat || {};
    var extra = '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">hours</span><span>' +
      esc((rec.hours || []).join(" / ") || "07/11/19/23") + "</span></div>";
    extra += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">schtask</span><span>' +
      esc(hb.task || hb.schtask || hb.kind || "UNMEASURED") + "</span></div>";
    paintNamed("scheduler", extra);
  }

  function paint() {
    var age = $("backupAge");
    if (age) {
      age.textContent = state.err
        ? state.err
        : (state.rec
          ? ("GET /api/v1/backup · " + (state.rec.kind || "ok") +
            (state.rec.iso ? " · " + state.rec.iso : ""))
          : "unread");
      age.className = state.err ? "tiny bad" : "dim tiny age";
    }
    if (!state.rec && !state.err) {
      SUITE.forEach(function (s) {
        fillPane(s.id, '<div class="empty">backup unread — GET /api/v1/backup has not answered</div>', "");
      });
      return;
    }
    if (state.err && !state.rec) {
      SUITE.forEach(function (s) {
        fillPane(s.id, '<div class="empty">GET /api/v1/backup — ' + esc(state.err) +
          ". Not an empty backup. This pane never runs a backup.</div>", "BROKE");
      });
      return;
    }
    paintClock();
    paintVerified();
    paintLocal();
    paintOffsite();
    paintSurface();
    paintGenerate();
    paintScheduler();
    paintSearch();
    paintProfiles();
    paintRestore();
    bindHands();
    if (window.layoutDeckView) window.layoutDeckView("backup");
  }

  function bindHands() {
    document.querySelectorAll("#cdeck-more [data-bak]").forEach(function (b) {
      if (b._bakBound) return;
      b._bakBound = true;
      b.addEventListener("click", function () { runSuite(b.getAttribute("data-bak")); });
    });
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-bak-name]").forEach(function (b) {
      if (b._bakBound) return;
      b._bakBound = true;
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        state.bak = b.getAttribute("data-bak-name") || "";
        paint();
      });
    });
    document.querySelectorAll("[data-dest]").forEach(function (b) {
      if (b._bakBound) return;
      b._bakBound = true;
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        state.dest = b.getAttribute("data-dest") || "";
        paint();
      });
    });
  }

  function runSuite(action) {
    if (!state.api || !action) return;
    if (action === "generate" && !state.dest) {
      state.action = { action: action, error: "refuses without dest" };
      paint();
      return;
    }
    if (action === "restore" && !state.bak) {
      state.action = { action: action, error: "refuses without bak" };
      paint();
      return;
    }
    var body = { action: action };
    if (action === "restore") body.bak = state.bak || "";
    if (action === "generate") body.dest = state.dest || "";
    state.api("/api/v1/backup", { method: "POST", body: body }).then(function (rec) {
      state.err = "";
      state.action = rec;
      if (rec && rec.kind) state.rec = rec;
      if (rec && rec.dests) state.dests = rec.dests;
      paint();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.action = { error: String((e && e.message) || e), action: action };
      paint();
    });
  }

  function tabOn() {
    return typeof window.deckTabVisible === "function" && window.deckTabVisible("backup");
  }
  function tick() {
    if (!tabOn()) return;
    if (!state.api) {
      paint();
      return;
    }
    state.api("/api/v1/backup").then(function (rec) {
      state.err = "";
      state.rec = rec;
      paint();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.err = String((e && e.message) || e);
      paint();
    });
    if (!state.surfaces) {
      state.api("/api/v1/surfaces").then(function (rec) {
        state.surfaces = rec || {};
        paint();
      }).catch(function () {
        paint();
      });
    }
    if (!state.profiles) {
      state.api("/api/v1/profiles").then(function (rec) {
        state.profiles = rec || {};
        paint();
      }).catch(function () {
        paint();
      });
    }
  }

  window.initDeckBackup = function (api) {
    state.api = api || null;
    tick();
    if (state.timer) clearInterval(state.timer);
    if (state.api) state.timer = setInterval(tick, POLL_MS);
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_forge.js =====
/* Forge profile extra pane: CCr main + N adversarial coders + job token estimate.
   Cost = tokens × chosen model's rate card (GET /model_rater). CCr provides the
   initial 24k/8k; override is explicit. Header and JACK'S MESH are untouched. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var state = {
    api: null,
    seats: [],
    costs: null,
    job: null,
    initial: { tokens_in: 24000, tokens_out: 8000 },
    models: [],
    viaOptions: [],
    rails: [],
    maxAdv: 24,
    // What Core answered the last WRITE, and which pane the control was on.
    // Every POST here used to end in `.catch(function () { load(); })`, so a
    // seat Core REFUSED (forge.ccr is a locked seat — unassigning it is a
    // refusal, not a no-op) reloaded the same value back into the select and
    // the operator saw a control that appeared to do nothing.
    say: null,
    err: ""
  };

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  function usd(n) {
    if (n == null || n === "") return "—";
    var x = Number(n);
    if (!isFinite(x)) return "—";
    if (x === 0) return "$0";
    if (x < 0.01) return "$" + x.toFixed(4);
    return "$" + x.toFixed(2);
  }
  function forgeSeats() {
    return (state.seats || []).filter(function (s) { return s.profile === "forge"; });
  }
  function ccrSeat() {
    var rows = forgeSeats();
    var i;
    for (i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      if (rows[i].seat === "ccr") return rows[i];
    }
    return null;
  }
  function advSeats() {
    return forgeSeats().filter(function (s) {
      return String(s.seat || "").indexOf("adv_") === 0;
    });
  }

  /* Core's own words for a write, on the pane the button was on. A refusal is
     rendered as a refusal — not swallowed, and never re-labelled as success. */
  function sayHtml(where) {
    var s = state.say;
    if (!s || s.where !== where) return "";
    if (s.state === "sending") {
      return '<p class="dim tiny forge-say">POST ' + esc(s.path) + " …</p>";
    }
    if (s.state === "bad") {
      return '<p class="tiny bad forge-say">POST ' + esc(s.path) + " REFUSED — " +
        esc(s.text) + ". Nothing changed.</p>";
    }
    return '<p class="tiny ok forge-say">' + esc(s.text) + "</p>";
  }

  /* One write path so every control answers the same way. `okText` reads what
     Core came back with, not what the click intended. */
  function post(path, body, where, describe) {
    if (!state.api) return;
    state.say = { where: where, state: "sending", path: path };
    paintAll();
    state.api(path, { method: "POST", body: body }).then(function (rec) {
      // A 200 whose payload says refused is still a refusal.
      if (rec && (rec.refused || rec.error)) {
        state.say = { where: where, state: "bad", path: path,
          text: String(rec.error || rec.kind || rec.detail || "refused") };
      } else {
        state.say = { where: where, state: "ok", path: path, text: describe(rec) };
      }
      ingest(rec);
      load();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.say = { where: where, state: "bad", path: path,
        text: String((e && e.message) || e) };
      load();
    });
  }

  function applyJob(job) {
    state.job = job || state.job;
    var tin = $("forgeTin");
    var tout = $("forgeTout");
    var ov = $("forgeOverride");
    if (!state.job) return;
    if (tin && document.activeElement !== tin) tin.value = String(state.job.tokens_in);
    if (tout && document.activeElement !== tout) tout.value = String(state.job.tokens_out);
    if (ov) ov.checked = !!state.job.override;
  }

  function paintCcr() {
    var box = $("bd-forge-ccr");
    if (!box) return;
    var s = ccrSeat();
    var line = (state.costs && state.costs.lines || []).filter(function (L) {
      return L.seat === "ccr";
    })[0];
    var model = (s && s.model) || "grok-4.6";
    var via = (s && s.via) || "cli:grok";
    box.innerHTML =
      (state.err ? '<div class="read-stale">last good read shown · ' + esc(state.err) + "</div>" : "") +
      '<div class="dim tiny">One CCr at a time. GBW/CCr runs as a <b>background grok CLI</b> unless called forward onto the Forge left pane (Session). This card is the CCr model and via. Adversarial coders propose; CCr disposes.</div>' +
      '<dl class="kv"><dt>seat</dt><dd>forge.ccr</dd>' +
      "<dt>model</dt><dd>" + esc(model) + "</dd>" +
      "<dt>via</dt><dd>" + esc(via) + railPresent(via) + "</dd>" +
      "<dt>this job</dt><dd>" +
      (line && line.usd != null ? usd(line.usd) + (line.free ? " · free card" : "") : "UNMEASURED — CLI occupancy is not an OpenRouter card") +
      "</dd></dl>" +
      '<div class="dim tiny">model</div><div class="mr-chiprow" id="forgeCcrModel">' +
      modelChipHtml(model, false) + "</div>" +
      '<div class="dim tiny">via</div><div class="mr-chiprow" id="forgeCcrVia">' +
      viaChipHtml(via) + "</div>" +
      '<p class="dim tiny">No unassigned option: forge.ccr is one writer and Core REFUSES unassigning it. A model or via change is POST /api/v1/model_rater/seat. Full catalog is Model Rater — not a 400-row dropdown.</p>' +
      sayHtml("ccr");
    var sel = $("forgeCcrModel");
    var vsel = $("forgeCcrVia");
    function ccrAssign() {
      assign("forge", "ccr", cv(sel), "ccr", cv(vsel));
    }
    if (sel) sel.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
      if (openRaterClick(ev)) return;
      ccrAssign();
    });
    if (vsel) vsel.addEventListener("click", ccrAssign);
  }

  function paintJob() {
    var note = $("forgeJobNote");
    var total = $("forgeJobTotal");
    var src = $("forgeJobSrc");
    if (note && state.job) note.textContent = state.job.note || "";
    if (src && state.job) {
      src.textContent = state.job.override
        ? "override — cost uses these tokens, not the CCr initial"
        : "CCr initial — 24k in / 8k out for a MOTIF BUILD work-order";
    }
    if (total) {
      total.innerHTML = (state.costs
        ? "parallel total <b>" + usd(state.costs.total_usd) + "</b>" +
          (state.costs.unassigned ? " · " + state.costs.unassigned + " unassigned" : "")
        : '<span class="dim">parallel total UNMEASURED — GET /api/v1/model_rater unread</span>') +
        sayHtml("job");
    }
  }

  function cv(el) {
    if (typeof el === "string") el = $(el);
    if (window.chipValue) return window.chipValue(el) || "";
    return (el && el.value) || "";
  }
  function openRaterClick(ev) {
    if (ev && ev.target && ev.target.classList && ev.target.classList.contains("mr-open-rater")) {
      if (window.openModelRater) window.openModelRater();
      else if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("models");
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function modelChipHtml(selected, allowUnassigned) {
    var picks = [];
    var seen = {};
    function add(id, name) {
      var key = id || "";
      if (!key && allowUnassigned === false) return;
      if (seen[key]) return;
      seen[key] = 1;
      picks.push({ id: key, name: name || id || "unassigned" });
    }
    if (allowUnassigned !== false) add("", "unassigned");
    add(selected, selected);
    (state.models || []).forEach(function (m) {
      if (m.favored || m.id === selected) add(m.id, m.name || m.id);
    });
    ["grok-4.6", "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it:free", "composer-2.5"].forEach(function (id) {
      add(id, id);
    });
    var h = "";
    picks.forEach(function (m) {
      h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (m.id === (selected || "") ? " on" : "") +
        '" data-val="' + esc(m.id) + '">' + esc(m.name) + "</button>";
    });
    h += '<button type="button" class="chip mr-open-rater">open rater</button>';
    return h;
  }
  function viaChipHtml(selected) {
    var opts = (state.viaOptions && state.viaOptions.length) ? state.viaOptions : [
      { id: "cli:grok", label: "CLI · grok" },
      { id: "cli:gemini", label: "CLI · gemini" },
      { id: "cli:codex", label: "CLI · Codex" },
      { id: "cli:hermes", label: "CLI · Hermes" },
      { id: "openrouter-api", label: "API · OpenRouter (incl. :free)" },
      { id: "groq-api", label: "API · GroqCloud" },
      { id: "gem-api", label: "API · Vertex Joanna" },
      { id: "vertex-coding", label: "API · Vertex $300" },
      { id: "oa-api", label: "API · OpenAI" },
      { id: "sgh-api", label: "API · xAI console" },
      { id: "cursor-api", label: "API · Cursor" },
      { id: "dom", label: "DOM · browser" }
    ];
    var h = "";
    opts.forEach(function (v) {
      h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (v.id === (selected || "") ? " on" : "") +
        '" data-val="' + esc(v.id) + '" data-via="' + esc(v.id) + '">' +
        esc(v.label || v.id) + "</button>";
    });
    return h;
  }
  function modelOptions(selected, allowUnassigned) {
    return modelChipHtml(selected, allowUnassigned);
  }
  function viaOptionHtml(selected) {
    return viaChipHtml(selected);
  }
  function railPresent(via) {
    if (!via || via.indexOf("cli:") === 0 || via === "dom") return "";
    var rows = state.rails || [];
    var hit = rows.filter(function (r) {
      return (r.link_id || r.id) === via;
    })[0];
    if (!rows.length) return "";
    if (!hit) return " · not in /rails";
    if (hit.verified === true) return " · registered";
    if (hit.verified === false) return " · FAIL";
    return " · UNKNOWN";
  }

  function paintAdv() {
    var box = $("bd-forge-adv");
    if (!box) return;
    var rows = advSeats();
    var lines = (state.costs && state.costs.lines) || [];
    var h = '<div class="dim tiny">Any number of adversarial coders in parallel (cap ' +
      state.maxAdv + "). Pick a model and a <b>via</b> — registered API rail, CLI, DOM, or a :free OpenRouter pin. They propose; they do not hold the CCr pen.</div>";
    if (window.deckConsensusRulesHtml) {
      h += '<div class="studio-cons-rules" data-cons-where="forge">' +
        window.deckConsensusRulesHtml("forge") + "</div>";
    } else {
