      h += '<div class="dim tiny">Consensus bar lives on Studio MOTIF CONSENSUS (plurality / majority / complete). Different families. No peeking. No third model resolves.</div>';
    }
    h += '<div class="formrow"><div class="mr-chiprow" id="forgeAddVia">' +
      viaChipHtml("openrouter-api") +
      '</div><button type="button" id="forgeAddAdv"' +
      (rows.length >= state.maxAdv ? " disabled" : "") + ">+ adversarial coder</button>" +
      '<span class="dim tiny">' + rows.length + " of " + state.maxAdv + " seats</span></div>";
    h += sayHtml("adv");
    if (!rows.length) {
      h += '<div class="empty">no adversarial coders — add one, then pick a model and via. Empty is honest: Core is holding no adversarial seat.</div>';
    } else {
      rows.forEach(function (s) {
        var line = lines.filter(function (L) { return L.seat === s.seat; })[0] || {};
        h += '<div class="forge-adv" data-seat="' + esc(s.seat) + '">' +
          "<div class=\"forge-adv-hd\"><b>" + esc(s.label || s.seat) + "</b>" +
          '<button type="button" class="forge-drop" data-seat="' + esc(s.seat) +
          '" title="remove this coder">remove</button></div>' +
          '<div class="dim tiny">model</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-forge-assign="' +
          esc(s.seat) + '">' + modelChipHtml(s.model) + "</div>" +
          '<div class="dim tiny">via</div><div class="mr-chiprow" data-forge-via="' +
          esc(s.seat) + '">' + viaChipHtml(s.via || "openrouter-api") + "</div>" +
          '<div class="dim tiny">this job ' +
          (line.usd != null ? usd(line.usd) + (line.free ? " · free card" : "") : "—") +
          railPresent(s.via || "") + "</div></div>";
      });
    }
    box.innerHTML = h;
    var add = $("forgeAddAdv");
    if (add) add.addEventListener("click", function () { addAdv(); });
    box.querySelectorAll(".forge-drop").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () { dropAdv(b.getAttribute("data-seat")); });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-forge-assign]").forEach(function (sel) {
      sel.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
        if (openRaterClick(ev)) return;
        var seat = sel.getAttribute("data-forge-assign");
        var v = box.querySelector('[data-forge-via="' + seat + '"]');
        assign("forge", seat, cv(sel), "adv", cv(v));
      });
    });
    box.querySelectorAll("[data-forge-via]").forEach(function (sel) {
      sel.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var seat = sel.getAttribute("data-forge-via");
        var m = box.querySelector('[data-forge-assign="' + seat + '"]');
        assign("forge", seat, cv(m), "adv", cv(sel));
      });
    });
  }

  function bindGo(root) {
    if (!root) return;
    root.querySelectorAll(".studio-go").forEach(function (b) {
      b.addEventListener("click", function () {
        var tab = b.getAttribute("data-go");
        if (tab && window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab(tab);
      });
    });
  }

  function paintAll() {
    applyJob(state.job);
    paintCcr();
    paintJob();
    paintAdv();
  }

  function ingest(rec) {
    if (!rec) return;
    state.err = "";
    if (rec.seats) state.seats = rec.seats;
    if (rec.job_estimate) state.job = rec.job_estimate;
    if (rec.job_costs) state.costs = rec.job_costs;
    if (rec.ccr_initial) state.initial = rec.ccr_initial;
    if (typeof rec.max_adv === "number") state.maxAdv = rec.max_adv;
    if (rec.models && rec.models.length) state.models = rec.models;
    if (rec.via_options && rec.via_options.length) state.viaOptions = rec.via_options;
    paintAll();
  }

  function tabOn() {
    return typeof window.deckTabVisible === "function" && window.deckTabVisible("forge");
  }
  function load() {
    if (!state.api) return;
    if (!tabOn()) return;
    state.api("/api/v1/rails").then(function (rec) {
      state.rails = (rec && (rec.matrix || rec.rails)) || [];
    }).catch(function () { state.rails = state.rails || []; });
    state.api("/api/v1/model_rater?type=coding&sort=coding&limit=80").then(function (rec) {
      ingest(rec);
    }).catch(function () {
      state.api("/api/v1/model_rater?limit=80").then(ingest).catch(function (e) {
        // Seats on screen are the last ones Core confirmed. Keep them, and say
        // the read failed — an unread roster is not an empty one.
        state.err = "GET /api/v1/model_rater — " + String((e && e.message) || e);
        paintAll();
      });
    });
  }

  function addAdv() {
    var vsel = $("forgeAddVia");
    var via = cv(vsel) || "openrouter-api";
    post("/api/v1/model_rater/seat", { action: "add", via: via }, "adv", function (rec) {
      var n = ((rec && rec.seats) || []).filter(function (s) {
        return s.profile === "forge" && String(s.seat || "").indexOf("adv_") === 0;
      }).length;
      return "seat added via " + via + " — " + n + " adversarial coder" +
        (n === 1 ? "" : "s") + " now seated.";
    });
  }

  function dropAdv(seat) {
    if (!seat) return;
    post("/api/v1/model_rater/seat", { action: "remove", seat: seat }, "adv", function () {
      return "removed " + seat + ".";
    });
  }

  function assign(profile, seat, model, where, via) {
    if (!seat) return;
    var body = { profile: profile, seat: seat, model: model || "" };
    if (via) body.via = via;
    post("/api/v1/model_rater/seat",
      body,
      where || "adv",
      function (rec) {
        var got = ((rec && rec.seats) || []).filter(function (s) {
          return s.profile === profile && s.seat === seat;
        })[0];
        // Report the seat Core is HOLDING, which is not always the one asked
        // for: a locked seat answers 200 and keeps its own model.
        var now = (got && got.model) || model || "unassigned";
        var nowVia = (got && got.via) || via || "";
        return profile + "." + seat + " = " + now +
          (nowVia ? " via " + nowVia : "") +
          (model && now !== model ? " — Core kept its own model, not " + model : "");
      });
  }

  function saveOverride() {
    var tin = parseInt(($("forgeTin") && $("forgeTin").value) || "0", 10) || 0;
    var tout = parseInt(($("forgeTout") && $("forgeTout").value) || "0", 10) || 0;
    var ov = $("forgeOverride");
    var isOv = !ov || ov.checked;
    post("/api/v1/model_rater/job_estimate",
      isOv ? { tokens_in: tin, tokens_out: tout, override: true } : { reset: true },
      "job",
      function (rec) {
        var j = (rec && rec.job_estimate) || null;
        if (!j) return isOv ? "override filed." : "reset to the CCr initial.";
        return (j.override ? "override" : "CCr initial") + " · " +
          j.tokens_in + " in / " + j.tokens_out + " out.";
      });
  }

  function bindJob() {
    if (bindJob._on) return;
    bindJob._on = true;
    ["forgeTin", "forgeTout"].forEach(function (id) {
      var el = $(id);
      if (!el) return;
      el.addEventListener("change", function () {
        var ov = $("forgeOverride");
        if (ov) ov.checked = true;
        saveOverride();
      });
    });
    var ov = $("forgeOverride");
    if (ov) ov.addEventListener("change", function () {
      if (!ov.checked) saveOverride();
      else saveOverride();
    });
    var reset = $("forgeReset");
    if (reset) reset.addEventListener("click", function () {
      var o = $("forgeOverride");
      if (o) o.checked = false;
      saveOverride();
    });
  }

  window.paintDeckForge = function (patch) {
    if (patch && patch.seats) state.seats = patch.seats;
    paintAll();
  };

  window.initDeckForge = function (api) {
    state.api = api || null;
    bindJob();
    paintAll();
    load();
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_profiles.js =====
/* Per-profile MOTIF engine skins. Does not start MOTIF. Does not publish. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }

  window.initDeckProfiles = function (api) {
    if (!api || !$("panel-profiles")) return;
    var state = {
      profile: (window.deckInstanceProfile && window.deckInstanceProfile()) || "website",
      stage: "define",
      view: "motif",
      rec: null,
      dirty: false
    };
    if (state.profile === "") state.profile = "website";

    function engine() {
      return (state.rec && state.rec.engine) || {};
    }

    function paintSchematic() {
      var box = $("pfSchematic");
      if (!box) return;
      var rows = (state.rec && state.rec.profiles) || [];
      var stages = (state.rec && state.rec.stages) || [];
      if (!rows.length) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">schematic unread — GET /api/v1/profiles</div>';
        return;
      }
      box.innerHTML = rows.map(function (p) {
        var on = p.id === state.profile ? " on" : "";
        var pipeSrc = p.motif_top ? stages
          : ((p.skin_tabs && p.skin_tabs.length) ? p.skin_tabs : stages);
        var pipe = pipeSrc.map(function (s, i) {
          return (i ? '<span class="pf-arrow">→</span>' : "") +
            '<span class="pf-node">' + esc(s.label || s.name || s.id) + "</span>";
        }).join("");
        return '<button type="button" class="pf-stream' + on + '" data-pf="' +
          esc(p.id) + '"><div class="pf-stream-h">' + esc(p.label) +
          "<small>" + esc(p.kind) + " · " + esc(p.status || "") +
          "</small></div><div class=\"pf-pipe\">" + pipe + "</div></button>";
      }).join("");
      box.querySelectorAll("[data-pf]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          openFull(b.getAttribute("data-pf"));
        });
      });
    }

    function openFull(pid) {
      harvest();
      state.profile = pid || state.profile;
      state.stage = "";
      load(function () {
        if (state.rec && state.rec.motif_top) {
          state.view = "motif";
          state.stage = "define";
        } else {
          state.view = "tool";
          var tabs = skinTabs();
          if (tabs.length) state.stage = tabs[0].id;
        }
        paintMotifTop();
        paintStages();
        paintSkin();
        var full = $("pfFull");
        if (full && full.scrollIntoView) {
          full.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
        }
      });
    }
    window.openProfileFull = openFull;
    window.openProfilePage = function (pid) {
      api("/api/v1/profiles?profile=" + encodeURIComponent(pid)).then(function (rec) {
        paintProfilePage(pid, rec);
      }).catch(function (e) {
        var el = document.getElementById("pfPage-" + pid);
        if (el) {
          el.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">GET /api/v1/profiles?profile=' +
            esc(pid) + " — " + esc(String(e && e.message || e)) + "</div>";
        }
      });
    };
    var onView = document.querySelector("#deckStage > .deck-view.on");
    var onTab = onView && onView.getAttribute("data-tab");
    if (onTab && document.getElementById("pfPage-" + onTab)) {
      window.openProfilePage(onTab);
    }

    function paintPick() {
      var box = $("pfPick");
      if (!box) return;
      var rows = (state.rec && state.rec.profiles) || [];
      box.innerHTML = rows.map(function (p) {
        var on = p.id === state.profile ? " on" : "";
        return '<button type="button" class="pf-chip' + on + '" data-pf="' +
          esc(p.id) + '">' + esc(p.label) + "<small>" + esc(p.kind) +
          "</small></button>";
      }).join("");
      box.querySelectorAll("[data-pf]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          openFull(b.getAttribute("data-pf"));
        });
      });
    }

    var park = {};

    function skinTabs() {
      var tabs = (state.rec && state.rec.skin_tabs) || [];
      if (tabs.length) return tabs;
      return ((state.rec && state.rec.stages) || []).map(function (s) {
        return { id: s.id, label: s.n + " " + s.name, kind: "motif", hint: s.hint };
      });
    }

    function tabMeta() {
      var tabs = skinTabs();
      var i;
      for (i = 0; i < tabs.length; i++) {
        if (tabs[i].id === state.stage) return tabs[i];
      }
      return tabs[0] || { id: "define", label: "PROBLEM / GOAL", kind: "motif", hint: "" };
    }

    function unmountSkin() {
      Object.keys(park).forEach(function (id) {
        var el = document.getElementById(id);
        var ph = park[id];
        if (el && ph && ph.parentNode) ph.parentNode.insertBefore(el, ph);
      });
    }
    window.unmountProfileSkin = unmountSkin;

    function mountIds(ids, dest) {
      (ids || []).forEach(function (id) {
        var el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (!el || !dest) return;
        if (!park[id]) {
          var ph = document.createElement("div");
          ph.setAttribute("data-park", id);
          ph.style.display = "none";
          if (el.parentNode) el.parentNode.insertBefore(ph, el);
          park[id] = ph;
        }
        dest.appendChild(el);
        el.hidden = false;
        el.style.display = "";
      });
    }

    function paintMotifTop() {
      var box = $("pfMotifTop");
      if (!box) return;
      var onTop = !!(state.rec && state.rec.motif_top);
      box.hidden = !onTop;
      if (!onTop) {
        box.innerHTML = "";
        return;
      }
      var stages = (state.rec && state.rec.stages) || [];
      var h = '<div class="pf-motif-h">MOTIF sequence</div><div class="pf-pipe" role="list">';
      stages.forEach(function (s, i) {
        if (i) h += '<span class="pf-arrow">→</span>';
        var sel = state.view === "motif" && state.stage === s.id ? " on" : "";
        h += '<button type="button" class="pf-node pf-motif-step' + sel +
          '" data-ms="' + esc(s.id) + '" role="listitem"><b>' + s.n +
          "</b> " + esc(s.name) + "</button>";
      });
      h += "</div>";
      h += '<p class="dim tiny">Each step is a setup pane. SAVE does not start MOTIF.</p>';
      box.innerHTML = h;
      box.querySelectorAll("[data-ms]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          harvest();
          state.view = "motif";
          state.stage = b.getAttribute("data-ms");
          paintMotifTop();
          paintStages();
          paintSkin();
        });
      });
    }

    function paintStages() {
      var box = $("pfStages");
      if (!box) return;
      var tabs = skinTabs();
      if (!state.stage && tabs.length) state.stage = tabs[0].id;
      var motifTop = !!(state.rec && state.rec.motif_top);
      box.innerHTML = tabs.map(function (s) {
        var on = s.id === state.stage && (!motifTop || state.view === "tool") ? " on" : "";
        return '<button type="button" class="pf-stage' + on + '" data-st="' +
          esc(s.id) + '">' + esc(s.label || s.id) + "</button>";
      }).join("");
      box.querySelectorAll("[data-st]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          harvest();
          var id = b.getAttribute("data-st");
          var tabs = skinTabs();
          var meta = tabs.filter(function (t) { return t.id === id; })[0] || {};
          state.view = (meta.kind === "motif") ? "motif" : "tool";
          state.stage = id;
          paintMotifTop();
          paintSkin();
          paintStages();
        });
      });
    }

    function stageMeta() {
      var stages = (state.rec && state.rec.stages) || [];
      var i;
      for (i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
        if (stages[i].id === state.stage) return stages[i];
      }
      return { id: "define", n: 1, name: "PROBLEM / GOAL", hint: "" };
    }

    function destCatalog() {
      return (state.rec && state.rec.dest_catalog) || [];
    }

    function paintMotifForm(box, s) {
      var en = engine();
      var h = "<h3>" + (s.n || "") + " · " + esc(s.name || s.label || s.id) + "</h3>";
      h += '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(s.hint || "") + "</p>";
      if (s.id === "define") {
        h += '<p class="dim tiny">PROBLEM STATEMENT / STATED GOAL for <b>' +
          esc((state.rec && state.rec.label) || state.profile) +
          "</b>. Frozen verbatim. SAVE does not start MOTIF.</p>";
        h += '<textarea id="pfDefine" rows="12" spellcheck="false" placeholder="WHAT&#10;WHY&#10;acceptance (live emit)&#10;off-limits">' +
          esc((en.define && en.define.text) || "") + "</textarea>";
      } else if (s.id === "improve") {
        h += '<p class="dim tiny">IMPLEMENT (was IMPROVE). Dest depends on this profile. GitHub/GitLab run Gitur. Publish is Keith\'s click — this TUI does not publish.</p>';
        h += '<div class="studio-targets" id="pfDest">';
        destCatalog().forEach(function (d) {
          var kind = (en.dest && en.dest.kind) || "staged";
          var on = kind === d.id;
          var lab = d.label || d.id;
          if (d.id === "staged") lab += " · READY";
          if (d.id === "publish") lab += " · REFUSED this TUI";
          h += '<label><input type="radio" name="pfDest" value="' + esc(d.id) + '"' +
            (on ? " checked" : "") + (d.id === "publish" ? " data-publish-refuse=\"1\"" : "") +
            "> " + esc(lab) + "</label>";
        });
        h += "</div>";
        h += '<label class="dim tiny">path / URL / repo<textarea id="pfDestPath" rows="2" spellcheck="false">' +
          esc((en.dest && en.dest.path) || "") + "</textarea></label>";
        var gitur = en.dest && en.dest.via_gitur;
        h += '<div class="dim tiny" id="pfDestGitur">' +
          (gitur ? "Gitur before the live tree" : "not Gitur — write stays on this dest") +
          "</div>";
      } else {
        if (s.id === "build") {
          h += '<p class="dim tiny">Adversarial builders for this coding profile sit on the Seats left tab. This pane is how BUILD is set up (artifact, Gitur, N in parallel).</p>';
        }
        if (s.id === "research") {
          h += '<p class="dim tiny">SGH + GEM first, both, in parallel. Background free CLI coders (named :free, not the rotator) can run from this tab.</p>';
        }
        if (s.id === "research" || s.id === "arch" || s.id === "consensus1"
            || s.id === "build" || s.id === "critics" || s.id === "consensus2") {
          var st = ((en.step_setup || {})[s.id]) || {};
          h += '<div class="studio-targets" id="pfBar">';
          (state.rec.bar_catalog || []).forEach(function (b) {
            h += '<label><input type="radio" name="pfBar" value="' + esc(b.id) + '"' +
              ((st.bar || "majority") === b.id ? " checked" : "") + "> " +
              esc(b.label) + "</label>";
          });
          h += "</div>";
          h += '<div class="studio-cons-rules"><p class="dim tiny">Same DEFINE verbatim. Different families (swiss cheese). Dual-lane BUILD: no shared context. AUTO only when the bar is met; else CONTESTED — both positions, one line to Keith. No third model resolves. Disagreement is the signal.</p></div>';
          var bgOk = s.id === "research" || s.id === "arch"
            || s.id === "consensus1" || s.id === "consensus2";
          if (bgOk) {
            h += '<label class="dim tiny">N free CLI (1–5) <input id="pfNFree" type="number" min="1" max="5" value="' +
              esc(st.n_free || 3) + '"></label>';
            h += '<div class="formrow"><button type="button" class="studio-go" id="pfBgRun">RUN BACKGROUND FREE CLI</button>' +
              '<button type="button" id="pfBgFac">FACILITATE CONSENSUS</button></div>';
            var bg = ((state.rec.bg || {}).stages || {})[s.id] || {};
            h += '<p class="dim tiny">bg runs ' + esc(String(bg.n || 0)) +
              (bg.consensus ? (" · " + esc(bg.consensus.kind || "") + " bar " + esc(bg.consensus.bar || "")) : "") +
              " · CLI background only · does not write the live tree</p>";
          }
        }
        h += '<label class="dim tiny">how this profile runs stage ' + s.n +
          '<textarea id="pfNote" rows="8" spellcheck="false">' +
          esc((en.stages && en.stages[s.id]) || "") + "</textarea></label>";
      }
      h += '<div class="formrow"><button type="button" class="studio-go" id="pfSave">SAVE SKIN</button></div>';
      h += '<div class="tiny" id="pfSay"></div>';
      return h;
    }

    function bindMotifForm(box) {
      var en = engine();
      if ($("pfSave")) $("pfSave").addEventListener("click", save);
      if ($("pfBgRun")) $("pfBgRun").addEventListener("click", function () {
        harvest();
        save();
        api("/api/v1/profiles/bg", {
          method: "POST",
          body: { profile: "forge", stage: state.stage }
        }).then(function (rec) {
          say((rec && rec.error) ? String(rec.error) : ("spawned " + ((rec.spawned || []).length) + " CLI workers"));
          load();
        }).catch(function (e) { say(String((e && e.message) || e), true); });
      });
      if ($("pfBgFac")) $("pfBgFac").addEventListener("click", function () {
        harvest();
        api("/api/v1/profiles/bg", {
          method: "POST",
          body: { action: "facilitate", profile: "forge", stage: state.stage }
        }).then(function (rec) {
          say((rec && rec.kind) ? (rec.kind + " · bar " + rec.bar + " · met " + rec.met) : String(rec && rec.error || "ok"));
          load();
        }).catch(function (e) { say(String((e && e.message) || e), true); });
      });
      if ($("pfDest")) {
        box.querySelectorAll('input[name="pfDest"]').forEach(function (inp) {
          inp.addEventListener("change", function () {
            if (!en.dest) en.dest = {};
            en.dest.kind = inp.value;
            var git = inp.value === "github" || inp.value === "gitlab";
            var g = $("pfDestGitur");
            if (g) g.textContent = git
              ? "Gitur before the live tree"
              : "not Gitur — write stays on this dest";
            state.dirty = true;
          });
        });
      }
    }

    function paintSkin() {
      var box = $("pfSkin");
      if (!box) return;
      unmountSkin();
      if (state.rec && state.rec.motif_top && state.view === "motif") {
        box.innerHTML = paintMotifForm(box, stageMeta());
        bindMotifForm(box);
        return;
      }
      var t = tabMeta();
      if (t.kind === "mount") {
        box.innerHTML = "<h3>" + esc(t.label) + "</h3>" +
          '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(t.hint || "") + "</p>" +
          '<div id="pfMount" class="pf-mount"></div>';
        mountIds(t.mount || [], $("pfMount"));
        if (window.paintDeckForge) window.paintDeckForge();
        return;
      }
      var s = stageMeta();
      if (t.kind === "motif") s = t.id === s.id ? s : (function () {
        var stages = (state.rec && state.rec.stages) || [];
        var i;
        for (i = 0; i < stages.length; i++) {
          if (stages[i].id === t.id) return stages[i];
        }
        return { id: t.id, n: "", name: t.label, hint: t.hint };
      }());
      box.innerHTML = paintMotifForm(box, s);
      bindMotifForm(box);
    }

    function harvest() {
      var en = engine();
      if (!en.define) en.define = { text: "" };
      if (!en.stages) en.stages = {};
      if (!en.dest) en.dest = {};
      var ta = $("pfDefine");
      if (ta) en.define.text = ta.value;
      var note = $("pfNote");
      if (note) en.stages[state.stage] = note.value;
      var path = $("pfDestPath");
      if (path) en.dest.path = path.value;
      var destOn = document.querySelector('#pfDest input[name="pfDest"]:checked');
      if (destOn && destOn.value === "publish") {
        en.dest.kind = "staged";
      } else if (destOn) {
        en.dest.kind = destOn.value;
      } else if (!en.dest.kind) {
        en.dest.kind = "staged";
      }
      if (!en.step_setup) en.step_setup = {};
      var barOn = document.querySelector('#pfBar input[name="pfBar"]:checked');
      var nEl = $("pfNFree");
      if (barOn || nEl) {
        var st = en.step_setup[state.stage] || {};
        if (barOn) st.bar = barOn.value;
        if (nEl) st.n_free = nEl.value;
        st.via = "cli";
        en.step_setup[state.stage] = st;
      }
    }

    function say(text, bad) {
      var el = $("pfSay");
      if (!el) return;
      el.textContent = text || "";
      el.className = bad ? "tiny bad" : "tiny";
    }

    function save() {
      harvest();
      var en = engine();
      var body = {
        profile: state.profile,
        define: { text: (en.define && en.define.text) || "" },
        stages: en.stages || {},
        dest: en.dest || {},
        step_setup: en.step_setup || {}
      };
      say("saving…");
      api("/api/v1/profiles", { method: "POST", body: body }).then(function (rec) {
        if (rec && rec.error) {
          say(String(rec.error || rec.detail || "refused"), true);
          return;
        }
        state.rec = rec;
        state.dirty = false;
        paintSchematic();
        paintPick();
        paintMotifTop();
        paintStages();
        paintSkin();
        say("skin saved. Does not start MOTIF. Does not publish.");
        ageLine(rec);
      }).catch(function (e) {
        say(String((e && e.message) || e), true);
      });
    }

    var ROLE_HINT = {
      forge: "CCr (cli:grok) · adversarial adv_N · RESEARCH named :free pins",
      crucible: "plaintiff · defense · judge",
      diligence: "bull · bear · independent risk",
      docket: "applicant · examiner · prior-art",
      ups: "spectra · UPS-JUDGE (named load)",
      differentiator: "independent clinical opinions — anonymize is a gate",
      website: "copy · layout · a11y"
    };
    var WEBSITE_STACK = [
      { id: "mochahost", label: "MochaHost cPanel",
        hint: "Shared hosting. IMPLEMENT dest cpanel. This TUI does not publish." },
      { id: "namecheap", label: "NameCheap",
        hint: "Registrar. Keith's click. ~60 .coms named as a count, not listed here." },
      { id: "cloudflare", label: "CloudFlare",
        hint: "Registrar / DNS. Keith's click. Not a second Core." },
      { id: "oceanwp", label: "OceanWP Pro + Ecommerce Treasure Box",
        hint: "WordPress theme stack already paid. Dest is WordPress, not Framer lock-in." },
      { id: "generatepress", label: "GeneratePress Pro",
        hint: "Lifetime sub. WordPress dest. 10Web-like: AI on WP you own." },
      { id: "steal", label: "Stolen UX (not their runtime)",
        hint: "Durable: DEFINE brief → MOTIF RESEARCH. v0: generate to staged, you own it. Webflow: CMS as MOTIF stages. 10Web: WordPress dest. This TUI does not publish." }
    ];
    /* Named occupancy seats as cards (not dropdowns). Crucible + UPS + diligence +
       docket + differentiator + website. UPS-JUDGE stays NAMED. No invented occupancy POST. */
    var PROFILE_SEATS = {
      crucible: [
        { id: "plaintiff", label: "Plaintiff",
          hint: "Legal occupancy. Studio research_call FILE/INGEST. LEGAL_OMITTED." },
        { id: "defense", label: "Defense",
          hint: "Legal occupancy. Disagreeing seat. Not USPTO. Not mining." },
        { id: "judge", label: "Judge",
          hint: "Legal occupancy. Crucible judge seat. One Core :8770." }
      ],
      ups: [
        { id: "spectra", label: "Spectra",
          hint: "PhD / physics analyzer occupancy. July rebuild needs Keith — do not invent the app." },
        { id: "ups_judge", label: "UPS-JUDGE",
          hint: "NAMED until Physics Profile. GEM Vertex full-context judge — not a DOI/Crossref verifier." }
      ],
      diligence: [
        { id: "bull", label: "Bull",
          hint: "Deal diligence occupancy. Studio research_call FILE/INGEST." },
        { id: "bear", label: "Bear",
          hint: "Disagreeing diligence seat. bull · bear · independent risk." },
        { id: "independent_risk", label: "Independent risk",
          hint: "Independent risk reviewer. One Core :8770." }
      ],
      docket: [
        { id: "applicant", label: "Applicant",
          hint: "IP MOTIF occupancy. LEGAL_OMITTED. This TUI does not click USPTO." },
        { id: "examiner", label: "Examiner",
          hint: "Disagreeing docket seat. Not USPTO. Not mining." },
        { id: "prior_art", label: "Prior art",
          hint: "Prior-art reviewer. Not USPTO filing." }
      ],
      differentiator: [
        { id: "clinical_a", label: "Clinical A",
          hint: "Independent clinical opinion seat A. No PHI invented." },
        { id: "clinical_b", label: "Clinical B",
          hint: "Independent clinical opinion seat B. Disagreeing lane." },
        { id: "anonymize", label: "Anonymize",
          hint: "GATE — not a model. Anonymize before clinical lanes." }
      ],
      website: [
        { id: "copy", label: "Copy",
          hint: "Site MOTIF copy seat. Durable-like DEFINE brief. This TUI does not publish." },
        { id: "layout", label: "Layout",
          hint: "OceanWP Pro / GeneratePress Pro. WordPress dest, not Framer lock-in." },
        { id: "a11y", label: "a11y",
          hint: "Accessibility seat. Independent of copy/layout. Swiss cheese." }
      ]
    };

    function paintProfilePage(pid, rec) {
      var box = $("pfPage-" + pid);
      if (!box) return;
      rec = rec || {};
      var en = rec.engine || {};
      var stages = rec.stages || [];
      var setup = en.step_setup || {};
      var dests = rec.dest_catalog || [];
      var seats = PROFILE_SEATS[pid] || [];
      var h = '<div class="panel-hd"><h2>' + esc(rec.label || pid) +
        "</h2><span class=\"dim tiny\">MOTIF setup · SAVE does not start MOTIF</span></div>";
      h += '<div class="panel-bd">';
      h += '<div class="pf-motif-top pf-page-motif">';
      h += '<div class="pf-motif-h">MOTIF sequence</div><div class="pf-pipe" role="list">';
      stages.forEach(function (s, i) {
        if (i) h += '<span class="pf-arrow">→</span>';
        h += '<span class="pf-node" role="listitem"><b>' + s.n + "</b> " +
          esc(s.name) + "</span>";
      });
      h += "</div></div>";
      h += '<p class="dim tiny">Each MOTIF step is its own section: data folders, files, prompt, and who fills the seats. ' +
        esc(ROLE_HINT[pid] || "") + "</p>";
      if (seats.length) {
        h += '<div class="set-cards pf-seat-cards" role="list">';
        seats.forEach(function (seat) {
          h += '<div class="set-card pf-seat-card" role="listitem" data-seat="' +
            esc(seat.id) + '"><h3>' + esc(seat.label) + "</h3>" +
            '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(seat.hint || "") + "</p></div>";
        });
        h += "</div>";
      }
      if (pid === "website") {
        h += '<div class="set-cards pf-seat-cards" role="list">';
        WEBSITE_STACK.forEach(function (card) {
          h += '<div class="set-card pf-seat-card" role="listitem" data-stack="' +
            esc(card.id) + '"><h3>' + esc(card.label) + "</h3>" +
            '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(card.hint || "") + "</p></div>";
        });
        h += "</div>";
        h += '<p class="dim tiny">Own-your-stack: MochaHost cPanel + NameCheap/CloudFlare + OceanWP Pro + GeneratePress Pro. ~60 .coms is a named count, not a fabricated list. 10Web-like WordPress dest. Durable: DEFINE → RESEARCH. v0: staged, you own it. This TUI does not publish.</p>';
        h += '<div class="formrow"><button type="button" class="studio-go pf-jump-rc">' +
          "Studio research_call · DEFINE brief</button></div>";
      }
      if (pid === "ups" || pid === "crucible" || pid === "diligence" || pid === "docket") {
        h += '<div class="formrow"><button type="button" class="studio-go pf-jump-rc">' +
          "Studio research_call · FILE / INGEST</button></div>";
        var jumpNote = "Not a second Core. ";
        if (pid === "ups") {
          jumpNote += "UPS-JUDGE is NAMED until Physics Profile. ";
        } else if (pid === "crucible" || pid === "docket") {
          jumpNote += "Legal invoke LEGAL_OMITTED. Not USPTO. This TUI does not click USPTO. ";
        } else {
          jumpNote += "Deal diligence ingest via research_call FILE/INGEST. ";
        }
        h += '<p class="dim tiny">MOTIF RESEARCH ingest is GET/POST /api/v1/research_call on Core :8770. ' +
          jumpNote +
          "Crucible and UPS are both occupancy, not one-or-the-other.</p>";
      }
      if (pid === "crucible") {
        h += '<section class="pf-step pf-cru-round" id="pfCruRound">';
        h += "<h3>RUN CRUCIBLE ROUND</h3>";
        h += '<p class="dim tiny">POST /api/v1/crucible — composed critics on Core. Empty critics = the attached pool. Sources are docs-role names only (not V:\\\\, not Legal). 501 CRUCIBLE_NOT_RUNNABLE if none attached. Not occupancy POST. Not USPTO.</p>';
        h += '<label class="dim tiny">sources (one docs name per line)<textarea id="pfCruSources" rows="3" spellcheck="false">FINAL_ARCHITECTURE.md</textarea></label>';
        h += '<div class="mr-chiprow" id="pfCruDocChips">';
        ["FINAL_ARCHITECTURE.md", "COSMOS_PIPELINE.md", "CCR.md", "MOTIF.md",
         "AGENT_BOUNDARIES.md"].forEach(function (n, i) {
          h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (i === 0 ? " on" : "") +
            '" data-doc="' + esc(n) + '">' + esc(n) + "</button>";
        });
        h += "</div>";
        h += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" id="pfCruRun">RUN ROUND</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="studio-go" id="pfCruRuns">open RUNS</button>';
        h += '<div class="tiny" id="pfCruRunSay"></div>';
        h += '<pre class="tiny" id="pfCruRunLog"></pre>';
        h += "</section>";
      }
      stages.forEach(function (s) {
        var st = setup[s.id] || {};
        h += '<section class="pf-step" data-step="' + esc(s.id) + '">';
        h += "<h3>" + s.n + " · " + esc(s.name) + "</h3>";
        h += '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(s.hint || "") + "</p>";
        if (s.id === "define") {
          h += '<label class="dim tiny">PROBLEM STATEMENT / STATED GOAL<textarea class="pf-page-define" rows="8" spellcheck="false">' +
            esc((en.define && en.define.text) || "") + "</textarea></label>";
        }
        if (s.id === "research" && (pid === "ups" || pid === "crucible"
            || pid === "diligence" || pid === "docket")) {
          h += '<div class="formrow"><button type="button" class="studio-go pf-jump-rc">' +
            "jump · Studio research_call FILE / INGEST</button></div>";
        }
        h += '<label class="dim tiny">prompt instructions<textarea class="pf-page-prompt" rows="4" spellcheck="false">' +
          esc(st.prompt || (en.stages && en.stages[s.id]) || "") + "</textarea></label>";
        h += '<label class="dim tiny">data folders (path)<input class="pf-page-folders" type="text" spellcheck="false" value="' +
          esc(st.folders || "") + '"></label>';
        h += '<label class="dim tiny">files (names; upload lists names only — no blob store)<input class="pf-page-files" type="text" spellcheck="false" value="' +
          esc(st.files || "") + '"><input class="pf-page-upload" type="file" multiple></label>';
        h += '<label class="dim tiny">agent roles / who fills them<textarea class="pf-page-roles" rows="3" spellcheck="false" placeholder="' +
          esc(ROLE_HINT[pid] || "named seats") + '">' +
          esc(st.roles || "") + "</textarea></label>";
        if (s.id === "improve") {
          h += '<div class="studio-targets pf-page-dest">';
          dests.forEach(function (d) {
            var kind = (en.dest && en.dest.kind) || "staged";
            var on = kind === d.id;
            var lab = d.label || d.id;
            if (d.id === "staged") lab += " · READY";
            if (d.id === "publish") lab += " · REFUSED this TUI";
            h += '<label><input type="radio" name="pfPageDest-' + esc(pid) + '" value="' +
              esc(d.id) + '"' + (on ? " checked" : "") +
              (d.id === "publish" ? " data-publish-refuse=\"1\"" : "") +
              "> " + esc(lab) + "</label>";
          });
          h += "</div>";
          h += '<label class="dim tiny">dest path<textarea class="pf-page-destpath" rows="2">' +
            esc((en.dest && en.dest.path) || "") + "</textarea></label>";
        }
        h += "</section>";
      });
      h += '<div class="formrow"><button type="button" class="studio-go pf-page-save">SAVE SETUP</button></div>';
      h += '<div class="tiny pf-page-say"></div>';
      h += "</div>";
      box.innerHTML = h;
      var up = box.querySelectorAll(".pf-page-upload");
      up.forEach(function (inp) {
        inp.addEventListener("change", function () {
          var names = [];
          var i;
          for (i = 0; i < (inp.files || []).length; i++) names.push(inp.files[i].name);
          var dest = inp.parentNode && inp.parentNode.querySelector(".pf-page-files");
          if (dest) dest.value = names.join("\n");
        });
      });
      box.querySelectorAll(".pf-seat-card").forEach(function (card) {
        card.addEventListener("click", function () {
          var sid = card.getAttribute("data-seat") || "";
          var lab = (card.querySelector("h3") && card.querySelector("h3").textContent) || sid;
          var roles = box.querySelector(".pf-step[data-step=\"research\"] .pf-page-roles") ||
            box.querySelector(".pf-page-roles");
          if (roles) {
            var cur = String(roles.value || "").trim();
            if (cur.indexOf(lab) < 0) {
              roles.value = cur ? (cur + "\n" + lab) : lab;
            }
          }
          box.querySelectorAll(".pf-seat-card").forEach(function (c) {
            c.classList.toggle("on", c === card);
          });
          if (pid === "crucible" && typeof window.openModelRater === "function") {
            window.openModelRater();
          }
        });
      });
      box.querySelectorAll(".pf-jump-rc").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          if (typeof window.openStudioResearchCall === "function") {
            window.openStudioResearchCall();
          }
        });
      });
      var runsBtn = box.querySelector("#pfCruRuns");
      if (runsBtn) {
        runsBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
          if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("runs");
        });
      }
      var runBtn = box.querySelector("#pfCruRun");
      if (runBtn) {
        runBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
          var say = box.querySelector("#pfCruRunSay");
          var log = box.querySelector("#pfCruRunLog");
          var raw = ((box.querySelector("#pfCruSources") || {}).value || "").split(/\n/);
          var sources = raw.map(function (s) { return String(s || "").trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
          box.querySelectorAll("#pfCruDocChips [data-doc].on").forEach(function (c) {
            var n = c.getAttribute("data-doc") || "";
            if (n && sources.indexOf(n) < 0) sources.push(n);
          });
          var bad = sources.filter(function (s) {
            var l = s.toLowerCase();
            return l.indexOf("legal") >= 0 || l.indexOf(":\\") >= 0
              || s.charAt(0) === "/" || s.indexOf("..") >= 0;
          });
          if (bad.length) {
            if (say) say.textContent = "LEGAL_OMITTED / IDENTITY_MISMATCH — docs-role names only";
            return;
          }
          if (!sources.length) sources = ["FINAL_ARCHITECTURE.md"];
          if (say) say.textContent = "POST /api/v1/crucible …";
          api("/api/v1/crucible", {
            method: "POST",
            body: { sources: sources, priority: "high" }
          }).then(function (rec) {
            if (say) {
              say.textContent = rec && rec.job_id
                ? ("201 job " + rec.job_id + " · " + (rec.outcome || rec.error || "queued"))
                : String((rec && (rec.error || rec.detail)) || "returned");
            }
            if (log) log.textContent = JSON.stringify({
              job_id: rec && rec.job_id,
              outcome: rec && rec.outcome,
              error: rec && rec.error,
              detail: rec && rec.detail
            }, null, 2);
          }).catch(function (e) {
            if (say) say.textContent = String((e && e.message) || e);
          });
        });
      }
      var saveBtn = box.querySelector(".pf-page-save");
      if (saveBtn) saveBtn.addEventListener("click", function () {
        harvestPage(box, pid, rec);
        var en = rec.engine || {};
        var sayEl = box.querySelector(".pf-page-say");
        api("/api/v1/profiles", {
          method: "POST",
          body: {
            profile: pid,
            define: { text: (en.define && en.define.text) || "" },
            stages: en.stages || {},
            dest: en.dest || {},
            step_setup: en.step_setup || {}
          }
        }).then(function (out) {
          if (out && out.error) {
            if (sayEl) sayEl.textContent = String(out.error || out.detail || "refused");
            return;
          }
          paintProfilePage(pid, out);
          if (box.querySelector(".pf-page-say")) {
            box.querySelector(".pf-page-say").textContent =
              "skin saved. Does not start MOTIF. Does not publish.";
          }
        }).catch(function (e) {
          if (sayEl) sayEl.textContent = String((e && e.message) || e);
        });
      });
    }

    function harvestPage(box, pid, rec) {
      rec = rec || {};
      if (!rec.engine) rec.engine = {};
      var en = rec.engine;
      if (!en.step_setup) en.step_setup = {};
      if (!en.stages) en.stages = {};
      if (!en.dest) en.dest = {};
      var def = box.querySelector(".pf-page-define");
      if (def) {
        if (!en.define) en.define = {};
        en.define.text = def.value;
      }
      box.querySelectorAll(".pf-step").forEach(function (sec) {
        var sid = sec.getAttribute("data-step");
        if (!sid) return;
        var st = en.step_setup[sid] || {};
        var p = sec.querySelector(".pf-page-prompt");
        var f = sec.querySelector(".pf-page-folders");
        var files = sec.querySelector(".pf-page-files");
        var r = sec.querySelector(".pf-page-roles");
        if (p) {
          st.prompt = p.value;
          en.stages[sid] = p.value;
        }
        if (f) st.folders = f.value;
        if (files) st.files = files.value;
        if (r) st.roles = r.value;
        en.step_setup[sid] = st;
      });
      var destOn = box.querySelector(".pf-page-dest input:checked");
      if (destOn && destOn.value === "publish") {
        en.dest.kind = "staged";
      } else if (destOn) {
        en.dest.kind = destOn.value;
      } else if (!en.dest.kind) {
        en.dest.kind = "staged";
      }
      var path = box.querySelector(".pf-page-destpath");
      if (path) en.dest.path = path.value;
    }

    function ageLine(rec) {
      var age = $("pfAge");
      if (!age) return;
      age.textContent = rec && rec.kind === "NO_SOURCE"
        ? "GET /api/v1/profiles · no skin yet — SAVE SKIN"
        : ("GET /api/v1/profiles · " + ((rec && rec.label) || state.profile) +
          " · " + ((rec && rec.engine && rec.engine.saved_at) || rec.kind || "ok"));
    }

    function load(after) {
      if (typeof window.deckTabVisible === "function" &&
          !window.deckTabVisible("profiles") &&
          !window.deckTabVisible(state.profile)) return;
      var age = $("pfAge");
      if (age) age.textContent = "loading…";
      api("/api/v1/profiles?profile=" + encodeURIComponent(state.profile)).then(function (rec) {
        state.rec = rec;
        paintSchematic();
        paintPick();
        paintMotifTop();
        paintStages();
        paintSkin();
        ageLine(rec);
        if (after) after();
      }).catch(function (e) {
        if (age) age.textContent = String((e && e.message) || e);
      });
    }

    load();
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_settings.js =====
/* Settings: Linear-style section rail + OpenRouter Activity metric cards.
   GET never invents tokens. Keith does money. Does not complete a charge. */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  var POLL_MS = 10000;
  var state = { api: null, timer: null, page: "profile", open: false };
  var PAGE_MAP = {
    profile: { account: 1, pins: 1 },
    billing: { plan: 1, pay: 1 },
    quota: { usage: 1 }
  };

  function card(title, val, note) {
    return '<article class="set-card"><h3>' + esc(title) + "</h3>" +
      '<p class="set-val">' + esc(val) + "</p>" +
      (note ? '<p class="dim tiny">' + esc(note) + "</p>" : "") +
      "</article>";
  }
  function metric(k, v, note) {
    return '<article class="set-metric"><span class="set-k">' + esc(k) +
      "</span><b>" + esc(v) + "</b>" +
      (note ? '<span class="dim tiny">' + esc(note) + "</span>" : "") +
      "</article>";
  }

  function showPage(id) {
    state.page = id || "profile";
    if (!PAGE_MAP[state.page]) state.page = "profile";
    var nav = $("settingsNav");
    if (nav) {
      nav.querySelectorAll("[data-set]").forEach(function (b) {
        b.classList.toggle("on", b.getAttribute("data-set") === state.page);
      });
    }
    var root = $("bd-settings");
    if (!root) return;
    var want = PAGE_MAP[state.page] || PAGE_MAP.profile;
    root.querySelectorAll(".set-page").forEach(function (p) {
      p.classList.toggle("on", !!want[p.getAttribute("data-set")]);
    });
  }

  function paint(pair) {
    var st = (pair && pair[0]) || {};
    var us = (pair && pair[1]) || {};
    var sp = (pair && pair[2]) || {};
    var cred = (pair && pair[3]) || {};
    var age = $("settingsAge");
    var acc = $("settingsAccount");
    if (acc) {
      acc.innerHTML =
        card("Tree", st.tree_id || "UNMEASURED", "GET /status tree_id") +
        card("Core", st.ready ? "ready · :8770" : "UNMEASURED", "live Core") +
        card("Account", "Keith BBF — operator", "Secrets stay on the rail.");
    }
    var keys = $("settingsKeys");
    if (keys) {
      var rows = cred.sources || [];
      if (!rows.length) {
        keys.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">' +
          esc((cred.kind || "unread") + " — GET /api/v1/cred") + "</div>";
      } else {
        keys.innerHTML = rows.slice(0, 16).map(function (r) {
          return card(r.label || r.id, r.led || "NO_SOURCE",
            (r.kind || "API") + (r.docs ? " · Keith pastes" : ""));
        }).join("");
      }
    }
    var box = $("settingsUsage");
    if (box) {
      if (us.kind === "MEASURED") {
        box.innerHTML =
          metric("Spend", us.cost == null ? "UNMEASURED" : ("$" + us.cost), "GET /usage") +
          metric("Tokens", us.total_tokens == null ? "UNMEASURED" : String(us.total_tokens), "prompt + completion") +
          metric("In", us.prompt_tokens == null ? "UNMEASURED" : String(us.prompt_tokens), "prompt_tokens") +
          metric("Out", us.completion_tokens == null ? "UNMEASURED" : String(us.completion_tokens), "completion_tokens") +
          metric("Cached", us.cached_tokens == null ? "UNMEASURED" : String(us.cached_tokens), "cached_tokens") +
          metric("n", us.n_obs == null ? "UNMEASURED" : String(us.n_obs), "observations");
        var br = $("settingsUsageBreakdown");
        if (br) {
          var rows = us.by_source || us.per_source || us.sources ||
            us.by_account || us.per_account || us.accounts;
          if (!Array.isArray(rows) || !rows.length) {
            br.innerHTML =
              card("API", "UNMEASURED", "Core did not send a per-API breakdown") +
              card("Sub-account", "UNMEASURED", "Core did not send a per-account breakdown");
          } else {
            br.innerHTML = rows.map(function (r) {
              r = r || {};
              return card(r.api || r.source || r.account || r.name || "UNMEASURED",
                "in " + (r.prompt_tokens == null ? "UNMEASURED" : r.prompt_tokens) +
                " · out " + (r.completion_tokens == null ? "UNMEASURED" : r.completion_tokens) +
                " · cached " + (r.cached_tokens == null ? "UNMEASURED" : r.cached_tokens),
                "spend " + (r.cost == null ? "UNMEASURED" : r.cost));
            }).join("");
          }
        }
      } else {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">' +
          esc((us.kind || "UNMEASURED") + " — GET /api/v1/usage n_obs=" +
            (us.n_obs == null ? "unread" : us.n_obs)) +
          ". Does not invent tokens.</div>";
      }
    }
    var hours = "UNMEASURED";
    if (sp && sp.uptime_s != null && isFinite(Number(sp.uptime_s))) {
      hours = (Number(sp.uptime_s) / 3600).toFixed(2) + " h (spend fold)";
    }
    var tm = $("settingsSubTime");
    if (tm) {
      tm.innerHTML =
        card("Hours", hours, "not a remaining-hours bar") +
        card("Term", "UNMEASURED — Keith names the term", "");
    }
    if (age) {
      age.textContent = "GET /status " + (st.ready ? "ready" : "unread") +
        " · /usage " + (us.kind || "unread") +
        " · /spend " + (sp && (sp.kind || "ok") || "unread") +
        " · /cred " + (cred.n != null ? ("n=" + cred.n) : "unread");
    }
  }

  function tabOn() {
    if (state.open) return true;
    return typeof window.deckTabVisible === "function" && window.deckTabVisible("settings");
  }

  function ensurePopup() {
    var pop = $("cdeck-settings-pop");
    var panel = $("panel-settings");
    if (!panel) return null;
    if (pop && pop.contains(panel)) return pop;
    pop = document.createElement("div");
    pop.id = "cdeck-settings-pop";
    pop.setAttribute("role", "dialog");
    pop.setAttribute("aria-modal", "true");
    pop.setAttribute("aria-labelledby", "settingsPopTitle");
    pop.setAttribute("data-no-pane", "true");
    if (panel.parentNode) panel.parentNode.insertBefore(pop, panel);
    pop.appendChild(panel);
    document.body.appendChild(pop);
    return pop;
  }

  function openPopup() {
    var pop = ensurePopup();
    if (!pop) return;
    state.open = true;
    pop.classList.add("on");
    showPage(state.page);
    tick();
  }

  function closePopup(restore) {
    var pop = $("cdeck-settings-pop");
    state.open = false;
    if (pop) pop.classList.remove("on");
    if (restore !== false && window.deckSettingsReturnTab && window.showDeckTab) {
      var back = window.deckSettingsReturnTab();
      if (back && back !== "settings") window.showDeckTab(back);
    }
  }

  window.openDeckSettingsPopup = openPopup;
  window.closeDeckSettingsPopup = closePopup;
  function tick() {
    if (!tabOn()) return;
    if (typeof state.api !== "function") return;
    Promise.all([
      state.api("/api/v1/status").catch(function () { return null; }),
      state.api("/api/v1/usage").catch(function () { return null; }),
      state.api("/api/v1/spend").catch(function () { return null; }),
      state.api("/api/v1/cred").catch(function () { return null; })
    ]).then(paint).catch(function (e) {
      var age = $("settingsAge");
      if (age) age.textContent = String((e && e.message) || e);
    });
  }

  function bindNav() {
    var nav = $("settingsNav");
    if (!nav || nav._bound) return;
    nav._bound = true;
    nav.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
      var b = e.target && e.target.closest ? e.target.closest("[data-set]") : null;
      if (!b) return;
      showPage(b.getAttribute("data-set"));
    });
  }

  window.initDeckSettings = function (api) {
    if (!api || !$("panel-settings")) return;
    state.api = api;
    ensurePopup();
    bindNav();
    var closer = $("settingsClose");
    if (closer && !closer._bound) {
      closer._bound = true;
      closer.addEventListener("click", function () { closePopup(true); });
    }
    if (!document._settingsEsc) {
      document._settingsEsc = true;
      document.addEventListener("keydown", function (e) {
        if (e.key === "Escape" && state.open) closePopup(true);
      });
    }
    showPage(state.page);
    tick();
    if (state.timer) clearInterval(state.timer);
    state.timer = setInterval(tick, POLL_MS);
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/deck_session_kit.js =====
/* Recents COS / autosave / auto-resession kit. GET/POST /api/v1/session_kit. */
(function () {
  "use strict";
  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  var COS = [
    { id: "rold", label: "ROLD — Rule of Law Desk" },
    { id: "tidyup", label: "TidyUP" },
    { id: "tu2", label: "TU2" },
    { id: "bu", label: "BU — BootUP pointer" }
  ];
  var AUTOSAVE = [5, 10, 20, 30];
  var state = { api: null, kit: null, dirty: false, say: null };

  function kit() {
    return state.kit || {
      cos: { rold: true, tidyup: true, tu2: true, bu: true },
      autosave_min: 10,
      resession: { time_s: 0, context_tokens: 0, on_compaction: true, on_token_count: true }
    };
  }
  function paintSay() {
    var el = $("sessionKitSay");
    if (!el) return;
    var s = state.say;
    if (!s) { el.textContent = ""; return; }
    if (s.state === "sending") { el.className = "dim tiny"; el.textContent = "POST /api/v1/session_kit …"; return; }
    if (s.state === "bad") { el.className = "tiny bad"; el.textContent = "REFUSED — " + s.text; return; }
    el.className = "tiny ok";
    el.textContent = s.text;
  }
  function paint() {
    var k = kit();
    var box = $("sessionCos");
    if (box && document.activeElement !== box) {
      var h = "";
      COS.forEach(function (c) {
        h += '<label><input type="checkbox" data-cos="' + esc(c.id) + '"' +
          (k.cos && k.cos[c.id] ? " checked" : "") + "> " + esc(c.label) + "</label>";
      });
      box.innerHTML = h;
      box.querySelectorAll("input").forEach(function (cb) {
        cb.addEventListener("change", function () {
          kit().cos[cb.getAttribute("data-cos")] = !!cb.checked;
          state.dirty = true;
        });
      });
    }
    var as = $("sessionAutosave");
    if (as) {
      var ah = "";
      AUTOSAVE.forEach(function (n) {
        ah += '<label><input type="radio" name="sessAuto" value="' + n + '"' +
          (k.autosave_min === n ? " checked" : "") + "> " + n + " min</label>";
      });
      as.innerHTML = ah;
      as.querySelectorAll("input").forEach(function (inp) {
        inp.addEventListener("change", function () {
          kit().autosave_min = parseInt(inp.value, 10);
          state.dirty = true;
        });
      });
    }
    var t = $("sessionTimeS");
    var c = $("sessionCtxTok");
    var oc = $("sessionOnCompaction");
    var ot = $("sessionOnTokens");
    var rs = k.resession || {};
    if (t && document.activeElement !== t) t.value = String(rs.time_s || 0);
    if (c && document.activeElement !== c) c.value = String(rs.context_tokens || 0);
    if (oc) oc.checked = rs.on_compaction !== false;
    if (ot) ot.checked = rs.on_token_count !== false;
    var age = $("sessionKitAge");
    if (age && state.kit) {
      age.textContent = "GET /api/v1/session_kit · " + (state.kit.kind || "ok") +
        (state.kit.updated_at ? " · " + state.kit.updated_at : "");
    }
    paintSay();
  }
  function save() {
    var k = kit();
    var auto = document.querySelector('#sessionAutosave input:checked');
    if (auto) k.autosave_min = parseInt(auto.value, 10);
    var t = $("sessionTimeS");
    var c = $("sessionCtxTok");
    k.resession = k.resession || {};
    if (t) k.resession.time_s = parseInt(t.value, 10) || 0;
    if (c) k.resession.context_tokens = parseInt(c.value, 10) || 0;
    var oc = $("sessionOnCompaction");
    var ot = $("sessionOnTokens");
    if (oc) k.resession.on_compaction = !!oc.checked;
    if (ot) k.resession.on_token_count = !!ot.checked;
    if (!state.api) return;
    state.say = { state: "sending" };
    paintSay();
    state.api("/api/v1/session_kit", { method: "POST", body: {
      cos: k.cos, autosave_min: k.autosave_min, resession: k.resession
    }}).then(function (rec) {
      if (rec && rec.error) {
        state.say = { state: "bad", text: String(rec.error || rec.detail) };
      } else {
        state.kit = rec;
        state.dirty = false;
        state.say = { state: "ok", text: "SESSION KIT saved. Does not fire a resession." };
      }
      paint();
    }).catch(function (e) {
      state.say = { state: "bad", text: String((e && e.message) || e) };
      paintSay();
    });
  }
  window.initDeckSessionKit = function (api) {
    state.api = api || null;
    var saveB = $("sessionKitSave");
    if (saveB && !saveB._on) {
      saveB._on = true;
      saveB.addEventListener("click", save);
    }
    paint();
    if (!state.api) return;
    if (typeof window.deckTabVisible === "function" && !window.deckTabVisible("recents")) return;
    state.api("/api/v1/session_kit").then(function (rec) {
      state.kit = rec;
      paint();
    }).catch(function () { paint(); });
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/model_rater.js =====
/* cDeck MODEL RATER pane — OpenRouter catalog via Core, not a direct vendor call.
   Catalog is a spreadsheet pane at the bottom of the Models tab: one row per
   model, buttons on the row. No 400-row dropdown. Filters and vias are chips. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  function $(id) { return document.getElementById(id); }
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s == null ? "" : s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return ({ "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;" })[c];
    });
  }
  function axis(v) {
    if (v == null || v === "") return '<span class="dim">UNMEASURED</span>';
    var n = Number(v);
    return isFinite(n) ? (Math.round(n * 10) / 10).toFixed(n % 1 ? 1 : 0) : esc(v);
  }
  function unixShort(v) {
    if (v == null || v === "") return '<span class="dim">UNMEASURED</span>';
    var n = Number(v);
    if (!isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return '<span class="dim">UNMEASURED</span>';
    var d = new Date(n * 1000);
    return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? esc(v) : d.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  }
  function chipOn(row, attr, val) {
    if (!row) return;
    row.querySelectorAll(".chip").forEach(function (b) {
      b.classList.toggle("on", String(b.getAttribute(attr) || "") === String(val || ""));
    });
  }
  function chipVal(row, attr) {
    if (!row) return "";
    var on = row.querySelector(".chip.on");
    return on ? (on.getAttribute(attr) || "") : "";
  }

  window.initModelRater = function (api) {
    if (!api || !$("panel-model-rater")) return;
    var selected = "";
    var seatSel = { profile: "crucible", seat: "plaintiff" };
    var loaded = false;
    var sort = "quality";
    var desc = false;
    var type = "coding";
    var category = "programming";
    var family = "";
    var showBanned = false;
    var pulledAA = false;
    var viaOptions = [];
    var viaPick = { 1: "", 2: "", 3: "" };
    var seatCache = [];
    var effort = "medium";
    var capUsd = "0";
    var tin = 4000;
    var tout = 800;
    var poroLoc = "0";
    var poroSev = "1";
    var policy = {
      favored_models: [], favored_families: [],
      banned_models: [], banned_families: []
    };
    var lastModels = [];

    function openPane() {
      var more = $("cdeck-more");
      if (more) {
        more.classList.remove("min");
        if (window.applyWinFloor) window.applyWinFloor();
      }
      if (window.showDeckTab) window.showDeckTab("models");
      var pan = $("panel-model-rater");
      if (pan && pan.scrollIntoView) pan.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth", block: "start" });
      load(false);
    }
    window.openModelRater = openPane;

    function qs() {
      var p = new URLSearchParams();
      p.set("sort", sort || "quality");
      if (desc) p.set("desc", "1");
      p.set("type", type || "");
      p.set("category", category || "");
      if (family) p.set("q", family);
      if (showBanned) p.set("show_banned", "1");
      p.set("limit", "250");
      return "/api/v1/model_rater?" + p.toString();
    }

    function markSortHead() {
      chipOn($("mrSort"), "data-sort", sort);
      var table = $("mrTable");
      if (!table) return;
      table.querySelectorAll("th[data-sort]").forEach(function (th) {
        var on = th.getAttribute("data-sort") === sort;
        th.classList.toggle("on", on);
        th.setAttribute("aria-sort", on ? (desc ? "ascending" : "descending") : "none");
      });
    }

    var modelCaps = {};

    function fillVia(which, cur) {
      var row = $("mrVia" + (which === 1 ? "" : String(which)));
      if (!row) return;
      var opts = [{ id: "", label: "via —" }].concat(viaOptions);
      row.innerHTML = opts.map(function (v) {
        return '<button type="button" class="chip' +
          (v.id === (cur || "") ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-via="' + esc(v.id) + '">' + esc(v.label || v.id || "via —") +
          "</button>";
      }).join("");
      row.querySelectorAll(".chip").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          viaPick[which] = b.getAttribute("data-via") || "";
          chipOn(row, "data-via", viaPick[which]);
        });
      });
    }

    function paintSeats(seats) {
      var box = $("mrSeats");
      if (!box) return;
      var groups = [];
      var seen = {};
      (seats || []).forEach(function (s) {
        var g = s.group || s.profile || "roles";
        if (!seen[g]) { seen[g] = 1; groups.push(g); }
      });
      var h = "";
      groups.forEach(function (g) {
        h += '<div class="mr-group">' + esc(g) + "</div>";
        (seats || []).forEach(function (s) {
          if ((s.group || s.profile || "roles") !== g) return;
          var on = s.profile === seatSel.profile && s.seat === seatSel.seat ? " on" : "";
          var cap = s.cap_usd != null ? " · $" + s.cap_usd : "";
          var ef = s.effort ? " · " + s.effort : "";
          var fb = (s.model_2 || s.model_3)
            ? " · fb " + (s.model_2 || "—") + " / " + (s.model_3 || "—")
            : "";
          h += '<div class="mr-seat-row' + on + '" data-profile="' +
            esc(s.profile) + '" data-seat="' + esc(s.seat) + '">' +
            '<span class="mr-seat-lab">' + esc(s.label) + "</span>" +
            '<span class="mr-seat-picks">' + esc(s.model || "unassigned") +
            " · " + esc(s.via || "via —") +
            esc(cap) + esc(ef) + esc(fb) + "</span></div>";
        });
      });
      box.innerHTML = h || '<div class="empty">no roles match</div>';
      seatCache = seats || [];
      bindSeatRows(box);
    }

    function bindSeatRows(box) {
      if (!box) return;
      box.querySelectorAll(".mr-seat-row").forEach(function (b) {
        b.onclick = function () {
          seatSel.profile = b.getAttribute("data-profile");
          seatSel.seat = b.getAttribute("data-seat");
          box.querySelectorAll(".mr-seat-row").forEach(function (x) {
            x.classList.toggle("on", x === b);
          });
          var hit = (seatCache || []).filter(function (s) {
            return s.profile === seatSel.profile && s.seat === seatSel.seat;
          })[0];
          if (hit) {
            viaPick[1] = hit.via || "";
            viaPick[2] = hit.via_2 || "";
            viaPick[3] = hit.via_3 || "";
            fillVia(1, viaPick[1]);
            fillVia(2, viaPick[2]);
            fillVia(3, viaPick[3]);
            effort = hit.effort || "medium";
            chipOn($("mrEffort"), "data-effort", effort);
            if (hit.cap_usd != null) {
              capUsd = String(hit.cap_usd);
              chipOn($("mrCap"), "data-cap", capUsd);
            }
          }
        };
      });
    }

    function paintAgents(rec) {
      var box = $("mrAgentsBody");
      if (!box) return;
      var ag = (rec && rec.agents && rec.agents.agents) || [];
      if (!ag.length) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">agents unread — GET /api/v1/model_rater</div>';
        return;
      }
      box.innerHTML = ag.map(function (a) {
        var cls = a.led === "PRESENT" ? "ok" : "dim";
        return '<div class="cred-row"><span class="cred-led ' + cls + '"></span><b>' +
          esc(a.label) + "</b> <small>" + esc(a.kind) + " · " + esc(a.led) +
          (a.host ? " · " + esc(a.host) : "") + "</small></div>";
      }).join("");
    }

    function paintDefs(rec) {
      var box = $("mrDefsBody");
      if (!box) return;
      var defs = (rec && rec.bench_defs) || [];
      var cite = (rec && rec.bench_cite) || "";
      if (!defs.length) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">definitions unread — GET /api/v1/model_rater</div>';
        return;
      }
      var groups = [];
      var seen = {};
      defs.forEach(function (d) {
        var g = d.group || "Other";
        if (!seen[g]) { seen[g] = 1; groups.push(g); }
      });
      var h = "";
      groups.forEach(function (g) {
        h += '<div class="mr-def-group">' + esc(g) + "</div>";
        defs.forEach(function (d) {
          if ((d.group || "Other") !== g) return;
          h += '<div class="mr-def"><strong>' + esc(d.label || d.id) +
            "</strong> — " + esc(d.def || "") +
            (d.source ? ' <span class="dim">(' + esc(d.source) + ")</span>" : "") +
            "</div>";
        });
      });
      if (cite) h += '<p class="dim tiny">cite: ' + esc(cite) + "</p>";
      box.innerHTML = h;
    }

    function paintFam(models) {
      var row = $("mrFam");
      if (!row) return;
      var seen = {};
      var fams = [];
      (models || []).forEach(function (m) {
        var f = m.family || "";
        if (f && !seen[f]) { seen[f] = 1; fams.push(f); }
      });
      fams.sort();
      var h = '<button type="button" class="chip' + (family ? "" : " on") +
        '" data-fam="">all families</button>';
      fams.forEach(function (f) {
        h += '<button type="button" class="chip' + (family === f ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-fam="' + esc(f) + '">' + esc(f) + "</button>";
      });
      row.innerHTML = h;
      row.querySelectorAll(".chip").forEach(function (b) {
        b.addEventListener("click", function () {
          family = b.getAttribute("data-fam") || "";
          chipOn(row, "data-fam", family);
          load(false);
        });
      });
    }

    function paintRows(models) {
      lastModels = models || [];
      var box = $("mrCards") || $("mrRows");
      if (!box) return;
      if (!models || !models.length) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">no models match — catalog empty, stale, or banned-hidden</div>';
        return;
      }
      var heads = [
        ["name", "model"], ["name", "family"],
        ["quality", "Q"], ["coding", "COD"], ["intelligence", "INT"], ["agentic", "AGT"],
        ["quality_per_cost", "Q/$"], ["blended", "blend"],
        ["price", "in $/M"], ["price", "out $/M"], ["context", "ctx"],
        ["popularity", "weekly"], ["speed", "thru"], ["porosity", "porosity"],
        ["", "types"], ["", "hands"]
      ];
      var h = '<table class="mr-table" id="mrTable"><thead><tr>';
      heads.forEach(function (pair) {
        var key = pair[0];
        var lab = pair[1];
        if (key) {
          h += '<th data-sort="' + esc(key) + '">' + esc(lab) + "</th>";
        } else {
          h += "<th>" + esc(lab) + "</th>";
        }
      });
      h += "</tr></thead><tbody>";
      models.forEach(function (m) {
        var on = m.id === selected ? " on" : "";
        var mark = m.favored ? " ★" : (m.banned ? " BAN" : "");
        var cap = modelCaps[m.id] != null ? modelCaps[m.id] : "";
        var weekly = m.weekly_tokens != null ? m.weekly_tokens : m.popularity;
        var thru = m.throughput != null ? m.throughput : m.speed;
        h += '<tr class="mr-row' + on + (m.banned ? " ban" : "") +
          (m.favored ? " fav" : "") + '" data-id="' + esc(m.id) +
          '" data-family="' + esc(m.family || "") + '">';
        h += '<td class="mr-name" title="' + esc(m.id) + '"><strong>' +
          esc(m.name) + esc(mark) + "</strong>" +
          '<div class="dim tiny">' + esc(m.id) +
          (m.created ? " · " + unixShort(m.created) : "") + "</div></td>";
        h += "<td>" + esc(m.family || "") + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.quality) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.coding) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.intelligence) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.agentic) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.quality_per_cost) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.blended_per_m) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.prompt_per_m) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.completion_per_m) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.context) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(weekly) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(thru) + "</td>";
        h += "<td>" + axis(m.porosity) + "</td>";
        h += '<td class="dim tiny">' + esc((m.types || []).join(" · ")) + "</td>";
        h += '<td class="mr-row-hands">';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-assign" data-which="1">ASSIGN</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-assign" data-which="2">2ND</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-assign" data-which="3">3RD</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-fav">FAVOR</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-ban">BAN</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-favfam">FAVOR FAM</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-banfam">BAN FAM</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-cap" data-cap="0">cap off</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-cap' + (String(cap) === "1" ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-cap="1">$1</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-cap' + (String(cap) === "5" ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-cap="5">$5</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-cap' + (String(cap) === "10" ? " on" : "") +
          '" data-cap="10">$10</button>';
        h += '<button type="button" class="tiny mr-row-poro">RECORD POROSITY</button>';
        h += "</td></tr>";
      });
      h += "</tbody></table>";
      box.innerHTML = h;
      var table = $("mrTable");
      if (table) {
        table.querySelectorAll("th[data-sort]").forEach(function (th) {
          th.addEventListener("click", function () {
            var v = th.getAttribute("data-sort") || "quality";
            if (sort === v) desc = !desc;
            else { sort = v; desc = false; }
            chipOn($("mrSort"), "data-sort", sort);
            load(false);
          });
        });
      }
      box.querySelectorAll(".mr-row").forEach(function (row) {
        var id = row.getAttribute("data-id") || "";
        var fam = row.getAttribute("data-family") || "";
        row.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
          if (ev.target && ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest("button")) return;
          selected = id;
          box.querySelectorAll(".mr-row").forEach(function (x) {
            x.classList.toggle("on", x === row);
          });
          estimate();
        });
        row.querySelectorAll(".mr-row-assign").forEach(function (b) {
          b.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
            ev.stopPropagation();
            selected = id;
            assignWhich(parseInt(b.getAttribute("data-which"), 10) || 1);
          });
        });
        var fav = row.querySelector(".mr-row-fav");
        var ban = row.querySelector(".mr-row-ban");
        if (fav) fav.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
          ev.stopPropagation();
          postPolicy({ favored_models: toggleList(policy.favored_models, id) });
        });
        if (ban) ban.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
          ev.stopPropagation();
          postPolicy({ banned_models: toggleList(policy.banned_models, id) });
        });
        var ff = row.querySelector(".mr-row-favfam");
        var bf = row.querySelector(".mr-row-banfam");
        if (ff) ff.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
          ev.stopPropagation();
          if (!fam) return;
          postPolicy({ favored_families: toggleList(policy.favored_families, fam) });
        });
        if (bf) bf.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
          ev.stopPropagation();
          if (!fam) return;
          postPolicy({ banned_families: toggleList(policy.banned_families, fam) });
        });
        row.querySelectorAll(".mr-row-cap").forEach(function (b) {
          b.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
            ev.stopPropagation();
            selected = id;
            capUsd = b.getAttribute("data-cap") || "0";
            chipOn($("mrCap"), "data-cap", capUsd);
            setCap(id, capUsd);
          });
        });
        var poro = row.querySelector(".mr-row-poro");
        if (poro) poro.addEventListener("click", function (ev) {
          ev.stopPropagation();
          selected = id;
          recordPoro(id);
        });
      });
      markSortHead();
    }

    function paintComplement(rec) {
      var box = $("mrPoroC");
      if (!box) return;
      if (!rec) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">complement tensor C unread — GET /api/v1/porosity</div>';
        return;
      }
      var n = rec.n_obs != null ? rec.n_obs : rec.n;
      var h = '<p class="dim tiny">GET /api/v1/porosity — pair tensor T + complement C. ' +
        "UNMEASURED until observed. GET never invents a score.</p>";
      h += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">obs</span><span>' +
        esc(String(n != null ? n : "UNMEASURED")) + " · " +
        esc(rec.schema || "") + "</span></div>";
      if (rec.note) h += '<div class="dim tiny">' + esc(rec.note) + "</div>";
      box.innerHTML = h;
    }

    function seatedAgents() {
      var ids = [];
      var seen = {};
      (seatCache || []).forEach(function (s) {
        var m = s && s.model;
        if (!m || seen[m]) return;
        seen[m] = 1;
        ids.push(m);
      });
      if (selected && !seen[selected]) ids.push(selected);
      lastModels.slice(0, 8).forEach(function (m) {
        if (m && m.id && !seen[m.id] && m.favored) {
          seen[m.id] = 1;
          ids.push(m.id);
        }
      });
      return ids;
    }

    function paintCoverage(rec) {
      var box = $("mrCoverage");
      var age = $("mrCovAge");
      if (age) {
        age.textContent = rec
          ? ("GET /api/v1/porosity · " + (rec.kind || "ok") +
            " · pairs " + (rec.n_pairs != null ? rec.n_pairs : "UNMEASURED"))
          : "unread";
      }
      if (!box) return;
      if (!rec) {
        box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">coverage unread — GET /api/v1/porosity</div>';
        return;
      }
      var cov = rec.coverage || rec;
      var h = '<p class="dim tiny">' +
        esc(cov.goal || rec.note ||
          "Maximize error-discovery coverage per token. UNMEASURED until observed.") +
        "</p>";
      var order = cov.order || [];
      if (order.length) {
        h += '<div class="ops-row"><span class="ops-k">seat order</span><span>' +
          esc(order.join(" → ")) + "</span></div>";
      }
      var pairs = cov.pairs || rec.pairs || [];
      if (!pairs.length) {
        h += '<div class="empty">no agent pairs — assign seats or pick catalog rows. ' +
          "Does not invent a tensor.</div>";
        box.innerHTML = h;
        return;
      }
      h += '<div class="mr-table-wrap"><table class="mr-table"><thead><tr>' +
        "<th>a</th><th>b</th><th>axis</th><th>n</th><th>freq</th><th>|v|</th>" +
        "<th>xor</th><th>cofail</th><th>signed</th><th>kind</th></tr></thead><tbody>";
      pairs.forEach(function (p) {
        h += "<tr class=\"mr-row\">" +
          "<td>" + esc(p.model_a) + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + esc(p.model_b) + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + esc(p.axis || "") + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + esc(String(p.n != null ? p.n : "UNMEASURED")) + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + axis(p.freq) + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + axis(p.mag != null ? p.mag : p.orthogonality) + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + axis(p.xor_err) + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + axis(p.cofail) + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + axis(p.signed) + "</td>" +
          "<td>" + esc(p.kind || "UNMEASURED") + "</td></tr>";
      });
      h += "</tbody></table></div>";
      box.innerHTML = h;
    }

    function loadCoverage() {
      var ids = seatedAgents();
      var q = "/api/v1/porosity?profile=forge";
      if (ids.length >= 2) q += "&agents=" + encodeURIComponent(ids.join(","));
      return api(q).then(function (rec) {
        paintComplement(rec);
        paintCoverage(rec);
      }).catch(function () {
        paintComplement(null);
        paintCoverage(null);
      });
    }

    function load(forceRefresh) {
      var age = $("mrAge");
      if (age) age.textContent = "loading…";
      var go = forceRefresh
        ? api("/api/v1/model_rater/refresh", { method: "POST", body: {} }).then(function () {
          return api(qs());
        })
        : api(qs());
      return go.then(function (rec) {
        loaded = true;
        if (rec && !forceRefresh && !pulledAA && (rec.stale || !rec.quality_source)) {
          pulledAA = true;
          return api("/api/v1/model_rater/refresh", { method: "POST", body: {} })
            .then(function () { return api(qs()); });
        }
        return rec;
      }).then(function (rec) {
        if (age) {
          age.textContent = rec.stale
            ? "STALE " + (rec.fetched_at || "never")
            : ((rec.n_catalog || 0) + " models · Q on " +
              (rec.n_quality != null ? rec.n_quality : "—") +
              " · " + (rec.fetched_at || ""));
          age.className = rec.stale ? "age unknown" : "age";
        }
        modelCaps = rec.model_caps || {};
        policy = rec.policy || policy;
        viaOptions = rec.via_options || viaOptions;
        fillVia(1, viaPick[1]);
        fillVia(2, viaPick[2]);
        fillVia(3, viaPick[3]);
        paintSeats(rec.roles && rec.roles.length ? rec.roles : rec.seats);
        paintRows(rec.models);
        paintFam(rec.models);
        paintDefs(rec);
        paintAgents(rec);
        loadCoverage();
        markSortHead();
        chipOn($("mrType"), "data-type", type);
        chipOn($("mrCategory"), "data-cat", category);
        var nEl = $("mrRoleN");
        if (nEl) nEl.textContent = (rec.n_roles != null ? rec.n_roles : (rec.seats || []).length) +
          " roles · GET /api/v1/model_rater/roles";
        var say = $("mrCapSay");
        if (say && selected && modelCaps[selected] != null) {
          say.textContent = selected + " cap $" + modelCaps[selected] +
            " — rater limit, not the Core spend gate";
        }
      }).catch(function (e) {
        if (age) {
          age.textContent = String(e && e.message || e);
          age.className = "age unknown";
        }
      });
    }

    function estimate() {
      var el = $("mrEst");
      if (!el || !selected) return;
      api("/api/v1/model_rater/estimate", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { model: selected, tokens_in: tin, tokens_out: tout }
      }).then(function (rec) {
        el.textContent = rec.free
          ? selected + " · $0 (free card) · " + tin + "/" + tout + " tok"
          : selected + " · ~$" + rec.usd + " · " + tin + "/" + tout + " tok";
      }).catch(function (e) {
        el.textContent = String(e && e.message || e);
      });
    }

    function seatBody(which) {
      var body = {
        profile: seatSel.profile,
        seat: seatSel.seat,
        effort: effort || "",
        cap_usd: capUsd
      };
      if (which === 2) {
        body.model_2 = selected;
        body.via_2 = viaPick[2] || "";
      } else if (which === 3) {
        body.model_3 = selected;
        body.via_3 = viaPick[3] || "";
      } else {
