        body.model = selected;
        body.via = viaPick[1] || "";
      }
      return body;
    }

    function postPolicy(patch) {
      var body = {
        favored_models: policy.favored_models || [],
        favored_families: policy.favored_families || [],
        banned_models: policy.banned_models || [],
        banned_families: policy.banned_families || []
      };
      Object.keys(patch || {}).forEach(function (k) { body[k] = patch[k]; });
      return api("/api/v1/model_rater/policy", { method: "POST", body: body })
        .then(function () { return load(false); });
    }

    function toggleList(arr, val) {
      var out = [];
      var hit = false;
      var low = String(val || "").toLowerCase();
      (arr || []).forEach(function (x) {
        if (String(x).toLowerCase() === low) hit = true;
        else out.push(x);
      });
      if (!hit && val) out.push(val);
      return out;
    }

    function setCap(model, cap) {
      var say = $("mrCapSay");
      api("/api/v1/model_rater/cap", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { model: model, cap_usd: cap }
      }).then(function () {
        if (say) say.textContent = "cap saved for " + model + " — rater limit, not the Core spend gate";
        return load(false);
      }).catch(function (e) {
        if (say) say.textContent = String(e && e.message || e);
      });
    }

    function recordPoro(model) {
      var say = $("mrCapSay");
      api("/api/v1/model_rater/porosity", {
        method: "POST",
        body: { model: model, loc_per_100: poroLoc, severity: poroSev, source: "local" }
      }).then(function (rec) {
        var fold = rec && rec.fold;
        if (say) {
          say.textContent = model + " porosity " +
            (fold && fold.porosity != null ? fold.porosity : "UNMEASURED") +
            " — local obs, federation does not invent";
        }
        return load(false);
      }).catch(function (e) {
        if (say) say.textContent = String(e && e.message || e);
      });
    }

    if ($("mrRefresh")) $("mrRefresh").addEventListener("click", function () { load(true); });
    function bindChipRow(id, attr, apply) {
      var row = $(id);
      if (!row) return;
      row.addEventListener("click", function (e) {
        var b = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest(".chip");
        if (!b || !row.contains(b)) return;
        apply(b.getAttribute(attr) || "");
        chipOn(row, attr, b.getAttribute(attr) || "");
      });
    }
    bindChipRow("mrType", "data-type", function (v) { type = v; load(false); });
    bindChipRow("mrCategory", "data-cat", function (v) { category = v; load(false); });
    bindChipRow("mrSort", "data-sort", function (v) {
      if (sort === v) desc = !desc;
      else { sort = v || "quality"; desc = false; }
      load(false);
    });
    bindChipRow("mrEffort", "data-effort", function (v) { effort = v || "medium"; });
    bindChipRow("mrCap", "data-cap", function (v) { capUsd = v || "0"; });
    bindChipRow("mrTin", "data-tin", function (v) {
      tin = parseInt(v, 10) || 0;
      estimate();
    });
    bindChipRow("mrTout", "data-tout", function (v) {
      tout = parseInt(v, 10) || 0;
      estimate();
    });
    bindChipRow("mrPoroLoc", "data-loc", function (v) { poroLoc = v || "0"; });
    bindChipRow("mrPoroSev", "data-sev", function (v) { poroSev = v || "1"; });
    var bannedChip = $("mrShowBanned");
    if (bannedChip) bannedChip.addEventListener("click", function () {
      showBanned = !showBanned;
      bannedChip.classList.toggle("on", showBanned);
      load(false);
    });
    function searchRoles() {
      var nEl = $("mrRoleN");
      if (nEl) nEl.textContent = "searching roles…";
      return Promise.all([
        load(false),
        api("/api/v1/profiles").catch(function () { return null; }),
        api("/api/v1/studio").catch(function () { return null; })
      ]).then(function (parts) {
        var extra = [];
        var pf = parts[1];
        ((pf && pf.profiles) || []).forEach(function (p) {
          extra.push({
            group: "profiles", profile: p.id, seat: p.id,
            label: p.label || p.id, model: "", via: ""
          });
        });
        var st = parts[2] || {};
        (((st.research || {}).models) || []).forEach(function (m) {
          extra.push({
            group: "studio research", profile: "studio", seat: m.id,
            label: m.label || m.id, model: m.model || "", via: m.via || ""
          });
        });
        (((st.build || {}).agents) || []).forEach(function (m) {
          extra.push({
            group: "studio build", profile: "studio", seat: m.id,
            label: m.label || m.id, model: m.model || "", via: m.via || ""
          });
        });
        (((st.critics || {}).agents) || []).forEach(function (m) {
          extra.push({
            group: "studio critics", profile: "studio", seat: m.id,
            label: m.label || m.id, model: m.model || "", via: m.via || ""
          });
        });
        if (!extra.length) {
          if (nEl) nEl.textContent = (nEl.textContent || "roles") +
            " · SEARCH also GET /profiles + /studio — none extra matched";
          return;
        }
        var box = $("mrSeats");
        if (!box) return;
        var more = '<div class="mr-group">discovered · GET /profiles + GET /studio</div>';
        extra.forEach(function (s) {
          more += '<div class="mr-seat-row" data-profile="' + esc(s.profile) +
            '" data-seat="' + esc(s.seat) + '">' +
            '<span class="mr-seat-lab">' + esc(s.label) + "</span>" +
            '<span class="mr-seat-picks">' + esc(s.model || "unassigned") +
            " · " + esc(s.via || "via —") + "</span></div>";
        });
        box.insertAdjacentHTML("beforeend", more);
        seatCache = (seatCache || []).concat(extra);
        bindSeatRows(box);
        if (nEl) nEl.textContent = (nEl.textContent || "") +
          " · +" + extra.length + " from /profiles + /studio";
      });
    }
    if ($("mrRoleSearch")) $("mrRoleSearch").addEventListener("click", function () { searchRoles(); });
    function assignWhich(n) {
      if (!selected && n === 1) return;
      api("/api/v1/model_rater/seat", {
        method: "POST",
        body: seatBody(n)
      }).then(function () { return load(false); }).catch(function (e) {
        var age = $("mrAge");
        if (age) age.textContent = String(e && e.message || e);
      });
    }
    if ($("mrAssign")) $("mrAssign").addEventListener("click", function () { assignWhich(1); });
    if ($("mrAssign2")) $("mrAssign2").addEventListener("click", function () { assignWhich(2); });
    if ($("mrAssign3")) $("mrAssign3").addEventListener("click", function () { assignWhich(3); });
    if ($("mrSetCap")) $("mrSetCap").addEventListener("click", function () {
      if (!selected) return;
      setCap(selected, capUsd);
    });
    if ($("mrFav")) $("mrFav").addEventListener("click", function () {
      if (!selected) return;
      postPolicy({ favored_models: toggleList(policy.favored_models, selected) });
    });
    if ($("mrBan")) $("mrBan").addEventListener("click", function () {
      if (!selected) return;
      postPolicy({ banned_models: toggleList(policy.banned_models, selected) });
    });
    if ($("mrFavFam")) $("mrFavFam").addEventListener("click", function () {
      var card = document.querySelector("#mrCards .mr-row.on");
      var fam = card && card.getAttribute("data-family");
      if (!fam) return;
      postPolicy({ favored_families: toggleList(policy.favored_families, fam) });
    });
    if ($("mrPoro")) $("mrPoro").addEventListener("click", function () {
      if (!selected) return;
      recordPoro(selected);
    });
    if ($("mrBanFam")) $("mrBanFam").addEventListener("click", function () {
      var card = document.querySelector("#mrCards .mr-row.on");
      var fam = card && card.getAttribute("data-family");
      if (!fam) return;
      postPolicy({ banned_families: toggleList(policy.banned_families, fam) });
    });
    chipOn($("mrType"), "data-type", type);
    chipOn($("mrCategory"), "data-cat", category);
    chipOn($("mrSort"), "data-sort", sort);
    chipOn($("mrEffort"), "data-effort", effort);
    chipOn($("mrCap"), "data-cap", capUsd);
    chipOn($("mrTin"), "data-tin", String(tin));
    chipOn($("mrTout"), "data-tout", String(tout));
    if (typeof window.deckTabVisible !== "function" || window.deckTabVisible("models")) load(false);
  };
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/ui/kdash_native.js =====
/* cDeck boot — make the lifted KDash page work as a native dashboard.
   Not an overlay on dead BTS fetches. This file owns Core HTTP and the
   side-panel numbers. Signal Core art stays this file's drawCore (the lift).
   Jukebox YouTube is the lift in index.html — do not clobber #ytplayer. */
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var PLANETS = ["jupiter", "mars", "venus", "mercury", "saturn", "uranus", "neptune"];
  var player = null;
  var AC = null;
  var gainNode = null;
  var bufSrc = null;
  var buffers = {};
  var idx = 0;
  var mutedFlag = false;
  var volEl = document.getElementById("vol");
  var masterFlag = volEl ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, parseInt(volEl.value, 10) / 100)) : 0.8;
  var jukeVol = (function () {
    var s = parseInt(localStorage.getItem("jukeVol"), 10);
    return isNaN(s) ? 70 : Math.max(0, Math.min(100, s));
  })();
  var poller = null;
  var ytEmbed = window.embed;

  var win = document.getElementById("win");
  function applyWinFloor() {
    if (!win) return;
    win.style.position = "relative";
    win.style.top = "0";
    win.style.left = "0";
    win.style.width = "100%";
    win.style.borderRadius = "0";
    if (win.classList.contains("min")) {
      win.style.height = "auto";
      win.style.flex = "0 0 auto";
      win.style.minHeight = "0";
      win.style.maxHeight = "none";
      win.style.overflow = "visible";
    } else {
      win.style.height = "auto";
      win.style.flex = "1 1 auto";
      win.style.minHeight = "0";
      win.style.maxHeight = "none";
      win.style.overflow = "auto";
    }
  }
  applyWinFloor();
  window.applyWinFloor = applyWinFloor;

  function setText(id, text, color) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = text;
    if (color) el.style.color = color;
  }
  function setHtml(id, html) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.innerHTML = html;
  }
  function setNp(html) {
    setHtml("np", html);
    var m = document.getElementById("mNp");
    if (m) m.textContent = html.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, "").replace(/^♪\s*/, "");
  }

  function gain() {
    if (mutedFlag) return 0;
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, masterFlag * (jukeVol / 100)));
  }

  function ac() {
    var Ctor = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    if (!Ctor) return null;
    if (!AC) {
      try { AC = new Ctor(); } catch (e) { return null; }
    }
    if (AC.state === "suspended") {
      try { AC.resume(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (!gainNode) {
      gainNode = AC.createGain();
      gainNode.connect(AC.destination);
    }
    gainNode.gain.value = gain();
    return AC;
  }

  function applyVol() {
    if (gainNode) {
      try { gainNode.gain.value = gain(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (player) {
      try {
        player.muted = mutedFlag;
        player.volume = gain();
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    if (typeof window.applyJukeVol === "function") {
      try { window.applyJukeVol(); } catch (e) {}
    } else if (window.ytReady && window.ytP && window.ytP.setVolume) {
      try {
        window.ytP.setVolume(jukeVol);
        if (mutedFlag || jukeVol === 0) window.ytP.mute();
        else window.ytP.unMute();
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    var sl = document.getElementById("jvol");
    var out = document.getElementById("jvolv");
    if (sl) sl.value = String(jukeVol);
    if (out) out.textContent = String(jukeVol);
  }

  function planetSrc(name) {
    return "planets/" + name + ".mp3";
  }

  function ensurePlayer() {
    if (player) return player;
    player = document.getElementById("jukeEl");
    if (!player) {
      player = document.createElement("audio");
      player.id = "jukeEl";
      player.preload = "auto";
      player.controls = true;
      player.style.cssText = "width:100%;height:36px;flex:0 0 auto;outline:none";
    }
    var wrap = document.getElementById("ytwrap");
    if (wrap && wrap.parentNode && player.parentNode !== wrap.parentNode) {
      /* Never wipe #ytwrap — that destroys the YouTube IFrame player. */
      player.style.cssText = "display:none";
      wrap.parentNode.insertBefore(player, wrap.nextSibling);
    }
    return player;
  }

  function paintNow(name) {
    ensurePlayer();
    var label = document.getElementById("jukeNow");
    if (label) label.textContent = String(name || "").toUpperCase();
    document.querySelectorAll(".presets .pz").forEach(function (el) {
      el.style.borderColor = el.dataset.id === name ? "var(--cyan)" : "";
      el.style.color = el.dataset.id === name ? "var(--cyan)" : "";
    });
  }

  function stopBuf() {
    if (bufSrc) {
      try { bufSrc.onended = null; bufSrc.stop(0); } catch (e) {}
      bufSrc = null;
    }
  }

  function startSrc(a, src, name) {
    a.src = src;
    applyVol();
    paintNow(name);
    var pr = a.play();
    if (pr && pr.catch) {
      pr.catch(function (err) {
        setNp("♪ " + name + " — " + ((err && err.message) || "play failed"));
      });
    }
    setNp(mutedFlag ? "♪ <b>" + name + "</b> · MUTED" : "♪ <b>" + name + "</b>");
  }

  function playDecoded(name, buf) {
    var ctx = ac();
    if (!ctx || !buf) return false;
    stopBuf();
    if (player) {
      try { player.pause(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    bufSrc = ctx.createBufferSource();
    bufSrc.buffer = buf;
    bufSrc.connect(gainNode);
    bufSrc.onended = function () {
      bufSrc = null;
      if (!autoNext) return;
      playPlanetTrack(PLANETS[(idx + 1) % PLANETS.length]);
    };
    applyVol();
    bufSrc.start(0);
    paintNow(name);
    setNp(mutedFlag ? "♪ <b>" + name + "</b> · MUTED" : "♪ <b>" + name + "</b>");
    return true;
  }

  function playPlanetTrack(name) {
    if (PLANETS.indexOf(name) < 0) name = PLANETS[idx % PLANETS.length];
    idx = PLANETS.indexOf(name);
    ac();
    var a = ensurePlayer();
    stopBuf();
    a.onended = function () {
      if (!autoNext) return;
      playPlanetTrack(PLANETS[(idx + 1) % PLANETS.length]);
    };
    /* Play in the same turn as the click. Fetch-then-decodeAudioData ran after
       the user-activation window, so WebView2 blocked autoplay and AUTO looked
       dead. HTML5 Audio + relative planets/*.mp3 is the player. */
    startSrc(a, planetSrc(name), name);
  }

  window.embed = function (id, t) {
    var name = String(t || "").toLowerCase();
    if (PLANETS.indexOf(name) >= 0) {
      if (window.ytP && window.ytP.pauseVideo) {
        try { window.ytP.pauseVideo(); } catch (e) {}
      }
      playPlanetTrack(name);
      return;
    }
    if (player) {
      try { player.pause(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    stopBuf();
    if (typeof ytEmbed === "function") return ytEmbed(id, t);
  };

  var sl = document.getElementById("jvol");
  if (sl) {
    sl.oninput = function () {
      jukeVol = parseInt(this.value, 10);
      try { localStorage.setItem("jukeVol", String(jukeVol)); } catch (e) {}
      applyVol();
    };
  }
  applyVol();

  /* Title-bar mute / volume were script-scoped and never reached the jukebox. */
  var snd = document.getElementById("sndBtn");
  if (snd) {
    snd.onclick = function () {
      mutedFlag = !mutedFlag;
      this.textContent = mutedFlag ? "✕" : "♪";
      this.style.color = mutedFlag ? "var(--red)" : "var(--ink)";
      applyVol();
      if (mutedFlag) {
        stopBuf();
        if (player) { try { player.pause(); } catch (e) {} }
        if (window.ytP && window.ytP.mute) {
          try { window.ytP.mute(); } catch (e) {}
        }
      } else if (window.ytP && window.ytP.unMute) {
        try { window.ytP.unMute(); } catch (e) {}
      }
    };
  }
  if (volEl) {
    volEl.oninput = function () {
      masterFlag = parseInt(this.value, 10) / 100;
      if (masterFlag > 0 && mutedFlag) {
        mutedFlag = false;
        if (snd) {
          snd.textContent = "♪";
          snd.style.color = "var(--ink)";
        }
        if (player && player.src) player.play().catch(function () {});
      }
      applyVol();
    };
  }

  /* Rollover zoom + hover Holst cues fight the jukebox. Off until the deck works. */
  document.querySelectorAll(".panel").forEach(function (p) {
    p.addEventListener("mouseenter", function (e) {
      e.stopImmediatePropagation();
      p.classList.remove("hot");
    }, true);
  });
  window.playPlanet = function () {};

  /* ---- Core HTTP (Tauri invoke). Honest UNMEASURED if the shell is missing. ---- */
  function shellInvoke() {
    var t = window.__TAURI__;
    if (t && t.core && typeof t.core.invoke === "function") return t.core.invoke.bind(t.core);
    return null;
  }

  function coreGet(path) {
    var inv = shellInvoke();
    if (!inv) {
      return Promise.reject(new Error("NO_SHELL"));
    }
    return inv("load_config").then(function (cfg) {
      var base = (cfg && (cfg.serverUrl || cfg.server_url)) || "http://127.0.0.1:8770";
      return inv("load_deck").then(function (deck) {
        var root = deck && deck.runtimeRoot ? String(deck.runtimeRoot).trim() : "";
        var tokP = root
          ? inv("load_install_token", { root: root }).catch(function () { return ""; })
          : Promise.resolve("");
        return tokP.then(function (tok) {
          return inv("api_request", {
            method: "GET",
            path: path,
            serverUrl: base,
            bearer: tok || null,
            body: null
          }).then(function (r) {
            if (!r || !r.ok) {
              var err = new Error("HTTP " + ((r && r.status) || "?"));
              err.status = r && r.status;
              throw err;
            }
            return r.json;
          });
        });
      });
    });
  }

  function ageTxt(ageS) {
    if (ageS == null || isNaN(ageS)) return "";
    if (ageS < 60) return Math.round(ageS) + "s";
    if (ageS < 3600) return Math.round(ageS / 60) + "m";
    if (ageS < 86400) return (ageS / 3600).toFixed(1) + "h";
    return Math.round(ageS / 86400) + "d";
  }

  function paintRailsFromCore(matrix) {
    matrix = matrix || [];
    /* Do not replace RING_NODES — that made MESH NODES flash then vanish. */
    if (typeof paintNodes === "function" && typeof RING_NODES !== "undefined") {
      var by = {};
      RING_NODES.forEach(function (n) {
        var hit = null;
        matrix.forEach(function (r) {
          var id = String(r.link_id || "").toLowerCase();
          if (n.id && id.indexOf(n.id) >= 0) hit = r;
        });
        by[n.id] = hit
          ? { status: hit.verified ? "live" : "UNMEASURED", topic: hit.route || n.sub, ts: null }
          : { status: "no data", topic: n.sub || "—", ts: null };
      });
      paintNodes(by);
    }
    setText("mNodes", String((typeof RING_NODES !== "undefined" && RING_NODES.length) || matrix.length));

    var list = document.getElementById("raillist");
    if (list) {
      list.innerHTML = matrix.map(function (r) {
        var col = r.verified ? "#22e3ff" : "#5f6ea0";
        var st = r.verified ? "live" : "UNMEASURED";
        return '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:10px;margin-bottom:4px">'
          + '<span><b style="color:' + col + '">' + r.link_id + "</b> "
          + '<span style="color:#5f6ea0">' + (r.rail_type || "") + "</span></span>"
          + '<span style="color:' + col + '">' + st + " · " + ageTxt(r.age_s) + "</span></div>";
      }).join("");
    }
  }

  function paintHealth(h, status) {
    var rows = (h && h.rows) || {};
    var events = Object.keys(rows).map(function (k) {
      var row = rows[k] || {};
      return {
        ts: new Date().toISOString(),
        actor: "core",
        target: k,
        event: row.ok ? "ok" : "ERROR",
        detail: row.detail || ""
      };
    });
    if (typeof renderSignals === "function") renderSignals(events);

    var verdict = (h && h.verdict) || "UNMEASURED";
    var reds = h && typeof h.reds === "number" ? h.reds : "—";
    setHtml("burnrows",
      '<div style="color:#cfe3ff">Core ' + ((status && status.tree_id) || "") + "</div>"
      + '<div style="color:' + (reds === 0 ? "#4dff9e" : "#ffb020") + '">' + verdict + "</div>");
    setText("burnlive", "Core :8770", "#4dff9e");

    var ready = status && status.ready;
    var seq = status && status.ledger_head && status.ledger_head.seq;
    setText("logage", ready ? "// Core ready · seq " + seq : "// UNMEASURED",
      ready ? "#3ddc84" : "#ffb020");
    var title = document.querySelector(".title");
    if (title && status && status.tree_id) {
      title.innerHTML = "JACK'S <b>MESH</b> COMMAND";
    }
    setHtml("inrows",
      '<div style="color:#8fa0c8;font-size:9px">ledger seq <b style="color:#22e3ff">'
      + (seq == null ? "—" : seq) + "</b> · "
      + ((status && status.ledger_head && status.ledger_head.event) || "—") + "</div>");
    setText("biasname", ready ? "Core live" : "UNMEASURED", ready ? "#4dff9e" : "#5f6ea0");
  }

  function paintUnmeasured(why) {
    setHtml("burnrows",
      '<div style="color:#ffb020">UNMEASURED</div>'
      + '<div style="color:#5f6ea0;font-size:9px">' + why + "</div>");
    setText("burnlive", "UNMEASURED", "#ffb020");
    setText("logage", "// UNMEASURED", "#ffb020");
    setText("biasname", "UNMEASURED", "#5f6ea0");
    if (typeof paintNodes === "function") paintNodes(null);
  }

  /* System tab owns GET /health (app.js SNAPSHOT_TAB). Cockpit 5s pulse is
     /status + /rails. /health only on boot and <TEST> (forceHealth).
     Never invent health scores — last measured, else UNMEASURED. */
  var lastHealth = null;

  var coreTickBusy = false;
  function tickCore(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    var forceHealth = !!(opts && opts.forceHealth);
    if (coreTickBusy && !forceHealth) return Promise.resolve();
    coreTickBusy = true;
    return coreGet("/api/v1/status").then(function (status) {
      var healthP = forceHealth
        ? coreGet("/api/v1/health").then(function (h) {
            lastHealth = h;
            return h;
          }, function () {
            /* Health fold failed — do not invent. */
            return lastHealth;
          })
        : Promise.resolve(lastHealth);
      return healthP.then(function (health) {
        return coreGet("/api/v1/rails").then(function (rails) {
          paintRailsFromCore(rails && rails.matrix);
          paintHealth(health, status);
        }, function () {
          paintHealth(health, status);
        });
      });
    }).catch(function (e) {
      paintUnmeasured((e && e.message) || "Core not reachable");
    }).then(function (v) { coreTickBusy = false; return v; },
            function (e) { coreTickBusy = false; throw e; });
  }

  var railBtn = document.getElementById("railtest");
  if (railBtn) {
    railBtn.onclick = function (ev) {
      if (ev) ev.stopPropagation();
      var b = railBtn;
      b.textContent = "…";
      tickCore({ forceHealth: true }).then(function () { b.textContent = "<TEST>"; },
                      function () { b.textContent = "<TEST>"; });
    };
  }

  tickCore({ forceHealth: true });
  if (poller) clearInterval(poller);
  poller = setInterval(function () { tickCore(); }, 5000);
})();

===== FILE: builds/cdeck/test_kdash_working.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env py -3.14
"""cDeck first paint IS pre-wipe KDash, and the jukebox actually plays.

Keith 2026-09-06: jukebox isn't working; pretty much nothing from KDash is
working; start with KDash and get it working, then build from there.

This file is the working gate. `test_kdash_lift.py` pins the superseded
Cowork-wrap wall (app.js hoist). First paint is `ui/index.html` = the lift,
plus `ui/kdash_native.js` so the jukebox is local planets/*.mp3 inside Tauri
(no YouTube iframe).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import pathlib
import sys

HERE = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
UI = HERE / "ui"
INDEX = (UI / "index.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
NATIVE = (UI / "kdash_native.js").read_text(encoding="utf-8") if (UI / "kdash_native.js").exists() else ""
CONF = (HERE / "src-tauri" / "tauri.conf.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
PLANETS = ["jupiter", "mars", "venus", "mercury", "saturn", "uranus", "neptune"]

RESULTS: list[tuple[str, bool, str]] = []


def check(label: str, ok: bool, detail: str = "") -> None:
    RESULTS.append((label, bool(ok), detail))


check("PAGE: first paint is JACK'S MESH COMMAND, not a Cowork wrap",
      "<title>JACK'S MESH COMMAND</title>" in INDEX
      and 'id="pCore"' in INDEX
      and 'id="pMusic"' in INDEX
      and 'id="home-kdash"' not in INDEX
      and "function drawCore(" in INDEX)

check("PAGE: Signal Core and jukebox chrome from the lift are present",
      "function drawCore(" in INDEX
      and 'id="jvol"' in INDEX
      and 'id="jvolv"' in INDEX
      and 'id="autoBtn"' in INDEX
      and 'id="ytwrap"' in INDEX)

check("AUDIO: Holst planets/*.mp3 shipped next to the page",
      all((UI / "planets" / f"{n}.mp3").is_file() for n in PLANETS),
      "missing " + ", ".join(n for n in PLANETS if not (UI / "planets" / f"{n}.mp3").is_file()))

check("NATIVE: overlay exists and is loaded after the lift script",
      bool(NATIVE)
      and 'src="kdash_native.js"' in INDEX
      and INDEX.find("</script>") < INDEX.find('src="kdash_native.js"'))

check("JUKEBOX: overlay plays planets/*.mp3 through HTML5 Audio",
      'createElement("audio")' in NATIVE
      and "planets/" in NATIVE
      and "jukeVol" in NATIVE
      and 'getElementById("jvol")' in NATIVE
      and "startSrc(a, planetSrc(name), name)" in NATIVE
      and "player.controls = true" in NATIVE
      and all(n in NATIVE for n in PLANETS))

check("JUKEBOX: YouTube IFrame API plays the lift pool; native does not clobber #ytplayer",

      and "function embed(id,t)" in INDEX

check("NO-IFRAME: extra-pane decks still do not inject a YouTube iframe of their own",

      and "googleapis.com" not in (HERE / "ui" / "app.js").read_text(encoding="utf-8").lower())

      "function readProjectionHttp(" in _APP

      and "if (!deck.runtimeRoot && !(f && f.ok))" in _APP
      and '"/api/v1/fleet"' in _APP
      and '"/api/v1/nodemap"' in _APP
      # A native shell tries disk FIRST and only then HTTP; a refusal keeps both
      # reasons rather than claiming NO_SHELL from a host that has one.
      and "return (p && p.ok) ? p : readProjectionHttp(name, p);" in _APP
      and "diskRefusal" in _APP)

      # fetched the transcript, painted it, and nobody could see it.

      and "/tui/open-sessions" not in _APP)

check("BOOT: a [data-g] this deck has no colour for cannot abort app.js",
      # renderCoreTelemetry() sweeps every [data-g] on the page and index.html's
      # lifted cockpit carries a third dial, data-g="tok", that CORE_G has no
      # entry for. Reading `.col` off undefined threw at MODULE level, above
      # `tick(); boot();`, so app.js never connected and every panel it owns sat
      # on its "not connected" markup while the header read "connected".
      "var spec = CORE_G[g.getAttribute(\"data-g\")];" in _APP
      and "if (!spec) return;" in _APP
      and "CORE_G[g.getAttribute(\"data-g\")].col" not in _APP
      and 'querySelectorAll("[data-g]"), coreGaugeShape' in _APP
      # The mesh keeps its own dial. This is an app.js guard, not an index edit.
      and 'data-g="tok"' in INDEX)
