===== FILE: builds/cdeck/src-tauri/src/lib.rs =====
//! cDeck — native Windows shell for the COSMOS HTTP API.
//!
//! The webview never talks to COSMOS directly (CORS + hang risk). All
//! `/api/v1/*` traffic goes through `api_request`, which enforces timeouts
//! so a down server is reported instead of freezing the deck.
//! The bearer token is an argument to each call and is never written to disk.

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::time::Duration;
use tauri::{AppHandle, Manager, PhysicalPosition, PhysicalSize, WebviewUrl, WebviewWindowBuilder};

/// Live COSMOS Core. Port 8791 serves the TRIAL kernel (root `trylive`,
/// `tree_id = KMesh-COSMOS-try`); defaulting there opened the deck on a tree
/// that is not the live one.
const DEFAULT_URL: &str = "http://127.0.0.1:8770";
/// COSMOS2 profiles (docs/PROFILES.md). A window is a skin, not a second Core.
const DECK_PROFILES: &[&str] = &[
    "forge",
    "crucible",
    "diligence",
    "docket",
    "ups",
    "differentiator",
    "website",
];
const MAX_PROFILE_WINDOWS: usize = 8;
/// Fast GETs. A snapshot poll that has not answered in 8s is a down server, and
/// the deck says so rather than waiting on it.
const FETCH_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(8);
/// Queue/ops folds walk the on-disk ledger. Isolated they are <1s; under a
/// cDeck poll stampede they can exceed the 8s "server down" budget and the
/// RUNS tab reports COSMOS down while GET /jobs still answers.
const FOLD_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(20);
const FOLD_PATHS: &[&str] = &[
    "/api/v1/jukebox",
    "/api/v1/runs_ops",
    "/api/v1/work_orders",
    "/api/v1/model_rater",
    "/api/v1/session_tools",
];
/// CVM P0 (`docs/CVM_ARCH.md` §9) — the one path that must OUTLIVE the brain.
/// `cosmos_brain.voice_client_timeout_s()` == `OPUS_TIMEOUT_S` 45 +
/// `GROK_FALLBACK_S` 20 + `VOICE_TURN_SLACK_S` 5 == **70s**. Both KDash clients
/// carry this rule verbatim; cDeck did not, so every voice turn that reached
/// Opus (a 45s budget on its own) was aborted by the client at 8s and reported
/// as "server may be down" — the deck breaking the turn and blaming COSMOS.
/// Pinned to the brain's own constants by `test_transport_parity.py`.
const VOICE_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(70);
const VOICE_PATH: &str = "/api/v1/voice";
const CONNECT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(3);

/// The read budget for one request. Per-path, because one flat budget cannot be
/// both "report a down server fast" and "do not abort the brain".
fn timeout_for(path: &str) -> Duration {
    let bare = path.split('?').next().unwrap_or(path);
    if bare == VOICE_PATH {
        VOICE_TIMEOUT
    } else if FOLD_PATHS.contains(&bare) {
        FOLD_TIMEOUT
    } else {
        FETCH_TIMEOUT
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct AppConfig {
    /// COSMOS base URL (loopback / LAN / Tailscale). No secrets.
    server_url: String,
}

impl Default for AppConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            server_url: DEFAULT_URL.to_string(),
        }
    }
}

fn config_dir(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf, String> {
    let dir = app
        .path()
        .app_config_dir()
        .map_err(|e| format!("config dir: {e}"))?;
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).map_err(|e| format!("create config dir: {e}"))?;
    Ok(dir)
}

fn config_path(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf, String> {
    Ok(config_dir(app)?.join("config.json"))
}

fn deck_path(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<std::path::PathBuf, String> {
    Ok(config_dir(app)?.join("deck.json"))
}

/// Hard cap on persisted deck state. Spend overlays, human-posted pools, and
/// node-map positions are small JSON; anything larger is a write we refuse.
const DECK_MAX_BYTES: usize = 256 * 1024;

fn forbidden_deck_key(k: &str) -> bool {
    let k = k.to_ascii_lowercase().replace('_', "").replace('-', "");
    matches!(
        k.as_str(),
        "token"
            | "bearer"
            | "password"
            | "secret"
            | "authorization"
            | "apikey"
            | "accesskey"
            | "privatekey"
            | "killtoken"
    )
}

fn deck_keys_ok(v: &serde_json::Value) -> Result<(), String> {
    match v {
        serde_json::Value::Object(map) => {
            for (k, child) in map {
                if forbidden_deck_key(k) {
                    return Err(format!(
                        "deck state must not contain secret-shaped keys ({k})"
                    ));
                }
                deck_keys_ok(child)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        serde_json::Value::Array(items) => {
            for child in items {
                deck_keys_ok(child)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        }
        _ => Ok(()),
    }
}

fn sanitize_deck(raw: serde_json::Value) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    if !raw.is_object() {
        return Err("deck state must be a JSON object".into());
    }
    deck_keys_ok(&raw)?;
    let body = serde_json::to_vec(&raw).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if body.len() > DECK_MAX_BYTES {
        return Err(format!(
            "deck state is {} bytes; cap is {DECK_MAX_BYTES}",
            body.len()
        ));
    }
    Ok(raw)
}

#[tauri::command]
fn load_deck(app: AppHandle) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    let path = deck_path(&app)?;
    if !path.exists() {
        return Ok(serde_json::json!({}));
    }
    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|e| format!("read deck: {e}"))?;
    match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&raw) {
        Ok(v) => match sanitize_deck(v) {
            Ok(clean) => Ok(clean),
            Err(_) => Ok(serde_json::json!({
                "warning": "deck.json is malformed or secret-shaped — using empty deck state"
            })),
        },
        Err(e) => Ok(serde_json::json!({
            "warning": format!("deck.json is malformed ({e}) — using empty deck state")
        })),
    }
}

#[tauri::command]
fn save_deck(app: AppHandle, state: serde_json::Value) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    let clean = sanitize_deck(state)?;
    let path = deck_path(&app)?;
    let body = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&clean).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    std::fs::write(&path, body).map_err(|e| format!("write deck: {e}"))?;
    Ok(clean)
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Local projection reader — the FLEET / host-battery source.
//
// Every other panel reaches COSMOS over HTTP. When Core is not serving, the
// running fleet is still writing projections to disk, and those are the only
// witness left. This reads exactly three of them, by NAME, never by a path the
// webview supplies: an allowlist, not a file API.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// name -> path under the runtime root. Mirrors `cosmos_fleet_panel.FEED_PARTS`
/// / `DRIVE_PARTS` and `cosmos_nodemap_panel.REGISTRY_PARTS`;
/// `test_fleet_panel.py` and `test_nodemap_panel.py` pin the lists together.
///
/// `registry` is the mesh itself: `cosmos-registry/1`, the rails prober's
/// projection of the nodes COSMOS has PROVEN live ("a node that does not answer
/// is not registered"). Without it the node map had no source but the HTTP
/// cache, so a deck opened while Core was down drew only the five boxes the UI
/// hard-codes — a diagram of a mesh nobody was observing.
const PROJECTIONS: &[(&str, &[&str])] = &[
    ("fleet", &["state", "cdeck", "feed.json"]),
    ("drive", &["state", "drive", "meter.json"]),
    ("registry", &["registry", "rails.json"]),
];

/// Same cap as `cosmos_fleet_panel.MAX_BYTES`. A projection past this is a
/// defect, not a payload.
const PROJECTION_MAX_BYTES: u64 = 4 * 1024 * 1024;

/// The runtime-root sentinel. Existence is not identity — the file's CONTENT is
/// what says this directory is a COSMOS root.
const SENTINEL: &str = ".cosmos-root.json";

#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct Projection {
    ok: bool,
    /// Machine-readable refusal kind when `ok` is false. Shares the vocabulary
    /// of `cosmos_fleet_panel.KINDS`.
    kind: Option<String>,
    detail: Option<String>,
    path: String,
    tree_id: Option<String>,
    json: Option<serde_json::Value>,
}

fn refuse(kind: &str, detail: String, path: String, tree_id: Option<String>) -> Projection {
    Projection {
        ok: false,
        kind: Some(kind.to_string()),
        detail: Some(detail),
        path,
        tree_id,
        json: None,
    }
}

/// Read the sentinel and return the tree_id it declares.
///
/// Refusal kinds are the resolver's, not this file's: `cosmos_paths` already
/// names these facts and `cosmos_fleet_panel` propagates them, so the deck says
/// the same word for the same failure. In particular a directory that exists
/// but carries no sentinel is IDENTITY_MISMATCH — existence is not identity.
fn root_tree_id(root: &std::path::Path) -> Result<String, (String, String)> {
    let sp = root.join(SENTINEL);
    if !root.exists() {
        return Err((
            "NOT_FOUND".to_string(),
            format!("root does not exist: {}", root.display()),
        ));
    }
    if !sp.exists() {
        return Err((
            "IDENTITY_MISMATCH".to_string(),
            format!(
                "no {SENTINEL} in {} — an existing directory without the sentinel is \
                 NOT a COSMOS root",
                root.display()
            ),
        ));
    }
    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(&sp)
        .map_err(|e| ("UNREADABLE".to_string(), format!("{}: {e}", sp.display())))?;
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(&raw)
        .map_err(|e| ("UNPARSEABLE".to_string(), format!("sentinel is not JSON: {e}")))?;
    if v.get("system").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) != Some("COSMOS") {
        return Err((
            "IDENTITY_MISMATCH".to_string(),
            "sentinel parses but does not identify a COSMOS root".to_string(),
        ));
    }
    match v.get("tree_id").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        Some(t) if !t.trim().is_empty() => Ok(t.to_string()),
        _ => Err((
            "IDENTITY_MISMATCH".to_string(),
            "sentinel carries no tree_id".to_string(),
        )),
    }
}

/// Read one allowlisted projection from a sentinel-verified runtime root.
///
/// Never returns `Err` for a missing or malformed source: an absent drive meter
/// is a fact the panel renders as UNAVAILABLE with a `kind`, not a reason to
/// blank the deck. `Err` is reserved for a caller that asked for a name which
/// does not exist.
#[tauri::command]
fn read_projection(
    root: String,
    name: String,
    expected_tree_id: Option<String>,
) -> Result<Projection, String> {
    let parts = PROJECTIONS
        .iter()
        .find(|(n, _)| *n == name.as_str())
        .map(|(_, p)| *p)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            let known: Vec<&str> = PROJECTIONS.iter().map(|(n, _)| *n).collect();
            format!("UNKNOWN_PROJECTION: {name} — known: {known:?}")
        })?;

    let root = root.trim();
    // NO_ROOT is the one kind that is the DECK's own, not the resolver's: it
    // means nobody has told this deck which filesystem to look at, which is the
    // ordinary state of a deck running on a phone or another machine.
    if root.is_empty() {
        return Ok(refuse(
            "NO_ROOT",
            "no runtime root set — this deck may not be running on the COSMOS host".into(),
            String::new(),
            None,
        ));
    }
    let root_path = std::path::Path::new(root);
    if !root_path.is_absolute() {
        return Ok(refuse(
            "NO_ROOT",
            format!("runtime root {root} is not an absolute path"),
            root.to_string(),
            None,
        ));
    }
    let mut path = root_path.to_path_buf();
    for p in parts {
        path.push(p);
    }
    let shown = path.display().to_string();

    let tree_id = match root_tree_id(root_path) {
        Ok(t) => t,
        Err((kind, detail)) => return Ok(refuse(&kind, detail, shown, None)),
    };
    if let Some(want) = expected_tree_id.as_deref().map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) {
        if want != tree_id {
            return Ok(refuse(
                "IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
                format!("sentinel tree_id {tree_id} != expected {want}"),
                shown,
                Some(tree_id),
            ));
        }
    }

    let meta = match std::fs::metadata(&path) {
        Ok(m) => m,
        Err(e) => return Ok(refuse("NO_SOURCE", e.to_string(), shown, Some(tree_id))),
    };
    if meta.len() > PROJECTION_MAX_BYTES {
        return Ok(refuse(
            "TOO_LARGE",
            format!("{} bytes > {PROJECTION_MAX_BYTES}", meta.len()),
            shown,
            Some(tree_id),
        ));
    }
    let raw = match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
        Ok(s) => s,
        Err(e) => return Ok(refuse("NO_SOURCE", e.to_string(), shown, Some(tree_id))),
    };
    match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&raw) {
        Ok(v) if v.is_object() => Ok(Projection {
            ok: true,
            kind: None,
            detail: None,
            path: shown,
            tree_id: Some(tree_id),
            json: Some(v),
        }),
        Ok(_) => Ok(refuse(
            "MALFORMED",
            "payload is not a JSON object".into(),
            shown,
            Some(tree_id),
        )),
        Err(e) => Ok(refuse("MALFORMED", e.to_string(), shown, Some(tree_id))),
    }
}

/// Reduce the pasted server URL to a bare ORIGIN (scheme://host[:port]).
///
/// This is a real parse, not string-prefix checking: anything carrying a
/// path, query, or fragment is rejected, so `format!("{base}{path}")`
/// downstream cannot be diverted onto a different resource.
fn sanitize_url(raw: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let raw = raw.trim();
    if raw.is_empty() {
        return Err("server URL is required".into());
    }
    if !(raw.starts_with("http://") || raw.starts_with("https://")) {
        return Err("server URL must start with http:// or https://".into());
    }
    let url = reqwest::Url::parse(raw).map_err(|e| format!("invalid server URL: {e}"))?;
    match url.scheme() {
        "http" | "https" => {}
        _ => return Err("server URL must start with http:// or https://".into()),
    }
    // Refuse embedded credentials so a pasted `http://user:token@host` cannot
    // land on disk. The bearer field is the only place a secret may live, and
    // it is memory-only.
    if !url.username().is_empty() || url.password().is_some() {
        return Err("server URL must not contain credentials (user:pass@host)".into());
    }
    let host = url
        .host_str()
        .ok_or_else(|| "server URL must include a host".to_string())?;
    if !matches!(url.path(), "" | "/") {
        return Err("server URL must be an origin only — remove the path".into());
    }
    if url.query().is_some() || url.fragment().is_some() {
        return Err("server URL must be an origin only — remove the query/fragment".into());
    }
    let mut origin = format!("{}://{host}", url.scheme());
    if let Some(port) = url.port() {
        origin.push_str(&format!(":{port}"));
    }
    Ok(origin)
}

fn sanitize_api_path(raw: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let path = raw.trim();
    if path.is_empty() || !path.starts_with('/') {
        return Err("path must be a root-relative API path (e.g. /api/v1/status)".into());
    }
    if !path.starts_with("/api/v1/") {
        return Err("only /api/v1/* is allowed".into());
    }
    if path.contains("://") || path.contains("..") || path.contains('\\') || path.contains('#') {
        return Err("path must stay on the configured COSMOS host".into());
    }
    Ok(path.to_string())
}

/// What `load_config` hands the UI: the config plus an optional non-fatal
/// warning (e.g. malformed config.json) so a silent reset never masquerades
/// as a clean load.
#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct LoadedConfig {
    server_url: String,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    warning: Option<String>,
}

#[tauri::command]
fn load_config(app: AppHandle) -> Result<LoadedConfig, String> {
    let path = config_path(&app)?;
    if !path.exists() {
        return Ok(LoadedConfig {
            server_url: AppConfig::default().server_url,
            warning: None,
        });
    }
    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|e| format!("read config: {e}"))?;
    let (mut cfg, warning) = match serde_json::from_str::<AppConfig>(&raw) {
        Ok(c) => (c, None),
        Err(e) => (
            AppConfig::default(),
            Some(format!(
                "config.json is malformed ({e}) — using default server URL {DEFAULT_URL}; \
                 CONNECT will rewrite it"
            )),
        ),
    };
    if cfg.server_url.trim().is_empty() {
        cfg.server_url = DEFAULT_URL.to_string();
    }
    // P-2 scar: a saved :8791 is the trial kernel (trylive / KMesh-COSMOS-try),
    // not live Core. Rewrite it so a persisted wrong tree cannot outlive the
    // default fix. Port check only — the URL string is not a new default.
    let mut warning = warning;
    if is_trial_kernel_url(&cfg.server_url) {
        cfg.server_url = DEFAULT_URL.to_string();
        warning = Some(
            "saved server URL was the trial kernel on :8791 — pointed at live Core :8770"
                .into(),
        );
        let path = config_path(&app)?;
        let body = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&cfg).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let _ = std::fs::write(&path, body);
    }
    Ok(LoadedConfig {
        server_url: cfg.server_url,
        warning,
    })
}

fn is_trial_kernel_url(url: &str) -> bool {
    let s = url.trim().trim_end_matches('/');
    s.ends_with(":8791")
}

#[tauri::command]
fn save_config(app: AppHandle, server_url: String) -> Result<AppConfig, String> {
    let cfg = AppConfig {
        server_url: sanitize_url(&server_url)?,
    };
    let path = config_path(&app)?;
    let body = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&cfg).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    std::fs::write(&path, body).map_err(|e| format!("write config: {e}"))?;
    Ok(cfg)
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct ApiResponse {
    status: u16,
    ok: bool,
    json: serde_json::Value,
}

fn transport_err(base: &str, budget: Duration, err: reqwest::Error) -> String {
    if err.is_timeout() {
        let secs = budget.as_secs();
        return format!(
            "timeout after {secs}s — COSMOS at {base} did not answer (server may be down)"
        );
    }
    if err.is_connect() {
        return format!("server down — cannot reach {base} ({err})");
    }
    format!("unreachable — {err}")
}

#[tauri::command]
async fn api_request(
    method: String,
    path: String,
    server_url: String,
    bearer: Option<String>,
    body: Option<serde_json::Value>,
) -> Result<ApiResponse, String> {
    let base = sanitize_url(&server_url)?;
    let path = sanitize_api_path(&path)?;
    let method = method.trim().to_uppercase();
    if method != "GET" && method != "POST" {
        return Err("only GET and POST are allowed".into());
    }
    let url = format!("{base}{path}");
    let budget = timeout_for(&path);

    let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(budget)
        .connect_timeout(CONNECT_TIMEOUT)
        // No automatic redirect following: a 3xx from the COSMOS host could
        // otherwise send the bearer to an arbitrary Location (SSRF). A 3xx is
        // surfaced to the UI as a plain non-ok status instead.
        .redirect(reqwest::redirect::Policy::none())
        .user_agent("cDeck/0.2.2")
        .build()
        .map_err(|e| format!("HTTP client: {e}"))?;

    let mut req = match method.as_str() {
        "POST" => client.post(&url),
        _ => client.get(&url),
    };
    req = req.header("Accept", "application/json");
    if let Some(tok) = bearer
        .as_deref()
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
    {
        req = req.header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {tok}"));
    }
    if let Some(body) = body {
        req = req.header("Content-Type", "application/json").json(&body);
    }

    let resp = req
        .send()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| transport_err(&base, budget, e))?;
    let status = resp.status().as_u16();
    let ok = resp.status().is_success();
    let text = resp
        .text()
        .await
        .map_err(|e| format!("read body: {e}"))?;
    let json = if text.trim().is_empty() {
        serde_json::json!({})
    } else {
        serde_json::from_str(&text)
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| serde_json::json!({ "raw": text }))
    };
    Ok(ApiResponse { status, ok, json })
}

/// C0 coding pane (ORCH_HOME_SPEC §5). Pure: given live grok PIDs, either
/// focus one or launch. Never spawn when a grok TUI is already running (H7).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum CodingAction {
    Focus(u32),
    Launch,
}

fn coding_decision(pids: &[u32], cow_pid: Option<u32>) -> CodingAction {
    if let Some(cow) = cow_pid {
        if pids.iter().any(|&p| p == cow) {
            return CodingAction::Focus(cow);
        }
    }
    if let Some(&pid) = pids.first() {
        return CodingAction::Focus(pid);
    }
    CodingAction::Launch
}

fn grok_pids() -> Vec<u32> {
    image_pids("grok.exe")
}

/// Console-subsystem helpers (tasklist / where / powershell) spawned from this
/// GUI app MUST set CREATE_NO_WINDOW. Without it Windows allocates a conhost
/// that flashes on every C0 poll (5s) — typing becomes impossible.
/// Same scar as `cosmos/hush.py` / schtasks (2026-08-26, 2026-09-01, 2026-09-04).
#[cfg(windows)]
fn hide_console(cmd: &mut std::process::Command) {
    use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
    const CREATE_NO_WINDOW: u32 = 0x08000000;
    cmd.creation_flags(CREATE_NO_WINDOW);
}

/// Live PIDs for one Windows image. The filter is a literal image name —
/// no extra `/FI`, no path, no shell metacharacters.
fn image_pids(image: &str) -> Vec<u32> {
    let want = image.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
    if want.is_empty()
        || !want.ends_with(".exe")
        || want.bytes().any(|b| {
            matches!(
                b,
                b'/' | b'\\' | b'"' | b'\n' | b'\r' | b' ' | b'&' | b'|' | b';' | b'%'
            )
        })
    {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    let filter = format!("IMAGENAME eq {want}");
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("tasklist");
    cmd.args(["/FI", &filter, "/FO", "CSV", "/NH"]);
    #[cfg(windows)]
    hide_console(&mut cmd);
    let out = cmd.output();
    let Ok(out) = out else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    if !out.status.success() {
        return Vec::new();
    }
    String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout)
        .lines()
        .filter_map(|line| {
            // "OpenWork.exe","2472","Console","1","12,345 K"
            let mut cols = line.split(',');
            let name = cols.next()?.trim().trim_matches('"').to_ascii_lowercase();
            if name != want {
                return None;
            }
            cols.next()?.trim().trim_matches('"').parse().ok()
        })
        .collect()
}

fn focus_pid(pid: u32) -> Result<(), String> {
    // Restore + AppActivate. Header ORC/CCr must bring the live window
    // forward — not open a cDeck tab and not spawn a second session.
    let script = format!(
        "$p = Get-Process -Id {pid} -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; \
         if (-not $p) {{ exit 2 }}; \
         Add-Type -Name W -Namespace Cdeck -MemberDefinition '[DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern bool SetForegroundWindow(IntPtr h); [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr h, int n);' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; \
         $h = $p.MainWindowHandle; \
         if ($h -ne [IntPtr]::Zero) {{ [Cdeck.W]::ShowWindow($h, 9) | Out-Null; [Cdeck.W]::SetForegroundWindow($h) | Out-Null }}; \
         $ok = (New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell).AppActivate({pid}); \
         if (-not $ok -and $h -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) {{ exit 3 }}"
    );
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("powershell");
    cmd.args(["-NoProfile", "-WindowStyle", "Hidden", "-Command", &script]);
    #[cfg(windows)]
    hide_console(&mut cmd);
    let status = cmd.status().map_err(|e| format!("focus: {e}"))?;
    if status.success() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(format!("focus pid {pid} failed (exit {status})"))
    }
}

fn focus_first(pids: &[u32]) -> Result<u32, String> {
    let mut last = "no pid".to_string();
    for &pid in pids {
        match focus_pid(pid) {
            Ok(()) => return Ok(pid),
            Err(e) => last = e,
        }
    }
    Err(last)
}

fn grok_exe() -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    let mut where_cmd = std::process::Command::new("where");
    where_cmd.arg("grok");
    #[cfg(windows)]
    hide_console(&mut where_cmd);
    if let Ok(out) = where_cmd.output() {
        if out.status.success() {
            if let Some(line) = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout)
                .lines()
                .map(str::trim)
                .find(|l| !l.is_empty())
            {
                return Some(std::path::PathBuf::from(line));
            }
        }
    }
    if let Ok(home) = std::env::var("USERPROFILE") {
        let p = std::path::PathBuf::from(home).join(".grok").join("bin").join("grok.exe");
        if p.is_file() {
            return Some(p);
        }
    }
    None
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct CodingOpen {
    action: String,
    pid: Option<u32>,
    pids: Vec<u32>,
    detail: String,
    cwd: Option<String>,
    session_id: Option<String>,
    snapped: bool,
}

#[derive(Serialize, Clone)]
struct CodingSession {
    id: String,
    title: String,
    updated_at: String,
    created_at: String,
    last_turn: String,
    cwd: String,
    kind: String,
    model: String,
    agent: String,
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
struct CodingHistory {
    cwd: String,
    sessions: Vec<CodingSession>,
    live_pids: Vec<u32>,
    detail: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
enum CodingLaunch {
    Continue,
    Resume(String),
    Fresh,
}

fn strip_verbatim(p: std::path::PathBuf) -> std::path::PathBuf {
    let s = p.to_string_lossy();
    if let Some(rest) = s.strip_prefix(r"\\?\") {
        std::path::PathBuf::from(rest)
    } else {
        p
    }
}

fn looks_like_repo(p: &std::path::Path) -> bool {
    p.join("cosmos").join("cosmos.py").is_file()
        && p.join("docs").join("FINAL_ARCHITECTURE.md").is_file()
}

fn walk_repo_from(start: &std::path::Path) -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    let mut cur = Some(strip_verbatim(start.to_path_buf()));
    for _ in 0..10 {
        let p = cur?;
        if looks_like_repo(&p) {
            return Some(p);
        }
        cur = p.parent().map(|x| strip_verbatim(x.to_path_buf()));
    }
    None
}

fn coding_repo() -> Result<std::path::PathBuf, String> {
    if let Ok(exe) = std::env::current_exe() {
        if let Some(p) = walk_repo_from(&exe) {
            return Ok(p);
        }
    }
    if let Ok(cwd) = std::env::current_dir() {
        if let Some(p) = walk_repo_from(&cwd) {
            return Ok(p);
        }
    }
    Err(
        "NO_REPO: cDeck cannot see the COSMOS tree — Open coding needs that folder as --cwd so Grok sessions persist"
            .into(),
    )
}

fn grok_home() -> std::path::PathBuf {
    if let Ok(h) = std::env::var("GROK_HOME") {
        let t = h.trim();
        if !t.is_empty() {
            return std::path::PathBuf::from(t);
        }
    }
    std::path::PathBuf::from(std::env::var("USERPROFILE").unwrap_or_default()).join(".grok")
}

fn same_repo_path(a: &std::path::Path, b: &std::path::Path) -> bool {
    let na = strip_verbatim(a.to_path_buf());
    let nb = strip_verbatim(b.to_path_buf());
    na.to_string_lossy()
        .eq_ignore_ascii_case(&nb.to_string_lossy())
}

fn percent_decode(s: &str) -> String {
    let bytes = s.as_bytes();
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(bytes.len());
    let mut i = 0;
    while i < bytes.len() {
        if bytes[i] == b'%' && i + 2 < bytes.len() {
            if let Ok(hex) = std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[i + 1..i + 3]) {
                if let Ok(v) = u8::from_str_radix(hex, 16) {
                    out.push(v);
                    i += 3;
                    continue;
                }
            }
        }
        out.push(bytes[i]);
        i += 1;
    }
    String::from_utf8_lossy(&out).into_owned()
}

fn session_id_ok(s: &str) -> bool {
    let s = s.trim();
    if s.len() != 36 {
        return false;
    }
    let b = s.as_bytes();
    if b[8] != b'-' || b[13] != b'-' || b[18] != b'-' || b[23] != b'-' {
        return false;
    }
    b.iter().enumerate().all(|(i, c)| {
        matches!(i, 8 | 13 | 18 | 23) || c.is_ascii_hexdigit()
    })
}

/// grok -c continues last cwd session; grok -r <uuid> resumes any prior.
/// Never --session-id: that flag creates a NEW uuid, it does not resume.
fn coding_argv(cwd: &str, mode: &CodingLaunch) -> Result<Vec<String>, String> {
    if cwd.trim().is_empty() {
        return Err("NO_REPO: empty coding cwd".into());
    }
    let mut args = Vec::new();
    match mode {
        CodingLaunch::Continue => args.push("-c".into()),
        CodingLaunch::Resume(id) => {
            if !session_id_ok(id) {
                return Err("BAD_SESSION: resume id is not a UUID (use -r, never --session-id)".into());
            }
            args.push("-r".into());
            args.push(id.trim().to_string());
        }
        CodingLaunch::Fresh => {}
    }
    args.push("--cwd".into());
    args.push(cwd.to_string());
    Ok(args)
}

fn json_str(v: &serde_json::Value, pointer: &str) -> String {
    v.pointer(pointer)
        .and_then(|x| x.as_str())
        .unwrap_or("")
        .trim()
        .to_string()
}

fn list_sessions_for_repo(
    repo: &std::path::Path,
    home: &std::path::Path,
    limit: usize,
) -> Vec<CodingSession> {
    let root = home.join("sessions");
    let Ok(groups) = std::fs::read_dir(&root) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for ent in groups.flatten() {
        let Ok(ft) = ent.file_type() else { continue };
        if !ft.is_dir() {
            continue;
        }
        let group = ent.path();
        let group_cwd = percent_decode(&ent.file_name().to_string_lossy());
        let Ok(sess_dirs) = std::fs::read_dir(&group) else {
            continue;
        };
        for s in sess_dirs.flatten() {
            let Ok(sft) = s.file_type() else { continue };
            if !sft.is_dir() {
                continue;
            }
            let summary_path = s.path().join("summary.json");
            let Ok(raw) = std::fs::read_to_string(&summary_path) else {
                continue;
            };
            let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&raw) else {
                continue;
            };
            let cwd = json_str(&v, "/info/cwd");
            let cwd = if cwd.is_empty() { group_cwd.clone() } else { cwd };
            if !same_repo_path(std::path::Path::new(&cwd), repo)
                && !same_repo_path(std::path::Path::new(&group_cwd), repo)
            {
                continue;
            }
            let id = json_str(&v, "/info/id");
            let id = if session_id_ok(&id) {
                id
            } else {
                s.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned()
            };
            if !session_id_ok(&id) {
                continue;
            }
            let title = {
                let t = json_str(&v, "/generated_title");
                if t.is_empty() {
                    json_str(&v, "/session_summary")
                } else {
                    t
                }
            };
            let updated = json_str(&v, "/last_active_at");
            let updated = if updated.is_empty() {
                json_str(&v, "/updated_at")
            } else {
                updated
            };
            let agent = json_str(&v, "/agent_name");
            let model = json_str(&v, "/current_model_id");
            let skind = json_str(&v, "/session_kind");
            let kind = if agent.contains("grok-build") || skind == "headless" {
                "grok-build".to_string()
            } else {
                "grok".to_string()
            };
            out.push(CodingSession {
                id,
                title,
                updated_at: updated,
                created_at: json_str(&v, "/created_at"),
                last_turn: json_str(&v, "/last_turn_summary"),
                cwd,
                kind,
                model,
                agent,
            });
        }
    }
    out.sort_by(|a, b| b.updated_at.cmp(&a.updated_at));
    if out.len() > limit {
        out.truncate(limit);
    }
    out
}

fn spawn_grok(exe: &std::path::Path, cwd: &std::path::Path, args: &[String]) -> Result<u32, String> {
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);
    cmd.args(args).current_dir(cwd);
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
        const CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE: u32 = 0x00000010;
        cmd.creation_flags(CREATE_NEW_CONSOLE);
    }
    let child = cmd.spawn().map_err(|e| format!("launch grok: {e}"))?;
    Ok(child.id())
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
fn focus_result(pid: u32, pids: Vec<u32>, extra: &str) -> CodingOpen {
    let focus = match focus_pid(pid) {
        Ok(()) => format!("focused grok pid {pid}"),
        Err(e) => format!("grok pid {pid} is live; focus: {e}"),
    };
    CodingOpen {
        action: "focused".into(),
        pid: Some(pid),
        pids,
        detail: format!("{focus} — did not spawn (H7){extra}"),
        cwd: None,
        session_id: None,
        snapped: false,
    }
}

#[tauri::command]
fn coding_status() -> CodingOpen {
    let pids = grok_pids();
    if pids.is_empty() {
        CodingOpen {
            action: "idle".into(),
            pid: None,
            pids,
            detail: "no grok.exe — CCr/GBW is background grok CLI; call forward on Forge for the live pane".into(),
            cwd: None,
            session_id: None,
            snapped: false,
        }
    } else {
        CodingOpen {
            action: "live".into(),
            pid: Some(pids[0]),
            pids: pids.clone(),
            detail: format!(
                "{} grok process(es) live — Call forward opens Forge pane or snaps GBW to cDeck right",
                pids.len()
            ),
            cwd: None,
            session_id: None,
            snapped: false,
        }
    }
}

#[tauri::command]
fn list_coding_sessions() -> Result<CodingHistory, String> {
    let repo = coding_repo()?;
    let sessions = list_sessions_for_repo(&repo, &grok_home(), 50);
    let pids = grok_pids();
    let cwd = repo.display().to_string();
    let detail = if sessions.is_empty() {
        format!("no saved Grok sessions under {cwd}")
    } else {
        format!("{} saved session(s) for {cwd}", sessions.len())
    };
    Ok(CodingHistory {
        cwd,
        sessions,
        live_pids: pids,
        detail,
    })
}

fn launch_gbw_tui(
    session_id: Option<String>,
    fresh: bool,
) -> Result<(u32, Option<String>, String, String), String> {
    let exe = grok_exe().ok_or_else(|| {
        "no grok.exe on PATH or %USERPROFILE%\\.grok\\bin — cannot launch GBW".to_string()
    })?;
    let repo = coding_repo()?;
    let cwd = repo.display().to_string();
    let mode = if fresh {
        CodingLaunch::Fresh
    } else if let Some(id) = session_id.clone() {
        CodingLaunch::Resume(id)
    } else if list_sessions_for_repo(&repo, &grok_home(), 1).is_empty() {
        CodingLaunch::Fresh
    } else {
        CodingLaunch::Continue
    };
    let args = coding_argv(&cwd, &mode)?;
    let pid = spawn_grok(&exe, &repo, &args)?;
    let how = match &mode {
        CodingLaunch::Continue => "continued last COSMOS session (-c)".to_string(),
        CodingLaunch::Resume(id) => format!("resumed session {id} (-r)"),
        CodingLaunch::Fresh => "started a new COSMOS coding session".to_string(),
    };
    let sid = match mode {
        CodingLaunch::Resume(id) => Some(id),
        _ => None,
    };
    Ok((pid, sid, cwd, how))
}

/// Header CCr: open the live Grok TUI / IDE. One CCr. Never a second session.
/// If grok.exe is already running (background or minimized), restore it.
/// Does not `fresh`. Does not snap. Forge Call Forward is the second link.
#[tauri::command]
fn open_ccr() -> Result<CodingOpen, String> {
    let pids = grok_pids();
    if pids.is_empty() {
        return Ok(CodingOpen {
            action: "idle".into(),
            pid: None,
            pids,
            detail: "no live CCr (grok.exe). Header CCr does not start a new session. One CCr. Forge Call Forward continues the last session.".into(),
            cwd: coding_repo().ok().map(|p| p.display().to_string()),
            session_id: None,
            snapped: false,
        });
    }
    match focus_first(&pids) {
        Ok(pid) => Ok(CodingOpen {
            action: "focused".into(),
            pid: Some(pid),
            pids,
            detail: format!("CCr pid {pid} restored to foreground with IDE. Did not spawn (one CCr)."),
            cwd: coding_repo().ok().map(|p| p.display().to_string()),
            session_id: None,
            snapped: false,
        }),
        Err(e) => Ok(CodingOpen {
            action: "live".into(),
            pid: pids.first().copied(),
            pids,
            detail: format!("CCr is live; focus: {e}. Did not spawn a second session."),
            cwd: coding_repo().ok().map(|p| p.display().to_string()),
            session_id: None,
            snapped: false,
        }),
    }
}

/// Header ORC: open OpenWork (GFO seat) as its own window. Focus if live.
/// TALK is the second link (snap beside cDeck + stream picker).
#[tauri::command]
fn open_orc() -> Result<OpenWorkStatus, String> {
    let pids = image_pids("OpenWork.exe");
    if !pids.is_empty() {
        match focus_first(&pids) {
            Ok(pid) => {
                let mut rec = openwork_snapshot(
                    "focused",
                    Some(pid),
                    pids,
                    "ORC restored to foreground. Did not spawn a second orch.",
                );
                rec.live_session = true;
                rec.needs_stream = false;
                rec.snapped = false;
                return Ok(rec);
            }
            Err(e) => {
                let mut rec = openwork_snapshot(
                    "live",
                    pids.first().copied(),
                    pids,
                    &format!("ORC is live; focus: {e}. Did not spawn."),
                );
                rec.live_session = true;
                rec.needs_stream = false;
                rec.snapped = false;
                return Ok(rec);
            }
        }
    }
    let exe = openwork_exe().ok_or_else(|| {
        "NOT_INSTALLED: OpenWork.exe is not under %LOCALAPPDATA%\\Programs\\@openworkdesktop"
            .to_string()
    })?;
    let pid = spawn_openwork(&exe)?;
    let mut rec = openwork_snapshot(
        "launched",
        Some(pid),
        vec![pid],
        "ORC launched. Header does not open a stream picker — TALK does that.",
    );
    rec.live_session = false;
    rec.needs_stream = false;
    rec.snapped = false;
    Ok(rec)
}

#[tauri::command]
fn open_coding(
    app: AppHandle,
    session_id: Option<String>,
    fresh: Option<bool>,
    forward: Option<bool>,
) -> Result<CodingOpen, String> {
    let pids = grok_pids();
    let sid = session_id
        .as_deref()
        .map(str::trim)
        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
        .map(|s| s.to_string());
    if let Some(ref id) = sid {
        if !session_id_ok(id) {
            return Err("BAD_SESSION: resume id is not a UUID".into());
        }
    }
    let want_fresh = fresh.unwrap_or(false);
    let want_forward = forward.unwrap_or(false);
    let repo = coding_repo().ok();
    let cwd = repo.as_ref().map(|p| p.display().to_string());
    // Live GBW: Forge left pane (JS) plus own window snapped to cDeck right.
    // Cannot iframe grok TUI. H7 / one CCr: do not spawn a second grok if one
    // is live — bring the existing IDE to the foreground.
    if let Some(&pid) = pids.first() {
        let _ = focus_pid(pid);
        let (snapped, snap_msg) = snap_to_cdeck_right(&app, pid, "GBW");
        return Ok(CodingOpen {
            action: "live".into(),
            pid: Some(pid),
            pids,
            detail: format!(
                "GBW live — CCr brought to foreground with IDE. Own window {snap_msg}. Did not spawn a second TUI (one CCr)."
            ),
            cwd,
            session_id: sid,
            snapped,
        });
    }
    if want_forward {
        let (pid, launched_sid, launched_cwd, how) =
            launch_gbw_tui(sid.clone(), want_fresh)?;
        let (snapped, snap_msg) = snap_to_cdeck_right(&app, pid, "GBW");
        return Ok(CodingOpen {
            action: "forwarded".into(),
            pid: Some(pid),
            pids: vec![pid],
            detail: format!(
                "GBW launched ({how}). Forge left pane + {snap_msg}."
            ),
            cwd: Some(launched_cwd),
            session_id: launched_sid.or(sid),
            snapped,
        });
    }
    Ok(CodingOpen {
        action: "cli".into(),
        pid: None,
        pids: vec![],
        detail: "CCr/GBW is background grok CLI (via cli:grok). Call forward on Forge → left pane, or a live GBW snaps to cDeck right. Did not spawn a TUI.".into(),
        cwd,
        session_id: sid,
        snapped: false,
    })
}

/// OpenWork bottom C0 strip (ORCH_HOME_SPEC P5; Keith 2026-09-06 dropped the
/// main-left slot and gave that space to the KDash wall). Same honesty as C0:
/// focus the live desktop or launch it. Never iframe. Never read the
/// ui-control / server-tokens files (those hold a bearer).
const OPENWORK_EMBED: &str = "UNMEASURED — no HTML origin; Electron /health is API-only, not a webview. Do not iframe.";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct OpenWorkGrant {
    id: String,
    name: String,
    path: String,
}

#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct OpenWorkStatus {
    action: String,
    pid: Option<u32>,
    pids: Vec<u32>,
    detail: String,
    version: Option<String>,
    opencode_version: Option<String>,
    port: Option<u16>,
    workspace: Option<String>,
    workspace_id: Option<String>,
    exe: Option<String>,
    installed: bool,
    live: bool,
    health_ok: bool,
    cosmos_grant: bool,
    embed: String,
    snapped: bool,
    needs_stream: bool,
    live_session: bool,
}

/// Dock OpenWork to the right edge of cDeck. If there is not enough leftover
/// width, tile the work area: cDeck left, OpenWork right.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
struct PxRect {
    x: i32,
    y: i32,
    w: u32,
    h: u32,
}

fn tile_openwork(cdeck: PxRect, work: PxRect) -> (PxRect, PxRect) {
    let min_ow: i32 = 420;
    let min_cd: u32 = 480;
    let right = work.x + work.w as i32;
    let leftover = right - (cdeck.x + cdeck.w as i32);
    if leftover >= min_ow {
        let ow = PxRect {
            x: cdeck.x + cdeck.w as i32,
            y: cdeck.y,
            w: leftover as u32,
            h: cdeck.h,
        };
        (cdeck, ow)
    } else {
        let ow_w = 720u32.min(work.w / 2).max(min_ow as u32);
        let cd_w = work.w.saturating_sub(ow_w).max(min_cd);
        let cd = PxRect {
            x: work.x,
            y: work.y,
            w: cd_w,
            h: work.h,
        };
        let ow = PxRect {
            x: work.x + cd_w as i32,
            y: work.y,
            w: ow_w,
            h: work.h,
        };
        (cd, ow)
    }
}

fn openwork_decision(pids: &[u32]) -> CodingAction {
    if let Some(&pid) = pids.first() {
        CodingAction::Focus(pid)
    } else {
        CodingAction::Launch
    }
}

fn openwork_exe() -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    let local = std::env::var("LOCALAPPDATA").ok()?;
    let p = std::path::PathBuf::from(local)
        .join("Programs")
        .join("@openworkdesktop")
        .join("OpenWork.exe");
    p.is_file().then_some(p)
}

fn openwork_roaming() -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    std::env::var("APPDATA")
        .ok()
        .map(|a| std::path::PathBuf::from(a).join("com.differentai.openwork"))
}

fn parse_openwork_ports(raw: &str) -> Vec<(String, u16)> {
    let Ok(v) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(raw) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let Some(map) = v.get("workspacePorts").and_then(|x| x.as_object()) else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    for (k, val) in map {
        let Some(port) = val.as_u64() else { continue };
        if (1..=65535).contains(&port) {
            out.push((k.clone(), port as u16));
        }
    }
    out
}

fn port_for_workspace(ports: &[(String, u16)], path: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let key = path.replace('\\', "/").to_ascii_lowercase();
    ports
        .iter()
        .find(|(k, _)| k.replace('\\', "/").eq_ignore_ascii_case(&key))
        .map(|(_, p)| *p)
}

fn parse_openwork_grant(raw: &str) -> Option<OpenWorkGrant> {
    let v: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(raw).ok()?;
    let selected = v
        .get("activeId")
        .and_then(|x| x.as_str())
        .or_else(|| v.get("selectedId").and_then(|x| x.as_str()))
        .unwrap_or("");
    let list = v.get("workspaces")?.as_array()?;
    let pick = list
        .iter()
        .find(|w| w.get("id").and_then(|x| x.as_str()) == Some(selected));
    let pick = match pick {
        Some(w) => w,
        None if selected.is_empty() => list.first()?,
        None => return None,
    };
    let path = pick
        .get("path")
        .and_then(|x| x.as_str())
        .unwrap_or("")
        .trim();
    if path.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    Some(OpenWorkGrant {
        id: pick
            .get("id")
            .and_then(|x| x.as_str())
            .unwrap_or("")
            .to_string(),
        name: pick
            .get("name")
            .and_then(|x| x.as_str())
            .unwrap_or("")
            .to_string(),
        path: path.to_string(),
    })
}

fn read_openwork_ports() -> Vec<(String, u16)> {
    let Some(dir) = openwork_roaming() else {
        return Vec::new();
    };
    std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("openwork-server-state.json"))
        .ok()
        .map(|raw| parse_openwork_ports(&raw))
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

fn read_openwork_grant() -> Option<OpenWorkGrant> {
    let dir = openwork_roaming()?;
    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("openwork-workspaces.json")).ok()?;
    parse_openwork_grant(&raw)
}

fn loopback_get_json(port: u16, path: &str) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    use std::io::{Read, Write};
    use std::net::{IpAddr, Ipv4Addr, SocketAddr, TcpStream};
    if port == 0 {
        return Err("NO_PORT".into());
    }
    if !path.starts_with('/')
        || path.contains("://")
        || path.contains('\r')
        || path.contains('\n')
        || path.contains(' ')
    {
        return Err("BAD_PATH".into());
    }
    let addr = SocketAddr::new(IpAddr::V4(Ipv4Addr::LOCALHOST), port);
    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&addr, Duration::from_millis(400))
        .map_err(|e| format!("connect 127.0.0.1:{port}: {e}"))?;
    stream
        .set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_millis(600)))
        .ok();
    stream
        .set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_millis(400)))
        .ok();
    let req = format!(
        "GET {path} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: 127.0.0.1:{port}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n"
    );
    stream
        .write_all(req.as_bytes())
        .map_err(|e| format!("write: {e}"))?;
    let mut buf = Vec::new();
    stream
        .read_to_end(&mut buf)
        .map_err(|e| format!("read: {e}"))?;
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf);
    let body = text
        .split("\r\n\r\n")
        .nth(1)
        .or_else(|| text.split("\n\n").nth(1))
        .unwrap_or(&text);
    serde_json::from_str(body.trim()).map_err(|_| "health body is not JSON".into())
}

fn focus_openwork(pids: &[u32]) -> (u32, String) {
    let mut last_err = "no pid".to_string();
    for &pid in pids {
        match focus_pid(pid) {
            Ok(()) => {
                return (
                    pid,
                    format!("focused OpenWork pid {pid} — did not spawn"),
                )
            }
            Err(e) => last_err = e,
        }
    }
    let pid = pids.first().copied().unwrap_or(0);
    (
        pid,
        format!("OpenWork pid {pid} is live; focus: {last_err} — did not spawn"),
    )
}

fn spawn_openwork(exe: &std::path::Path) -> Result<u32, String> {
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        use std::os::windows::process::CommandExt;
        const DETACHED_PROCESS: u32 = 0x00000008;
        const CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB: u32 = 0x01000000;
        cmd.creation_flags(DETACHED_PROCESS | CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB);
    }
    let child = cmd
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|e| format!("launch OpenWork: {e}"))?;
    Ok(child.id())
}

fn openwork_snapshot(
    action: &str,
    pid: Option<u32>,
    pids: Vec<u32>,
    extra: &str,
) -> OpenWorkStatus {
    let exe = openwork_exe();
    let grant = read_openwork_grant();
    let ports = read_openwork_ports();
    let port = grant
        .as_ref()
        .and_then(|g| port_for_workspace(&ports, &g.path));
    let cosmos_grant = grant
        .as_ref()
        .map(|g| looks_like_repo(std::path::Path::new(&g.path)))
        .unwrap_or(false);
    let mut version = None;
    let mut opencode_version = None;
    let mut health_ok = false;
    let mut health_note = String::new();
    if let Some(p) = port {
        match loopback_get_json(p, "/health") {
            Ok(v) => {
                health_ok = v.get("ok").and_then(|x| x.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false);
                version = v
                    .get("version")
                    .and_then(|x| x.as_str())
                    .map(|s| s.to_string());
                opencode_version = v
                    .get("opencodeVersion")
                    .and_then(|x| x.as_str())
                    .map(|s| s.to_string());
                if !health_ok {
                    health_note = " /health ok=false".into();
                }
            }
            Err(e) => health_note = format!(" /health {e}"),
        }
    } else if extra.contains("launched") {
        health_note = " server port not in openwork-server-state.json yet".into();
    } else {
        health_note = " no server port in openwork-server-state.json".into();
    }
    let mut detail = extra.to_string();
    if cosmos_grant {
        detail = format!(
            "{detail} — grant looks like the COSMOS tree; OpenWork must not hold that pen"
        );
    }
    if !health_note.is_empty() {
        detail = format!("{detail}{health_note}");
    }
    if detail.is_empty() {
        detail = OPENWORK_EMBED.to_string();
    } else {
        detail = format!("{detail}. {OPENWORK_EMBED}");
    }
    let live = !pids.is_empty();
    OpenWorkStatus {
        action: action.into(),
        pid,
        pids,
        detail,
        version,
        opencode_version,
        port,
        workspace: grant.as_ref().map(|g| g.path.clone()),
        workspace_id: grant.as_ref().map(|g| g.id.clone()),
        exe: exe.as_ref().map(|p| p.display().to_string()),
        installed: exe.is_some(),
        live,
        health_ok,
        cosmos_grant,
        embed: OPENWORK_EMBED.into(),
        snapped: false,
        needs_stream: false,
        live_session: false,
    }
}

fn cdeck_and_work_rects(app: &AppHandle) -> Result<(PxRect, PxRect), String> {
    let win = app
        .get_webview_window("main")
        .ok_or_else(|| "NO_WINDOW: cDeck main window missing".to_string())?;
    let pos = win
        .outer_position()
        .map_err(|e| format!("cDeck position: {e}"))?;
    let size = win.outer_size().map_err(|e| format!("cDeck size: {e}"))?;
    let cdeck = PxRect {
        x: pos.x,
        y: pos.y,
        w: size.width,
        h: size.height,
    };
    let work = match win.current_monitor() {
        Ok(Some(mon)) => {
            let wa = mon.work_area();
            PxRect {
                x: wa.position.x,
                y: wa.position.y,
                w: wa.size.width,
                h: wa.size.height,
            }
        }
        _ => cdeck,
    };
    Ok((cdeck, work))
}

fn apply_cdeck_rect(app: &AppHandle, r: PxRect) -> Result<(), String> {
    let win = app
        .get_webview_window("main")
        .ok_or_else(|| "NO_WINDOW: cDeck main window missing".to_string())?;
    let _ = win.unmaximize();
    win.set_position(PhysicalPosition::new(r.x, r.y))
        .map_err(|e| format!("cDeck set_position: {e}"))?;
    win.set_size(PhysicalSize::new(r.w, r.h))
        .map_err(|e| format!("cDeck set_size: {e}"))?;
    Ok(())
}

fn apply_openwork_rect(pid: u32, r: PxRect) -> Result<(), String> {
    let script = format!(
        "$p = Get-Process -Id {pid} -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue; \
         if (-not $p) {{ exit 2 }}; \
         Add-Type -Name W -Namespace CdeckSnap -MemberDefinition '[DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern bool SetWindowPos(IntPtr h, IntPtr i, int x, int y, int cx, int cy, uint f); [DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern bool ShowWindow(IntPtr h, int n);'; \
         $h = $p.MainWindowHandle; \
         if ($h -eq [IntPtr]::Zero) {{ exit 3 }}; \
         [CdeckSnap.W]::ShowWindow($h, 9) | Out-Null; \
         if (-not [CdeckSnap.W]::SetWindowPos($h, [IntPtr]::Zero, {x}, {y}, {w}, {h}, 0x0040)) {{ exit 4 }}",
        x = r.x,
        y = r.y,
        w = r.w,
        h = r.h,
    );
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("powershell");
    cmd.args(["-NoProfile", "-WindowStyle", "Hidden", "-Command", &script]);
    #[cfg(windows)]
    hide_console(&mut cmd);
    let status = cmd.status().map_err(|e| format!("snap window: {e}"))?;
    if status.success() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(format!("snap pid {pid} failed (exit {status})"))
    }
}

fn snap_to_cdeck_right(app: &AppHandle, pid: u32, label: &str) -> (bool, String) {
    let Ok((cdeck, work)) = cdeck_and_work_rects(app) else {
        return (false, "cDeck rect UNMEASURED — did not snap".into());
    };
    let (cd, ow) = tile_openwork(cdeck, work);
    let mut bits = Vec::new();
    if cd != cdeck {
        match apply_cdeck_rect(app, cd) {
            Ok(()) => bits.push("cDeck tiled left".into()),
            Err(e) => bits.push(e),
        }
    }
    let mut snapped = false;
    for _ in 0..20 {
        match apply_openwork_rect(pid, ow) {
            Ok(()) => {
                snapped = true;
                bits.push(format!(
                    "{label} snapped to cDeck right edge {}x{}+{}+{}",
                    ow.w, ow.h, ow.x, ow.y
                ));
                break;
            }
            Err(_) => std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(150)),
        }
    }
    if !snapped {
        bits.push(format!("{label} hwnd not ready to snap (pid {pid})"));
    }
    (snapped, bits.join(" — "))
}

fn snap_pair(app: &AppHandle, ow_pid: u32) -> (bool, String) {
    snap_to_cdeck_right(app, ow_pid, "OpenWork")
}

#[tauri::command]
fn openwork_status() -> OpenWorkStatus {
    let pids = image_pids("OpenWork.exe");
    let action = if pids.is_empty() { "idle" } else { "live" };
    let pid = pids.first().copied();
    let extra = if pids.is_empty() {
        "no OpenWork.exe — OPEN launches the installed desktop"
    } else {
        "OpenWork live — OPEN focuses, does not spawn"
    };
    openwork_snapshot(action, pid, pids, extra)
}

#[tauri::command]
fn open_openwork(app: AppHandle) -> Result<OpenWorkStatus, String> {
    let pids = image_pids("OpenWork.exe");
    match openwork_decision(&pids) {
        CodingAction::Focus(_) => {
            let (pid, msg) = focus_openwork(&pids);
            let (snapped, snap_msg) = snap_pair(&app, pid);
            let mut rec = openwork_snapshot(
                "focused",
                Some(pid),
                pids,
                &format!("{msg}. {snap_msg}. Current OpenWork session goes live with this cDeck."),
            );
            rec.snapped = snapped;
            rec.live_session = true;
            rec.needs_stream = false;
            Ok(rec)
        }
        CodingAction::Launch => {
            let exe = openwork_exe().ok_or_else(|| {
                "NOT_INSTALLED: OpenWork.exe is not under %LOCALAPPDATA%\\Programs\\@openworkdesktop"
                    .to_string()
            })?;
            let pid = spawn_openwork(&exe)?;
            let mut wait_pid = pid;
            for _ in 0..20 {
                let live = image_pids("OpenWork.exe");
                if let Some(&p) = live.first() {
                    wait_pid = p;
                    break;
                }
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(150));
            }
            let (snapped, snap_msg) = snap_pair(&app, wait_pid);
            let mut rec = openwork_snapshot(
                "launched",
                Some(wait_pid),
                vec![wait_pid],
                &format!(
                    "launched OpenWork pid {wait_pid}. {snap_msg}. Pick a stream — TidyUP/TU2 reconstructs BU if the last shutdown was partial."
                ),
            );
            rec.snapped = snapped;
            rec.live_session = false;
            rec.needs_stream = true;
            Ok(rec)
        }
    }
}

/// P7 — GEM search is Chrome **Ask Gemini** side panel on Keith BBF
/// `Profile 2` + Alt+G. Not `gemini.google.com/app` (Sign in; measured).
/// Not Vertex. Browser-served deck refuses and copies the query.
const GEM_PROFILE_DIR: &str = "Profile 2";
const GEM_WRONG_HOST: &str = "gemini.google.com/app";

#[derive(Serialize)]
#[serde(rename_all = "camelCase")]
struct GemSearchOpen {
    action: String,
    pid: Option<u32>,
    profile: String,
    query_copied: bool,
    detail: String,
}

fn chrome_exe() -> Option<std::path::PathBuf> {
    let pf = std::env::var("ProgramFiles").ok()?;
    let p = std::path::PathBuf::from(pf)
        .join("Google")
        .join("Chrome")
        .join("Application")
        .join("chrome.exe");
    if p.is_file() {
        Some(p)
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn gem_chrome_args() -> Vec<String> {
    vec![format!("--profile-directory={GEM_PROFILE_DIR}")]
}

fn clipboard_set(text: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
    use std::io::Write;
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("powershell");
    cmd.args([
        "-NoProfile",
        "-WindowStyle",
        "Hidden",
        "-Command",
        "$input | Set-Clipboard",
    ]);
    cmd.stdin(std::process::Stdio::piped());
    #[cfg(windows)]
    hide_console(&mut cmd);
    let mut child = cmd
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|e| format!("clipboard spawn: {e}"))?;
    if let Some(mut sin) = child.stdin.take() {
        sin.write_all(text.as_bytes())
            .map_err(|e| format!("clipboard write: {e}"))?;
    }
    let st = child
        .wait()
        .map_err(|e| format!("clipboard wait: {e}"))?;
    if st.success() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(format!("clipboard exit {st}"))
    }
}

fn send_alt_g() -> Result<(), String> {
    let script = "Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms; \
         Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500; \
         [System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait('%g')";
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new("powershell");
    cmd.args(["-NoProfile", "-WindowStyle", "Hidden", "-Command", script]);
    #[cfg(windows)]
    hide_console(&mut cmd);
    let st = cmd.status().map_err(|e| format!("Alt+G: {e}"))?;
    if st.success() {
        Ok(())
    } else {
        Err(format!("Alt+G exit {st}"))
    }
}

#[tauri::command]
fn open_gem_search(query: Option<String>) -> Result<GemSearchOpen, String> {
    let q = query.unwrap_or_default();
    let q = q.trim();
    let copied = if !q.is_empty() {
        clipboard_set(q).is_ok()
    } else {
        false
    };
    let exe = chrome_exe().ok_or_else(|| {
        "NO_CHROME: Chrome.exe not under Program Files — cannot open Profile 2 side panel"
            .to_string()
    })?;
    let args = gem_chrome_args();
    debug_assert!(
        !args.iter().any(|a| a.contains(GEM_WRONG_HOST)),
        "P7 must never launch gemini.google.com/app"
    );
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(&exe);
    cmd.args(&args);
    let child = cmd
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|e| format!("launch Chrome Profile 2: {e}"))?;
    let pid = child.id();
    let alt = send_alt_g();
    let alt_note = match alt {
        Ok(()) => "Alt+G sent for Ask Gemini".to_string(),
        Err(e) => format!("Alt+G UNMEASURED ({e}) — press Alt+G"),
    };
    let clip_note = if q.is_empty() {
        "no query".to_string()
    } else if copied {
        "query on clipboard — paste in the side panel".to_string()
    } else {
        "query copy failed — type it in the side panel".to_string()
    };
    Ok(GemSearchOpen {
        action: "launched".into(),
        pid: Some(pid),
        profile: GEM_PROFILE_DIR.into(),
        query_copied: copied,
        detail: format!(
            "Chrome {GEM_PROFILE_DIR} pid {pid} — {alt_note}. {clip_note}. Not {GEM_WRONG_HOST}. Not Vertex."
        ),
    })
}

/// Load the install bearer from a sentinel-verified runtime root.
/// Memory only — the webview must not persist it (forbidden_deck_key).
/// Error strings never include the token bytes.
#[tauri::command]
fn load_install_token(root: String) -> Result<String, String> {
    let root = root.trim();
    if root.is_empty() {
        return Err("NO_ROOT: no runtime root".into());
    }
    let root_path = std::path::Path::new(root);
    if !root_path.is_absolute() {
        return Err("NO_ROOT: runtime root is not an absolute path".into());
    }
    if root_tree_id(root_path).is_err() {
        return Err("IDENTITY_MISMATCH: that path is not a COSMOS runtime root".into());
    }
    let path = root_path.join("config").join("api_token.txt");
    let raw = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).map_err(|_| {
        "NOT_FOUND: install token file is missing (config/api_token.txt)".to_string()
    })?;
    let tok = raw.trim().to_string();
    if tok.is_empty() {
        return Err("EMPTY: install token file is blank".into());
    }
    if tok.len() > 8192 {
        return Err("TOO_LARGE: install token file is not a bearer".into());
    }
    Ok(tok)
}

fn normalize_profile(raw: &str) -> Result<&'static str, String> {
    let s = raw.trim().to_ascii_lowercase();
    DECK_PROFILES
        .iter()
        .copied()
        .find(|id| *id == s)
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            format!("UNKNOWN_PROFILE: {raw} — not a COSMOS2 profile (docs/PROFILES.md)")
        })
}

fn title_for_profile(profile: &str) -> String {
    let pretty = match profile {
        "ups" => "UPS".to_string(),
        "website" => "Website GC".to_string(),
        other => {
            let mut c = other.chars();
            match c.next() {
                Some(f) => format!("{}{}", f.to_ascii_uppercase(), c.as_str()),
                None => other.to_string(),
            }
        }
    };
    format!("cDeck — {pretty}")
}

fn next_profile_label(app: &AppHandle, profile: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let mut n = 0usize;
    for w in app.webview_windows().keys() {
        if w.starts_with("profile-") {
            n += 1;
        }
    }
    if n >= MAX_PROFILE_WINDOWS {
        return Err(format!(
            "INSTANCE_CAP: {MAX_PROFILE_WINDOWS} profile windows already open — not a second Core"
        ));
    }
    for i in 1..=MAX_PROFILE_WINDOWS {
        let label = format!("profile-{profile}-{i}");
        if app.get_webview_window(&label).is_none() {
            return Ok(label);
        }
    }
    Err("INSTANCE_CAP: no free profile label".into())
}

/// `tree_id` is only ever the one the caller already read back from
/// `GET /api/v1/status`. A window that has not connected yet carries none, and
/// the record says so rather than guessing which tree it is bound to.
fn measured_tree_id(raw: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
    let t = raw.map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty())?;
    if t.len() > 200 {
        return None;
    }
    Some(t.to_string())
}

/// Stamp the instance before first paint. Query strings on an App PathBuf are
/// not portable, so the profile and the Core the opener is on ride in on an
/// initialization script; JS also reads `?profile=` for `/cdeck/` over HTTP.
fn instance_boot_script(profile: &str, core_base: &str) -> String {
    format!(
        "window.__CDECK_PROFILE__={};window.__CDECK_CORE_BASE__={};",
        serde_json::to_string(profile).unwrap_or_else(|_| "\"forge\"".into()),
        serde_json::to_string(core_base).unwrap_or_else(|_| "\"\"".into())
    )
}

fn instance_record(
    profile: &str,
    label: &str,
    core_base: &str,
    tree_id: Option<String>,
) -> serde_json::Value {
    serde_json::json!({
        "schema": "cdeck-instance/1",
        "profile": profile,
        "label": label,
        "core_base": core_base,
        "peer": serde_json::Value::Null,
        "tree_id": match tree_id {
            Some(t) => serde_json::Value::String(t),
            None => serde_json::Value::Null,
        },
        "note": "Same Core, second client. A federated peer host is NO_HOST until Keith names one. Does not spawn a second Core.",
    })
}

/// Open a parallel profile window. Same Core (or a named peer later). Not a
/// second `cosmos.py serve`. Peer host stays unset — federation is NO_HOST
/// until Keith names an address.
#[tauri::command]
fn open_profile_window(
    app: AppHandle,
    profile: String,
    core_base: Option<String>,
    tree_id: Option<String>,
) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    let profile = normalize_profile(&profile)?;
    let base = match core_base.as_deref().map(str::trim).filter(|s| !s.is_empty()) {
        Some(raw) => sanitize_url(raw)?,
        None => DEFAULT_URL.to_string(),
    };
    if is_trial_kernel_url(&base) {
        return Err(
            "REFUSED: trial kernel :8791 is not a federated peer and not live Core".into(),
        );
    }
    let label = next_profile_label(&app, profile)?;
    WebviewWindowBuilder::new(&app, &label, WebviewUrl::App("index.html".into()))
        .title(title_for_profile(profile))
        .inner_size(1600.0, 980.0)
        .initialization_script(&instance_boot_script(profile, &base))
        .build()
        .map_err(|e| format!("window: {e}"))?;
    Ok(instance_record(
        profile,
        &label,
        &base,
        measured_tree_id(tree_id.as_deref()),
    ))
}

/// Attached cDeck window for Open Sessions, Backup, or Talk/OpenWork.
/// Talk also focuses or launches OpenWork.exe as a companion window.
#[tauri::command]
fn open_pane_window(app: AppHandle, pane: String) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    let raw = pane.trim().to_lowercase();
    let pane = match raw.as_str() {
        "sessions" | "recents" => "recents",
        "backup" => "backup",
        "talk" | "openwork" | "open" => "open",
        _ => return Err(format!("REFUSED: unknown attached pane {raw}")),
    };
    let mut openwork: Option<OpenWorkStatus> = None;
    if pane == "open" {
        openwork = open_openwork(app.clone()).ok();
    }
    let title = if pane == "backup" {
        "cDeck — Backup"
    } else if pane == "open" {
        "cDeck — Talk / OpenWork"
    } else {
        "cDeck — Open Sessions"
    };
    let n = std::time::SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .map(|d| d.as_secs())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let label = format!("pane-{pane}-{n}");
    let boot = format!(
        "window.__CDECK_PANE__={};",
        serde_json::to_string(pane).unwrap_or_else(|_| "\"recents\"".into())
    );
    WebviewWindowBuilder::new(&app, &label, WebviewUrl::App("index.html".into()))
        .title(title)
        .inner_size(1280.0, 860.0)
        .initialization_script(&boot)
        .build()
        .map_err(|e| format!("window: {e}"))?;
    let note = if pane == "open" {
        "Attached cDeck Talk window plus OpenWork.exe companion. Not an iframe."
    } else {
        "Attached cDeck window. Same Core. Does not spawn OpenWork.exe."
    };
    Ok(serde_json::json!({
        "schema": "cdeck-pane/1",
        "pane": pane,
        "label": label,
        "openwork": openwork,
        "note": note,
    }))
}

#[cfg_attr(mobile, tauri::mobile_entry_point)]
pub fn run() {
    tauri::Builder::default()
        .invoke_handler(tauri::generate_handler![
            load_config,
            save_config,
            load_deck,
            save_deck,
            read_projection,
            api_request,
            open_coding,
            open_ccr,
            coding_status,
            list_coding_sessions,
            open_openwork,
            open_orc,
            openwork_status,
            open_gem_search,
            load_install_token,
            open_profile_window,
            open_pane_window
        ])
        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
        .expect("cDeck failed to start");
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn profile_normalize_is_the_profiles_md_list() {
        assert_eq!(normalize_profile("Forge").unwrap(), "forge");
        assert_eq!(normalize_profile("CRUCIBLE").unwrap(), "crucible");
        assert_eq!(normalize_profile("website").unwrap(), "website");
        assert!(normalize_profile("chat").unwrap_err().contains("UNKNOWN_PROFILE"));
        assert!(normalize_profile("").unwrap_err().contains("UNKNOWN_PROFILE"));
        assert_eq!(title_for_profile("forge"), "cDeck — Forge");
        assert_eq!(title_for_profile("crucible"), "cDeck — Crucible");
        assert_eq!(title_for_profile("website"), "cDeck — Website GC");
        assert_eq!(title_for_profile("ups"), "cDeck — UPS");
    }

    #[test]
    fn tree_id_is_measured_or_absent_never_guessed() {
        assert_eq!(measured_tree_id(None), None);
        assert_eq!(measured_tree_id(Some("   ")), None);
        assert_eq!(measured_tree_id(Some("x".repeat(201).as_str())), None);
        assert_eq!(
            measured_tree_id(Some("  KMesh-COSMOS-live  ")),
            Some("KMesh-COSMOS-live".to_string())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn instance_record_leaves_the_peer_unnamed() {
        let rec = instance_record("crucible", "profile-crucible-1", DEFAULT_URL, None);
        assert_eq!(rec["schema"], "cdeck-instance/1");
        assert_eq!(rec["profile"], "crucible");
        assert_eq!(rec["core_base"], DEFAULT_URL);
        assert!(rec["peer"].is_null(), "federation stays NO_HOST until named");
        assert!(rec["tree_id"].is_null(), "an unconnected window has no tree");

        let bound = instance_record(
            "forge",
            "profile-forge-1",
            DEFAULT_URL,
            measured_tree_id(Some("KMesh-COSMOS-live")),
        );
        assert_eq!(bound["tree_id"], "KMesh-COSMOS-live");
        assert!(bound["peer"].is_null());
    }

    #[test]
    fn boot_script_stamps_the_profile_and_the_same_core() {
        let s = instance_boot_script("crucible", "http://127.0.0.1:8770");
        assert!(s.contains("window.__CDECK_PROFILE__=\"crucible\""));
        assert!(s.contains("window.__CDECK_CORE_BASE__=\"http://127.0.0.1:8770\""));
        assert!(sanitize_url("http://x\";alert(1);//").is_err());
        let hostile = instance_boot_script("forge", "http://x\";alert(1);//");
        assert!(hostile.contains("\\\";alert"), "quote must be escaped: {hostile}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn trial_kernel_port_is_detected_without_becoming_the_default() {
        assert!(is_trial_kernel_url("http://127.0.0.1:8791"));
        assert!(is_trial_kernel_url("http://127.0.0.1:8791/"));
        assert!(!is_trial_kernel_url(DEFAULT_URL));
        assert!(!is_trial_kernel_url("http://127.0.0.1:8770"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn coding_c0_focuses_existing_and_does_not_spawn() {
        assert_eq!(coding_decision(&[27252, 18472], None), CodingAction::Focus(27252));
        assert_eq!(
            coding_decision(&[18472, 27252], Some(27252)),
            CodingAction::Focus(27252)
        );
        assert_eq!(coding_decision(&[], None), CodingAction::Launch);
        // stale cow pid + other grok live → focus the live one, never launch
        assert_eq!(
            coding_decision(&[18472], Some(7088)),
            CodingAction::Focus(18472)
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn openwork_c0_focuses_existing_and_does_not_spawn() {
        assert_eq!(openwork_decision(&[2472, 8616]), CodingAction::Focus(2472));
        assert_eq!(openwork_decision(&[]), CodingAction::Launch);
    }

    #[test]
    fn openwork_snaps_to_cdeck_right_edge_when_room() {
        let cdeck = PxRect { x: 0, y: 0, w: 1200, h: 800 };
        let work = PxRect { x: 0, y: 0, w: 1920, h: 1080 };
        let (cd, ow) = tile_openwork(cdeck, work);
        assert_eq!(cd, cdeck);
        assert_eq!(ow.x, 1200);
        assert_eq!(ow.y, 0);
        assert_eq!(ow.w, 720);
        assert_eq!(ow.h, 800);
    }

    #[test]
    fn openwork_tiles_work_area_when_cdeck_is_full_width() {
        let cdeck = PxRect { x: 0, y: 0, w: 1920, h: 1080 };
        let work = PxRect { x: 0, y: 0, w: 1920, h: 1080 };
        let (cd, ow) = tile_openwork(cdeck, work);
        assert_eq!(cd.x, 0);
        assert_eq!(ow.x, cd.w as i32);
        assert_eq!(cd.w + ow.w, 1920);
        assert!(ow.w >= 420);
        assert!(cd.w >= 480);
    }

    #[test]
    fn p7_gem_args_are_profile_2_not_gemini_web_app() {
        let args = gem_chrome_args();
        assert_eq!(args, vec!["--profile-directory=Profile 2".to_string()]);
        assert!(!args.iter().any(|a| a.contains("gemini.google.com")));
        assert!(!GEM_WRONG_HOST.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn openwork_ports_from_server_state() {
        let raw = r#"{"version":4,"workspacePorts":{"c:/users/papa/openwork chat":57383},"preferredPort":null}"#;
        let ports = parse_openwork_ports(raw);
        assert_eq!(ports, vec![("c:/users/papa/openwork chat".into(), 57383)]);
        assert_eq!(
            port_for_workspace(&ports, r"C:\Users\Papa\OpenWork Chat"),
            Some(57383)
        );
        assert_eq!(
            port_for_workspace(&ports, r"V:\OPENWORK\COSMOS 2"),
            None
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn openwork_grant_parser_keeps_path_drops_token_fields() {
        let raw = r#"{
          "selectedId": "ws_1",
          "activeId": "ws_1",
          "workspaces": [{
            "id": "ws_1",
            "name": "OpenWork Chat",
            "path": "C:\\Users\\Papa\\OpenWork Chat",
            "openworkToken": "secret-must-not-leak",
            "openworkClientToken": "also-secret"
          }]
        }"#;
        let g = parse_openwork_grant(raw).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(g.path, r"C:\Users\Papa\OpenWork Chat");
        assert_eq!(g.id, "ws_1");
        assert_eq!(g.name, "OpenWork Chat");
        let dumped = format!("{g:?}");
        assert!(!dumped.contains("secret"));
        assert!(!dumped.contains("Token"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn openwork_grant_parser_reads_cosmos2_path() {
        let raw = r#"{
          "selectedId": "ws_726c64a2afa5",
          "activeId": "ws_726c64a2afa5",
          "workspaces": [{
            "id": "ws_726c64a2afa5",
            "name": "COSMOS_2",
            "path": "V:\\OPENWORK\\COSMOS_2"
          }]
        }"#;
        let g = parse_openwork_grant(raw).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(g.path, r"V:\OPENWORK\COSMOS_2");
        assert_eq!(g.name, "COSMOS_2");
        assert!(!looks_like_repo(std::path::Path::new(&g.path)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn openwork_grant_parser_reads_streams_path() {
        let raw = r#"{
          "selectedId": "ws_12669390bcf4",
          "activeId": "ws_12669390bcf4",
          "workspaces": [{
            "id": "ws_12669390bcf4",
            "name": "COSMOS",
            "path": "V:\\Streams\\openwork"
          }]
        }"#;
        let g = parse_openwork_grant(raw).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(g.path, r"V:\Streams\openwork");
        assert_eq!(g.name, "COSMOS");
        assert!(!looks_like_repo(std::path::Path::new(&g.path)));
    }

    #[test]

        assert_eq!(args, vec!["-c", "--cwd", r"V:\A\Ai\COSMOS"]);

    #[test]

        assert_eq!(args, vec!["--cwd", r"V:\A\Ai\COSMOS"]);

    #[test]

            std::path::Path::new(r"V:\A\Ai\COSMOS")
        ));
